# API index

Generated by `zig build docs` from the files `src/lcgf.zig` exports, in export order: every public type with its fields, and every public function and constant with the first sentence of its doc comment. The sources are the definitions; this is the map.

## `lcgf.simd` (src/core/simd.zig)

simd: vector width, column access and branch-free scalar selects.

### Top level

- `lanes: comptime_int = build_options.lanes orelse (std.simd.suggestVectorLength(f32) orelse if (small_mcu) 1 else 4)`
- `F = @Vector(lanes, f32)`
- `U = @Vector(lanes, u32)`
- `B = @Vector(lanes, bool)`
- `Bytes = @Vector(lanes, u8)`
- `Mask = @Int(.unsigned, lanes)`: One bit per lane.
- `iota: U = std.simd.iota(u32, lanes)`
- `fn padded(n: usize) usize`: Rows are rounded up to whole vectors so batch loops need no scalar tail.
- `fn rows(cap: usize) usize`: Rows to allocate per column for a table of `cap` rows (contract rule 1).
- `fn loadF(col: []const f32, i: usize) F`
- `fn storeF(col: []f32, i: usize, v: F) void`
- `fn loadU(col: []const u32, i: usize) U`
- `fn storeU(col: []u32, i: usize, v: U) void`
- `fn f(x: f32) F`
- `fn u(x: u32) U`
- `fn bits(m: B) Mask`
- `fn selU(c: bool, a: u32, b: u32) u32`: `c ? a : b` without a branch.
- `fn selF(c: bool, a: f32, b: f32) f32`: `c ? a : b` without a branch.
- `fn maxF(a: anytype, b: @TypeOf(a)) @TypeOf(a)`: `a > b ? a : b`, for f32 or a vector of f32: a tie or a NaN `a` gives `b`, the same on every target.
- `fn minF(a: anytype, b: @TypeOf(a)) @TypeOf(a)`: `a < b ? a : b`: as `maxF`, a tie or a NaN `a` gives `b`.
- `fn b2f(c: bool) f32`: 1.0 or 0.0, built from the bit pattern so that it stays a mask operation instead of becoming a compare-and-jump.
- `fn andB(a: bool, b: bool) bool`: `a and b` as one bitwise AND, with no short-circuit jump.
- `fn orB(a: bool, b: bool) bool`: `a or b` as one bitwise OR.
- `fn find(col: []const u32, len: u32, needle: u32) u32`: First index of `needle` in `col[0..len]`, or `none`.
- `fn rank(col: []const u32, len: u32, bound: u32) u32`: Number of values in `col[0..len]` that are below `bound`.
- `fn emit(comptime T: type, out: []T, w: u32, v: T, keep: bool) u32`: One step of a filtering loop: `out[w] = v` always, then the cursor moves by 0 or 1.
- `fn compact(cols: anytype, len: u32, key: []const u32, want: u32) u32`: Stream compaction over a set of columns: keeps, in order, the rows `r` of every slice field of `cols` for which `key[r] == want`, and returns the new length.

## `lcgf.input` (src/core/input.zig)

Input: one plain struct per frame, filled by the host from any window layer.

### `Input` (struct)
Everything the core reads from the host for one frame.

Fields: `time`, `dt`, `viewport`, `scale`, `pointer`, `buttons`, `wheel`, `scroll`, `mods`, `keys`, `text`, `events`, `contacts`, `safe_area`, `appearance`, `primary_pointer`, `redraw`

- `outside: [2]f32 = .{ -1, -1 }`: `pointer` when the pointer is outside the window.
- `Buttons = bitpack struct(u8) { left: bool = false, right: bool = false, middle: bool = false, _: u5 = 0 }`: Mouse buttons held down this frame, one bit each.
- `Mods = bitpack struct(u8) { shift: bool = false, ctrl: bool = false, alt: bool = false, cmd: bool = false, _: u4 = 0 }`: Modifier keys held down this frame, one bit each.
- `Pointer = enum(u8) { fine, coarse }`
- `none = types.none`: No contact has the id (`contact` result).
- `fn contact(in: *const Input, id: u32) u32`: Row of the contact with pointer id `id` in `contacts`, or `none`.
- `fn typed(in: *const Input, e: Event) []const u8`: The UTF-8 a `.text` event typed; empty for key events.
- `fn stamp(in: *const Input) u64`: Fingerprint of everything except the clock.

### `Input.Keys` (struct)
256 key states as a bit set.

Fields: `bits`

- `fn press(k: *Keys, code: u8) void`
- `fn down(k: *const Keys, code: u8) bool`

### `Input.Appearance` (struct)
The system's appearance setting, as far as the shell can read it.

Fields: `dark`, `accent`


### `Input.Event` (struct)
One key transition or one piece of typed text.

Fields: `kind`, `mods`, `code`, `_pad`, `at`, `len`

- `Kind = enum(u8) { press, release, repeat, text }`

### `Input.Contact` (struct)
One finger or pen.

Fields: `id`, `kind`, `down`, `_pad`, `pos`, `pressure`, `tilt`

- `Kind = enum(u8) { touch, pen, eraser }`

### `Replay` (struct)
Recorded input, replayed one frame per `next` call, so tests can drive the core with input captured from a host.

Fields: `src`, `at`, `line`, `in`, `started`

- `Buffers = struct { text: []u8, events: []Input.Event, contacts: []Input.Contact }`: Caller-owned storage for the per-frame slices of the returned Input.
- `Error = error{ Syntax, BufferFull }`
- `fn next(r: *Replay, buf: Buffers) Error!?*const Input`: The next frame, or null after the last.

## `lcgf.types` (src/core/types.zig)

Small value types of the core: identities, indices, colours, rects, sizing and style rows.

### Top level

- `none: u32 = 0xffff_ffff`: "No index" for every u32 link in the core.
- `Axis = enum(u1) { x = 0, y = 1 }`
- `inf = std.math.inf(f32)`
- `Texture = enum(u16) { white = 0, _ }`
- `Material = enum(u16) { solid = 0, _ }`
- `Font = enum(u16) { default = 0, _ }`

### `Id` (enum)
Stable identity of a widget across frames.

- `fn from(comptime name: []const u8) Id`: Compile-time hash of a name: `Id.from("save")`.
- `fn src(comptime loc: std.lang.SourceLocation) Id`: Compile-time hash of a call site: `Id.src(@src())`.
- `fn hash(bytes: []const u8) Id`: Run-time hash, for ids built from data.
- `fn with(self: Id, n: u32) Id`: Derives a child id, for widgets created in a loop: `row_id.with(i)`.

### `Node` (enum)
Index of a node in the current frame's `Tree`.

- `fn ix(n: Node) u32`

### `Color` (struct)
RGBA8, laid out so the bytes in memory are r, g, b, a on little-endian hosts.

Fields: `r`, `g`, `b`, `a`

- `transparent: Color = .{}`
- `white: Color = .{ .r = 255, .g = 255, .b = 255, .a = 255 }`
- `black: Color = .{ .a = 255 }`
- `fn rgb(r: u8, g: u8, b: u8) Color`
- `fn rgba(r: u8, g: u8, b: u8, a: u8) Color`
- `fn hex(v: u32) Color`: 0xRRGGBBAA
- `fn bitsOf(c: Color) u32`

### `Rect` (struct)

Fields: `x0`, `y0`, `x1`, `y1`

- `fn w(r: Rect) f32`
- `fn h(r: Rect) f32`
- `fn array(r: Rect) [4]f32`
- `fn intersect(a: Rect, b: Rect) Rect`: Stored as (x0, y0, -x1, -y1), the intersection of two rects is one @max.
- `fn contains(r: Rect, x: f32, y: f32) bool`

### `Size` (struct)
How a node is sized along one axis.

Fields: `kind`, `v`

- `Kind = enum(u8) { fit, px, grow, pct }`
- `fit: Size = .{}`: As large as the content.
- `fill: Size = .{ .kind = .grow, .v = 1 }`: Take all free space (weight 1).
- `c_max = [4]f32{ inf, 0, inf, inf }`
- `fn px(v: f32) Size`: Exactly `v` pixels.
- `fn grow(weight: f32) Size`: A share of the parent's free space on its main axis; stretch on its cross axis.
- `fn pct(fraction: f32) Size`: A fraction (0..1) of the parent's inner size.

### `Align` (enum)

- `factor = [3]f32{ 0, 0.5, 1 }`: Share of the leftover space placed before the children, indexed by the tag.

### `Sense` (struct)
What kinds of pointer interaction a node takes part in.

Fields: `hover`, `click`, `drag`, `focus`

- `button: Sense = .{ .hover = true, .click = true, .focus = true }`
- `any: Sense = .{ .hover = true, .click = true, .drag = true, .focus = true }`

### `Response` (struct)
Result of `Frame.interact`.

Fields: `hovered`, `held`, `pressed`, `released`, `clicked`, `focused`


### `Role` (enum)
What a node is to assistive technology.


### `Cursor` (enum)
Handles the renderer gives out.


### `Participation` (struct)
Everything a node can be told at creation.

Fields: `layout`, `paint`, `hit`, `semantics`

- `all: Participation = .{}`
- `hidden: Participation = .{ .paint = false, .hit = false, .semantics = false }`
- `collapsed: Participation = .{ .layout = false, .paint = false, .hit = false, .semantics = false }`

### `Style` (struct)

Fields: `participation`, `cursor`, `dir`, `size`, `min`, `max`, `pad`, `gap`, `main`, `cross`, `shrink`, `float`, `offset`, `clip`, `scroll`, `fill`, `border_color`, `border`, `radius`, `texture`, `material`, `params`, `radii`, `opacity`, `transform`, `transform_origin`, `uv`, `paint_reference`, `paint_outset`, `role`, `value`


### `TextStyle` (struct)

Fields: `px`, `color`, `font`, `wrap`


## `lcgf.mem` (src/core/mem.zig)

Columns carved from a caller-owned buffer.

### Top level

- `column_align = 64`: Each column starts on its own cache line.
- `fn copyRows(comptime S: type, dst: *const S, dst_at: usize, src: *const S, src_at: usize, n: usize) void`: Copies rows [src_at, src_at + n) of every column in `src` to rows starting at `dst_at` in `dst`.
- `fn copyRowsExcept(comptime S: type, dst: *const S, dst_at: usize, src: *const S, src_at: usize, n: usize, comptime skip: []const []const u8) void`: `copyRows` of every column but those named in `skip`.

### `Carver` (struct)
Bump pointer over a caller-owned buffer.

Fields: `buf`, `off`, `zeroed`

- `fn slice(c: *Carver, comptime T: type, n: usize) []T`: Next `n` rows of `T`, zeroed.
- `fn columns(c: *Carver, comptime S: type, n: usize) S`: Fills a struct whose fields are `[]T` or `[k][]T` with zeroed columns of `n` rows each, carved in field order.

## `lcgf.tree` (src/core/tree.zig)

Tree: the node columns.

### Top level

- `Tree = TreeOf(u32, .slice)`: The runtime front end's tree: u32 indices, columns carved from the caller's buffer.
- `fn radiiOf(stored: [4]f32, radius: f32) [4]f32`: Corner radii as paint reads them: a stored row's own, or, for the shared defaults row's negative corners, the node's `Look.radius` on all four.
- `fn TreeOf(comptime Index: type, comptime storage: Storage) type`: A tree whose node links (`parent`, `end`, `push`'s rows) are `Index`, the smallest integer that fits the capacity, as `Ui().Index` is in prototype/comptime_ui_probe.zig.

### `Extra` (struct)
One row of a tree's paint extras (`Tree.Extras`).

Fields: `border`, `uv`, `radii`, `opacity`, `transform`, `transform_origin`, `paint_reference`, `paint_outset`, `prepared_texture`, `paint_revision`, `participation`, `active_participation`, `cursor`, `active_cursor`, `alpha`, `world`, `clip_radii`


### `Look` (struct)
How a node is painted.

Fields: `params`, `fill`, `border_color`, `radius`, `texture`, `material`


### `Template` (struct)
One node's row as `Tree.push` writes it, computed from a `Style`: sizing modes as coefficients, enums as factors, paint fields as their GPU row.

Fields: `min`, `max`, `grow`, `pct`, `pad_lo`, `pad_hi`, `offset`, `scroll`, `main`, `gap`, `flow`, `shrink`, `align_main`, `align_cross`, `clips`, `sense`, `look`, `role`, `value`, `text`, `custom`, `own`, `border`, `uv`, `radii`, `opacity`, `transform`, `transform_origin`, `paint_reference`, `paint_outset`, `prepared_texture`, `paint_revision`, `participation`, `cursor`

- `collapsed = .{ "min", "max", "grow", "pct", "pad_lo", "pad_hi", "flow", "gap" }`: The layout inputs a collapsed node (`Participation.layout` off) has zeroed: `pct` to -1, the others to 0 (`Tree.pushRow`; tools/abi-gen writes the bindings' copy from it).
- `fn from(s: *const Style) Template`

### `FlatRow` (struct)
A `Template` as the C ABI lays it out (`lcgf_row` in src/abi/lcgf.h): one 32-bit word per field, `look` spelled out.

Fields: `min`, `max`, `grow`, `pct`, `pad_lo`, `pad_hi`, `offset`, `scroll`, `main`, `gap`, `flow`, `shrink`, `align_main`, `align_cross`, `clips`, `sense`, `params`, `fill`, `border_color`, `radius`, `texture`, `material`, `role`, `value`, `text`, `custom`, `own`, `border`, `uv`, `radii`, `opacity`, `transform`, `transform_origin`, `paint_reference`, `paint_outset`, `prepared_texture`, `paint_revision`, `participation`, `cursor`


### `Storage` (union)
Where the columns live: slices carved from a caller's buffer (`init`), or arrays inside the tree value itself for a capacity known at compile time (`empty`), as the comptime front end keeps its tables.

Fields: `array`


### `TreeOf(..)` (generic struct)
A tree whose node links (`parent`, `end`, `push`'s rows) are `Index`, the smallest integer that fits the capacity, as `Ui().Index` is in prototype/comptime_ui_probe.zig.

Fields: `len`, `cap`, `overflow`, `c`, `x`, `xlen`, `wide`, `t`, `parent`, `id`

- `scratch = [_][]const u8{ "fit", "nat", "grow_sum", "grow_inv", "used", "gap_total", "cursor" }`: The layout scratch columns: every layout run rewrites them before reading them (`layout.prepare` zeroes or sets the accumulators, `fit` writes the rest), and nothing outside a layout run reads them.
- `fn rows(cap: u32) usize`: Rows allocated for `cap` nodes: whole vectors plus one spare, so batch passes may read and write one vector past `len`.
- `fn init(carver: *mem.Carver, cap: u32) Self`: Carves every column from `carver`.
- `fn initSharing(carver: *mem.Carver, cap: u32, other: *const Self) Self`: `init`, except the `scratch` columns are `other`'s, not carved: two trees that are never laid out at the same time (the runtime front end's two generations) share them.
- `fn reset(t: *Self) void`: Empties the tree for the next frame.
- `fn pushRow(t: *Self, parent_in: Index, id: Id, row: anytype) Index`: `push` from a style already turned into its row (`Template.from`, once per style rather than once per node): the row is copied as it is, one store per column.
- `fn cursor(t: *const Self, i: u32) ?types.Cursor`: Effective pointer shape, available while building without an extra ancestor walk.
- `fn participation(t: *const Self, i: u32) types.Participation`: Effective subtree participation, available immediately while building.
- `fn border(t: *const Self, i: u32) f32`: Border width of node `i` (`Style.border`).
- `fn seal(t: *Self, i: Index) void`: Closes node `i`: everything pushed since belongs to its subtree.
- `fn rect(t: *const Self, i: u32) Rect`
- `fn find(t: *const Self, id: Id) u32`: Row of the node with `id`, or `none`.
- `fn check(t: *const Self) Invalid!void`: Checks the structural invariants every pass relies on: rows in depth-first order, parent before child, and subtree ranges [i, end[i]) that nest exactly as the parent links say.
- `fn validate(t: *const Self) void`: `check`, asserted in Debug and ReleaseSafe; compiled out in ReleaseFast and ReleaseSmall.

### `TreeOf(..).Columns` (struct)
One column per property, grouped by the pass that reads them, so a pass touches only the cache lines it needs.

Fields: `id`, `parent`, `end`, `min`, `max`, `grow`, `pct`, `pad_lo`, `pad_hi`, `offset`, `scroll`, `gap`, `nkids`, `flow`, `shrink`, `align_main`, `align_cross`, `main`, `fit`, `nat`, `grow_sum`, `grow_inv`, `used`, `gap_total`, `cursor`, `size`, `pos`, `rect`, `vis`, `child_clip`, `visible`, `clips`, `sense`, `look`, `role`, `value`, `text`, `extra`, `scope`, `custom`


### `TreeOf(..).Extras` (struct)
Side table of the paint extras a few nodes set (docs/perf/port-perf.md): one row per node with a non-default `border`, `uv`, `radii`, `opacity` or `transform`, or with `clip`, appended by `push` in node order.

Fields: `node`, `row`


## `lcgf.strings` (src/core/strings.zig)

Strings: frame-lifetime byte storage for text, so callers may pass temporaries.

### Top level

- `Span = struct { start: u32 = 0, bytes: u32 = 0 }`: A byte range in a frame's `Strings`.

### `Strings` (struct)

Fields: `buf`, `len`, `overflow`

- `fn add(s: *Strings, bytes: []const u8) Span`: Copies `bytes` to the end of the buffer and returns where they landed.
- `fn print(s: *Strings, comptime fmt: []const u8, args: anytype) Span`: Formats `args` (`std.fmt`) straight into the end of the buffer, as `add` would copy the result: no caller buffer.
- `fn get(s: *const Strings, span: Span) []const u8`
- `fn reset(s: *Strings) void`: Empties the storage for the next frame.

## `lcgf.text_format` (src/core/fmt.zig)

fmt: the format of a formatted label (`spec.text("Volume {.1} dB", .{.volume})`, a `.zon` `.text`), one meaning in every front end: Zig's comptime UIs and seam, and the static UIs of C++ and Rust, whose frames are Zig's.

### Top level

- `Kind = enum(u8) { literal = 0, str = 1, bool = 2, u32 = 3, u8 = 4, f32 = 5 }`: What a segment writes.
- `Seg = struct { kind: Kind, prec: u8 = 0, arg: u32 = 0, start: u32 = 0, len: u32 = 0 }`: A piece of the output: format text (`start`, `len` into it), or argument `arg` written as `kind` (with `prec` decimals, an f32).
- `max_prec = 6`
- `fn maxSegs(text: []const u8) usize`: An upper bound on the segments of `text`: one per placeholder or brace, one per text run.
- `fn parse(text: []const u8, args: []const Kind, out: []Seg) Error!usize`: The segments of `text` for arguments of kinds `args`, into `out` (at least `maxSegs` long); returns how many.
- `fn kindOf(comptime T: type) ?Kind`: The kind a field of Zig type `T` formats as, or null when it cannot be an argument.
- `fn write(segs: anytype, text: []const u8, args: anytype, sink: *Sink) void`: Writes `segs` of `text` to `sink`, reading argument values from `args`, anything with `str(i) []const u8`, `boolean(i) bool`, `uint(i) u64` (a u32), `byte(i) u64` (a u8) and `float(i) f32` for argument i.
- `fn Fields(comptime State: type, comptime names: []const []const u8) type`: The arguments of a format as fields of a Zig value: argument i is field `names[i]` of `state`.
- `fn kindsOf(comptime State: type, comptime names: []const []const u8) [names.len]Kind`: The arguments' kinds for fields `names` of `State`; a compile error naming a field that is missing or cannot be an argument.
- `fn compile(comptime State: type, comptime text: []const u8, comptime names: []const []const u8) []const Seg`: The segments of `text` over fields `names` of `State`, checked while compiling.

### `Sink` (struct)
Where formatted bytes go: the rest of a buffer.

Fields: `buf`, `len`, `cut`

- `fn put(s: *Sink, bytes: []const u8) void`
- `fn putBool(s: *Sink, v: bool) void`
- `fn putUint(s: *Sink, v: u64) void`
- `fn putF32(s: *Sink, v: f32, prec: u8) void`: `v` with `prec` decimals, as the header says.

### `Fields(..)` (generic struct)
The arguments of a format as fields of a Zig value: argument i is field `names[i]` of `state`.

Fields: `state`

- `fn str(a: Self, i: u32) []const u8`
- `fn boolean(a: Self, i: u32) bool`
- `fn uint(a: Self, i: u32) u64`
- `fn byte(a: Self, i: u32) u64`
- `fn float(a: Self, i: u32) f32`

## `lcgf.texts` (src/core/texts.zig)

Texts: the text-run side list.

### `Texts` (struct)

Fields: `len`, `cap`, `overflow`, `c`

- `fn init(carver: *mem.Carver, cap: u32) Texts`: Carves every column from `carver`, `simd.rows(cap)` rows each.
- `fn reset(tx: *Texts) void`: Empties the list for the next frame.
- `fn push(tx: *Texts, node: u32, span: Span, ts: *const TextStyle) u32`: Appends a run of `span` for `node` and returns its row.

### `Texts.Columns` (struct)

Fields: `node`, `start`, `bytes`, `px`, `color`, `font`, `wrap`, `w`, `h`, `avail`


## `lcgf.customs` (src/core/customs.zig)

Customs: the custom-draw side list.

### `Customs` (struct)

Fields: `len`, `cap`, `overflow`, `c`

- `fn init(carver: *mem.Carver, cap: u32) Customs`: Carves every column from `carver`, `simd.rows(cap)` rows each.
- `fn reset(cu: *Customs) void`: Empties the list for the next frame.
- `fn push(cu: *Customs, node: u32, id: Id, data: u64) u32`: Appends a custom draw for `node` and returns its row.

### `Customs.Columns` (struct)

Fields: `node`, `id`, `data`


## `lcgf.layout` (src/layout/layout.zig)

layout: a fixed sequence of linear passes over the tree columns.

### Top level

- `pass_call: std.builtin.CallModifier = if (inline_passes) .always_inline else .auto`: SIMD.
- `fn prepare(t: anytype) void`
- `fn applyText(t: anytype, tx: *const Texts, axis: Axis) void`: Scatters measured text extents into their leaf nodes for one axis.
- `fn fit(t: anytype, axis: Axis) void`: Bottom-up: natural size of every node on one axis.
- `fn fitFlex(t: *Tree, fx: *const Flex, axis: Axis) void`: `fit` with the extra sizing rules of `fx` (flex.zig).
- `fn fitFlexOpts(t: *Tree, fx: *const Flex, axis: Axis, comptime opts: FlexOptions) void`: `fitFlex` with the features of `opts` compiled in.
- `fn fitSpan(t: anytype, axis: Axis, first: u32, last: u32) void`: `fit` over rows [first, last), any `first < last <= len`.
- `fn fitSpanFlex(t: *Tree, fx: *const Flex, axis: Axis, first: u32, last: u32, comptime opts: FlexOptions) void`
- `fn resolve(t: anytype, axis: Axis, viewport: f32) void`: Top-down: final size of every node on one axis.
- `fn resolveFlex(t: *Tree, fx: *const Flex, axis: Axis, viewport: f32) void`: `resolve` with the extra sizing rules of `fx` (flex.zig).
- `fn resolveSpan(t: anytype, axis: Axis, first: u32, last: u32) void`: `resolve` over rows [first, last), any `first < last <= len`: every row after `first` from its parent, in row order, with `first`'s own size taken as given and only read.
- `fn resolveSpanFlex(t: *Tree, fx: *const Flex, axis: Axis, first: u32, last: u32) void`: `resolveSpan` with the extra sizing rules of `fx` (flex.zig), for grids on a flex page.
- `fn textAvail(t: anytype, tx: *Texts) void`: Gathers the width layout gave each text run.
- `fn place(t: anytype) void`: Top-down: position of every node on both axes.
- `fn placeFlex(t: *Tree, fx: *const Flex) void`: `place` with the extra placement rules of `fx` (flex.zig).
- `fn placeFlexOpts(t: *Tree, fx: *const Flex, comptime opts: FlexOptions) void`: `placeFlex` with the features of `opts` compiled in.
- `fn placeSpan(t: anytype, first: u32, last: u32) void`: `place` over the subtree [first, last), `last = end[first]` (incremental layout): every row after `first` from its parent, with `first`'s own position taken as given.
- `fn finalize(t: anytype) void`: Legacy whole-pixel finalization.
- `fn finalizeWith(t: anytype, options: rounding.Options) error{InvalidScale}!void`: Snap cumulative endpoints on the selected device grid, then derive exact transforms, ancestor clips and hit visibility.
- `fn finalizeSpan(t: anytype, first: u32, last: u32) void`: Legacy finalization over an incremental subtree.
- `fn snapSubtree(t: anytype, node: u32) void`: Puts the subtree under `node` on the pixel grid from `node`'s snapped origin instead of the window's, after `finalize`: its descendants move by the distance `node` snapped, and are finalized again.
- `fn finalizeSpanWith(t: anytype, first: u32, last: u32, options: rounding.Options) error{InvalidScale}!void`
- `fn run(t: anytype, tx: *Texts, viewport: [2]f32) void`: Diagonal: every pass in order, for text that does not wrap (or whose `w`/`h` the caller has already settled).
- `fn runFlex(t: *Tree, fx: *const Flex, tx: *Texts, viewport: [2]f32) void`: `run` with the extra sizing rules of `fx` (flex.zig), and wrapping rows (wrap.zig).
- `fn runFlexOpts(t: *Tree, fx: *const Flex, tx: *Texts, viewport: [2]f32, comptime opts: FlexOptions) void`: `runFlex` with the features of `opts` compiled in.

## `lcgf.overlay` (src/layout/overlay.zig)

overlay: nodes that sit on top of other nodes instead of beside them.

### Top level

- `fn zstack(t: *Tree, i: u32) void`: Turns node `i` into a z-stack: its children overlap at its content origin, later rows on top, and it is as large as its largest child on both axes.
- `fn anchor(t: *Tree, an: *const Anchors, viewport: Rect) void`: Moves every anchored node, with its subtree, so that its pivot sits on its anchor point.
- `fn order(t: *const Tree, ly: *Layers) void`: Fills `layer` (one forward sweep) and `order`, a stable counting sort of the rows by layer: a histogram, a prefix sum over the 256 layers, and a scatter.
- `fn lastRect(prev: *const Tree, id: types.Id) Rect`: Where the node with `id` was laid out in `prev`, the previous frame's tree, or a zero rect if it was not there.

### `Anchor` (enum)
A point of a box: an edge midpoint, a corner or the centre.

- `fx = [9]f32{ 0, 0.5, 1, 0, 0.5, 1, 0, 0.5, 1 }`
- `fy = [9]f32{ 0, 0, 0, 0.5, 0.5, 0.5, 1, 1, 1 }`

### `Place` (struct)
One anchored placement as a value (CSS `right`/`bottom` insets, `position: fixed`), for `Anchors.place`: point `pivot` of the node on point `at` of the box, moved by `offset`.

Fields: `at`, `pivot`, `offset`, `viewport`, `flip`


### `Anchors` (struct)
The anchor side list: one row per anchored node, so the pass loops over anchors that exist instead of testing every node.

Fields: `len`, `cap`, `overflow`, `c`

- `fn init(carver: *mem.Carver, cap: u32) Anchors`: Carves every column from `carver`, `simd.rows(cap)` rows each.
- `fn reset(an: *Anchors) void`
- `fn push(an: *Anchors, node: u32, at: Anchor, pivot: Anchor, offset: [2]f32) u32`: Puts point `pivot` of `node` on point `at` of its parent's content box, then moves it by `offset`.
- `fn pushRect(an: *Anchors, node: u32, box: Rect, at: Anchor, pivot: Anchor, offset: [2]f32) u32`: Like `push`, but the box is the absolute rect `box`: typically another node's rect from the previous frame (`lastRect`), for a tooltip or popover that belongs to one part of the tree and is drawn next to another.
- `fn place(an: *Anchors, node: u32, p: Place, viewport: Rect) u32`: `push`, or `pushRect` over `viewport` for a `Place.viewport` placement, with its flip.
- `fn flip(an: *Anchors, k: u32, axes: [2]bool) void`: Lets anchor row `k` flip on the axes set in `axes`: when the node would cross an edge of the viewport and the mirrored placement would not, `at`, `pivot` and `offset` are taken from the other side (a menu hung below a button opens above it near the bottom of the window).

### `Anchors.Columns` (struct)

Fields: `node`, `at`, `pivot`, `offset`, `rel`, `box`, `flip`


### `Layers` (struct)
The layer column: which layer each node is painted in, and the paint order that follows.

Fields: `c`

- `fn init(carver: *mem.Carver, cap: u32) Layers`: One row per node of a tree of capacity `cap`.
- `fn reset(ly: *Layers) void`: Puts every node back in layer 0, for the next frame.

### `Layers.Columns` (struct)

Fields: `own`, `layer`, `order`


## `lcgf.scroll` (src/layout/scroll.zig)

scroll: what scroll containers need from layout.

### Top level

- `fn extent(t: *const Tree, out: [2][]f32) void`: SIMD.
- `fn clamp(t: *Tree, ext: [2][]const f32) void`: SIMD.
- `fn intoView(t: *const Tree, ext: [2][]const f32, container: u32, target: u32) [2]f32`: The scroll offset of `container` that brings `target`, one of its descendants, fully into view with the least movement: unchanged if it is already visible, else its near edge is put at the near inner edge (scrolling back) or its far edge at the far inner edge (scrolling on).
- `fn stick(t: *Tree, st: *const Sticky) void`: Moves each sticky node, with its subtree, so it does not go past its scroller's inner start edge (plus inset) when the content is scrolled, but never out of its parent's content box: a section header sticks at the top of a list until the end of its section pushes it out.

### `Sticky` (struct)
The sticky side list: one row per sticky node, as CSS `position: sticky` with a start inset.

Fields: `len`, `cap`, `overflow`, `c`

- `fn init(carver: *mem.Carver, cap: u32) Sticky`
- `fn reset(st: *Sticky) void`
- `fn push(st: *Sticky, node: u32, scroller: u32, inset: [2]f32, axes: [2]bool) u32`: Makes `node` stick `inset` pixels from the inner start edge of `scroller` (an ancestor) on the axes in `axes`.

### `Sticky.Columns` (struct)

Fields: `node`, `scroller`, `inset`, `on`


## `lcgf.grid` (src/layout/grid.zig)

grid: two-dimensional layout in rows and columns of tracks, after CSS Grid (https://www.w3.org/TR/css-grid-1/, track sizing), cut down to what bounded passes can do: every track is sized once, with no re-measure loop.

### Top level

- `AutoFlow = enum(u8) { row = 0, column = 1, row_dense = 2, column_dense = 3 }`
- `Distribution = enum(u8) { start, center, end, space_between, space_around, space_evenly }`
- `Repeat = enum { auto_fill, auto_fit }`
- `fn implicitTracks(decl: Decl, items: []const Item) u32`: How many tracks past `decl`'s own auto-placement of `items` can need on `decl.growAxis()`, at most: every item past the last explicit cell, one after another.
- `fn repeatAuto(extent: f32, gap: f32, minimum: f32, occupied_tracks: u32, mode: Repeat, track: Track, out: []Track) error{ NoSpace, InvalidGeometry }![]const …`: Resolves an automatic repeat against a definite content extent, before constructing the grid.
- `fn expandTracks(explicit: []const Track, required: u32, pattern: []const Track, out: []Track) error{ NoSpace, MissingPattern }![]const Track`: Materializes bounded implicit tracks by repeating the caller's pattern after explicit tracks.
- `fn sizeTracks(gs: *Grids, k: u32, a: u1, inner: f32) f32`: Sizes the tracks of grid `k` on axis `a` inside `inner`, the grid's content size, writes each track's `size` and `start` (a running sum), and returns the total.
- `fn autoPlace(gs: *Grids) void`
- `fn fit(t: *Tree, gs: *Grids, axis: Axis) void`: `layout.fit` for a tree with grids.
- `fn fitWith(t: *Tree, gs: *Grids, fx: anytype, axis: Axis) void`: `fit` with the flex rules of `fx` (a `*const Flex`, or void for none) on the rows around the grids, for a page with both.
- `fn fitWithOpts(t: *Tree, gs: *Grids, fx: anytype, axis: Axis, comptime opts: FlexOptions) void`: `fitWith` with the flex features of `opts` compiled in (`layout.fitFlexOpts`).
- `fn resolve(t: *Tree, gs: *Grids, axis: Axis, viewport: f32) void`: `layout.resolve` for a tree with grids.
- `fn resolveWith(t: *Tree, gs: *Grids, fx: anytype, axis: Axis, viewport: f32) void`: `resolve` with the flex rules of `fx`, as `fitWith`.
- `fn run(t: *Tree, tx: *Texts, gs: *Grids, viewport: [2]f32) void`: Every pass in order, as `layout.run`, with `fit` and `resolve` taken from this file.

### `Track` (struct)
The size of one track (a column or a row).

Fields: `k_px`, `k_fit`, `k_fr`, `min`, `max`, `content_floor`

- `fit: Track = .{ .k_fit = 1 }`: As large as the largest item in it (CSS `auto` / `max-content`).
- `fn px(v: f32) Track`: Exactly `v` pixels.
- `fn fr(w: f32) Track`: A share `w` of the space the other tracks leave (CSS `fr`), never less than its content.
- `fn minmax(minimum: f32, maximum: f32) Track`: Content-sized track bounded by explicit logical-pixel minimum and maximum.
- `fn minmaxFr(minimum: f32, weight: f32, maximum: f32) Track`: Fractional growth above an explicit minimum, independently of intrinsic content.
- `fn fitContent(maximum: f32) Track`: Fit content up to a pixel limit.

### `Justify` (enum)
Where an item sits inside its cell on one axis (CSS `justify-self` / `align-self`).

- `k_stretch = [4]f32{ 1, 0, 0, 0 }`
- `factor = [4]f32{ 0, 0, 0.5, 1 }`

### `Grids` (struct)
The grid side tables.

Fields: `len`, `tracks_len`, `items_len`, `overflow`, `cap`, `g`, `tr`, `it`, `occupied`

- `Capacity = struct { grids: u32, tracks: u32, items: u32, cells: u32 = 1 }`: `cells` bounds columns x rows of any one grid that has auto-placed items.
- `fn init(carver: *mem.Carver, cap: Capacity) Grids`
- `fn reset(gs: *Grids) void`
- `fn grid(gs: *Grids, node: u32, columns: []const Track, rows: []const Track) u32`: Makes `node` a grid with these columns (tracks along x) and rows (along y).
- `fn item(gs: *Grids, k: u32, node: u32, cell: [2]u32) u32`: Places `node`, a floating child of grid `k`'s node, in the cell at column `cell[0]`, row `cell[1]`.
- `fn autoItem(gs: *Grids, k: u32, node: u32) u32`: Places `node`, a floating child of grid `k`'s node, in the next free cell in row order, chosen by `autoPlace`.
- `fn declare(gs: *Grids, node: u32, decl: Decl, implicit: u32) u32`: Makes `node` the grid `decl` describes, with `implicit` more tracks of `decl.auto` on the axis auto-placement grows (`implicitTracks` gives a bound).
- `fn place(gs: *Grids, k: u32, node: u32, at: Item) u32`: Makes `node`, a floating child of grid `k`'s node, the grid item `at` describes.
- `fn gaps(gs: *Grids, k: u32, g: [2]f32) void`: Sets grid `k`'s space between columns (`g[0]`) and between rows (`g[1]`).
- `fn autoFlow(gs: *Grids, k: u32, flow: AutoFlow) void`: Auto-placement traverses rows or columns, with optional hole filling from the start for every item.
- `fn boundedFractions(gs: *Grids, k: u32, enabled: bool) void`: Distribute fractional tracks subject to their minima/maxima.
- `fn distribute(gs: *Grids, k: u32, modes: [2]Distribution) void`: Align the group of tracks inside free content-box space on each axis.
- `fn justify(gs: *Grids, r: u32, j: [2]Justify) void`: Sets where item `r` sits in its cell across (`j[0]`) and down (`j[1]`).
- `fn span(gs: *Grids, r: u32, n: [2]u32) void`: Makes item `r` cover `n[0]` columns and `n[1]` rows from its cell, clamped to the tracks the grid has.
- `fn shareAxis(gs: *Grids, child: u32, parent_item: u32, axis: Axis) error{InvalidSubgrid}!void`: Shares an existing parent item's tracks with its child grid.

### `Grids.GridColumns` (struct)

Fields: `node`, `first`, `count`, `first_item`, `last_item`, `gap`, `flow`, `bounded`, `distribution`, `explicit`, `reserved`, `repeat_count`, `repeat_min`, `repeat_fit`, `justify`, `parent`, `parent_first`


### `Grids.TrackColumns` (struct)

Fields: `k_px`, `k_fit`, `k_fr`, `min`, `max`, `content_floor`, `content`, `size`, `start`


### `Grids.ItemColumns` (struct)

Fields: `node`, `grid`, `cell`, `span`, `auto`, `next`, `cell_size`, `cell_start`, `stretch`, `factor`, `subgrid`


### `Decl` (struct)
A grid container as one value (CSS `grid-template-*`, `grid-auto-*`, `gap`, `grid-auto-flow`, `justify-content`/`align-content`, `justify-items`/`align-items`), for `Grids.declare`.

Fields: `columns`, `rows`, `auto`, `gap`, `flow`, `distribution`, `justify`, `subgrid`, `repeat`

- `AutoRepeat = struct { mode: Repeat, track: Track, max: u32 = 64 }`
- `fn growAxis(d: Decl) u1`: The axis auto-placement adds tracks on: rows for row flow, columns for column flow.

### `Item` (struct)
A grid item as one value (CSS `grid-column`, `grid-row`, `grid-area`, `justify-self`, `align-self`), for `Grids.place`.

Fields: `cell`, `span`, `justify`


## `lcgf.rtl` (src/layout/rtl.zig)

rtl: right-to-left layout as a sign coefficient on the inline (x) axis, the way prototype/ui.zig makes the stacking direction a coefficient (`Tree.Columns.main`) instead of a tag.

### Top level

- `fn direction(t: *const Tree, r: *Rtl) void`: Every node's effective sign: its own direction, or its parent's when it inherits.
- `fn startEnd(t: *Tree, r: *const Rtl) void`: SIMD.
- `fn factor(a: Align, sign: f32) f32`: The share of leftover space placed on the left for `a` (prototype `Align.factor`) under `sign`: `f` left to right, `1 - f` right to left, as `f + (1 - sign) / 2 * (1 - 2f)`.
- `fn mirror(t: *Tree, r: *Rtl) void`: Reflects every child of a right-to-left parent along x inside the parent's content box, and carries the move down to its subtree.

### `Dir` (enum)
Inline direction of a node's content.

- `sign = [3]f32{ 0, 1, -1 }`

### `Rtl` (struct)
The direction columns, one row per node of a tree of capacity `cap`.

Fields: `c`

- `fn init(carver: *mem.Carver, cap: u32) Rtl`
- `fn reset(r: *Rtl) void`: Every node back to `inherit`, for the next frame.
- `fn pad(r: *Rtl, node: u32, start: f32, end: f32) void`: Gives `node` padding at the start and end of its inline axis instead of `Style.pad`'s left and right: start is the left in a left-to-right node, the right in a right-to-left one.
- `fn set(r: *Rtl, node: u32, d: Dir) void`

### `Rtl.Columns` (struct)

Fields: `dir`, `sign`, `shift`, `logical`, `start`, `end`


## `lcgf.hit_targets` (src/interact/targets.zig)

Optional visual-independent hit targets.

### Top level

- `Entry = struct { node: u32, policy: Policy }`
- `Error = error{ InvalidNode, InvalidPolicy, InvalidPath, InvalidLayers }`
- `fn validate(entries: []const Entry, nodes: usize) Error!void`
- `fn validatePolicy(policy: Policy) Error!void`
- `fn policyAt(entries: []const Entry, node: u32) Policy`: Find a policy in a validated sorted list.
- `fn rect(border: [4]f32, policy: Policy) [4]f32`
- `fn contains(border: [4]f32, radii: [4]f32, point: [2]f32, policy: Policy) bool`: The same local target calculation is shared by runtime and pure comptime hit testing.
- `fn pathContains(path: *const paths.Polyline, rule: paths.Rule, point: [2]f32) bool`: Point-in-path for the same flattened geometry and winding rules used by paths.fill.
- `fn nodeContains(tree: *const Tree, node: u32, screen: [2]f32, policy: Policy) bool`
- `fn at(tree: *const Tree, entries: []const Entry, screen: [2]f32, want: types.Sense, layers: []const u8) Error!u32`: Highest (layer,row) wins overlapping targets; an empty layers slice uses row order.

### `Shape` (union)

Fields: `path`


### `Policy` (struct)

Fields: `padding`, `minimum`, `shape`


## `lcgf.hit` (src/interact/hit.zig)

hit: which node is under a point.

### Top level

- `fn at(t: *const Tree, x: f32, y: f32, want: Sense) u32`: Topmost node under (x, y) that takes part in any interaction in `want`, or `none`.
- `fn atLayered(t: *const Tree, layer: []const u8, x: f32, y: f32, want: Sense) u32`: Like `at`, but honouring paint layers: the node under (x, y) with the highest (layer, row), which is the last match in `overlay.order`'s paint order.
- `fn near(t: *const Tree, x: f32, y: f32, want: Sense, slop: f32) u32`: Like `at`, for a finger: the node nearest to (x, y) within `slop` logical pixels that takes part in `want`, or `none`.

## `lcgf.flex` (src/layout/flex.zig)

Flex: sizing rules beyond the prototype's fit / px / grow / pct, as extra per-node columns the caller carves next to the tree and passes to `layout.fitFlex`, `resolveFlex` and `runFlex`.

### `FlexOptions` (struct)
What the flex passes compile in beyond the defaults, as a comptime argument of their `...Opts` forms; the plain forms take `.{}`.

Fields: `line_baseline`


### `Flex` (struct)

Fields: `c`

- `fn sink(fx: *const Flex) u32`: The row that collects the line writes of nodes outside any wrapping row: one past the last line a tree of `cap` nodes can have, inside the padding `simd.rows` adds.
- `fn init(carver: *mem.Carver, cap: u32) Flex`: Carves the columns from `carver` for a tree of `cap` nodes, every row `Style{}`.
- `fn set(fx: *Flex, i: u32, s: *const Style) void`: Writes node `i`'s row.
- `fn spaceShares(fx: *const Flex, t: *const Tree, i: usize) void`: wrap, once per node: node `i`'s lead and step factors over its in-flow children (`Style.space`, or its `Style.main` factor without one).
- `fn lineShares(fx: *const Flex, t: *const Tree, i: usize) void`: resolve, once node `i` has its size: how a wrapping row shares the height its lines leave (`Style.lines` and `line_space`), for placeFlex and its children's resolve.
- `fn secondRound(fx: *const Flex, t: *const Tree, a: u1, p: usize) void`: resolve, once node `p` has its size: the first round's leftover per unit of weight that is still free to grow, for `secondShare`.
- `fn secondShare(fx: *const Flex, t: *const Tree, a: u1, i: usize, p: usize, first: f32) f32`: resolve: node `i`'s second-round share, given its first-round size `first`.
- `fn margin(fx: *const Flex, a: u1, i: usize) f32`: Both margins of node `i` on axis `a`: what it takes beyond its own size.
- `fn selfCross(fx: *const Flex, i: usize, parent_factor: f32) f32`: place: the cross-axis factor for node `i`, its own or the parent's `parent_factor`.
- `fn scaledShrink(fx: *const Flex, t: *const Tree, i: usize, nat: f32) f32`: fit: what node `i` adds to its parent's `shrink_sum`.
- `fn mainShrinkCut(fx: *const Flex, t: *const Tree, a: u1, i: usize, p: usize, over: f32) f32`: resolve, along the parent's main axis: node `i`'s part of the parent's overflow (`over` = natural content minus inner size).
- `fn aspectNat(fx: *const Flex, t: *const Tree, a: u1, i: usize, nat: f32) f32`: fit: on y the height the resolved width gives; on x the width a px height gives (min and max equal on y) when the node has a ratio.
- `fn aspectSize(fx: *const Flex, t: *const Tree, a: u1, i: usize, s: f32) f32`: resolve: on y the height the resolved width gives when the node has a ratio.

### `Flex.Columns` (struct)
One row per tree node, `simd.rows(cap)` rows each.

Fields: `aspect`, `shrink`, `regrow`, `self_cross`, `margin_lo`, `margin_hi`, `wrap`, `align_lines`, `align_base`, `space`, `line_space`, `space_lead`, `space_step`, `line_lead`, `line_step`, `line_extra`, `shrink_sum`, `grow_extra`, `line`, `head`, `open`, `nlines`, `line_main`, `line_count`, `line_cross`, `line_off`, `line_cursor`, `line_base`, `line_below`, `text_base`, `text_has`, `base`, `base_kids`, `below_kids`


### `Flex.Style` (struct)
What `set` writes for one node.

Fields: `aspect`, `shrink`, `regrow`, `cross`, `margin`, `wrap`, `lines`, `baseline`, `space`, `line_space`


### `Flex.LineSpace` (enum)
`Style.line_space`: where a wrapping row's spare height goes, for `n` lines.

- `lines_k = [5]f32{ 1, 0, 0, 0, 0 }`
- `lead_k = [5]f32{ 0, 0, 0.5, 1, 0 }`
- `step_k = [5]f32{ 0, 1, 1, 1, 0 }`
- `stretch_k = [5]f32{ 0, 0, 0, 0, 1 }`
- `per_n = [5]f32{ 1, -1, 0, 1, 0 }`

### `Flex.Space` (enum)
`Style.space`: where free main-axis space goes, for `n` in-flow children.


## `lcgf.wrap` (src/layout/wrap.zig)

Wrapping rows.

### Top level

- `fn wrap(t: *const Tree, fx: *const Flex) void`: Forward sweep, after `resolveFlex(.x)`: puts every in-flow child of a wrapping row on a line, greedily, as CSS does: a child that would end past the row's inner width starts a new line, unless it is the first on its line.
- `fn wrapOpts(t: *const Tree, fx: *const Flex, comptime opts: FlexOptions) void`: `wrap` with the features of `opts` compiled in.
- `fn linesGap(t: *const Tree, fx: *const Flex, a: u1, i: usize) f32`: fitFlex: the gaps between a wrapping row's lines, on y.
- `fn foldLine(t: *const Tree, fx: *const Flex, a: u1, i: usize, p: usize, contrib: f32, folded: f32, comptime opts: FlexOptions) f32`: fitFlex: on y, child `i` of a wrapping row raises its line's cross size to its own extent, and the line joins the row's sum when its first child (the last one the reverse sweep sees) folds in.
- `fn lineCross(t: *const Tree, fx: *const Flex, a: u1, i: usize, p: usize, inner: f32) f32`: resolveFlex: how much more than the parent's inner size a stretching child of a wrapping row gets on y: its line's cross size instead of the whole row.
- `fn openLine(t: *const Tree, fx: *const Flex, i: usize, p: usize) void`: placeFlex, before child `i` is placed: the first child of a line sets the line's offset down the row: the row's cursor (which for a wrapping row runs across it) plus the share of the row's spare height that `Style.lines` (or `Style.line_space`) puts before its lines.
- `fn inLine(t: *const Tree, fx: *const Flex, comptime a: u1, i: usize, p: usize, inner: f32, cross: f32, in_flow: f32, comptime opts: FlexOptions) f32`: placeFlex: child `i`'s offset inside a wrapping row's content box.
- `fn advance(t: *const Tree, fx: *const Flex, i: usize, p: usize, plain: f32) f32`: placeFlex, after child `i` is placed: it moves its line's cursor along x, and a line's first child moves the row's cursor down past the line.

## `lcgf.baseline` (src/layout/baseline.zig)

Baselines.

### Top level

- `fn apply(t: *const Tree, fx: *const Flex, tx: *const Texts, ascent: []const f32) void`: Scatters each text run's first baseline (`ascent[k]` for run k, measured from the run's top, as the font reports it with `w` and `h`) into its node, and marks which nodes carry text this frame.
- `fn settle(t: *const Tree, fx: *const Flex, i: usize, nat: f32) void`: fitFlex, once node `i`'s children are folded and its natural height `nat` is known: its baseline.
- `fn fold(t: *const Tree, fx: *const Flex, i: usize, p: usize) void`: fitFlex: folds in-flow child `i`'s baseline (below its top margin) into parent `p`: the first child's in a column (the reverse sweep sees it last, so it overwrites), the largest in a row.
- `fn content(t: *const Tree, fx: *const Flex, a: u1, i: usize, plain: f32) f32`: fitFlex, on y: a baseline-aligned row's content height is its children's highest part above the shared baseline plus their deepest part below it, not the tallest child.
- `fn across(t: *const Tree, fx: *const Flex, i: usize, p: usize, plain: f32) f32`: placeFlex, on y: in a baseline-aligned row, child `i` sits so its baseline is at the row's (`base_kids`, below the row's padding), unless it has its own cross alignment.

## `lcgf.layout_check` (src/layout/check.zig)

Checks for rewritten layout passes: random trees, and the prototype's passes kept verbatim as the reference a faster pass must agree with.

### Top level

- `tolerance: f32 = 4e-3`: How far a regrouped pass may drift from the reference, in pixels.
- `fn randomTree(t: *Tree, r: std.Random, n: u32) void`: Pushes `n` nodes (n <= t.cap) of random structure and style: every sizing mode, padding, gaps, floating, shrinking, min/max, alignment, offsets and scroll.
- `fn randomRuns(t: *Tree, r: std.Random, n: u32) void`: `randomTree` with runs of siblings: now and then the innermost open node gets up to 40 leaves in a row, so a pass that works run by run (runs.zig) meets runs of every length around a whole vector.
- `fn fitRef(t: *Tree, a: usize, first: u32, last: u32) void`: `layout.fit`'s fold as it was row by row, kept as the reference for `runs.fit`: rows after `first` up to `last`, last row first, each into its parent's `fit` and `grow_sum` on axis `a`.
- `fn resolveRef(t: *Tree, axis: Axis, viewport: f32) void`: prototype/ui.zig `layout.resolve`, verbatim: the reference for the K-coefficient form.
- `fn maxDiff(want: []const f32, got: []const f32, n: u32) f32`: Largest absolute difference between two columns over the first `n` rows.

## `lcgf.coefs` (src/layout/coefs.zig)

coefs: what the two top-down sweeps read, regrouped by a SIMD pass into one row per node, so each sweep follows the parent link into two rows instead of a dozen columns (fewer streams per loop: Andrew Kelley, Practical Data-Oriented Design).

### Top level

- `fn coefs(t: *const Tree, k: *Coefs, axis: Axis) void`: SIMD.
- `fn resolveK(t: *Tree, k: *const Coefs, axis: Axis, viewport: f32) void`: Top-down: final size of every node on one axis, from the rows of `coefs`.
- `fn placeRows(t: *const Tree, k: *Coefs) void`: SIMD.
- `fn placeK(t: *Tree, k: *const Coefs) void`: Top-down: position of every node on both axes, from the rows of `placeRows`.

### `Child` (struct)
What resolve reads about a node as a child, on one axis.

Fields: `k0`, `k1`, `k2`, `room`, `min`, `max`, `flow`, `parent`


### `Parent` (struct)
What resolve reads about a node as a parent, on one axis.

Fields: `pad`, `taken`


### `Place` (struct)
What place reads about a node as a parent, both axes.

Fields: `origin`, `inner`, `lead`, `main`, `align_cross`, `gap`


### `Coefs` (struct)

Fields: `cap`, `c`

- `fn init(carver: *mem.Carver, cap: u32) Coefs`: Carves every column for `cap` nodes, `simd.rows(cap)` rows each.

### `Coefs.Columns` (struct)
Both axes have their own rows, so the x rows survive until `resolveK(.x)` while the y rows are written after `fit(.y)`.

Fields: `child`, `parent`, `place`


## `lcgf.levels` (src/layout/levels.zig)

levels: a level-order index derived from the depth-first tree, so resolve sweeps one level at a time with SIMD instead of following the parent link row by row.

### Top level

- `fn build(t: *const Tree, lv: *Levels) void`: Builds the level-order index of `t`.
- `fn resolve(t: *Tree, lv: *const Levels, axis: Axis, viewport: f32) void`: SIMD per level.

### `Levels` (struct)

Fields: `cap`, `count`, `c`

- `fn init(carver: *mem.Carver, cap: u32) Levels`: Carves every column for `cap` nodes, `simd.rows(cap)` rows each.

### `Levels.Columns` (struct)

Fields: `order`, `parent`, `start`, `depth`


## `lcgf.runs` (src/layout/runs.zig)

runs: layout passes over runs of siblings.

### Top level

- `fn whole(parent: []const u32, i: usize, last: usize, p: u32) bool`: Whether the `W` rows from `i` are all children of `p` and before `last`: a whole vector of the run.
- `fn survey(t: *Tree) void`: Sets `t.wide`: whether at least an eighth of the rows head a whole vector of siblings, `W` rows next to each other with one parent.
- `fn place(t: *Tree) void`: `layout.place` over the whole tree, run by run.
- `fn fit(t: *Tree, a: usize, first: usize, last: usize) void`: `layout.fit`'s fold over the rows after `first` up to `last`, last row first: each row's natural size on axis `a`, summed or maxed into its parent, a run of siblings at a time.

## `lcgf.incremental` (src/layout/incremental.zig)

incremental: lay out again only what a change can reach.

### Top level

- `fn boundaries(t: *const Tree, d: *Dirty) void`: SIMD.
- `fn propagate(t: *const Tree, d: *Dirty) void`: Moves every mark to the relayout boundary above it: a marked node can change its parent's fit, so it can change everything up to the first boundary ancestor of its parent, and nothing outside that ancestor's subtree.
- `fn refit(t: *Tree, tx: *const Texts, axis: Axis, root: u32) void`: Natural sizes again for the subtree [root, end[root]) on one axis: clear its fit scratch, apply the text extents of its runs, recompute its gap totals, fit it.
- `fn update(t: *Tree, tx: *Texts, d: *Dirty, viewport: [2]f32) void`: Lays out what the marks reach and clears them: per axis, each outermost span is fitted and resolved again; then every outermost span of either axis is placed and finalized.
- `fn keepable(t: *const Tree, r: u32) bool`: Whether row `r` may be kept: a boundary on both axes with at least one descendant.
- `fn prepareKept(t: *Tree, kept: []const u32) void`: `layout.prepare` that leaves the kept rows' scratch (fit, grow_sum, used, cursor) alone: the passes below do not visit their interiors, so those keep the values they were copied with.
- `fn applyTextKept(t: *Tree, tx: *const Texts, axis: Axis, kept: []const u32) void`: `layout.applyText` for the runs outside the kept subtrees (roots included: a root's fit is its copied total).
- `fn fitKept(t: *Tree, axis: Axis, kept: []const u32) void`: `layout.fit` that does not visit kept interiors: bottom-up, gap by gap.
- `fn resolveKept(t: *Tree, axis: Axis, viewport: f32, kept: []const u32) void`: `layout.resolve` that does not visit kept interiors: top-down, gap by gap.
- `fn placeKept(t: *Tree, kept: []const u32) void`: `layout.place` that does not visit the interior of a kept root that did not move.
- `fn finalizeKept(t: *Tree, prev: *const Tree, kept: []const u32, sources: []const u32) void`: Finalize a tree with copied, fixed-size subtrees.
- `fn runKept(t: *Tree, tx: *Texts, viewport: [2]f32, kept: []const u32) void`: `layout.run` with kept subtrees: every pass in order, each skipping the kept interiors.

### `Dirty` (struct)

Fields: `cap`, `c`

- `fn init(carver: *mem.Carver, cap: u32) Dirty`
- `fn mark(d: *Dirty, i: u32, axis: Axis) void`: Records that node `i`'s inputs on `axis` changed.

### `Dirty.Columns` (struct)

Fields: `mark`, `bound`, `span`


## `lcgf.interaction` (src/interact/interaction.zig)

interaction: hover, press, release, click and focus as one pure step per frame.

### Top level

- `fn Touches(comptime n: comptime_int) type`: Press, release and click for up to `n` fingers or pens at once, from `Input.contacts`.

### `Interaction` (struct)
Pointer state carried from frame to frame.

Fields: `hot`, `under`, `active`, `focus`, `pressed`, `released`, `clicked`, `hot_node`, `active_node`, `pointer`, `delta`, `buttons`, `double_clicked`, `long_pressed`, `double_time`, `long_time`, `last_click`, `click_at`, `press_at`, `long_fired`, `dragging`, `drag_delta`, `drag_threshold`, `drag_from`, `hover_intent`, `hover_delay`, `hot_at`

- `fn step(s: *Interaction, prev: *const Tree, in: *const Input) void`: Advances by one frame, from last frame's geometry and this frame's pointer.
- `fn stepWithHit(s: *Interaction, prev: *const Tree, in: *const Input, selected: u32) void`: Caller-supplied hit from the completed tree, for layers, enlarged targets or a custom scene query.
- `fn cancel(s: *Interaction, id: types.Id) void`: Cancel capture, focus and pending activation when a built control stops participating.
- `fn drag(s: *const Interaction, id: types.Id) [2]f32`: Pointer movement this frame while `id` is dragged; zero otherwise.
- `fn response(s: *const Interaction, id: types.Id) types.Response`: What happened to widget `id` this frame.

### `Touches(..)` (generic struct)
Press, release and click for up to `n` fingers or pens at once, from `Input.contacts`.

Fields: `pointer`, `under`, `active`, `pressed`, `released`, `clicked`, `down`, `x`, `y`, `slop`

- `fn step(t: *Self, prev: *const Tree, in: *const Input) void`: Advances by one frame, from last frame's geometry and this frame's contacts.
- `fn stepWithHits(t: *Self, prev: *const Tree, in: *const Input, selected: []const u32) error{InvalidHits}!void`: One caller-supplied hit row per contact, in `Input.contacts` order.
- `fn response(t: *const Self, id: types.Id) types.Response`: What any contact did to widget `id` this frame.

## `lcgf.focus` (src/interact/focus.zig)

focus: keyboard focus on top of `Interaction.focus`.

### Top level

- `fn scan(t: *const Tree, sc: *Scopes) void`: Fills `group`, `lead`, `stop` and `trap` from `kind`, `parent`, `sense` and `id`.
- `fn seek(t: *const Tree, col: []const u8, mask: u8, lo: u32, hi: u32, cur: u32, back: bool) u32`: The row after `cur` (before it with `back`) among rows `[lo, hi)` of `t` whose `col` has a bit of `mask` set and whose id is not 0, wrapping around the range; `none` when there is no such row.

### `Keymap` (struct)
Scancodes of the keys that move focus.

Fields: `tab`, `right`, `left`, `down`, `up`, `home`, `end`


### `Scopes` (struct)
The focus scope column set: which nodes group their focusable descendants (a toolbar, a radio group, a listbox, a tab list), and what `scan` derives from that.

Fields: `c`, `modal`

- `group: u8 = 1`: `kind` bits.
- `trap: u8 = 2`: A modal layer (dialog, sheet, open menu): while it is in the tree, focus stays among its descendants, as the WAI-ARIA APG modal dialog pattern asks.
- `fn init(carver: *mem.Carver, cap: u32) Scopes`: One row per node of a tree of capacity `cap`, plus the spare rows every column has.
- `fn reset(sc: *Scopes) void`: Clears every `kind`, for the next frame.

### `Scopes.Columns` (struct)

Fields: `kind`, `group`, `lead`, `stop`


### `Focus` (struct)
Keyboard-focus state carried from frame to frame.

Fields: `visible`, `ring_width`, `keys`, `left`, `last`, `layers`, `saved`, `depth`, `taken`

- `max_layers = 8`
- `fn step(s: *Focus, it: *Interaction, prev: *const Tree, sc: *const Scopes, in: *const Input) void`: Advances by one frame.
- `fn ring(s: *const Focus, it: *const Interaction, t: *const Tree) Rect`: Where to draw the focus ring this frame: the focused node's rect in `t`, grown by `ring_width`.

## `lcgf.keyboard` (src/interact/keyboard.zig)

keyboard: key events and typed text, delivered to the focused widget.

### Top level

- `Purpose = enum(u8) { normal, secure, terminal, number, phone, email, url }`: What the text input purpose is, so the host picks the right keyboard and IME behaviour (Iced 0.14 `input_method::Purpose`, plus the content types the on-screen keyboards of iOS, Android, the web and Wayland's text-input-v3 lay out for).
- `fn fire(sh: *const Shortcuts, k: *Keyboard, out: []u16) u32`: Writes to `out`, in event order, the action of every row of `sh` whose key went down (or repeated) this frame with exactly its modifiers, and returns how many.

### `Keyboard` (struct)
This frame's keyboard input, addressed to one widget.

Fields: `in`, `target`, `taken`

- `fn route(it: *const Interaction, fo: *const Focus, in: *const Input) Keyboard`: Events of `in` for `it.focus`, minus those `fo` used to move focus.
- `fn events(k: *const Keyboard, id: Id) Iterator`: The events delivered to `id`, in order.
- `fn pressed(k: *const Keyboard, id: Id, code: u8, mods: Input.Mods) bool`: `id` is focused and key `code` went down (or repeated) this frame with exactly `mods` held, in an event the focus keys did not use.
- `fn commands(k: *const Keyboard, id: Id, map: ClipboardKeys) Commands`: The clipboard commands `id` was given this frame: empty unless it is focused.
- `fn composition(k: *const Keyboard, id: Id, ime: *const Composition) Composition`: The composition for `id`: `ime` with its range clamped into its text and ordered, or an empty composition unless `id` is focused.
- `fn down(k: *const Keyboard, id: Id, code: u8) bool`: `id` is focused and key `code` is held this frame (`Input.keys`).
- `fn text(k: *const Keyboard, id: Id) []const u8`: The UTF-8 typed this frame (`Input.text`) if `id` is focused, else empty.

### `Keyboard.Iterator` (struct)

Fields: `k`, `i`

- `fn next(r: *Iterator) ?Input.Event`

### `Commands` (struct)
The clipboard commands a focused widget can be given.

Fields: `select_all`, `copy`, `cut`, `paste`


### `ClipboardKeys` (struct)
Keys of the clipboard commands.

Fields: `mods`, `select_all`, `copy`, `cut`, `paste`


### `Clipboard` (struct)
Clipboard requests to the host, as platform output (prototype/ui.zig `Platform.clipboard`): filled by widgets during the frame, applied by the host after it.

Fields: `set`, `paste`

- `fn reset(o: *Clipboard) void`: Clears the requests, for the next frame.
- `fn copy(o: *Clipboard, text: []const u8) void`: Copy and cut: put `text` on the clipboard.
- `fn requestPaste(o: *Clipboard) void`: Paste: ask the host for the clipboard's text.

### `Composition` (struct)
What an input method (IME) is composing, filled by the host each frame beside `Input`, as Iced 0.14's input method events carry it: the preedit text with an optional range in it.

Fields: `text`, `range`


### `TextInput` (struct)
A request to the host for text input, as platform output (prototype/ui.zig `Platform.wants_text` and `ime_rect`): the focused text widget fills it during the frame; the host shows the on-screen keyboard or enables the IME, puts its candidate window at `rect`, and turns both off when no widget asked.

Fields: `wants_text`, `rect`, `purpose`

- `fn reset(o: *TextInput) void`
- `fn request(o: *TextInput, rect: types.Rect, purpose: Purpose) void`: Asks for text input at `rect`.

### `Shortcuts` (struct)
The shortcut table: one row per key combination the application binds to an action of its own (Ctrl+S to save, F5 to run).

Fields: `len`, `cap`, `overflow`, `c`

- `fn init(carver: *mem.Carver, cap: u32) Shortcuts`
- `fn reset(sh: *Shortcuts) void`
- `fn add(sh: *Shortcuts, code: u8, mods: Input.Mods, action: u16) u32`: Binds `code` with exactly `mods` to `action`.

### `Shortcuts.Columns` (struct)

Fields: `code`, `mods`, `action`


## `lcgf.gestures` (src/interact/gestures.zig)

gestures: wheel and scroll routing, and the gestures built from pointer and touch input.

### Top level

- `fn route(t: *const Tree, sc: *const Scrollables, in: *const Input, line: f32) Wheel`: Routes the wheel and pixel scroll of `in` on each axis to the innermost node that scrolls on that axis and whose visible rect holds the pointer: a horizontal strip inside a vertical list takes x and leaves y to the list.

### `Scrollables` (struct)
Which nodes scroll, and on which axes.

Fields: `c`

- `x: u8 = 1`: `axes` bits.
- `y: u8 = 2`
- `fn init(carver: *mem.Carver, cap: u32) Scrollables`
- `fn reset(sc: *Scrollables) void`: Nothing scrolls, for the next frame.

### `Scrollables.Columns` (struct)

Fields: `axes`


### `Wheel` (struct)
Where this frame's wheel goes, per axis.

Fields: `node`, `id`, `delta`


### `Kinetic` (struct)
Kinetic scrolling: one row per scroll axis that moves on its own after a fling, stepped together as prototype/ui.zig `Anims.step` steps its springs.

Fields: `len`, `cap`, `c`, `friction`, `stiffness`, `damping`, `resistance`

- `fn init(carver: *mem.Carver, cap: u32) Kinetic`
- `fn slot(k: *Kinetic, id: u32, initial: f32, lo: f32, hi: f32) u32`: Row for `id`, created at rest at offset `initial` with range [lo, hi] on first use; the range is updated every call (content and viewport change).
- `fn scroll(k: *Kinetic, i: u32, delta: f32) void`: A wheel notch or touchpad delta (`Wheel.delta`): moves at once, kept in range, and stops any coasting.
- `fn drag(k: *Kinetic, i: u32, delta: f32, dt: f32) void`: The content is held and moved by `delta` this frame of `dt` seconds (a finger or a mouse drag, in scroll direction).
- `fn release(k: *Kinetic, i: u32) void`: Lets go: the row coasts at its velocity (a fling), or springs back if past an end.
- `fn step(k: *Kinetic, dt: f32) f32`: SIMD.

### `Kinetic.Columns` (struct)

Fields: `id`, `x`, `v`, `lo`, `hi`, `held`


### `Pinch` (struct)
Pinch zoom, from two fingers on a touch screen (`Input.contacts`) or from Ctrl+wheel, which is how touchpad pinches reach a window on Windows, on Linux and in browsers.

Fields: `fingers`, `spread`, `mid`, `notch`, `active`, `scale`, `center`, `pan`

- `fn step(p: *Pinch, in: *const Input) void`

### `Targets` (struct)
Drop targets: which kinds of dragged payload each node accepts.

Fields: `c`

- `fn init(carver: *mem.Carver, cap: u32) Targets`
- `fn reset(tg: *Targets) void`

### `Targets.Columns` (struct)

Fields: `accept`


### `DragDrop` (struct)
Drag and drop inside the application, on top of `Interaction`'s drag: the application starts it (`begin`) when a widget it wants to drag shows up in `Interaction.dragging`, with a payload kind; `step` then follows the pointer to the topmost node that accepts that kind, and on release reports the drop.

Fields: `source`, `kind`, `over`, `over_node`, `dropped`, `dropped_kind`

- `fn begin(d: *DragDrop, source: types.Id, kind: u32) void`: Starts dragging `source` (an id) with payload `kind`.
- `fn cancel(d: *DragDrop) void`: Drops nothing and ends the drag (Escape, say).
- `fn step(d: *DragDrop, it: *const Interaction, prev: *const Tree, tg: *const Targets, in: *const Input) void`: Advances by one frame, after `it.step(prev, in)`.

### `Files` (struct)
Files dragged in from outside the application, filled by the host each frame beside `Input` (Input has no file field): the paths while files hover over the window, and the frame they are dropped.

Fields: `names`, `ends`, `pos`, `hover`, `drop`

- `fn count(fs: *const Files) usize`
- `fn path(fs: *const Files, k: usize) []const u8`: Path `k`, for `k < count()`.
- `fn route(fs: *const Files, prev: *const Tree, tg: *const Targets, kind: u32) FileDrop`: Where these files go: the topmost node under `pos` whose `Targets.accept` includes `kind` (the application's bit for files).

### `FileDrop` (struct)
Where external files are this frame (`Files.route`).

Fields: `over`, `over_node`, `dropped`


## `lcgf.spec` (src/comptime/spec.zig)

Spec: a UI declared as a compile-time value.

### Top level

- `Kind = enum(u8) { col, row, label, checkbox, slider }`: What a spec node is.
- `TokenTarget = enum(u8) { pad_left, pad_top, pad_right, pad_bottom, gap, width, height, min_width, min_height, max_width, max_height, border, radius, fill, bo…`: A style field that a run-time theme token sets (docs/plans/schemify.md §2): a length of the layout or paint style, a colour, or the text style's size or colour.
- `TokenUse = struct { target: TokenTarget, token: []const u8 }`: Field `target` takes the token named `token`: a field of the page's `Tokens` struct, read from the caller's `theme_table.ThemeTable(Tokens)`.
- `Format = struct { text: []const u8, args: []const []const u8 }`: A formatted label's text (src/core/fmt.zig's language) and its arguments, state fields.
- `Landmark = enum { group, article, region, banner, navigation, main, complementary, content_info, form, search }`: Document containers whose role is independent of their layout direction.
- `fn tokened(comptime s: Spec, comptime uses: []const TokenUse) Spec`: `s` with some style fields read from run-time tokens when its row is pushed: `tokened(col(.{}, .{...}), &.{ .{ .target = .pad_top, .token = "space" } })`.
- `fn applyTokens(comptime uses: []const TokenUse, style: *Style, text_style: *types.TextStyle, values: anytype) void`: Writes the token values `values` (a `Tokens`) into the fields `uses` names.
- `fn col(comptime style: Style, comptime children: anytype) Spec`: A vertical container.
- `fn row(comptime style: Style, comptime children: anytype) Spec`: A horizontal container; as `col`, with `dir` always `.x`.
- `fn label(comptime caption: anytype) Spec`: A literal caption (`label("Save")`) or a bound string field (`label(.title)`).
- `fn landmark(comptime role: Landmark, comptime style: Style, comptime children: anytype) Spec`: Preserve the layout and children while exposing this row's document role.
- `fn heading(comptime level: u8, comptime caption: anytype) Spec`: A document heading whose accessibility name is its displayed literal, bound or formatted text: `heading(1, "Mixer")`, `heading(2, .title)` or `heading(1, text("Gain {.1} dB", .{.g}))`.
- `fn stringCapacity(comptime tree: Spec, comptime dynamic_bytes: u32) u32`: Frame string bytes for all declared literals and accessible names, plus the caller's budget when a bound label or a widget hook can append runtime text.
- `fn labelError(comptime s: Spec) ?[]const u8`: Check the two sources before emitting a label.
- `fn labelBytes(comptime s: Spec, state: anytype) []const u8`: Resolve a label's source at compile time.
- `fn text(comptime format: []const u8, comptime args: anytype) Spec`: Text of the state fields `args` written into `format`: `text("Volume {.1} dB", .{.volume})`.
- `fn checkbox(comptime id: @EnumLiteral(), comptime bind_or_options: anytype) Spec`: A checkbox named `id`, showing and toggling a bool field.
- `fn slider(comptime id: @EnumLiteral(), comptime bind_or_options: anytype) Spec`: A slider named `id`, showing and setting an f32 field, 0 to 1: `slider(.volume, .volume)`, or with options as `checkbox`'s, `slider(.volume, .{ .name = "Volume" })`.
- `fn styled(comptime s: Spec, comptime style: Style) Spec`: `s` laid out by `style`: `styled(slider(.volume, .{ .name = "Volume" }), .{ .size = .{ .px(80), .px(10) } })`.
- `fn textStyled(comptime s: Spec, comptime text_style: types.TextStyle) Spec`: Label `s` drawn in `text_style` instead of the page theme's: `textStyled(heading(1, "Mixer"), .{ .px = 28, .color = .rgb(230, 230, 235) })`.
- `fn inheritText(comptime s: Spec, comptime text_style: ?types.TextStyle) Spec`: `s` with the text style `text_style` when it has none of its own: how a widget hands the text style set on it (`textStyled`) to the labels it holds.
- `fn paragraph(comptime s: Spec, comptime p: @import("../text/layout/stack.zig").Paragraph) Spec`: Label `s` with paragraph style `p`: `paragraph(label(.a), .{ .line_height = .{ .px = 20 } })`.
- `fn decorated(comptime s: Spec, comptime d: @import("../text/layout/decorations.zig").Style) Spec`: Label `s` with text decoration `d`: `decorated(label(.a), .{ .underline = true })`.
- `fn spans(comptime s: Spec, comptime runs: []const @import("../text/protocol.zig").Span) Spec`: Label `s` as one paragraph in several styles: from each span's `start` byte on, its text style's size, colour and font: `spans(label("a bold word"), &.{ .{ .start = 2, .style = bold }, .{ .start = 6, .style = plain } })`.
- `fn flexed(comptime s: Spec, comptime flex: FlexStyle) Spec`: `s` laid out with the flex rules `flex` as well as its style: `flexed(label(.a), .{ .margin = .{ 6, 2, 6, 2 } })`.
- `fn grid(comptime style: Style, comptime decl: grid_zig.Decl, comptime children: anytype) Spec`: A grid container: its children in the cells of `decl`'s tracks, one per child unless `cell` places one: `grid(.{}, .{ .columns = &.{ .px(80), .fr(1) } }, .{ label("A"), ...
- `fn cell(comptime s: Spec, comptime item: grid_zig.Item) Spec`: `s`, a child of a `grid`, placed at `item` (CSS `grid-column`/`grid-row`/`grid-area`): `cell(label("A"), .{ .cell = .{ 1, 0 }, .span = .{ 2, 1 } })`.
- `fn anchored(comptime s: Spec, comptime place: overlay.Place) Spec`: `s`, a floating node, placed by `place` instead of at its parent's content origin plus its offset: `anchored(styled(label("New"), .{ .float = true }), .{ .at = .top_right, .pivot = .top_right })`.
- `fn sticky(comptime s: Spec, comptime top: f32) Spec`: `s` held at least `top` pixels below the top of its scroller while its parent's content box has room, as CSS `position: sticky; top: …`.
- `fn layer(comptime s: Spec, comptime z: u8) Spec`: `s` and its subtree painted in stacking layer `z` (CSS `z-index` on a positioned box): above every row of a lower layer, in row order within one.
- `fn gridItems(comptime n: Spec) []const grid_zig.Item`: The cells `n`'s children ask for, for `grid.implicitTracks`.
- `fn named(comptime s: Spec, comptime name: []const u8) Spec`: `s` with the accessible name `name`: `named(slider(.volume, .volume), "Volume")`.
- `fn list(comptime count: @EnumLiteral(), comptime max: comptime_int, comptime style: Style, comptime template: Spec) Spec`: A container (laid out by `style`, `dir` included) of `max` copies of `template`, of which the first `count` are live, `count` being the run-time value of the integer state field bound here: `list(.len, 8, .{ .gap = 2 }, row(...))`.
- `fn virtualList(comptime count: @EnumLiteral(), comptime scroll: @EnumLiteral(), comptime visible: u32, comptime height: f32, comptime template: Spec) Spec`: A clipped vertical list of `visible` fixed-height items.
- `fn when(comptime condition: @EnumLiteral(), comptime subtree: Spec) Spec`: `subtree`, live only while the bool state field `condition` is true: `when(.advanced, col(...))`.
- `fn choice(comptime selector: @EnumLiteral(), comptime style: Style, comptime alternatives: anytype) Spec`: A container (laid out by `style`) of `alternatives`, of which only the one at the index of the state field `selector` (an enum, by its value, an integer, or a bool, false selecting the first) is live: `choice(.mode, .{}, .{ compact, detailed })`, or an if/else as `choice(.on, .{}, .{ otherwise, then })`.

### `Spec` (struct)
One node of the declaration.

Fields: `kind`, `id`, `bind`, `text`, `text_style`, `paragraph`, `decoration`, `spans`, `style`, `flex`, `grid`, `cell`, `anchor`, `sticky`, `layer`, `children`, `widget`, `custom`, `name`, `region`, `format`, `tokens`

- `fn shows(s: Spec, field: []const u8) bool`: The state fields this node shows: its `bind`, or a formatted label's arguments.

### `Region` (struct)
What makes a node's rows dynamic, and the state field that decides which are live.

Fields: `kind`, `bind`, `window`


## `lcgf.protocol` (src/text/protocol.zig)

The measure / wrap protocol: how text extents reach layout.

### Top level

- `Span = struct { start: u32, style: types.TextStyle, decoration: Decoration = .{} }`: One style run of a paragraph: from byte `start` of its text on (to the next span's start), `style`'s size, colour and font instead of the run's.
- `Features = struct { spans: bool = true, decorations: bool = true }`: Which inputs a `MonoParagraphsOf` reads.
- `MonoParagraphs = MonoParagraphsOf(.{})`: `MonoParagraphsOf` with every input.
- `fn insertAfter(d: *DrawList, j: u32, first: u32) u32`: Puts quads `first..d.quad_len` (just appended after `paint.emit`) right after op `j`, the text op of their run: their ops after it, and the quads themselves moved to where the next quad op's quads begin, so every run of consecutive quad ops still names consecutive quads (`paint.encode` merges them).
- `fn MonoParagraphsOf(comptime with: Features) type`: `Mono` with paragraph styles on some runs: run `rows[i]` of `Texts` takes `table[styles[i]]` (`rows` ascending; draw list run k is texts row k).
- `fn StaticAtlas(comptime strings: []const []const u8) type`: Pack only the byte cells referenced by a static vocabulary, using the same pixels and glyph metrics as Mono's 16x16 atlas.

### `Mono` (struct)
A fixed-advance font: every byte is `advance` ems wide, every line `line` ems tall.

Fields: `advance`, `line`

- `atlas: Atlas = .{}`: Mono's glyph atlas: a 16 x 16 grid of cells indexed by byte.
- `Glyphs = struct { first: []u32, count: []u32 }`: Where glyph quads for run k landed in the output of `quads`.
- `fn measure(font: Mono, tx: *Texts) void`: SIMD.
- `fn wrap(font: Mono, tx: *Texts) void`: SIMD.
- `fn quads(font: Mono, d: *const DrawList, out: []Quad, glyphs: Glyphs) u32`: Expands the draw list's text runs into glyph quads for a 16x16 byte-indexed atlas: byte b samples cell (b & 15, b >> 4).
- `fn quadsFor(font: Mono, comptime Grid: type, d: *const DrawList, out: []Quad, glyphs: Glyphs) error{GlyphOutsideSubset}!u32`: The same metrics and geometry, with UVs from a static atlas subset.
- `fn quadsForCommands(font: Mono, comptime Grid: type, d: *const DrawList, out: []Quad, glyphs: Glyphs) error{GlyphOutsideSubset}!u32`: Expands only runs referenced by text commands.

### `Styled` (struct)
Styled runs of a frame, for any font: run `rows[i]` of `Texts` takes `table[styles[i]]`, and `decorations` and `spans`, when not empty, have one row per `table` row.

Fields: `rows`, `styles`, `table`, `decorations`, `spans`


### `MonoParagraphsOf(..)` (generic struct)
`Mono` with paragraph styles on some runs: run `rows[i]` of `Texts` takes `table[styles[i]]` (`rows` ascending; draw list run k is texts row k).

Fields: `mono`, `rows`, `styles`, `table`, `decorations`, `spans`, `strings`

- `fn measure(p: Self, tx: *Texts) void`: Mono's measure, then the single-line extent of every styled run.
- `fn wrap(p: Self, tx: *Texts) void`: Mono's wrap, then every styled run in its `avail`: re-flowed when it wraps, cut to it when it shows an ellipsis.
- `fn quads(p: Self, d: *const DrawList, tx: *const Texts, out: []Quad, glyphs: Mono.Glyphs) u32`: `Mono.quads` with the styled runs laid out as `wrap` measured them (from the frame's `tx`: each run's wrap flag and width), each glyph in its span's size and colour.
- `fn decorate(p: Self, d: *DrawList, tx: *const Texts) void`: After `paint.emit`: one solid quad per line and decoration of every decorated run, painted right after the run's text and encoded into commands again.
- `fn styled(p: Self) Styled`: The styled runs this font measures, for a real shaper (`text_stack.Stack.style`).

### `Atlas` (struct)
Where each byte's glyph sits in a bitmap font atlas: `columns` x `rows` equal cells, each `cell` of the texture in UV, holding the bytes from `first` to `last` in order, row by row.

Fields: `columns`, `rows`, `first`, `last`, `missing`, `cell`

- `fn grid(columns: u8, rows: u8, first: u8, last: u8) Atlas`: `columns` x `rows` cells filling the texture, holding the bytes `first` to `last`.
- `fn uv(a: Atlas, byte: u8) [4]f32`: The UV rect (u0, v0, u1, v1) of `byte`'s cell.

### `StaticAtlas(..)` (generic struct)
Pack only the byte cells referenced by a static vocabulary, using the same pixels and glyph metrics as Mono's 16x16 atlas.

- `count = table.count`
- `columns = @min(count, 16)`
- `rows = (count + 15) / 16`
- `fn uv(byte: u8) error{GlyphOutsideSubset}![4]f32`
- `fn pixels(comptime Pixel: type, comptime cell: [2]usize, comptime full: []const Pixel) [columns * rows * cell[0] * cell[1]]Pixel`: Copy each referenced cell's scanlines from the full source atlas at compile time.

### `Cache` (struct)
Remembers measured extents across frames, keyed by a hash of the run's bytes, its font and its size, so an expensive shaper measures only text it has not seen.

Fields: `mask`, `c`

- `fn init(carver: *mem.Carver, slots: u32) Cache`: Carves a table of `slots` entries (a power of two), all empty.
- `fn clear(cache: *Cache) void`: Forgets every entry, e.g.
- `fn key(bytes: []const u8, font: u16, px: f32) u64`: The key of a run: Wyhash of its bytes, seeded with font and size.
- `fn lookup(cache: *const Cache, tx: *Texts, strings: *const Strings, misses: []u32) u32`: Writes `w`, `h` of every run of `tx` found in the cache and appends the rows of the others to `misses` (at least `tx.len` long).
- `fn store(cache: *Cache, tx: *const Texts, strings: *const Strings, rows: []const u32) void`: Records the measured `w`, `h` of the runs listed in `rows` (the misses, once measured).

### `Cache.Columns` (struct)

Fields: `key`, `w`, `h`


## `lcgf.flatten` (src/comptime/flatten.zig)

Flatten: a comptime `Spec` turned into the same rows the runtime front end builds with `Tree.push`, as read-only tables.

### Top level

- `fn count(comptime s: Spec) comptime_int`: Nodes in `s`, itself included: the row count of the flattened spec.
- `fn Flat(comptime spec: Spec) type`: Everything about the structure of `spec` that is known before the program runs.

### `Flat(..)` (generic struct)
Everything about the structure of `spec` that is known before the program runs.

- `node_count = n`
- `Index = std.math.IntFittingRange(0, n)`: The smallest integer that can index every node and hold `end`, which reaches `n`: u4 for the probe's 8 nodes.
- `parent: [n]Index = tables.parent`: Row of each node's parent; the root is its own parent.
- `end: [n]Index = tables.end`: One past each node's last descendant: the subtree of i is rows [i, end[i]).
- `depth: [n]Index = tables.depth`: Edges from the root to each node: 0 for the root, depth[parent] + 1 otherwise.

## `lcgf.validate` (src/comptime/validate.zig)

Validate: mistakes in a comptime spec become compile errors, before anything is flattened.

### Top level

- `fn check(comptime State: type, comptime spec: Spec, comptime options: Options) void`: Fails compilation with a message naming the first broken rule of `spec` over the state struct `State`.
- `fn regionError(comptime State: type, comptime r: spec_zig.Region) ?[]const u8`: Validate the runtime inputs of a declared region before building either frontend.

### `Options` (struct)
Which optional rules `check` applies.

Fields: `require_names`, `viewport`, `fractional_coordinates`


## `lcgf.columns` (src/comptime/columns.zig)

Columns: which of the runtime tree's columns a comptime UI can keep as read-only tables and which it must keep in RAM.

### Top level

- `Class = enum { constant, dynamic }`
- `fn Coord(comptime viewport: ?[2]f32) type`: The integer type for coordinates on a screen of at most `viewport` px: the smallest of i8, i16 and i32 that holds -m..m for the larger side m, so a rect may start as far left or above the screen as the screen is wide or high.
- `fn Columns(comptime spec: Spec, comptime options: Options) type`: The column split of the UI declared by `spec`.

### `PalettedLook` (struct)
A `Look` whose two colours are indices into the UI's palette (`Columns.palette`): 28 bytes instead of 32.

Fields: `params`, `radius`, `texture`, `material`, `fill`, `border_color`


### `Coord(..)` (generic struct)
The integer type for coordinates on a screen of at most `viewport` px: the smallest of i8, i16 and i32 that holds -m..m for the larger side m, so a rect may start as far left or above the screen as the screen is wide or high.


### `Columns(..)` (generic struct)
The column split of the UI declared by `spec`.

- `node_count = n`
- `specification = spec`
- `slots = @import("dynamic.zig").Slots(spec)`
- `slot_count = slots.count`
- `Coordinate = if (options.fractional_coordinates) f32 else Coord(options.viewport)`: The type of `rect` and `vis` rows in `Dynamic`: `Coord(options.viewport)`.
- `names = info.field_names`: The names of `Tree.Columns`, in declaration order.
- `paletted = classOf("look") == .constant and !isDropped("look") and palette.len <= 256`: `look` stores palette indices (`PalettedLook`) when the palette fits a byte.
- `palette_table: [palette.len]u32 = palette[0..palette.len].*`: The palette, as the read-only table `PalettedLook` rows index.
- `string_table: [strings.len]u8 = strings[0..strings.len].*`: The string table as an array, the form that lands in read-only data.
