Script itemisation: split UTF-8 text into runs of one script, the unit a shaper takes.

### Top level

- `Run = struct { end: usize, script: Script }`

### `Scripts` (struct)

Fields: `text`, `pos`, `open`, `depth`

- `fn next(it: *Scripts) ?Run`: The next run; it starts where the previous one ended (0 first).

## `lcgf.raster` (src/text/raster/raster.zig)

Glyph rasteriser: TrueType outlines to 8-bit coverage, by accumulated signed area.

### Top level

- `subpixel_bins = 4`: Horizontal subpixel positions a glyph is rasterised at: a quarter pixel apart.
- `Pen = struct { pixel: i32, bin: u32 }`: A pen x split for rasterising: the whole pixel the glyph's box is placed from, and the subpixel bin (0 to `subpixel_bins - 1`) whose image is drawn there.
- `test_font = @embedFile("../font/testdata/test.ttf")`
- `Diff = struct { max: u32 = 0, sum: u64 = 0, pixels: u64 = 0 }`: Largest per-pixel difference, and the sum of differences, between `got` (box `gb`) and the reference, over the union of both boxes (pixels outside a box are 0).
- `fn accLen(b: Box) usize`: Floats of accumulation buffer `fill` needs for `b`: one cell per pixel, the two cells an edge on the last row's right border writes past the end, padded to whole vectors for `coverage`.
- `fn imageLen(b: Box) usize`: Bytes of coverage image `coverage` writes for `b`: `b.w * b.h` padded to whole vectors.
- `fn pen(x: f32) Pen`
- `fn shift(bin: u32) f32`: The rasterising shift of subpixel `bin`, in pixels.
- `fn box(o: *const Outline, scale: f32, dx: f32) Box`: The pixel box covering every point of `o` at `scale` pixels per font unit, shifted right by `dx` pixels.
- `fn fill(o: *const Outline, scale: f32, dx: f32, b: Box, acc: []f32) void`: Accumulates the signed area of every contour of `o` (scaled by `scale`, shifted right by `dx` pixels) into `acc`, which it zeroes first.
- `fn segments(o: *const Outline, scale: f32, dx: f32, b: Box, out: *Segments) error{NoSpace}!void`: Appends the edges of `o` (curves flattened as `fill` flattens them) to `out`, in the pixel space of box `b`: x right from its left side, y down from its top.
- `fn coverage(acc: []const f32, out: []u8, n: usize) void`: SIMD.
- `fn compare(gb: Box, got: []const u8, r: Ref) Diff`
- `fn pixel(b: Box, px: []const u8, x: i32, y: i32) u8`: The pixel at column `x` (from the pen) and row `y` (rows above the baseline, the top row of the image being `b.top`), 0 outside the box.
- `fn render(face: opentype.Face, r: Ref, o: *Outline, acc: []f32, out: []u8) struct { Box, []u8 }`: Rasterises one reference glyph here, into the caller's buffers.

### `Box` (struct)
Pixel box of a glyph image.

Fields: `left`, `top`, `w`, `h`


### `Segments` (struct)
Line segments as columns the caller owns; `len` counts the rows in use, the slice lengths are the capacity.

Fields: `x0`, `y0`, `x1`, `y1`, `len`


### `Ref` (struct)
One reference image of testdata/ref.bin.

Fields: `cp`, `px`, `offset`, `b`, `pixels`


### `RefIter` (struct)

Fields: `at`, `left`

- `fn next(it: *RefIter) ?Ref`

### `TestOutline` (struct)
Columns for one test glyph's outline.

Fields: `x`, `y`, `on`, `ends`

- `fn outline(t: *TestOutline) Outline`

## `lcgf.raster_gamma` (src/text/raster/gamma.zig)

Gamma-correct blending tables for glyph coverage.

### Top level

- `fn luminance(r: u8, g: u8, b: u8) u8`: Encoded luminance of an sRGB colour, Rec.

### `Gamma` (struct)

Fields: `rows`

- `buckets = 8`: Text luminances are quantised to this many buckets, black and white included.
- `fn init(gamma: f32) Gamma`: The tables for display gamma `gamma` (2.2 for sRGB; 1 gives the identity).
- `fn bucket(lum: u8) u32`: The bucket of a text colour of luminance `lum` (0 black to 255 white, encoded).
- `fn apply(g: *const Gamma, lum: u8, cov: []const u8, out: []u8) void`: `out[i] = corrected(cov[i])` for text of luminance `lum`.

## `lcgf.raster_atlas` (src/text/raster/atlas.zig)

Glyph atlas: one 8-bit coverage texture that glyph images are packed into.

### Top level

- `fn key(face: u32, glyph: u16, px: u32, bin: u32) u32`: Key of one glyph image: face (< 64), glyph id, pixel size (< 256) and subpixel bin (< 4), packed into 32 bits, so two different images never share a key.

### `Atlas` (struct)

Fields: `w`, `h`, `pixels`, `sky`, `sky_len`, `dirty`

- `Skyline = struct { x: []u32, y: []u32, w: []u32 }`: The skyline, left to right: segment i spans x[i] ..
- `Rect = struct { x: u32, y: u32, w: u32, h: u32 }`: A packed rectangle.
- `fn init(w: u32, h: u32, pixels: []u8, sky: Skyline) Atlas`: An empty atlas over the caller's `pixels` (`w * h`, cleared here) and skyline columns.
- `fn clear(a: *Atlas) void`: Forgets every packed rectangle and clears the texture.
- `fn pack(a: *Atlas, w: u32, h: u32) ?Rect`: Room for a `w` x `h` rectangle, or null when the atlas has none.
- `fn put(a: *Atlas, r: Rect, src: []const u8) void`: Copies a `r.w` x `r.h` image (row by row, `src.len >= r.w * r.h`) into the texture at `r`.
- `fn takeDirty(a: *Atlas) ?Upload`: The part of the texture to upload this frame: a region of `pixels` the GPU copy has not seen, as the arguments of a texture write (wgpu `queue.writeTexture`: data `pixels[offset..]`, `bytes_per_row`, origin `x`, `y`, extent `w`, `h`).
- `fn uv(a: *const Atlas, r: Rect) [4]f32`: Texture coordinates of `r`: left, top, right, bottom in 0..1 (a quad's `uv`).

### `Atlas.Upload` (struct)

Fields: `x`, `y`, `w`, `h`, `offset`, `bytes_per_row`


### `Cache` (struct)
The atlas plus its glyph table: one row per image in the atlas.

Fields: `atlas`, `rows`, `len`

- `fn init(atlas: Atlas, rows: Rows) Cache`
- `fn find(c: *const Cache, k: u32) u32`: Row of the image with key `k`, or `none`.
- `fn insert(c: *Cache, k: u32, w: u32, h: u32, frame: u32) u32`: Room for a `w` x `h` image with key `k` drawn in `frame`: a new row with its rectangle, or `none` when the atlas or the table is full.
- `fn evict(c: *Cache, keep_from: u32) void`: Drops every image last drawn before frame `keep_from`.
- `fn repack(c: *Cache, scratch: []u8) u32`: Packs the remaining images again from an empty skyline, tallest first, moving their pixels: `scratch` (at least `atlas.w * atlas.h` bytes) holds the old texture meanwhile.
- `fn reserve(c: *Cache, k: u32, w: u32, h: u32, frame: u32, scratch: []u8) struct { row: u32, fresh: bool }`: The common case in one call: the row of image `k` for `frame`, marked drawn, inserted if new (`fresh` says the caller must rasterise into it).

### `Cache.Rows` (struct)
Capacity is `rows(cap)` rows per column (the simd padding contract, for `find`).

Fields: `key`, `rect`, `used`


## `lcgf.raster_sdf` (src/text/raster/sdf.zig)

Signed-distance glyphs: optional, for text drawn at many sizes or transformed from one image.

### Top level

- `fn box(o: *const Outline, scale: f32, dx: f32, spread: u32) Box`: The distance image's box: the coverage box grown by `spread` pixels on each side.
- `fn fill(o: *const Outline, scale: f32, dx: f32, spread: u32, b: Box, segs: *raster.Segments, acc: []f32, out: []u8) error{NoSpace}!void`: Writes the distance image of `o` for box `b` (`box(o, scale, dx, spread)`) into `out` (`raster.imageLen(b)` bytes), using `acc` (`raster.accLen(b)` floats) for the coverage that signs it and `segs` for the flattened edges.
- `fn distances(segs: *const raster.Segments, n: usize, w: u32, h: u32, img: []u8, levels: f32) void`: SIMD over the segments.
- `fn decode(v: u8, spread: u32) u8`: Coverage from a distance byte at the scale it was made for: the rule a shader applies.

## `lcgf.shape` (src/text/shape/shape.zig)

Simple shaper: UTF-8 text to positioned glyphs for one face.

### Top level

- `test_font = @embedFile("testdata/shape.ttf")`
- `fn shape(face: *const Face, text: []const u8, run: *Run) error{NoSpace}!void`: Shapes `text` with every feature: the common case in one call.
- `fn width(run: *const Run) i32`: Total advance of the run, font units.
- `fn measure(shaper: *const Shaper, font: u16, tx: *Texts, strings: *const Strings, run: *Run) void`: The measure step of the protocol for `face`: shapes every run of `tx` whose `font` column is `font` and writes its single-line extent, `w` the shaped width and `h` the face's line height (ascender - descender + line gap), in pixels.
- `fn hbCases(features: []const u8, buf: *[1024]u8, out: []HbCase) !usize`: The cases of hb.txt shaped with exactly the feature set `features`.
- `fn expectHb(features: []const u8, opts: Features) !usize`: Shapes every case of `features` with `shaper` and compares each column with HarfBuzz's.

### `Run` (struct)
A shaped glyph run: `len` rows of glyph columns in visual (here: logical) order.

Fields: `glyph`, `cluster`, `advance`, `x_offset`, `y_offset`, `len`


### `Features` (struct)
Which steps `Shaper.shape` runs.

Fields: `liga`, `kern`, `mark`


### `Shaper` (struct)
A face with its layout tables located and the lookups of each feature collected, once.

Fields: `face`, `gsub`, `liga`, `gpos`, `marks`, `classes`

- `fn init(face: Face) Shaper`
- `fn isMark(s: *const Shaper, g: u16) bool`: Whether glyph `g` is a mark (GDEF glyph class 3).
- `fn shape(s: *const Shaper, text: []const u8, run: *Run, features: Features) error{NoSpace}!void`: Shapes `text` into `run` (replacing what it held).

### `TestRun` (struct)
Columns for a test run.

Fields: `glyph`, `cluster`, `advance`, `x_offset`, `y_offset`

- `fn run(t: *TestRun) Run`

### `HbCase` (struct)
One line of hb.txt: the features HarfBuzz shaped with, the text, and its glyphs.

Fields: `features`, `text`, `expected`, `len`


## `lcgf.shape_layout` (src/text/shape/layout.zig)

OpenType layout tables (GSUB, GPOS, GDEF): the common structures the shaper reads.

### Top level

- `Error = error{ Truncated, Unsupported }`
- `ignore_marks = 0x0008`: Lookup flag bits.
- `fn read(comptime T: type, data: []const u8, at: usize) Error!T`: A big-endian `T` at `at`.
- `fn at16(data: []const u8, at: usize) Error![]const u8`: `data` from the 16-bit offset stored at `at` (relative to `data`), to its end.
- `fn featureLookups(table: []const u8, feature: u32, out: *Lookups) Error!void`: Appends to `out` the lookups of feature `feature` in GSUB or GPOS `table`, for the default language system of script `DFLT`, else `latn`, else the first script.
- `fn lookup(table: []const u8, index: u16) Error![]const u8`: Lookup `index` of GSUB or GPOS `table`.
- `fn subtable(lk: []const u8, s: usize, extension: u16) Error!struct { kind: u16, data: []const u8 }`: Subtable `s` of `lk` with its type, extension subtables (GSUB 7, GPOS 9) resolved.
- `fn coverageIndex(cov: []const u8, g: u16) Error!?u16`: Index of `g` in a Coverage table, or null.
- `fn classOf(def: []const u8, g: u16) Error!u16`: Class of `g` in a ClassDef table; 0 for a glyph it does not list.

### `Lookups` (struct)
Lookup indices of a feature, at most `max_lookups`, in the order the feature lists them.

Fields: `index`, `len`

- `max_lookups = 32`
- `fn slice(l: *const Lookups) []const u16`

## `lcgf.shape_cluster` (src/text/shape/cluster.zig)

Cluster mapping: where the cursor goes in a shaped run, and which text offset a click hits.

### Top level

- `fn carets(run: *const shape.Run, text: []const u8, out: *Carets) error{NoSpace}!void`: The stops of `run`, shaped from `text`.
- `fn caretX(c: *const Carets, offset: u32) i32`: The x of a caret at byte `offset`: that of the last stop at or before it, so an offset inside a grapheme snaps back to the grapheme's start.
- `fn hit(c: *const Carets, x: i32) u32`: The byte offset of the stop nearest to `x` (font units from the run's start); the earlier one on a tie.

### `Carets` (struct)
Caret stops of a run, in text order: `offset[k]` is a grapheme boundary, `x[k]` the pen x of a caret there.

Fields: `offset`, `x`, `len`


## `lcgf.shape_cache` (src/text/shape/cache.zig)

Shaping cache: shaped glyph runs kept across frames, so a run is shaped once while its text, font and features stay the same.

### Top level

- `Identity = struct { font: u64, generation: u64 = 0 }`: Caller identity is independent of table position.
- `Context = struct { identity: Identity, engine: u64 = 0, configuration: u64 = 0 }`: An optional external adapter hashes every shaping-affecting setting into configuration.

### `Cache` (struct)

Fields: `mask`, `per_slot`, `keys`, `lens`, `glyphs`

- `fn init(carver: *mem.Carver, slots: u32, per_slot: u32) Cache`: Carves a table of `slots` entries (a power of two) of up to `per_slot` glyphs each, all empty.
- `fn clear(c: *Cache) void`: Forgets every entry, e.g.
- `fn key(text: []const u8, font: u16, features: shaping.Features) u64`: The key of a run: Wyhash of its bytes, seeded with the font and the features.
- `fn keyContext(text: []const u8, context: Context) u64`
- `fn keyVersioned(text: []const u8, identity: Identity, features: shaping.Features) u64`
- `fn get(c: *const Cache, k: u64) ?shaping.Run`: The cached run of key `k`, as a view of its slot (valid until the slot is replaced), or null.
- `fn put(c: *Cache, k: u64, run: *const shaping.Run) bool`: Records `run` under key `k`, replacing what the slot held.
- `fn shape(c: *Cache, shaper: *const shaping.Shaper, font: u16, text: []const u8, features: shaping.Features, scratch: *shaping.Run) error{NoSpace}!shaping.Run`: The common case in one call: the shaped run of `text` in `font` with `features`, from the cache, else shaped into `scratch` and stored.
- `fn shapeVersioned(c: *Cache, shaper: *const shaping.Shaper, identity: Identity, text: []const u8, features: shaping.Features, scratch: *shaping.Run) error{No…`: Generation-aware diagonal convenience; get/put and the uncached shaper remain public.

## `lcgf.render_iface` (src/render/iface.zig)

The back-end contract: what every renderer (software, Vulkan, Metal, D3D12, GL ES, web, wgpu) provides so one `DrawList` draws on any of them.

### Top level

- `required = [_][]const u8{ "Queue", "Pass", "prepare", "drawBatch", "drawText" }`: The declarations `check` requires of a back end.
- `fn check(comptime B: type) void`: Compile error unless `B` declares everything in `required`.
- `fn drawsCanvas(comptime B: type) bool`: Whether back end `B` draws `canvas` commands: it declares `drawCanvas(*const B, B.Pass, k: u32)`, drawing `DrawList.canvases[k]` inside `customs[k]`.
- `fn drawUntilCustom(b: anytype, pass: @TypeOf(b.*).Pass, draw: *const DrawList, from: u32) u32`: Draws commands `from`, `from + 1`, ...
- `fn glyphSpan(g: Glyphs, list: anytype, i: *u32, run: u32) [2]u32`: Text command `i.*`'s glyphs (run `run`) and those of the text commands after it, while each run's glyphs start where the last one's end; `i.*` is left at the last command taken.
- `fn render(b: anytype, pass: @TypeOf(b.*).Pass, draw: *const DrawList) void`: Diagonal: the whole list, skipping custom draws.

### `Glyphs` (struct)
Glyph quads for the frame's text runs, produced by `protocol.Mono.quads` or by your own shaper.

Fields: `quads`, `first`, `count`, `texture`

- `fn span(g: Glyphs, k: u32) [2]u32`: Run `k`'s quads as `{ first, count }`; `{ 0, 0 }` for a run past `first`, so a frame with text runs and empty `Glyphs` (no shaper yet) draws its quads and no text.

## `lcgf.render_conformance` (src/render/conformance.zig)

Conformance scenes: small frames every back end must draw the same way.

### Top level

- `Source = enum(u32) { quads, glyphs }`: Which instance buffer a draw reads.
- `Draw = struct { source: Source, first: u32, count: u32 }`: One expected instanced draw: 4 vertices, `count` instances from `first`.
- `max_quads = 8`: Capacity a draw list needs for any scene (`DrawList.init(carver, nodes, texts, customs)`).
- `max_runs = 2`
- `max_customs = 2`
- `max_glyphs = 32`
- `font: Mono = .{}`: The scenes' font: `Mono` defaults.
- `fn fill(s: *const Scene, d: *DrawList) void`: Copies a scene into a draw list carved with at least the `max_*` capacities.

### `Scene` (struct)

Fields: `name`, `viewport`, `quads`, `runs`, `customs`, `strings`, `cmds`, `draws`


### `canvas_scene` (struct)
The canvas batch (lcgf.canvas): every kind and cap, fills, strokes, dashes across a chain, a non-convex polygon, glyphs turned 0 and 1 quarter turns, an override, a dead row and a hidden layer, under a camera that pans (a far i64 origin), zooms (zoom-scaled widths) and offsets, in a node clipped by its parent.

- `node = [4]f32{ 20, 20, 780, 580 }`: Logical pixels: the node, and the clip its parent gives it.
- `clip = [4]f32{ 40, 30, 760, 570 }`
- `far: i64 = 2_000_000_000`: Twenty world units per pixel, centred on a point two billion units out.
- `camera: canvas.Camera = .{ .origin = .{ far, -far }, .scale = 0.05, .offset = .{ 10, 5 }, .zoom = 2 }`
- `palette: canvas.Palette = .{ .layers = &layers, .zoom_scale = true }`
- `marked = [_]i32{ 1, 6, 17, 40, -1 }`: Rows marked (`Instances.mark`), -1 for the selection's first, and their colours.
- `mark_colors = [_]Color{ .rgb(255, 60, 60), .rgb(60, 255, 255), .rgb(255, 0, 200), .rgb(255, 160, 0), .rgb(255, 255, 255) }`
- `viewport = [2]f32{ 800, 600 }`
- `clear = Color.rgb(13, 13, 15)`: What the golden is drawn over (sw-play's 0d0d0f).
- `tint = Color.rgb(80, 255, 120)`: The selection's override colour.
- `rows = 256`
- `verts = 64`
- `fn w(x: i32, y: i32) [2]i32`: World point at logical (x, y) from the camera's origin.
- `fn build(s: *canvas.Instances) [2]canvas.Range`

### `canvas_scene.inset` (struct)
A second canvas node over the first, under its parents' turn of 0.3 rad about its centre and a clip with rounded corners: the whole batch again, panned to the rects and arcs.

- `node = [4]f32{ 540, 360, 760, 560 }`
- `clip = [4]f32{ 550, 370, 750, 550 }`
- `radii = [4]f32{ 30, 10, 40, 0 }`
- `xform = [6]f32{ ca, sa, -sa, ca, cx - (ca * cx - sa * cy), cy - (sa * cx + ca * cy) }`
- `camera: canvas.Camera = .{ .origin = .{ far + 380 * 20, -far + 20 * 20 }, .scale = 0.05, .zoom = 2 }`

### `CanvasStorage` (struct)
The canvas scene as a draw list of one canvas command, in caller-owned memory.

Fields: `list_buf`, `shape_buf`, `draw`, `shapes`, `ranges`, `inset_ranges`, `overrides`, `rows`

- `fn load(st: *CanvasStorage) void`: Builds the scene; `st` must not move afterwards.

### `Storage` (struct)
One scene, ready to draw: the draw list and its glyph quads, in caller-owned memory.

Fields: `buf`, `glyph_quads`, `first`, `count`, `draw`, `glyph_len`

- `fn load(st: *Storage, s: *const Scene) void`: Fills the draw list and the glyphs for `s`.
- `fn glyphs(st: *const Storage) iface.Glyphs`

## `lcgf.quad_shader` (src/render/shaders/quad.zig)

The quad shader and the vertex layout that matches `draw_list.Quad`.

### Top level

- `stock_material = @embedFile("material.wgsl")`: The stock material (material.wgsl): fill or gradient times the bound texture, nine-slice uv.
- `wgsl = shader(stock_material)`: The stock shader: `Style.material = .solid`.
- `example_material = @embedFile("example_material.wgsl")`: An example user material (example_material.wgsl): diagonal stripes.
- `max_materials = 16`
- `Variant = struct { name: []const u8, wgsl: [:0]const u8 }`
- `Attr = struct { location: u32, offset: u32, format: []const u8 }`
- `stride = @sizeOf(Quad)`
- `fn shader(comptime material_fn: []const u8) [:0]const u8`: The full quad shader around `material_fn`, a WGSL `fn material(in: Frag) -> vec4f`.
- `fn stockFor(comptime use: Usage) [:0]const u8`: The stock shader with only the helpers needed by this UI.

### `Materials` (struct)
The material table a back end builds its quad pipelines from: entry i is the shader of `Material` i, and entry 0 is the stock shader (`Material.solid`).

Fields: `wgsl`, `len`

- `fn add(self: *Materials, material_wgsl: [:0]const u8) Material`: A material with your fragment logic; build `material_wgsl` with `shader`.
- `fn source(self: *const Materials, m: Material) [:0]const u8`: The shader of `m`.

## `lcgf.canvas_shader` (src/render/shaders/canvas.zig)

The canvas shader (canvas.wgsl) and the uniforms it reads, for every GPU back end.

### Top level

- `verts_width = 2048`: Texels a row of `wgsl_es`'s vertex texture.
- `max_overrides = 512`: The most overrides a draw can apply: the uniform array's length, 8 KB, so the whole uniform stays within GL ES 3.0's 16 KB.
- `max_layers = 256`
- `fn fill(u: *Uniforms, list: *const draw_list.DrawList, k: u32, scale: f32, target: [2]f32, row_base: u32, vert_base: u32) bool`: Fills the head, palette and overrides of canvas command `k` of `list` at `scale` physical pixels per logical one, on a `target` of physical pixels; the batch starts at `row_base` and `vert_base` in the GPU buffers.

### `Uniforms` (struct)
canvas.wgsl's `Draw`.

Fields: `view`, `rect`, `clip`, `size`, `opacity`, `row_base`, `vert_base`, `vert_len`, `overrides`, `layers`, `inv`, `inv_t`, `fwd_t`, `fwd`, `radii`, `clip_box`, `px_scale`, `inv_scale`, `rounded`, `_pad`, `palette`, `marks`

- `fn headBytes(layers: u32) usize`: Bytes of the head and the first `layers` palette rows: what `marks` does not cover.

## `lcgf.render_null` (src/render/null.zig)

The null back end: meets the contract in iface.zig, draws nothing, and records every call a GPU back end would make into a log the caller owns.

### Top level

- `Buffer = enum(u32) { quads, glyphs, globals }`: The buffers a GPU back end owns, as the handles the log names them by.
- `fn rebindsAfterHost(calls: []const Call) bool`: True when every draw after a `host` call is preceded, since that call, by a pipeline, both bind groups and a vertex buffer: the full state rebinding iface.zig requires.

### `Globals` (struct)
Prototype `Globals`: group 0, binding 0.

Fields: `viewport`, `scale`, `_pad`


### `Call` (union)

Fields: `write`, `pipeline`, `group`, `vertex_buffer`, `draw`


### `Log` (struct)
Caller-owned call log.

Fields: `calls`, `len`

- `fn push(l: *Log, c: Call) void`
- `fn recorded(l: *const Log) []const Call`

### `Null` (struct)

Fields: `glyphs`

- `Queue = *Log`
- `Pass = *Log`
- `fn prepare(n: *Null, queue: Queue, draw: *const DrawList, glyphs: iface.Glyphs, viewport: [2]f32, scale: f32) void`
- `fn drawBatch(_: *const Null, pass: Pass, batch: draw_list.Batch) void`
- `fn drawText(n: *const Null, pass: Pass, run: u32) void`
- `fn drawGlyphSpan(n: *const Null, pass: Pass, span: [2]u32) void`: The `span[1]` glyph quads from `span[0]`: consecutive text runs in one draw (`iface.drawUntilCustom`).

## `lcgf.gpu_desc` (src/render/gpu/desc.zig)

The descriptors of `lcgf_render.gpu`: WebGPU's object model as plain Zig data, shared by every back end's projection (src/render/<back end>/gpu.zig).

### Top level

- `TextureDimension = enum { @"1d", @"2d", @"3d" }`
- `TextureViewDimension = enum { @"1d", @"2d", @"2d_array", cube, cube_array, @"3d" }`
- `Extent3D = struct { width: u32, height: u32 = 1, depth_or_array_layers: u32 = 1 }`
- `Origin3D = struct { x: u32 = 0, y: u32 = 0, z: u32 = 0 }`
- `AddressMode = enum { clamp_to_edge, repeat, mirror_repeat }`
- `FilterMode = enum { nearest, linear }`
- `MipmapFilterMode = enum { nearest, linear }`
- `CompareFunction = enum { never, less, equal, less_equal, greater, not_equal, greater_equal, always }`
- `VertexStepMode = enum { vertex, instance }`
- `VertexAttribute = struct { format: VertexFormat, offset: u64, shader_location: u32 }`
- `PrimitiveTopology = enum { point_list, line_list, line_strip, triangle_list, triangle_strip }`
- `FrontFace = enum { ccw, cw }`
- `CullMode = enum { none, front, back }`
- `IndexFormat = enum { uint16, uint32 }`
- `BlendFactor = enum { zero, one, src, one_minus_src, src_alpha, one_minus_src_alpha, dst, one_minus_dst, dst_alpha, one_minus_dst_alpha, constant, one_minus_c…`
- `BlendOperation = enum { add, subtract, reverse_subtract, min, max }`
- `MultisampleState = struct { count: u32 = 1, mask: u32 = 0xffff_ffff, alpha_to_coverage_enabled: bool = false }`
- `LoadOp = enum { load, clear }`
- `StoreOp = enum { store, discard }`
- `Color = struct { r: f64 = 0, g: f64 = 0, b: f64 = 0, a: f64 = 0 }`
- `Target = struct { format: TextureFormat, samples: u32 = 1, width: u32 = 0, height: u32 = 0 }`: Where a frame's pixels go, as the host made it: the projection reads the format and sample count from here so a pipeline matches the pass it draws in.
- `Rect = struct { x: u32, y: u32, width: u32, height: u32 }`: A rectangle in framebuffer pixels, rows from the top (WebGPU's scissor convention).
- `fn scissor(rect: [4]f32, clip: [4]f32, scale: f32, width: u32, height: u32) Rect`: The scissor of a custom command (`DrawList.customs[k]`): its rect cut by its clip, scaled to pixels and kept inside a `width` × `height` target.

### `BufferUsage` (struct)

Fields: `map_read`, `map_write`, `copy_src`, `copy_dst`, `index`, `vertex`, `uniform`, `storage`, `indirect`


### `BufferDescriptor` (struct)

Fields: `label`, `size`, `usage`


### `TextureUsage` (struct)

Fields: `copy_src`, `copy_dst`, `texture_binding`, `storage_binding`, `render_attachment`


### `TextureFormat` (enum)
The formats every back end maps.

- `fn bytesPerPixel(f: TextureFormat) u32`

### `TextureDescriptor` (struct)

Fields: `label`, `size`, `format`, `usage`, `dimension`, `mip_level_count`, `sample_count`


### `SamplerDescriptor` (struct)

Fields: `label`, `address_mode_u`, `address_mode_v`, `address_mode_w`, `mag_filter`, `min_filter`, `mipmap_filter`, `lod_min_clamp`, `lod_max_clamp`, `compare`


### `VertexFormat` (enum)

- `fn size(f: VertexFormat) u32`

### `VertexBufferLayout` (struct)

Fields: `array_stride`, `step_mode`, `attributes`


### `PrimitiveState` (struct)

Fields: `topology`, `strip_index_format`, `front_face`, `cull_mode`


### `BlendComponent` (struct)

Fields: `operation`, `src_factor`, `dst_factor`


### `BlendState` (struct)

Fields: `color`, `alpha`


### `ColorWriteMask` (struct)

Fields: `red`, `green`, `blue`, `alpha`

- `all: ColorWriteMask = .{}`

### `ColorTargetState` (struct)

Fields: `format`, `blend`, `write_mask`


### `VertexState` (struct)

Fields: `entry_point`, `buffers`

Fields: `entry_point`, `targets`

## `lcgf.gpu_reflect` (src/render/gpu/reflect.zig)

WGSL reflection at compile time: the resource bindings (`@group(g) @binding(b) var ...`) and entry points (`@vertex fn`, `@fragment fn`, `@compute @workgroup_size(...) fn`) of a shader, read from its source by the Zig compiler.

### Top level

- `SampleType = enum { float, sint, uint, depth }`
- `ViewDimension = enum { @"1d", @"2d", @"2d_array", cube, cube_array, @"3d" }`
- `Access = enum { read, write, read_write }`
- `Stage = enum { vertex, fragment, compute }`
- `Failure = struct { what: []const u8 = "", at: []const u8 = "" }`: Why `parseInto` refused a shader: what it expected, and the source from there on (40 bytes).
- `Error = error{Wgsl}`
- `fn parse(comptime src: []const u8) Module`: Reflects WGSL source `src` while compiling.
- `fn parseInto(src: []const u8, text: []u8, bindings: []Binding, entries: []EntryPoint, failure: *Failure) Error!Module`: Reflects `src` at run time or at comptime, into the caller's memory: `text` (src.len bytes, the source without comments, which the names point into), `bindings` and `entries`.
- `fn groupInto(m: Module, g: u32, out: []Binding) []Binding`: The bindings of group `g` in `m`, ordered by binding index, into `out`: `Module.group` at run time.

### `Kind` (enum)

- `fn isBuffer(k: Kind) bool`

### `Binding` (struct)

Fields: `group`, `binding`, `name`, `kind`, `sample_type`, `view_dimension`, `multisampled`, `storage_format`, `access`, `runtime_sized`


### `EntryPoint` (struct)

Fields: `stage`, `name`, `workgroup_size`


### `Module` (struct)

Fields: `bindings`, `entry_points`

- `fn groupCount(comptime m: Module) u32`: Number of groups: one past the highest group index used.
- `fn group(comptime m: Module, comptime g: u32) []const Binding`: The bindings of group `g`, ordered by binding index.
- `fn entryPoint(comptime m: Module, comptime name: []const u8) EntryPoint`
- `fn Group(comptime m: Module, comptime g: u32, comptime Buffer: type, comptime TextureView: type, comptime Sampler: type) type`: The struct a bind group of group `g` is created from: one field per binding, named as in WGSL, of type `Buffer` for uniform and storage buffers, `TextureView` for textures and storage textures, `Sampler` for samplers.

### `Module.Group(..)` (generic struct)
The struct a bind group of group `g` is created from: one field per binding, named as in WGSL, of type `Buffer` for uniform and storage buffers, `TextureView` for textures and storage textures, `Sampler` for samplers.

The render conformance scene's custom draw (`custom.png`), written once against `lcgf_render.gpu` and compiled for every back end that has a projection.

- `fn Gradient(comptime gpu: type, comptime source: type) type`

### `Gradient(..)` (generic struct)

Fields: `pipeline`, `group`, `screen`

- `Shader = gpu.Shader(source)`
- `fn init(dev: gpu.Device, arena: *gpu.Arena, target: gpu.Target) !G`
- `fn deinit(g: *G, dev: gpu.Device) void`
- `fn upload(g: *const G, up: *gpu.Upload, width: u32, height: u32) void`: The target size, written in the frame's upload phase.
