## `lcgf.gpu_canvas` (src/render/gpu/canvas.zig)

The canvas batch on a GPU, written once against `lcgf_render.gpu`: every GPU back end's `drawCanvas` (src/render/iface.zig) is this type, so vk, metal, d3d12, gles, wgpu and web draw canvases with the same pipeline, the same uploads and the same shader (src/render/shaders/canvas.wgsl; sw's twin is src/render/sw/canvas.zig).

### Top level

- `max_batches = 32`: Ceilings of `Capacity.batches` and `draws` (what the fixed arrays hold), and of `frames`.
- `max_draws = 64`
- `max_frames = 3`
- `fn Canvas(comptime gpu: type, comptime source: type) type`

### `Capacity` (struct)

Fields: `rows`, `verts`, `frames`, `batches`, `draws`, `overrides`


### `Canvas(..)` (generic struct)

Fields: `arena`, `pipeline`, `cap`, `slots`, `row`, `zeros`, `row_written`, `atlas`, `atlas_len`, `batches`, `next_row`, `next_vert`, `frame`, `slot`, `draws`, `draw_len`, `atlas_group`, `size`, `scale`, `uploaded`, `err`

- `Shader = gpu.Shader(source)`
- `fn init(cv: *C, dev: gpu.Device, target: desc.Target, cap: Capacity) !void`: Fills `cv` in place (its arena must not move).
- `fn deinit(cv: *C, dev: gpu.Device) void`
- `fn prepare(cv: *C, dev: gpu.Device, up: ?*gpu.Upload, list: *const draw_list.DrawList, slot: u32, scale: f32, size: [2]u32, atlas: gpu.TextureView, atlas_key…`: Uploads the canvas commands of `list` for ring slot `slot` (0 when `frames` is 1), before the pass: `up` writes when the memory is not mapped.
- `fn draw(cv: *const C, pass: *gpu.RenderPass, list: *const draw_list.DrawList, k: u32) void`: Draws canvas command `k` of the last `prepare` into `pass`: one instanced draw per range, scissored to the node.
- `fn find(cv: *const C, shapes: *const Instances) ?u32`: The batch `shapes` is drawn from, if `placeAll` placed it.

## `lcgf.gpu_check` (src/render/gpu/check.zig)

The projection's own checks, written once against `lcgf_render.gpu` and run by every back end on a real device: the parts of WebGPU's model the conformance scene's gradient does not reach.

### Top level

- `fn RenderToTexture(comptime gpu: type, comptime shaders: type) type`: Upload a 2x2 pattern through staging, draw it into a 4x4 texture, sample that into an 8x8 one in a second pass (the render-to-texture convention), read it back: each source texel is a 4x4 block.
- `fn Compute(comptime gpu: type, comptime shaders: type) type`: A compute pass writes a storage buffer from its index and a uniform; the host reads it.
- `fn Instancing(comptime gpu: type, comptime shaders: type) type`: Instanced draws (instanced.wgsl): a vertex-step buffer of corners, an instance-step buffer of `sint32x2` and `uint32` attributes, and a second instance-step buffer of `sint32x4` bound at an offset, drawn three times with first instances 5, 0 and 2.
- `fn instancing(comptime gpu: type, comptime shaders: type, dev: gpu.Device, h: anytype) !void`
- `fn renderToTexture(comptime gpu: type, comptime shaders: type, dev: gpu.Device, h: anytype) !void`
- `fn compute(comptime gpu: type, comptime shaders: type, dev: gpu.Device, h: anytype) !void`

### `RenderToTexture(..)` (generic struct)
Upload a 2x2 pattern through staging, draw it into a 4x4 texture, sample that into an 8x8 one in a second pass (the render-to-texture convention), read it back: each source texel is a 4x4 block.

Fields: `arena`, `host_arena`, `staging`, `pattern`, `middle`, `final`, `smp`, `quad`, `tint`, `pipeline`, `first`, `second`

- `width = 8`
- `height = 8`
- `fn init(r: *R, dev: gpu.Device) !void`: Fills `r` in place: the arenas must not move once resources live in them.
- `fn deinit(r: *R, dev: gpu.Device) void`
- `fn record(r: *R, up: *gpu.Upload) !void`
- `fn verify(px: []const u8) bool`: Whether `px` (the final texture's RGBA8 rows) has each source texel as a 4x4 block.

### `Compute(..)` (generic struct)
A compute pass writes a storage buffer from its index and a uniform; the host reads it.

Fields: `shared`, `staging`, `out`, `base`, `pipeline`, `group`

- `S = gpu.Shader(shaders.fill)`
- `count = 200`
- `fn init(k: *K, dev: gpu.Device) !void`
- `fn deinit(k: *K, dev: gpu.Device) void`
- `fn record(k: *K, up: *gpu.Upload) void`
- `fn verify(values: []const u32) bool`: Whether element i of `values` is i * 3 + 7.

### `Instancing(..)` (generic struct)
Instanced draws (instanced.wgsl): a vertex-step buffer of corners, an instance-step buffer of `sint32x2` and `uint32` attributes, and a second instance-step buffer of `sint32x4` bound at an offset, drawn three times with first instances 5, 0 and 2.

Fields: `arena`, `host_arena`, `staging`, `target`, `corners`, `rows`, `checks`, `pipeline`

- `width = 8`
- `drawn = [width]bool{ true, false, true, false, false, true, true, false }`: The columns drawn: instances 5 and 6, then 0, then 2.
- `fn init(r: *I, dev: gpu.Device) !void`
- `fn deinit(r: *I, dev: gpu.Device) void`
- `fn color(i: u32) [4]u8`: Column `i`'s colour, as `unpack4x8unorm` reads it (red in the low byte).
- `fn record(r: *I, up: *gpu.Upload) void`
- `fn verify(px: []const u8) bool`: Whether `px` (the target's RGBA8 row) has each drawn column's colour and the clear colour elsewhere.

## `lcgf.gpu_naga` (src/render/gpu/naga.zig)

naga's translations, finished for each back end at compile time.

### Top level

- `SlotKind = enum { buffer, texture, sampler, image, uniform, storage }`
- `Slot = struct { group: u32, binding: u32, slot: u32 }`
- `fn slots(comptime m: reflect.Module, comptime split_buffers: bool, comptime split_images: bool) []const Slot`: The flat slot of every binding of `m`, numbered per class.
- `fn slotOf(comptime table: []const Slot, comptime group: u32, comptime binding: u32) u32`
- `fn glslSlots(comptime m: reflect.Module) []const Slot`: GL ES's slots: uniform buffers, storage buffers, textures and images each numbered apart.
- `fn glsl(comptime m: reflect.Module, comptime src: []const u8) [:0]const u8`: naga's GLSL ES with `binding = k` written into each storage block's and image's layout qualifier.
- `fn glslName(line: []const u8) ?[2]u32`: The (group, binding) of a GLSL line declaring naga's `_group_G_binding_B_<stage>`, if any.
- `fn glslBlocks(comptime text: []const u8, comptime name: []const u8) []const [:0]const u8`: The uniform block names naga gave `name` (`_group_G_binding_B_<stage>`) in `text`: the `uniform <Block> {` on the line that declares it.
- `fn hlsl(comptime src: []const u8) [:0]const u8`: naga's HLSL with its sampler heap removed: each `static const SamplerState x = nagaSamplerHeap[nagaGroupGSamplerIndexArray[B]];` becomes `SamplerState x : register(sB, spaceG);`, and the heap and index arrays go.
- `fn mslSlots(comptime m: reflect.Module) []const Slot`: Metal's slots: buffers (uniform and storage) together, textures with storage textures.
- `fn mslSizesSlot(comptime m: reflect.Module) u32`: Buffer slot of the runtime-sized arrays' lengths (`_buffer_sizes`): after every buffer.
- `fn mslVertexBuffer(slot: u32) u32`: Vertex buffer slot `slot` of a pipeline: counted down from the top of Metal's 31 buffer slots, so it never meets a resource buffer.
- `fn mslSized(comptime m: reflect.Module) []const reflect.Binding`: The runtime-sized storage buffers, in the order naga's `_mslBufferSizes` lists them: the order the WGSL declares them.
- `fn msl(comptime m: reflect.Module, comptime src: []const u8) [:0]const u8`: naga's MSL with every resource at its slot.
- `fn slotsInto(m: reflect.Module, split_buffers: bool, split_images: bool, scratch: []reflect.Binding, out: []Slot) []Slot`: `slots` at run time: one per binding, into `out` (at least `m.bindings.len` long).
- `fn slotIn(table: []const Slot, group: u32, binding: u32) ?u32`: `slotOf` at run time.
- `fn glslAlloc(gpa: std.mem.Allocator, table: []const Slot, src: []const u8) ![:0]u8`: `glsl` at run time: naga's GLSL ES with storage blocks and images given their bindings.
- `fn glslBlocksInto(gpa: std.mem.Allocator, text: []const u8, name: []const u8, out: *std.ArrayList([]const u8)) !void`: `glslBlocks` at run time: the uniform blocks of `text` that hold `name`, appended to `out`.
- `fn hlslAlloc(gpa: std.mem.Allocator, src: []const u8) ![:0]u8`: `hlsl` at run time, for lcgf-gen: naga's samplers taken out of its heap, one `register(sB, spaceG)` each, line endings kept.
- `fn mslSizesSlotIn(m: reflect.Module) u32`: `mslSizesSlot` at run time.
- `fn mslSizedIndex(m: reflect.Module, group: u32, binding: u32) ?u32`: The index of `@group(g) @binding(b)` among the runtime-sized arrays (`mslSized`), if it is one.
- `fn mslAlloc(gpa: std.mem.Allocator, m: reflect.Module, table: []const Slot, src: []const u8) ![:0]u8`: `msl` at run time, for lcgf-gen: naga's MSL with every resource at its slot (`table` from `slotsInto(m, false, false, ...)`).

## `lcgf.context` (src/runtime/context.zig)

Context: the runtime front end's state that outlives a frame (tier 1).

### Top level

- `Cursor = types.Cursor`
- `Purpose = @import("../interact/keyboard.zig").Purpose`
- `root_id = types.Id.from("ui.root")`

### `Limits` (struct)
Capacities.

Fields: `nodes`, `texts`, `customs`, `strings`, `anims`, `tweens`, `flips`, `signals`, `depth`, `timers`, `memos`, `damage`


### `Status` (struct)
Capacity problems this frame.

Fields: `nodes`, `texts`, `customs`, `strings`, `depth`, `anims`, `tweens`, `flips`

- `fn ok(s: Status) bool`

### `Platform` (struct)
Effects the host should apply, returned as data instead of called back.

Fields: `cursor`, `wants_text`, `ime_rect`, `purpose`, `surrounding`, `selection`, `clipboard`, `paste`


### `Output` (struct)
What a frame produced.

Fields: `draw`, `tree`, `texts`, `platform`, `status`, `wake`, `pace`


### `Context` (struct)
Everything that outlives a frame.

Fields: `limits`, `trees`, `texts`, `customs`, `strings`, `cur`, `draw`, `signals`, `anims`, `tweens`, `flips`, `stack`, `depth`, `inter`, `focus`, `scopes`, `previous_scope_kind`, `previous_scoped`, `scoped`, `platform`, `text_input_node`, `status`, `frame`, `settle`, `last_stamp`, `track_pointer`, `settings`, `motion`, `timers`, `waker`, `memos`, `levels`, `damage`, `damage_hook`, `redraw_all`, `last_view`, `canvas_hook`, `canvas_frame`

- `needsFrame = scheduler.needsFrame`
- `fn trackDamage(ctx: *Context) void`: From the next frame on, keeps in the draw list only the quads that reach what changed since the last frame, with `DrawList.damage` saying where: for a host that keeps last frame's pixels and redraws only that (`sw.Setup.draw`, the X11 and Wayland shells, the bare-metal flush).
- `fn enableCanvas(ctx: *Context, rows: []draw_list.CanvasDraw) void`: Lets frames make canvas nodes (`Frame.canvas`): `rows` (at least `2 * limits.customs` of them, the caller's, alive as long as the context) hold `DrawList.canvases`, one per custom row, in two generations as the customs are, so `Frame.reuse` can carry a canvas over from the last frame.
- `fn canvasRows(ctx: *Context) [2][]draw_list.CanvasDraw`: This frame's canvas rows and last frame's.
- `fn redrawAll(ctx: *Context) void`: With `trackDamage`: the next frame's draw list covers the whole window, as for a host that has lost last frame's pixels (a new buffer).
- `fn requiredBytes(limits: Limits) usize`: Bytes `init` needs.
- `fn init(buffer: []align(mem.column_align) u8, limits: Limits) error{BufferTooSmall}!Context`: The buffer is the only memory the library will ever use.
- `fn initZeroed(buffer: []align(mem.column_align) u8, limits: Limits) error{BufferTooSmall}!Context`: `init` over a buffer that is all zero bytes (a static buffer never used before, fresh `mmap` memory): it is not cleared, so capacity the frames never reach stays untouched and costs no resident memory.
- `fn begin(ctx: *Context, in: *const Input) Frame`: Starts a frame: swaps generations, empties the new one, steps interaction against last frame's geometry and opens the root, sized to the viewport.
- `fn beginWithHit(ctx: *Context, in: *const Input, node: u32) Frame`: Starts the ordinary frame with a caller-computed row in the previous completed tree (`ctx.trees[ctx.cur]` before this call).

## `lcgf.frame` (src/runtime/frame.zig)

Frame: one frame under construction in the runtime front end (tier 1).

### Top level

- `fn resolve(t: *Tree, lv: *const levels.Levels, axis: types.Axis, viewport: f32, kept: []const u32) void`: `incremental.resolveKept`, or `levels.resolve` (`lv` built) when nothing is kept.

### `CanvasOptions` (struct)
`Frame.canvas`'s camera, palette, the instance ranges it draws in order (layers; a selection on top) and the colour overrides (the last whose range holds an instance wins).

Fields: `camera`, `palette`, `ranges`, `overrides`


### `Frame` (struct)

Fields: `ctx`, `in`

- `timer = scheduler.timer`
- `reuse = @import("reuse.zig").reuse`
- `fn tree(f: *const Frame) *Tree`
- `fn texts(f: *const Frame) *Texts`
- `fn prevTree(f: *const Frame) *const Tree`
- `fn outline(f: *Frame, node: Node, options: outlines.Outline) Node`: Paint an outside ring at this point in the command order.
- `fn open(f: *Frame, id: Id, style: Style) callconv(style_call) Node`: Starts a node.
- `fn close(f: *Frame) void`
- `fn leaf(f: *Frame, id: Id, style: Style) callconv(style_call) Node`: A node with no children.
- `fn openRow(f: *Frame, id: Id, row: anytype) Node`: `open` from a style already turned into its row (`Template.from`; a `*const Template` or a C `*const FlatRow`): the row is pushed as it is.
- `fn leafRow(f: *Frame, id: Id, row: anytype) Node`: `leaf` from a row (`openRow`).
- `fn text(f: *Frame, str: []const u8, ts: TextStyle) Node`: A text leaf.
- `fn textf(f: *Frame, comptime fmt: []const u8, args: anytype, ts: TextStyle) Node`: A text leaf of `args` formatted by `fmt` (`std.fmt`), written straight into the frame's strings: `f.textf("Count: {d}", .{n}, ts)`, no buffer of the caller's.
- `fn room(f: *const Frame) []u8`: The free end of the frame's strings, to write text into before `textWritten`: how a caller with its own formatter (C++'s `std::format_to_n`, Rust's `fmt::Write`) puts formatted text in the frame without a buffer of its own.
- `fn textWritten(f: *Frame, n: usize, ts: TextStyle) Node`: A text leaf over the `n` bytes just written at `room()`, as `text` would push them; `n` past the room is cut to it, and reported (`Status.strings`).
- `fn textfStyled(f: *Frame, comptime fmt: []const u8, args: anytype, ts: TextStyle, style: Style) Node`: `textf` with full control over the leaf, as `textStyled`.
- `fn textStyled(f: *Frame, str: []const u8, ts: TextStyle, style: Style) Node`: `text` with full control over the leaf (padding, sizing, background).
- `fn custom(f: *Frame, id: Id, style: Style, data: u64) Node`: Reserves a rect that the caller draws itself.
- `fn customRow(f: *Frame, id: Id, row: anytype, data: u64) Node`: `custom` from a row (`openRow`).
- `fn canvas(f: *Frame, id: Id, style: Style, shapes: *const canvas_mod.Instances, o: CanvasOptions) Node`: A node that draws `o.ranges` of `shapes` under `o.camera` with `o.palette`: a `canvas` command in paint order, clipped to the node.
- `fn interact(f: *Frame, node: Node, sense: Sense) Response`: Makes `node` interactive and reports what happened to it this frame.
- `fn drag(f: *const Frame, id: Id) [2]f32`: Pointer movement this frame while `id` is dragged; zero otherwise.
- `fn lastRect(f: *const Frame, id: Id) Rect`: Where `id` was laid out last frame.
- `fn heldRect(f: *const Frame) Rect`: Last frame's rect of the widget the pointer is holding.
- `fn setFill(f: *Frame, node: Node, color: Color) void`
- `fn setCursor(f: *Frame, cursor: Cursor, when: bool) void`: Requests `cursor` if `when` holds.
- `fn focusScope(f: *Frame, node: Node, kind: u8) void`: Makes `node` a focus scope (`focus.Scopes`): `group` for a composite widget that is one Tab stop with arrows inside (a toolbar, a radio group), `trap` for a modal layer that keeps focus among its descendants.
- `fn animate(f: *Frame, id: Id, target: anytype, spring: Spring) values.Of(@TypeOf(target))`: A value that eases toward `target` across frames on a spring keyed by `id`.
- `fn animateLayout(f: *Frame, node: Node, spring: Spring) void`: Slides `node` (and its subtree) on a spring whenever its laid-out position changes, starting from where it was shown last frame.
- `fn sequence(f: *Frame, id: Id, keys: []const keyframes.Key) f32`: Plays `keys` once, from the first frame that asks for `id`, and returns its value now.
- `fn transition(f: *Frame, id: Id, target: Color, tr: Transition) Color`: A colour that moves to `target` along `tr` whenever `target` changes, instead of jumping: one tween per channel, keyed by `id.with(0..3)`.
- `fn setFillAnimated(f: *Frame, node: Node, id: Id, color: Color, tr: Transition) void`: `setFill` with a transition: a fill that changes between frames (hover, press, theme) fades to the new colour along `tr`.
- `fn tween(f: *Frame, id: Id, target: anytype, seconds: f32, ease: Ease) values.Of(@TypeOf(target))`: A value that moves to `target` over `seconds` along `ease`, keyed by `id`: when the target changes, a new leg starts from the current value.
- `fn end(f: *Frame, font: Mono) Output`: Diagonal: measure with `font`, lay out, paint.
- `fn layoutWith(f: *Frame, font: Mono) void`: Measure and arrange without painting: insert prepared textures or explicit passes, then call finish.
- `fn endFlex(f: *Frame, font: Mono, fx: *Flex) Output`: `end` with the flex rules (margins, wrap, align-self, aspect, baselines) the caller wrote into `fx` this frame, one row per node: `fx.set(@backingInt(node), &.{ .margin = ...
- `fn layoutFlex(f: *Frame, font: Mono, fx: *Flex) void`: The layout half of `endFlex`: `layout.runFlex`'s passes over the whole tree.
- `fn endFlexOpts(f: *Frame, font: anytype, fx: *Flex, comptime opts: FlexOptions) Output`: `endFlex` with the flex features of `opts` compiled in (`FlexOptions`), over `font` or a `paragraphs` font.
- `fn layoutFlexOpts(f: *Frame, font: anytype, fx: *Flex, comptime opts: FlexOptions) void`: The layout half of `endFlexOpts`.
- `fn endParagraphs(f: *Frame, font: anytype) Output`: `end` with paragraph styles (line height, spacing, line clamp, ellipsis) and text decorations on some runs: `font` lists them (a `protocol.MonoParagraphsOf`, such as `MonoParagraphs`; its `strings` are set to this frame's).
- `fn layoutParagraphs(f: *Frame, font: anytype) void`: The layout half of `endParagraphs`.
- `fn endFlexParagraphs(f: *Frame, font: anytype, fx: *Flex) Output`: `endFlex` with paragraph styles, as `endParagraphs`.
- `fn layoutFlexParagraphs(f: *Frame, font: anytype, fx: *Flex) void`: The layout half of `endFlexParagraphs`.
- `fn finishParagraphs(f: *Frame, font: anytype) Output`: The paint half of `endParagraphs`: `finish`, with `font`'s decorations painted after their runs, before the host reads the draw list.
- `fn endSides(f: *Frame, font: anytype, sides: Sides) Output`: `end` with the side tables in `sides`.
- `fn layoutSides(f: *Frame, font: anytype, sides: Sides) void`: The layout half of `endSides`: `grid.run`'s passes when there are grids, else `layout.run`'s (with flex rules, `runFlex`'s), with the font's measure and wrap; then the anchors and the sticky nodes.
- `fn layoutSidesOpts(f: *Frame, font: anytype, sides: Sides, comptime opts: FlexOptions) void`: `layoutSides` with the flex features of `opts` compiled in (`layoutFlexOpts`).
- `fn finishSides(f: *Frame, font: anytype, sides: Sides) Output`: The paint half of `endSides`: in layer order when `sides.layers` is given, then a `protocol.MonoParagraphsOf` font's decorations.
- `fn endGrid(f: *Frame, font: Mono, gs: *grid.Grids) Output`: `endSides` with grids only.
- `fn layoutGrid(f: *Frame, font: Mono, gs: *grid.Grids) void`: `layoutSides` with grids only.
- `fn endRounded(f: *Frame, font: Mono, options: rounding.Options) error{InvalidScale}!Output`: A frame on a caller-selected device grid; no second arrangement or finalization pass.
- `fn layoutRounded(f: *Frame, font: Mono, options: rounding.Options) error{InvalidScale}!void`
- `fn arrangeWith(f: *Frame, font: Mono) void`: Measure and place, leaving original fractional positions available for explicit RTL, grid, sticky or custom placement passes.
- `fn finalizeLayout(f: *Frame, options: rounding.Options) error{InvalidScale}!void`: Apply layout transitions after all placement passes, then snap, transform and clip.
- `fn endText(f: *Frame, stack: *TextStack, ascent: []f32) Output`: Font-backed counterpart of `end`.
- `fn endTextStyled(f: *Frame, stack: *TextStack, ascent: []f32, s: protocol.Styled, rows: text_stack.StyledRows, rects: []types.Rect) Output`: Font-backed measurement and arrangement only, the public half of endText.
- `fn layoutText(f: *Frame, stack: *TextStack, ascent: []f32) void`
- `fn endTextRounded(f: *Frame, stack: *TextStack, ascent: []f32, options: rounding.Options) error{InvalidScale}!Output`
- `fn layoutTextRounded(f: *Frame, stack: *TextStack, ascent: []f32, options: rounding.Options) error{InvalidScale}!void`
- `fn arrangeText(f: *Frame, stack: *TextStack, ascent: []f32) void`: Font-backed measurement and placement before caller passes and finalizeLayout.
- `fn finish(f: *Frame) Output`

### `Frame.Sides` (struct)
Caller-owned side tables for `endSides`, each null when the frame uses none.

Fields: `grids`, `anchors`, `sticky`, `layers`, `flex`


## `lcgf.text_lines` (src/text/layout/lines.zig)

Paragraph layout: shaped glyphs broken into lines and placed.

### Top level

- `slack: f32 = 1.0 / 64.0`: Widths may exceed the limit by this much and still fit: layout hands back the width it was given after adding and removing padding, which can differ from it in the last bit.
- `fn breakLines(g: *const Glyphs, text: []const u8, width: f32, lines: *Lines) error{NoSpace}!void`: Breaks the glyphs of `text` (shaped from it, so clusters are offsets into it) into lines no wider than `width` px.
- `fn breakFlow(g: *Glyphs, text: []const u8, width: f32, out: *Lines, flow: Flow) error{NoSpace}!void`: Breaks with word, grapheme, or mandatory-only wrapping and line-relative tab stops.
- `fn spacing(g: *Glyphs, text: []const u8, letter: f32, word: f32) void`: Adds spacing once per shaped grapheme cluster, after its last glyph.
- `fn ellipsize(lines: *Lines, g: *const Glyphs, text: []const u8, width: f32, max_lines: u32, ellipsis: f32) void`: Keeps at most `max_lines` lines (at least 1) and, when lines were dropped or the last one is wider than `width`, cuts the last line to end in an ellipsis `ellipsis` px wide: whole clusters are dropped from its end until its ink (trailing spaces hang, as always) and the ellipsis fit, or nothing is left.
- `fn isSpace(text: []const u8, at: u32) bool`: Whether the character at `at` hangs at a line's end: a space or a mandatory break.
- `fn place(lines: *Lines, g: *Glyphs, text: []const u8, metrics: []const Metrics, a: Align) void`: Stacks the lines from the paragraph's top, aligns each in `a`, and writes each glyph's pen x from the line's left edge.
- `fn graphemeClusters(g: *Glyphs, text: []const u8) void`: Coarsens a shaper's monotonic byte clusters to extended grapheme boundaries.
- `fn reorder(lines: *Lines, g: *Glyphs, text: []const u8, a: Align, s: BidiScratch) error{NoSpace}!void`: Call after `place`: resolves the bidi levels of the text (UAX #9 to L1, per line), writes `Glyphs.level`, and rewrites `Glyphs.x` so each line's glyphs sit in display order (L2).
- `fn stackHeight(lines: *const Lines) f32`: Height of the stacked lines, px.
- `fn widest(lines: *const Lines) f32`: Width of the widest line's ink, px.

### `Glyphs` (struct)
A paragraph's glyphs in pixels, in text order.

Fields: `fonts`, `glyph`, `cluster`, `advance`, `x_offset`, `y_offset`, `span`, `x`, `level`, `len`

- `Fonts = union(enum) { one: u16, spans: []const u16, indices }`: Actual shaper/atlas face identity, separate from the span used for metrics and paint.
- `fn fontAt(g: *const Glyphs, glyph: usize) u16`
- `fn append(g: *Glyphs, run: *const shape.Run, scale: f32, base: u32, span: u32) error{NoSpace}!void`: Appends a shaped run at `scale` px per font unit (px / units_per_em), shaped in span `span`.

### `Lines` (struct)
A paragraph's lines, top to bottom.

Fields: `first`, `end`, `ink`, `start`, `width`, `hard`, `x`, `top`, `baseline`, `height`, `extra`, `len`, `ellipsis`, `ellipsis_x`


### `Align` (struct)
Where lines sit in a box `width` px wide.

Fields: `width`, `line_widths`, `factor`, `justify`, `direction`, `physical`


### `Metrics` (struct)
Vertical metrics of a font at a size, px: ascender above the baseline, descender below it (positive), and the gap after a line.

Fields: `ascent`, `descent`, `gap`

- `fn of(face: *const @import("../font/opentype.zig").Face, px: f32) Metrics`: The face's hhea metrics at `px` pixels per em.
- `fn height(m: Metrics) f32`

### `Flow` (struct)
Breaking options which require glyph advances to be resolved at a line's actual origin.

Fields: `wrap`, `tab_width`

- `Wrap = enum { word, grapheme, none }`

### `BidiScratch` (struct)
Scratch for `reorder`.

Fields: `cps`, `at`, `orig`, `levels`, `cls`, `next`, `pair`, `order`


## `lcgf.text_spans` (src/text/layout/spans.zig)

Rich text: one paragraph in several styles.

### Top level

- `fn shape(shapers: []const shape_.Shaper, text: []const u8, spans: *const Spans, run: *shape_.Run, g: *lines.Glyphs) error{NoSpace}!void`: Shapes every span of `text` with `shapers[spans.font[k]]` and its size into `g`, which it empties first.
- `fn shapeFeatures(shapers: []const shape_.Shaper, text: []const u8, spans: *const Spans, run: *shape_.Run, g: *lines.Glyphs, features: shape_.Features) error{…`: The same segmentation with explicit shaping features.
- `fn shapeCached(shapers: []const shape_.Shaper, text: []const u8, spans: *const Spans, run: *shape_.Run, g: *lines.Glyphs, features: shape_.Features, cache: ?…`: Optional caller-owned shaping cache.
- `fn metrics(shapers: []const shape_.Shaper, spans: *const Spans, out: []lines.Metrics) void`: Vertical metrics of each span, for `lines.place`.

### `Spans` (struct)
Style runs of a paragraph.

Fields: `start`, `font`, `px`, `color`, `len`

- `fn push(s: *Spans, start: u32, font: u16, px: f32, color: u32) error{NoSpace}!u32`: Appends a span from byte `start` on.
- `fn at(s: *const Spans, byte: u32) u32`: The span byte `at` is in: the last one starting at or before it.
- `fn end(s: *const Spans, k: u32, text_len: usize) u32`: Byte offset where span `k` ends.

## `lcgf.text_query` (src/text/layout/query.zig)

Position queries on a laid-out paragraph: index to position (where the caret for a byte offset is drawn) and position to index (which offset a click hits).

### Top level

- `Stop = struct { offset: u32, x: f32 }`: A caret stop: a byte offset and the caret's x, px from the paragraph's left edge.
- `Caret = struct { x: f32, top: f32, baseline: f32, height: f32, line: u32 }`: Where the caret goes: x, and the line's top, baseline and height (px from the paragraph's top left), and the line.
- `fn caret(l: *const Lines, g: *const Glyphs, text: []const u8, index: u32) Caret`: The caret for byte offset `index`: on the last line starting at or before it, at the last stop at or before it (an offset inside a grapheme snaps back to its start).
- `fn hit(l: *const Lines, g: *const Glyphs, text: []const u8, x: f32, y: f32) u32`: The byte offset a click at (`x`, `y`) hits, px from the paragraph's top left: on the line whose band holds `y` (the first above the paragraph, the last below it), the stop nearest to `x`, the earlier on a tie.
- `fn selection(l: *const Lines, g: *const Glyphs, text: []const u8, from: u32, to: u32, out: []Rect) u32`: The text from byte `from` to `to` (grapheme boundaries) as rectangles to highlight, px from the paragraph's top left: one per run of selected glyphs at one bidi level on each line, so a selection across a change of direction covers on screen only what it selects.

### `Stops` (struct)
The caret stops of line `k` in text order (left to right unless `lines.reorder` moved glyphs): the grapheme boundaries of its text, each at the leading edge of the cluster it starts (its left edge, the right edge of a right-to-left one), or a share of the cluster it falls inside; the line's end at the trailing edge of its last cluster.

Fields: `l`, `g`, `text`, `k`, `it`, `base`, `stop`, `i`, `cs`, `ce`, `cx`, `cw`, `rtl`, `tail`, `started`, `final`

- `fn init(l: *const Lines, g: *const Glyphs, text: []const u8, k: u32) Stops`
- `fn next(s: *Stops) ?Stop`

## `lcgf.text_stack` (src/text/layout/stack.zig)

The full text stack on the measure / wrap protocol (src/text/protocol.zig): shaping, line breaking and placement for every run of `Texts`, at the two points the caller sequences.

### Top level

- `emit = @import("emit.zig")`
- `fn breakWidth(tx: *const Texts, k: usize) f32`: The width run `k` is broken at: `avail` when it wraps, else infinite.

### `Paragraph` (struct)
Paragraph inputs, passed directly or in caller-owned rows.

Fields: `alignment`, `last_align`, `direction`, `wrap`, `whitespace`, `tab_size`, `tab_width`, `letter_spacing`, `word_spacing`, `line_height`, `leading`, `max_lines`, `ellipsis`, `features`, `baseline_source`

- `Alignment = enum { start, center, end, left, right, justify }`
- `LineHeight = union(enum) { font, px: f32, multiple: f32 }`

### `Fallback` (struct)
Optional plain-paragraph fallback.

Fields: `chain`, `runs`, `metrics`


### `Rich` (struct)
Optional rich paragraph row.

Fields: `spans`, `metrics`, `normalized_spans`


### `StyledRows` (struct)
Caller-owned rows for `Stack.style`: a paragraph and a rich row per text, spans for the styled runs that have them (their span columns: every span plus one per run), and metrics scratch for the longest span list (plus one).

Fields: `paragraphs`, `rich`, `spans`, `start`, `font`, `px`, `color`, `metrics`


### `Stack` (struct)
Shapers by font index, and scratch for one paragraph.

Fields: `shapers`, `cache`, `font_ids`, `font_generations`, `run`, `g`, `l`, `bidi`, `direction`, `paragraphs`, `default_paragraph`, `normalization`, `fallback`, `rich`, `paint_colors`, `span_px`, `px`, `text`, `normalized`, `ellipsis_glyph`, `ellipsis_font`, `ellipsis_px`, `overflow`

- `fn style(st: *Stack, tx: *const Texts, s: protocol.Styled, rows: StyledRows) error{NoSpace}!void`: Points the stack at the styled runs `s` (the description `MonoParagraphs` measures, as `spec.paragraph`, `spec.spans` and the markup lower to): each listed row's paragraph, and for a run with spans a rich row whose first span is the run's own style.
- `fn decorate(st: *Stack, tx: *const Texts, strings: *const Strings, s: protocol.Styled, d: *DrawList, rects: []Rect) void`: After `paint.emit`: the decorations of `s`'s decorated runs as solid quads right after each run's text (`protocol.insertAfter`), each run laid out again as drawn (`paragraph`) and decorated by `text_decorations.quads` with its face's metrics.
- `fn serves(st: *const Stack, tx: *const Texts, k: usize) bool`: Whether this stack measures row `k`.
- `fn layoutAt(st: *Stack, tx: *const Texts, strings: *const Strings, k: usize, width: f32) error{NoSpace}!void`: Lays row `k` of `tx` out into the scratch (`g`, `l`), start aligned, broken at `width`: shaped with its font at its size, then `lines.breakLines`, `lines.place` and, with `bidi`, `lines.reorder`.
- `fn paragraph(st: *Stack, tx: *const Texts, strings: *const Strings, k: usize) error{NoSpace}!void`: Row `k` laid out as `wrap` left it: broken at `avail` when it wraps, else not at all.
- `fn sourceOffset(st: *const Stack, display: u32) u32`: Maps a current layout/query byte offset back to the original text after collapse.
- `fn pxAt(st: *const Stack, glyph: usize) f32`: Actual pixel size for a glyph's font/atlas lookup, independent of metric-span identity.
- `fn quads(st: *const Stack, run: @import("../../draw/draw_list.zig").TextRun, images: emit.Images, out: []@import("../../draw/draw_list.zig").Quad) emit.Error…`: Run-aware rendering convenience over the same public emission pass and caller images.
- `fn measure(st: *Stack, tx: *Texts, strings: *const Strings, ascent: []f32) void`: Step 1 of the protocol: every run's extent unbroken (newlines still break): `w` the widest line, `h` the stacked lines, `ascent` the first baseline.
- `fn wrap(st: *Stack, tx: *Texts, strings: *const Strings, ascent: []f32) void`: Step 2: runs flagged `wrap` broken at `avail` (written by `layout.textAvail`); `w`, `h` and `ascent` rewritten.

## `lcgf.text_layout_tests` (src/text/layout/tests.zig)

Text layout tests across the lane's files: every golden picture, that the pictures catch mistakes, invariants of breaking, alignment, ellipsis and queries over many random paragraphs, and Unicode cases.

### Top level

- `golden = @import("golden.zig")`: For src/text/layout/conformance.zig, a separate test root.

## `lcgf.scheduler` (src/runtime/scheduler.zig)

Scheduler: when the host must run a frame and when it may sleep (tier 1).

### Top level

- `TimerMode = enum { once, repeat }`
- `fn needsFrame(ctx: *const Context, in: *const Input) bool`: False when a frame built from `in` would be identical to the last one: the input is unchanged (its stamp leaves out the clock, and the pointer position where it changes nothing: see `stamp`), nothing moves, no settle frame is owed and no signal changed.
- `fn wake(ctx: *const Context, in: *const Input) f32`: Seconds until a frame is due with no new input: 0 while a settle frame is owed, a spring moves or a `Wake` was posted during the frame, else the time left to `deadline`, else inf (sleep until the next event).
- `fn deadline(ctx: *const Context) f64`: Earliest clock time (`Input.time`) at which a frame is due without new input, or inf: a timer of `Frame.timer`, a held press reaching `long_time`, the hot widget reaching `hover_delay`.
- `fn pace(ctx: *const Context) Pace`: The pace of the frame just built; the strongest reason wins.
- `fn timer(f: *Frame, id: Id, seconds: f32, mode: TimerMode) bool`: True on the first frame at least `seconds` after the frame that first asked for timer `id`.
- `fn settle(ctx: *Context, in: *const Input) void`: Called by `begin`.
- `fn Mailbox(comptime T: type) type`: A bounded queue for results computed off the UI thread, over a buffer the caller owns.

### `Pace` (enum)
How the host should wait for the next frame.


### `Timers` (struct)
One row per armed timer.

Fields: `len`, `cap`, `c`

- `fn init(carver: *mem.Carver, cap: u32) Timers`
- `fn earliest(t: *const Timers) f64`: The nearest deadline, or inf when no timer is armed.
- `fn sweep(t: *Timers, frame_no: u32) void`: Drops timers nobody asked for in frame `frame`, so a timer the UI stopped asking for cannot keep waking the host.

### `Timers.Columns` (struct)

Fields: `id`, `at`, `seen`, `node`


### `Wake` (struct)
A frame request that any thread may post: a background job posts it after pushing its result to a `Mailbox`, `needsFrame` then says yes, and `begin` clears it.

Fields: `flag`

- `fn post(w: *Wake) void`

### `Mailbox(..)` (generic struct)
A bounded queue for results computed off the UI thread, over a buffer the caller owns.

Fields: `buf`, `head`, `tail`

- `fn init(buf: []T) Self`: `buf.len` must be a power of two, so the free-running u32 counters wrap cleanly.
- `fn push(m: *Self, item: T) bool`: Producer side.
- `fn pop(m: *Self) ?T`: Consumer side.

## `lcgf.reuse` (src/runtime/reuse.zig)

Reuse: signals-driven skipping of unchanged subtrees in the runtime front end (tier 1).

### Top level

- `fn reuse(f: *Frame, id: Id, deps: []const Signal) bool`: Signals-driven skipping.

### `Memos` (struct)
One row per memoized subtree.

Fields: `len`, `cap`, `c`, `kept`, `kept_source`, `kept_len`

- `fn init(carver: *mem.Carver, cap: u32) Memos`: Carves every column from `carver`, `simd.rows(cap)` rows each, so `simd.find` can scan the id column in whole vectors.

### `Memos.Columns` (struct)

Fields: `id`, `node`, `frame`, `stamp`, `focus`


## `lcgf.widgets_base` (src/widgets/base.zig)

Widgets (tier 2): the base every widget is built on, and the four reference widgets (label, button, checkbox, slider) that prove it in both front ends.

### Top level

- `StateColors = [4]Color`: One colour per interaction state, indexed by `hovered | held << 1`.
- `OnOff = [2]Color`: One colour per value of a two-state widget, indexed by the value (false 0, true 1).
- `reference_replay = @embedFile("testdata/reference.replay")`: Input for `Reference(.default)` at 800 x 600, one commented line per frame.
- `fn unit(x: f32) f32`: `x` clamped to 0..1: a position along a track, a fraction of a range.
- `fn stateIndex(r: Response) u2`: The row of a `StateColors` table for response `r`: `hovered | held << 1`.
- `fn fieldError(comptime State: type, comptime widget: []const u8, comptime name: []const u8, comptime T: type) ?[]const u8`: Why `State`'s field `name` cannot be bound by a `widget` that needs a `T`, or null when it can: the message `ComptimeUi` stops compilation with.
- `fn ctResponse(ui: anytype, comptime row: u32, event: anytype, in: *const Input) Response`: What a custom widget at spec row `row` of comptime front end `ui` got this frame, as the runtime front end's `Frame.interact` reports it: hover and held (`response`), clicked (this frame's pointer `event` is on it) and focused (`ui.focus`).
- `fn labelText(th: *const Theme) TextStyle`
- `fn buttonStyle(th: *const Theme) Style`
- `fn buttonFill(th: *const Theme, r: Response) Color`: State selects the colour by table index.
- `fn checkboxStyle(th: *const Theme) Style`
- `fn checkboxBox(th: *const Theme, value: bool) Style`
- `fn sliderTrack(th: *const Theme) Style`
- `fn sliderThumb(th: *const Theme, frac: f32) Style`: The filled part of the track, `frac` of its width.
- `fn toggle(value: bool, clicked: bool) bool`: Toggling is an xor with "clicked".
- `fn slide(value: f32, held: bool, along: f32, lo: f32, hi: f32) f32`: While held, the value follows the pointer: `along` is its position across the track, 0 at the left edge to 1 at the right (clamped).
- `fn fraction(value: f32, lo: f32, hi: f32) f32`: Where `value` sits between `lo` and `hi`, 0 to 1.
- `fn label(f: *Frame, th: *const Theme, str: []const u8) Node`
- `fn button(f: *Frame, th: *const Theme, id: Id, str: []const u8) Response`
- `fn checkbox(f: *Frame, th: *const Theme, id: Id, value: *bool, str: []const u8) Response`
- `fn slider(f: *Frame, th: *const Theme, id: Id, value: *f32, lo: f32, hi: f32) Response`
- `fn ComptimeUi(comptime State: type, comptime spec: Spec, comptime th: Theme) type`: The comptime front end running the reference widgets of `spec`: state in `Signals`, input through `Events`, look and behaviour through the functions above.
- `fn ComptimeUiTokens(comptime State: type, comptime spec: Spec, comptime th: Theme, comptime Tokens: type) type`: `ComptimeUi` whose rows also read run-time theme tokens (`spec.tokened`) from a caller-owned `theme_table.ThemeTable(Tokens)` (`ui.tokens`) when they are pushed, so a changed table lays the page out again on the next frame (`themeChanged`).
- `fn sameDraw(a: *const DrawList, b: *const DrawList) bool`: Whether two draw lists are the same: quad bytes, text runs and commands, in order.
- `fn expectSameFace(comptime caps: context.Limits, comptime build: fn (*Frame, u1) void, at: [2]f32) !void`: Checks that `build(f, 0)` and `build(f, 1)` draw the same list, each in its own context of `caps`, over three frames in a 400 x 300 window with the pointer at `at`: at rest, pressed and released.
- `fn expectReplayAgrees(h: anytype, src: []const u8) !u32`: Runs the replay `src` through harness `h` from `init`: every frame must agree in both front ends (`Harness.frame`).
- `fn Harness(comptime App: type, comptime Ui: type, comptime caps: context.Limits) type`: Runs one UI through both front ends, frame by frame, and checks they agree.
- `fn Semantic(comptime cap: u32) type`: What assistive technology sees of both front ends' current frame in a `Harness`: each tree run through `names`, `states` and `flatten` (a11y/tree.zig), as a bridge would.
- `fn Reference(comptime th: Theme) type`: The reference page: the prototype example's toolbar (a button, a checkbox, a slider and a label on a panel) in both front ends, in theme `th`.

### `Theme` (struct)
Design tokens: every colour and size a widget uses comes from here, so restyling is one value.

Fields: `text`, `text_px`, `panel`, `control`, `control_hot`, `control_held`, `accent`, `radius`, `pad`, `gap`

- `default: Theme = .{}`: The default tokens.
- `fn controlFills(th: *const Theme) StateColors`: A control's face by interaction state: rest, hot, held, held and hot.
- `fn marks(th: *const Theme) OnOff`: A mark (a checkbox's box) by value: off, on.

### `FocusStop` (struct)
The `Spec.widget` of a node inside a custom widget that is a focus stop of its own (a radio option, a range slider's thumb, a chip's remove button): its owner pushes and steps it, so it only says `focusable`.

- `focusable = true`
- `fn push(ui: anytype, comptime n: Spec, parent: u32, next: *u32, in: *const Input) void`

### `ct` (struct)
The reference widgets as spec nodes.

- `fn label(comptime th: Theme, comptime text: anytype) Spec`: A literal string or the value of a string field.
- `fn button(comptime th: Theme, comptime id: @EnumLiteral(), comptime text: anytype) Spec`: A button named `id` showing the string field `text`; its click is its event.
- `fn checkbox(comptime th: Theme, comptime id: @EnumLiteral(), comptime bind: @EnumLiteral(), comptime text: anytype) Spec`: A checkbox named `id` toggling the bool field `bind`, captioned by the string field `text`.
- `fn custom(comptime id: @EnumLiteral(), comptime style: Style, comptime data: u64) Spec`: A slider named `id` setting the f32 field `bind`, 0 to 1.
- `fn slider(comptime th: Theme, comptime id: @EnumLiteral(), comptime bind: @EnumLiteral()) Spec`

### `ComptimeUi(..)` (generic struct)
The comptime front end running the reference widgets of `spec`: state in `Signals`, input through `Events`, look and behaviour through the functions above.


### `ComptimeUiTokens(..)` (generic struct)
`ComptimeUi` whose rows also read run-time theme tokens (`spec.tokened`) from a caller-owned `theme_table.ThemeTable(Tokens)` (`ui.tokens`) when they are pushed, so a changed table lays the page out again on the next frame (`themeChanged`).

Fields: `buf`, `tokens`, `seen`, `tables`, `signals`, `springs`, `pointer`, `rects`, `focus`, `focus_visible`, `frame_settings`, `failure`, `input_mask`, `previous_input_mask`, `build_hit`, `building`, `platform_cursor`, `cursor_set`, `kept`, `built`, `tree_rows`, `window_selection`, `font`, `paragraphs`

- `Events = E`
- `Signals = S`
- `Springs = @import("../anim/anims.zig").BoundSprings(State)`
- `theme = th`: The theme the page was built with, for widgets that build their own rows.
- `default_tokens: Table = if (Tokens == void) {} else .{}`: The table `tokens` points at until the caller sets it: the defaults.
- `Output = struct { draw: *const DrawList, tree: *const Tree, event: ?E.Event, wake: f32 = types.inf, failure: ?anyerror = null, cursor: types.Cursor = .arrow }`
- `fn themeChanged(ui: *const Self) bool`: Whether the token table changed since the last frame, which lays the page out with the new values.
- `fn needsAnimationFrame(ui: *const Self) bool`: True while a declared spring moves or its target has changed since the last frame.
- `fn animated(ui: *const Self, comptime target: S.Field) f32`: The displayed value of a bool/f32 field, interpolated if State declares a spring for it.
- `fn frame(ui: *Self, in: *const Input) Output`: Steps input against last frame's rects, applies each widget's behaviour to the state, then builds, lays out and paints the frame.
- `fn frameWithSettings(ui: *Self, in: *const Input, settings: *const @import("../a11y/settings_data.zig").Settings) Output`: Uses the same accessibility settings as the runtime context.
- `fn frameWithPresentation(ui: *Self, in: *const Input, settings: *const @import("../a11y/settings_data.zig").Settings, presentation: @import("../style/locale.…`: Optional caller-owned direction rows and device grid, sequenced at the same points as style.locale.end.
- `fn rebuild(ui: *Self, in: *const Input) Output`: Builds, lays out and paints the frame again from the state as it is now, applying no input: for a caller that changed what the layout reads after `frame` (a fit region's kept size, `ct_interop.setFit`), so the frame shows it without waiting for the next.
- `fn frameText(ui: *Self, in: *const Input, text: Shaped) Output`
- `fn push(ui: *Self, comptime n: Spec, parent: u32, next: *u32, in: *const Input) void`: Pushes `n` (spec row `next.*`) and its subtree under tree row `parent`.
- `fn semantics(ui: *Self, s: *a11y.Semantics) void`: Names each live node of this frame that has an accessible name (`Spec.name`, set with `spec.named`) in `s` (`Semantics.label`), its bytes added to this frame's strings.
- `fn keep(ui: *Self, comptime W: type) *[4]f32`: Hover and held for the widget at spec row `row`, as `Interaction.response` reports them: while a widget is held, only it shows hover.
- `fn treeRow(ui: *const Self, row: u32) u32`: Current tree row of a spec descriptor, or types.none when its subtree is inactive.
- `fn lastRect(ui: *const Self, row: u32) types.Rect`: During a widget step, geometry of a descriptor in the last completed frame.
- `fn fail(ui: *Self, err: anyerror) void`: Custom widgets keep the existing non-error-returning frame API and report failure as output data.
- `fn setCursor(ui: *Self, cursor: types.Cursor, when: bool) void`: Explicit override as on Frame.
- `fn rowsOf(comptime row: u32) u32`: Rows of the spec node at row `row`, itself included.
- `fn response(ui: *const Self, row: u32, in: *const Input) Response`
- `fn field(comptime name: []const u8) S.Field`

### `Shaped` (struct)
A real shaper for `ComptimeUi.frameText`: the caller's stack, `ascent` scratch (a row per label), and for a page with styled labels the rows `Stack.style` points the stack at and `rects` scratch for `Stack.decorate` (at least the glyph rows), as `Frame.endTextStyled`.

Fields: `stack`, `ascent`, `rows`, `rects`


### `Harness(..)` (generic struct)
Runs one UI through both front ends, frame by frame, and checks they agree.

Fields: `buffer`, `ctx`, `app`, `ui`, `frames`

- `Pair = struct { runtime: context.Output, comptime_: Ui.Output }`: What one frame produced in each front end.
- `fn init(h: *Self) void`: Carves the runtime context from the harness's buffer and resets both front ends.
- `fn frame(h: *Self, in: *const Input) error{FrontEndsDiffer}!Pair`: Builds frame `in` in both front ends.

### `Semantic(..)` (generic struct)
What assistive technology sees of both front ends' current frame in a `Harness`: each tree run through `names`, `states` and `flatten` (a11y/tree.zig), as a bridge would.

Fields: `buf`, `out`, `strings`

- `fn run(x: *Self, h: anytype) error{FrontEndsDiffer}!void`: Exports the frame harness `h` built last.
- `fn name(x: *const Self, side: u1, r: u32) []const u8`: The accessible name of export row `r` of front end `side` (0 runtime, 1 comptime).
- `fn row(x: *const Self, node: u32) ?u32`: The export row of tree row `node` (the runtime front end's numbering, which the comptime one shares), or null when the node is not exported.

### `Reference(..)` (generic struct)
The reference page: the prototype example's toolbar (a button, a checkbox, a slider and a label on a panel) in both front ends, in theme `th`.

- `Ui = ComptimeUi(State, spec, th)`
- `page_limits: context.Limits = .{ .nodes = 64, .texts = 16, .customs = 1, .strings = 1024, .depth = 8 }`: Room for the page in the runtime front end.
- `Run = Harness(App, Ui, page_limits)`: Both front ends over this page.

### `Reference(..).App` (struct)
Runtime front end: the application owns the values.

Fields: `resets`, `wireframe`, `exposure`, `clicked`

- `fn build(app: *App, f: *Frame) void`

### `Reference(..).State` (struct)
Comptime front end: the same toolbar as a spec over a state struct.

Fields: `reset_text`, `wireframe`, `wire_text`, `exposure`, `layer`


## `lcgf.widgets` (src/widgets/kit.zig)

Kit: the runtime front end's common widgets behind one value that holds the frame and the theme, each taking its options by name.

### Top level

- `fn on(f: *Frame, th: *const Theme) Kit`: The kit over frame `f` with theme `th`, for this frame.
- `fn idOf(id: anytype) Id`: `id` as an `Id`: an `Id` as it is, an enum literal hashed while compiling.

### `Kit` (struct)

Fields: `f`, `theme`

- `ButtonOptions = struct { label: []const u8 = "" }`
- `CheckboxOptions = struct { label: []const u8 = "" }`
- `SliderOptions = struct { min: f32 = 0, max: f32 = 1 }`
- `TextfieldOptions = struct { hint: []const u8 = "" }`
- `fn label(ui: Kit, str: []const u8) Node`: Text showing `str`, copied (it may be a temporary).
- `fn text(ui: Kit, comptime fmt: []const u8, args: anytype) Node`: Text of `args` formatted by `fmt` (`std.fmt`), written into the frame's strings: no buffer of the caller's.
- `fn button(ui: Kit, id: anytype, o: ButtonOptions) Response`: A button; `clicked` is set the frame it fires.
- `fn checkbox(ui: Kit, id: anytype, value: anytype, o: CheckboxOptions) Response`: A checkbox over `value` (a `*bool` or a `*Check`), toggled by a click or Space.
- `fn slider(ui: Kit, id: anytype, value: *f32, o: SliderOptions) Response`: A slider over `value`, from `min` at the left end to `max` at the right.
- `fn textfield(ui: Kit, id: anytype, state: anytype, o: TextfieldOptions) Response`: A one-line text field over `state` (a `*textfield.Text(cap)`), showing `hint` while empty; what the input did to the text is `state.result`.

## `lcgf.widgets_label` (src/widgets/label.zig)

Label: static text in the theme's size and colour.

### Top level

- `Bind = []const u8`: The type a comptime label's bound field must have.
- `fn bindError(comptime State: type, comptime name: []const u8) ?[]const u8`: Why `State`'s field `name` cannot be shown by a label, or null when it can.
- `fn labelText(th: *const Theme) TextStyle`: The text: the theme's size and colour.
- `fn labelStyle(th: *const Theme) Style`: The leaf around the text: sized to it (the default `fit`), no fill, static text to assistive technology.
- `fn label(f: *Frame, th: *const Theme, str: []const u8) Node`: A text leaf showing `str`.

### `ct` (struct)

- `fn label(comptime th: Theme, comptime text: anytype) Spec`: A literal string or a bound string field: `ct.label(th, "Title")` or `ct.label(th, .title)`.

### `Page` (struct)
The label's example page: three labels in a column on a panel, in both front ends.

- `spec = spec_zig.col(panel, .{ ct.label(th, .title), ct.label(th, .layer), ct.label(th, .exposure) })`
- `Ui = base.ComptimeUi(State, spec, th)`
- `Run = base.Harness(App, Ui, .{ .nodes = 16, .texts = 4, .customs = 1, .strings = 256, .depth = 8 })`

### `Page.State` (struct)

## `lcgf.widgets_button` (src/widgets/button.zig)

Button: a caption on a face that reports a click.

### Top level

- `fn buttonStyle(th: *const Theme) Style`: The face: a row padded by the theme's `pad`, caption centred, corners of `radius`.
- `fn buttonFill(th: *const Theme, r: Response) Color`: The face's colour: the theme's control fills, picked by table index from hover and held.
- `fn activated(clicked: bool, focused: bool, in: *const Input) bool`: Whether the button fires this frame: clicked, or focused and Enter went down or Space came up (the WAI-ARIA APG button pattern; Space on release, as native buttons do, so holding it does not repeat).
- `fn button(f: *Frame, th: *const Theme, id: Id, str: []const u8) Response`: A button named `id` showing `str`; `clicked` is set the frame it fires (`activated`): the left button goes up over it after going down on it, or, while it has focus, Enter or Space.
- `fn buttonStyled(f: *Frame, th: *const Theme, id: Id, str: []const u8, ts: TextStyle) Response`: `button` with its caption drawn in `ts` instead of the theme's label text.
- `fn buttonFace(f: *Frame, th: *const Theme, id: Id, str: []const u8, ts: TextStyle, face: Style) Response`: `buttonStyled` with its face laid out by `face` instead of `buttonStyle(th)`, as `spec.styled` restyles `ct.button` (CSS on a `<button>`); the fill still follows hover and held.
- `fn Comptime(comptime name: [:0]const u8) type`: The comptime button named `name` (`Spec.widget`): it builds the same rows as `button`, with the comptime front end's hover and held, and turns Enter and Space on it while focused into its event, as `button` sets `clicked`.

### `ct` (struct)

- `fn button(comptime th: Theme, comptime id: @EnumLiteral(), comptime text: anytype) Spec`: A button named `id` showing the string field `text`; its click is its event: `ct.button(th, .reset, .reset_text)`.
- `fn buttonStyled(comptime th: Theme, comptime id: @EnumLiteral(), comptime text: anytype, comptime text_style: TextStyle) Spec`: `button` with its caption drawn in `text_style` instead of the page theme's (CSS `color` and `font-size` on a `<button>`): `ct.buttonStyled(th, .go, "Go", .{ .px = 18 })`.

### `Comptime(..)` (generic struct)
The comptime button named `name` (`Spec.widget`): it builds the same rows as `button`, with the comptime front end's hover and held, and turns Enter and Space on it while focused into its event, as `button` sets `clicked`.

- `focusable = true`
- `fn push(ui: anytype, comptime n: Spec, parent: u32, next: *u32, in: *const Input) void`
- `fn step(ui: anytype, comptime row: u32, event: anytype, in: *const Input) @TypeOf(event)`

### `Page` (struct)
The button's example page: two buttons on a panel, in both front ends, counting their clicks.

- `State = struct { reset_text: []const u8 = "Reset", apply_text: []const u8 = "Apply" }`
- `spec = spec_zig.row(panel, .{ ct.button(th, .reset, .reset_text), ct.button(th, .apply, .apply_text) })`
- `Ui = base.ComptimeUi(State, spec, th)`
- `Run = base.Harness(App, Ui, .{ .nodes = 16, .texts = 4, .customs = 1, .strings = 256, .depth = 8 })`
- `replay = @embedFile("testdata/button.replay")`
- `golden_frames = .{ .{ "rest", 0 }, .{ "hover", 1 }, .{ "held", 2 } }`: Frames kept as golden images, by name: tools/golden/ref/widgets/button-<name>.png.

### `Page.App` (struct)

Fields: `resets`, `applies`

- `fn build(app: *App, f: *Frame) void`

## `lcgf.widgets_link` (src/widgets/link.zig)

Link: inline text in the accent colour that reports activation, as a page link does.

### Top level

- `linkFill = breadcrumb.linkFill`: The target's fill by state: none at rest, control-hot under the pointer, held pressed.
- `linkText = breadcrumb.linkText`: The text: the theme's size in the accent colour.
- `fn linkStyle(th: *const Theme) Style`: The target: a breadcrumb link's padded, rounded row, role link.
- `fn activated(clicked: bool, focused: bool, in: *const Input) bool`: Whether the link fires this frame: clicked, or focused and Enter went down (the WAI-ARIA APG link pattern: links take Enter, not Space).
- `fn link(f: *Frame, th: *const Theme, id: Id, str: []const u8) Response`: A link named `id` showing `str`; `clicked` is set the frame it fires (`activated`).
- `fn linkFace(f: *Frame, th: *const Theme, id: Id, str: []const u8, face: Style) Response`: `link` with its target laid out by `face` instead of `linkStyle(th)`, as `spec.styled` restyles `ct.link` (CSS on an `<a href>`); the fill still follows hover and held.
- `fn Comptime(comptime name: [:0]const u8) type`: The comptime link named `name` (`Spec.widget`): the same rows as `link`, and Enter on it while focused turned into its event, as `link` sets `clicked`.

### `ct` (struct)

- `fn link(comptime th: Theme, comptime id: @EnumLiteral(), comptime text: anytype) Spec`: A link named `id` showing `text`, a literal or a string field (as `ct.label`); its activation is its event: `ct.link(th, .docs, "Read the docs")`.

### `Comptime(..)` (generic struct)
The comptime link named `name` (`Spec.widget`): the same rows as `link`, and Enter on it while focused turned into its event, as `link` sets `clicked`.

- `focusable = true`
- `fn push(ui: anytype, comptime n: Spec, parent: u32, next: *u32, in: *const Input) void`
- `fn step(ui: anytype, comptime row: u32, event: anytype, in: *const Input) @TypeOf(event)`

### `Page` (struct)
The link's example page: two links on a panel, in both front ends, counting activations.

- `State = struct { home_text: []const u8 = "Home" }`
- `spec = spec_zig.row(panel, .{ ct.link(th, .docs, "Docs"), ct.link(th, .home, .home_text) })`
- `Ui = base.ComptimeUi(State, spec, th)`
- `Run = base.Harness(App, Ui, .{ .nodes = 16, .texts = 4, .customs = 1, .strings = 256, .depth = 8 })`

## `lcgf.widgets_checkbox` (src/widgets/checkbox.zig)

Checkbox: a box and a caption; a click toggles it.

### Top level

- `fn bindError(comptime State: type, comptime name: []const u8) ?[]const u8`: Why `State`'s field `name` cannot be a checkbox's value, or null when it can: it must be a `bool` or a `Check`.
- `fn flip(v: anytype, clicked: bool) @TypeOf(v)`: `v` toggled by `clicked`, in its own type: a `bool` flips, a `Check` goes to `on` from `off` or `mixed` and to `off` from `on`.
- `fn toggled(clicked: bool, focused: bool, in: *const Input) bool`: Whether the checkbox toggles this frame: clicked, or focused and Space came up (WAI-ARIA APG checkbox pattern: Space toggles, Enter does not; on release, as native checkboxes do).
- `fn checkboxStyle(th: *const Theme) Style`: The row: box, then caption, centred across, `gap` apart; role checkbox.
- `fn boxFills(th: *const Theme) [3]Color`: The box's fill by value: off the control colour, on and mixed the accent.
- `fn checkboxBox(th: *const Theme, value: Check) Style`: The 16 px box, outlined, filled by `boxFills`; it centres the dash.
- `fn checkboxDash(th: *const Theme, value: Check) Style`: The mixed state's dash across the box: always there, transparent (so it paints nothing) unless the value is mixed, so the look is picked by table index and the rows never change.
- `fn checkbox(f: *Frame, th: *const Theme, id: Id, value: anytype, str: []const u8) Response`: A checkbox named `id` over `value` (a `*bool` or a `*Check`), captioned by `str`.
- `fn checkboxFace(f: *Frame, th: *const Theme, id: Id, value: anytype, str: []const u8, face: Style) Response`: `checkbox` with its row laid out by `face` instead of `checkboxStyle(th)`, as `spec.styled` restyles `ct.checkbox`.
- `fn Comptime(comptime name: [:0]const u8, comptime bind: []const u8) type`: The comptime checkbox named `name` over field `bind` (`Spec.widget`): the same rows as `checkbox`, and the same input (`toggled`, `flip`) applied to the comptime front end's state; Space on it while focused is also its event, as `checkbox` sets `clicked`.

### `Check` (enum)
A tri-state checkbox's value.

- `fn of(v: anytype) Check`: `v` as a `Check`: a `bool` is off or on, a `Check` is itself.
- `fn value(c: Check) f32`: What the tree's `value` column holds, which accessibility reads: off 0, on 1, mixed 0.5 (ARIA `aria-checked="mixed"`).

### `ct` (struct)

- `fn checkbox(comptime th: Theme, comptime id: @EnumLiteral(), comptime bind: @EnumLiteral(), comptime text: anytype) Spec`: A checkbox named `id` over the `bool` or `Check` field `bind`, captioned by the string field `text`: `ct.checkbox(th, .wire, .wireframe, .wire_text)`.

### `Comptime(..)` (generic struct)
The comptime checkbox named `name` over field `bind` (`Spec.widget`): the same rows as `checkbox`, and the same input (`toggled`, `flip`) applied to the comptime front end's state; Space on it while focused is also its event, as `checkbox` sets `clicked`.

- `focusable = true`
- `fn bindError(comptime State: type, comptime field: []const u8) ?[]const u8`
- `fn push(ui: anytype, comptime n: Spec, parent: u32, next: *u32, in: *const Input) void`
- `fn step(ui: anytype, comptime row: u32, event: anytype, in: *const Input) @TypeOf(event)`

### `Page` (struct)
The checkbox's example page: a tri-state "All" that starts mixed and two plain boxes on a panel, in both front ends.

- `Ui = base.ComptimeUi(State, spec, th)`
- `Run = base.Harness(App, Ui, .{ .nodes = 32, .texts = 4, .customs = 1, .strings = 256, .depth = 8 })`
- `replay = @embedFile("testdata/checkbox.replay")`
- `golden_frames = .{ .{ "rest", 0 }, .{ "on", 2 }, .{ "keys", 5 } }`: Frames kept as golden images, by name: tools/golden/ref/widgets/checkbox-<name>.png.

### `Page.App` (struct)

Fields: `all`, `wire`, `shadows`

- `fn build(app: *App, f: *Frame) void`

### `Page.State` (struct)

Fields: `all`, `all_text`, `wire`, `wire_text`, `shadows`, `shadows_text`


## `lcgf.widgets_radio` (src/widgets/radio.zig)

Radio group: a set of options of which exactly one is selected.

### Top level

- `Bind = u32`: The type of a radio group's bound field: the index of the selected option.
- `Step = struct { value: u32, focus: u32 }`: What one frame's input does to a group's selection and focus.
- `option_rows = 4`: Rows each option takes in the tree: the option, its ring, the dot, the caption.
- `fn bindError(comptime State: type, comptime name: []const u8) ?[]const u8`: Why `State`'s field `name` cannot hold a radio group's selection, or null when it can.
- `fn optionId(group: Id, index: u32) Id`: The id of option `index` of the group named `group`, in both front ends: the runtime front end derives it with `Id.with`, the comptime one from the option's spec name.
- `fn step(n: u32, clicked: u32, focused: u32, in: *const Input) ?Step`: The selection and focus of a group of `n` options after this frame's input, or null when nothing changes them.
- `fn groupStyle(th: *const Theme) Style`: The group: options stacked, `gap` apart.
- `fn optionStyle(th: *const Theme) Style`: One option: ring, then caption, centred across, `gap` apart.
- `fn ringStyle(th: *const Theme) Style`: The 16 px ring, outlined in the control's hot colour; it centres the dot.
- `fn dotStyle(th: *const Theme, selected: bool) Style`: The dot inside the ring, picked by table index from whether the option is selected: transparent (it paints nothing) or the accent.
- `fn radio(f: *Frame, th: *const Theme, id: Id, value: *u32, captions: []const []const u8) Response`: A radio group named `id` over `value`, the selected index, one option per caption.
- `fn radioFace(f: *Frame, th: *const Theme, id: Id, value: *u32, captions: []const []const u8, face: Style) Response`: `radio` with its group laid out by `face` instead of `groupStyle(th)`, as `spec.styled` restyles `ct.radio`; the options are the theme's.
- `fn paintSelection(t: *Tree, th: *const Theme, group: u32, value: u32, n: u32) void`: Writes the dots' fills, the options' value column (1 selected, 0 not) and the roles for the group at tree row `group` with `n` options, selection `value`: the group is a radio group and each option a radio, roles past `types.Role` written to the column directly.
- `fn Comptime(comptime name: [:0]const u8, comptime bind: []const u8, comptime n: u32) type`: The comptime radio group named `name` over field `bind`, with `n` options (`Spec.widget`): the same rows as `radio`, and the same input (`step`) applied to the comptime front end's state and focus.

### `ct` (struct)

- `fn radio(comptime th: Theme, comptime id: @EnumLiteral(), comptime bind: @EnumLiteral(), comptime captions: anytype) Spec`: A radio group named `id` selecting the `u32` field `bind`, one option per string field in `captions` (a tuple of enum literals): `ct.radio(th, .size, .size, .{ .s_text, .m_text, .l_text })`.

### `Comptime(..)` (generic struct)
The comptime radio group named `name` over field `bind`, with `n` options (`Spec.widget`): the same rows as `radio`, and the same input (`step`) applied to the comptime front end's state and focus.

- `focusable = true`
- `fn bindError(comptime State: type, comptime field: []const u8) ?[]const u8`
- `fn push(ui: anytype, comptime g: Spec, parent: u32, next: *u32, in: *const Input) void`
- `fn step(ui: anytype, comptime row: u32, event: anytype, in: *const Input) @TypeOf(event)`

### `Page` (struct)
The radio group's example page: three quality levels on a panel, in both front ends.

- `spec = spec_zig.col(panel, .{ct.radio(th, .quality, .quality, .{ .low, .medium, .high })})`
- `Ui = base.ComptimeUi(State, spec, th)`
- `Run = base.Harness(App, Ui, .{ .nodes = 32, .texts = 4, .customs = 1, .strings = 256, .depth = 8 })`
- `replay = @embedFile("testdata/radio.replay")`
- `golden_frames = .{ .{ "rest", 0 }, .{ "clicked", 2 }, .{ "down", 3 } }`: Frames kept as golden images, by name: tools/golden/ref/widgets/radio-<name>.png.
- `fn semantics(t: *const Tree, s: *a11y.Semantics, str: *Strings) void`: The page's accessibility beyond the tree, for either front end's tree `t`: the group's name, which no caption on the page shows (`Semantics.label`, added to the frame's strings `str`).

### `Page.App` (struct)

Fields: `quality`

- `fn build(app: *App, f: *Frame) void`

### `Page.State` (struct)

Fields: `quality`, `low`, `medium`, `high`

## `lcgf.widgets_toggle` (src/widgets/toggle.zig)

Toggle button: a button that stays pressed until it is pressed again (bold, mute, pin).

## `lcgf.widgets_slider` (src/widgets/slider.zig)

Slider: a value between two ends, set by dragging along a track.

### Top level

- `Bind = f32`: The type of a slider's bound field.
- `Range = struct { lo: f32 = 0, hi: f32 = 1 }`: The ends of a comptime slider's value.
- `fn bindError(comptime State: type, comptime name: []const u8) ?[]const u8`: Why `State`'s field `name` cannot be a slider's value, or null when it can.
- `fn keyed(value: f32, lo: f32, hi: f32, in: *const Input) ?f32`: The value after this frame's keys on a focused slider over `lo`..`hi`, or null when no key of the slider's went down: Right and Up add a hundredth of the range, Left and Down take one away, Page Up and Page Down a tenth, Home goes to `lo` and End to `hi` (WAI-ARIA APG slider pattern), in event order, clamped to the ends.
- `fn sliderTrack(th: *const Theme) Style`: The track: as wide as it is given (at least 40), 8 tall, rounded, the control colour; role slider.
- `fn sliderThumb(th: *const Theme, frac: f32) Style`: The filled part of the track, `frac` of its width, in the accent.
- `fn slider(f: *Frame, th: *const Theme, id: Id, value: *f32, lo: f32, hi: f32) Response`: A slider named `id` over `value`, from `lo` at the left end to `hi` at the right.
- `fn sliderFace(f: *Frame, th: *const Theme, id: Id, value: *f32, lo: f32, hi: f32, face: Style) Response`: `slider` with its track laid out by `face` instead of `sliderTrack(th)`, as `spec.styled` restyles `ct.slider`; the thumb is the theme's.
- `fn semantics(t: *const Tree, s: *a11y.Semantics, id: Id, lo: f32, hi: f32) void`: The range of the slider named `id` in `t`, for assistive technology: `lo` to `hi`, stepped by a hundredth of it as its arrow keys are (`keyed`).
- `fn Comptime(comptime name: [:0]const u8, comptime bind: []const u8, comptime range: Range) type`: The comptime slider named `name` over field `bind`, running `range` (`Spec.widget`): the same rows as `slider`, and the same input applied to the comptime front end's state: while held the value follows the pointer across the track's last rect (`base.slide`), while focused the keys move it (`keyed`).

### `ct` (struct)

- `fn slider(comptime th: Theme, comptime id: @EnumLiteral(), comptime bind: @EnumLiteral(), comptime range: Range) Spec`: A slider named `id` over the `f32` field `bind`, running `range.lo` to `range.hi`: `ct.slider(th, .volume, .volume, .{ .hi = 100 })`; the range rides in its `Spec.widget` type.

### `Comptime(..)` (generic struct)
The comptime slider named `name` over field `bind`, running `range` (`Spec.widget`): the same rows as `slider`, and the same input applied to the comptime front end's state: while held the value follows the pointer across the track's last rect (`base.slide`), while focused the keys move it (`keyed`).

- `focusable = true`
- `Payload = f32`: Its event is its value, each frame it is held or a key moves it, as the reference slider's (and the seam's) is.
- `fn bindError(comptime State: type, comptime field: []const u8) ?[]const u8`
- `fn push(ui: anytype, comptime n: Spec, parent: u32, next: *u32, in: *const Input) void`
- `fn step(ui: anytype, comptime row: u32, event: anytype, in: *const Input) @TypeOf(event)`

### `Page` (struct)
The slider's example page: Volume (0..100) and Gain (-12..12), each under its label, on a panel, in both front ends.

- `Ui = base.ComptimeUi(State, spec, th)`
- `Run = base.Harness(App, Ui, .{ .nodes = 16, .texts = 4, .customs = 1, .strings = 256, .depth = 8 })`
- `replay = @embedFile("testdata/slider.replay")`
- `golden_frames = .{ .{ "rest", 0 }, .{ "pressed", 1 }, .{ "keys", 6 } }`: Frames kept as golden images, by name: tools/golden/ref/widgets/slider-<name>.png.
- `fn semantics(t: *const Tree, s: *a11y.Semantics) void`: The page's accessibility beyond the tree, for either front end's tree `t`: each slider labelled by the caption above it, and its range.

### `Page.App` (struct)

Fields: `volume`, `gain`

- `fn build(app: *App, f: *Frame) void`
