## `lcgf.widgets_popover` (src/widgets/popover.zig)

Popover (tier 2): a button that opens a floating panel next to it, the WAI-ARIA APG non-modal dialog pattern.

### Top level

- `Open = bool`: The application state a popover binds: whether it is open.
- `toggle = base.toggle`: Clicking the trigger toggles the popover.
- `overlay_sense: types.Sense = .{ .hover = true, .click = true }`: How the scrim and the panel take the pointer: they are hit, so what is under them is not.
- `role: a11y.Role = .dialog`: The panel's role: a non-modal dialog (WAI-ARIA APG dialog pattern).
- `replay = @embedFile("testdata/popover.replay")`: Input for `Example(.default)` at 800 x 600, one commented line per frame.
- `fn partName(comptime name: []const u8, comptime part: []const u8) [:0]const u8`: Ids of a popover's parts, from its name: the trigger is `name`, the scrim `name_scrim`, the panel `name_panel`.
- `fn checkOpen(comptime State: type, comptime name: []const u8, comptime open: []const u8) ?[]const u8`: `null` if `State` can hold a popover named `name` bound to `open`, else the compile error: `open` must be a bool field.
- `fn checkLabels(comptime State: type, comptime spec: Spec) ?[]const u8`: `null` if every label of `spec` shows a string field of `State`, else the compile error.
- `fn Popover(comptime th: Theme, comptime name: @EnumLiteral(), comptime open: @EnumLiteral()) type`: The popover named `name` bound to the bool field `open`, in theme `th`: its spec builders and the hooks `ComptimePage` calls.
- `fn scrimStyle(viewport: [2]f32) Style`: The scrim: a transparent float over the whole viewport.
- `fn panelStyle(th: *const Theme, at: Rect) Style`: The panel, floating `th.gap` below the trigger's rect `at` (last frame's), left edges aligned: a panel-coloured column with the control outline, padded like a button.
- `fn anchored(face: Style, placed: Style) Style`: `face` floating where `placed` (this file's `panelStyle`, or a menu's or a tooltip's style below its anchor) puts it: the `…Face` variants of the overlays restyle the look, not the placement.
- `fn plainPart(comptime th: Theme, comptime n: Spec, r: Response) Part`: The style of a row no overlay hook claims: a label as specified, a container with an id (a button) filled by its state, anything else as specified.
- `fn keyPressed(in: *const Input, code: u8) bool`: Key `code` went down (or repeated) in this frame's events.
- `fn keyCount(in: *const Input, code: u8, mods: Input.Mods, exact: bool) u32`: Times key `code` went down this frame, auto-repeat included; with `exact`, only with exactly `mods` held.
- `fn dismiss(open: bool, outside: bool, escape: bool) bool`: An open popover stays open unless the pointer went down outside it (on the scrim) or Escape was pressed.
- `fn isWidget(w: anytype, comptime name: []const u8) bool`: Whether optional event or widget `w` (a `?Events.Event` or `?Events.Widget`) is the widget named `name`.
- `fn trigger(f: *Frame, th: *const Theme, comptime name: []const u8, open: *bool, text: []const u8) Response`: The trigger: a button named `name` showing `text`.
- `fn begin(f: *Frame, th: *const Theme, comptime name: []const u8, open: *bool) bool`: Opens the scrim and the panel of popover `name` if it is open, and returns whether it did; the caller then adds the content and calls `end`.
- `fn beginFace(f: *Frame, th: *const Theme, comptime name: []const u8, open: *bool, face: Style) bool`: `begin` with the panel laid out by `face` instead of `panelStyle`; it still floats below the trigger (`anchored`).
- `fn end(f: *Frame) void`: Closes the panel and the scrim `begin` opened.
- `fn semantics(t: *const Tree, s: *a11y.Semantics, comptime name: []const u8) void`: The semantics of popover `name` the tree's columns cannot hold, written into `s` after `Semantics.clear` and before `a11y.names` and `a11y.states`: the trigger is expandable, and expanded while the panel is in `t` (`aria-expanded`); the panel is named by the trigger (`aria-labelledby`), so the dialog reads as "Details".
- `fn Semantic(comptime cap: u32) type`: Room for the accessibility export of a tree of up to `cap` rows: `a11y.Semantics` and an `a11y.Export` in one buffer, for a widget's tests and for applications that check one.
- `fn sameSemantics(a: *const a11y.Export, b: *const a11y.Export) bool`: Whether two exports say the same to assistive technology: rows, roles, states, values, ranges and names (by hash, as the front ends keep their bytes in different buffers).
- `fn ComptimePage(comptime State: type, comptime spec: Spec, comptime th: Theme, comptime H: type) type`: The comptime front end of a page with an overlay widget.
- `fn rowOf(comptime n: Spec, comptime name: []const u8) u32`: Depth-first row of the spec node with id `name`; a compile error if there is none.
- `fn Comptime(comptime th: Theme, comptime name: [:0]const u8, comptime open: []const u8) type`: The custom widgets (`Spec.widget`) of `ct.button` and `ct.popover`: the button's `step` toggles on its click and closes on a press on the scrim or Escape, as `trigger` and `begin` do at run time, and keeps the button's rect for the panel's `push`.
- `fn Example(comptime th: Theme) type`: The example page: a toolbar with the trigger and a label, and the popover's panel with two labels, in both front ends, in theme `th`.
- `fn exportRow(e: *const a11y.Export, node: u32) u32`: The export row of tree row `node`; `none` if it is not exported.

### `Part` (struct)
What `ComptimePage` pushes for one spec row: its style, and a role from the accessibility roles (`a11y.Role`) where `types.Role` has none, written to the `role` column after `push`.

Fields: `style`, `role`, `text`, `content`


### `Popover(..)` (generic struct)
The popover named `name` bound to the bool field `open`, in theme `th`: its spec builders and the hooks `ComptimePage` calls.

- `trigger_name = id`
- `scrim_name = scrim`
- `panel_name = panel`
- `fn button(comptime text: anytype) Spec`: The button that opens and closes the popover, showing the string field `text`.
- `fn layer(comptime children: anytype) Spec`: The scrim and the panel holding `children`, shown while the popover is open.
- `fn check(comptime State: type) ?[]const u8`
- `fn step(sig: anytype, v: anytype) void`: The trigger's click toggles; a press on the scrim or Escape closes.
- `fn part(comptime n: Spec, st: anytype, v: anytype) ?Part`

### `keys` (struct)
Scancodes of the keys the overlay widgets read, as USB HID usage ids (as `focus.Keymap`; what SDL and the web's `KeyboardEvent.code` map to).

- `enter: u8 = 0x28`
- `escape: u8 = 0x29`
- `tab: u8 = 0x2b`
- `space: u8 = 0x2c`
- `f10: u8 = 0x43`
- `right: u8 = 0x4f`
- `left: u8 = 0x50`
- `down: u8 = 0x51`
- `up: u8 = 0x52`
- `home: u8 = 0x4a`
- `end: u8 = 0x4d`
- `menu: u8 = 0x65`: The context-menu ("Application") key.

### `Semantic(..)` (generic struct)
Room for the accessibility export of a tree of up to `cap` rows: `a11y.Semantics` and an `a11y.Export` in one buffer, for a widget's tests and for applications that check one.

Fields: `buf`, `s`, `out`

- `fn begin(x: *Self) *a11y.Semantics`: Carves the columns and clears the inputs; write widget semantics into the result.
- `fn finish(x: *Self, t: *const Tree, tx: *const Texts, strings: []const u8) *const a11y.Export`: Names, states and the flat export of `t`, whose text runs are `tx` over `strings`.

### `ComptimePage(..)` (generic struct)
The comptime front end of a page with an overlay widget.

Fields: `buf`, `tables`, `signals`, `pointer`, `rects`, `last`, `tree_row`, `font`

- `Events = E`
- `Signals = S`
- `Output = struct { draw: *const DrawList, tree: *const Tree, event: ?E.Event }`
- `fn frame(ui: *Self, in: *const Input) Output`: Steps input against last frame's rects, runs the widget's behaviour on the state, then builds, lays out and paints the frame.

### `ComptimePage(..).Last` (struct)
Last frame's layout rect of every spec row; zero for a row that was not shown.

Fields: `rect`

- `fn of(l: *const Last, comptime name: []const u8) Rect`: The rect of the spec row with id `name`.

### `ComptimePage(..).View` (struct)

Fields: `in`, `last`, `event`, `pressed`, `response`, `page`

- `fn responseOf(v: *const View, comptime name: []const u8) Response`: The response of the widget named `name` this frame, as `part` sees its own.

### `ct` (struct)

- `fn button(comptime th: Theme, comptime name: @EnumLiteral(), comptime open: @EnumLiteral(), comptime text: anytype) Spec`: The button that opens and closes popover `name`, bound to the bool field `open`, showing `text` (a literal or a string field).
- `fn popover(comptime th: Theme, comptime name: @EnumLiteral(), comptime open: @EnumLiteral(), comptime children: anytype) Spec`: The scrim and the panel of popover `name` holding `children` (any spec nodes), live while `open` is true (`spec.when`); put it last in the page's root.
- `fn popoverFace(comptime th: Theme, comptime name: @EnumLiteral(), comptime open: @EnumLiteral(), comptime face: Style, comptime children: anytype) Spec`: `popover` with the panel laid out by `face` instead of `panelStyle`; it still floats below the button (`anchored`).

### `Comptime(..)` (generic struct)
The custom widgets (`Spec.widget`) of `ct.button` and `ct.popover`: the button's `step` toggles on its click and closes on a press on the scrim or Escape, as `trigger` and `begin` do at run time, and keeps the button's rect for the panel's `push`.


### `Comptime(..).Trigger` (struct)

- `fn bindError(comptime State: type, comptime bound: []const u8) ?[]const u8`
- `fn step(ui: anytype, comptime row: u32, event: anytype, in: *const Input) void`
- `fn push(ui: anytype, comptime n: Spec, parent: u32, next: *u32, in: *const Input) void`

### `Comptime(..).Layer` (struct)

- `holds_content = true`
- `fn push(ui: anytype, comptime n: Spec, parent: u32, next: *u32, in: *const Input) void`

### `Example(..)` (generic struct)
The example page: a toolbar with the trigger and a label, and the popover's panel with two labels, in both front ends, in theme `th`.

- `toolbar_style: Style = .{ .dir = .x, .size = .{ .fill, .fit }, .pad = @splat(8), .gap = 8, .cross = .center, .fill = th.panel }`
- `P = Popover(th, .details, .open)`
- `Ui = ComptimePage(State, spec, th, P)`
- `limits: context.Limits = .{ .nodes = 64, .texts = 16, .customs = 1, .strings = 1024, .depth = 8 }`
- `Run = base.Harness(App, Ui, limits)`

### `Example(..).App` (struct)

Fields: `open`

- `fn build(app: *App, f: *Frame) void`

### `Example(..).State` (struct)

Fields: `open`, `layer`, `exposure`


### `golden` (struct)
What tools/golden/ dumps: the page's harness over `replay`.

- `Run = Example(.default).Run`

## `lcgf.widgets_tooltip` (src/widgets/tooltip.zig)

Tooltip (tier 2): a short text that appears next to a widget once the pointer has rested on it, the WAI-ARIA APG tooltip pattern.

### Top level

- `delay: f32 = 0.5`: Seconds the pointer rests on the target before the tooltip shows: `Interaction.hover_delay`.
- `role: a11y.Role = .tooltip`: The bubble's role.
- `replay = @embedFile("testdata/tooltip.replay")`: Input for `Example(.default)` at 800 x 600, one commented line per frame.
- `fn checkTip(comptime State: type, comptime tip: []const u8) ?[]const u8`: `null` if `State` can hold a tooltip bound to `tip`, else the compile error: `tip` must be a `Tip` field.
- `fn Tooltip(comptime th: Theme, comptime target: @EnumLiteral(), comptime tip: @EnumLiteral()) type`: The tooltip of the widget named `target`, bound to the `Tip` field `tip`, in theme `th`: its spec builder and the hooks `popover.ComptimePage` calls.
- `fn bubbleStyle(th: *const Theme, at: Rect) Style`: The bubble, floating `th.gap` below the target's rect `at` (last frame's), left edges aligned: a small dark plate (the held-control colour) with the control outline.
- `fn advance(tip: Tip, hovered: bool, dt: f32, escape: bool) Tip`: One frame of a tooltip: while the target is hovered the timer runs, and the tooltip shows once it reaches `delay`; leaving the target resets it.
- `fn update(f: *const Frame, tip: *Tip, r: Response) void`: Steps `tip` by one frame from the target's response `r`.
- `fn show(f: *Frame, th: *const Theme, comptime target: []const u8, tip: *const Tip, text: []const u8) void`: Builds the bubble of the tooltip of `target` showing `text`, if it is shown.
- `fn showFace(f: *Frame, th: *const Theme, comptime target: []const u8, tip: *const Tip, text: []const u8, face: Style, ts: types.TextStyle) void`: `show` with the bubble laid out by `face` instead of `bubbleStyle` and its text drawn in `ts`; it still floats below the target (`popover.anchored`).
- `fn Bubble(comptime th: Theme, comptime target: []const u8, comptime tip: []const u8, comptime text: []const u8) type`: The custom widget (`Spec.widget`) of `ct.tooltip`: its `step` runs while the bubble is hidden too (`steps_hidden`), advancing the tip from the target's hover, and keeps the target's rect for `push`.
- `fn Example(comptime th: Theme) type`: The example page: a toolbar with a button and a label, and the button's tooltip, in both front ends, in theme `th`.

### `Tip` (struct)
A tooltip's state, owned by the application.

Fields: `rested`, `dismissed`, `shown`

- `fn visible(t: Tip) bool`: Whether the bubble is shown: what `spec.when` over this field selects by (`ct.tooltip`).

### `Tooltip(..)` (generic struct)
The tooltip of the widget named `target`, bound to the `Tip` field `tip`, in theme `th`: its spec builder and the hooks `popover.ComptimePage` calls.

- `fn bubble(comptime text: anytype) Spec`: The bubble showing the string field `text`.
- `fn check(comptime State: type) ?[]const u8`
- `fn step(sig: anytype, v: anytype) void`: Rests or resets the timer from the target's hover; Escape hides.
- `fn part(comptime n: Spec, st: anytype, v: anytype) ?Part`

### `ct` (struct)

- `fn tooltip(comptime th: Theme, comptime target: @EnumLiteral(), comptime tip: @EnumLiteral(), comptime text: []const u8) Spec`: The tooltip of the widget named `target` (elsewhere on the page), bound to the `Tip` field `tip`, showing `text`.

### `Bubble(..)` (generic struct)
The custom widget (`Spec.widget`) of `ct.tooltip`: its `step` runs while the bubble is hidden too (`steps_hidden`), advancing the tip from the target's hover, and keeps the target's rect for `push`.

- `steps_hidden = true`
- `fn bindError(comptime State: type, comptime bound: []const u8) ?[]const u8`
- `fn step(ui: anytype, comptime row: u32, event: anytype, in: *const Input) void`
- `fn push(ui: anytype, comptime n: Spec, parent: u32, next: *u32, in: *const Input) void`

### `Example(..)` (generic struct)
The example page: a toolbar with a button and a label, and the button's tooltip, in both front ends, in theme `th`.

- `toolbar_style: Style = .{ .dir = .x, .size = .{ .fill, .fit }, .pad = @splat(8), .gap = 8, .cross = .center, .fill = th.panel }`
- `T = Tooltip(th, .save, .tip)`
- `Ui = popover.ComptimePage(State, spec, th, T)`
- `limits: context.Limits = .{ .nodes = 64, .texts = 16, .customs = 1, .strings = 1024, .depth = 8 }`
- `Run = base.Harness(App, Ui, limits)`

### `Example(..).App` (struct)

Fields: `tip`

- `fn build(app: *App, f: *Frame) void`

### `Example(..).State` (struct)

Fields: `tip`, `save_text`, `scene`, `save_tip`

- `Run = Example(.default).Run`

## `lcgf.widgets_menu` (src/widgets/menu.zig)

Menu (tier 2): a button that opens a list of actions, one of which may open a submenu, the WAI-ARIA APG menu pattern (a menu button with a menu).

### Top level

- `no_item: u8 = 0xff`: No item.
- `file_menu: Def = .{ .items = &.{ "new", "open", "recent", "quit" }, .sub_at = 2, .sub = &.{ "scene1", "scene2" } }`: The example's menu: File with New, Open, Recent (a submenu of two scenes) and Quit.
- `file_texts = [_][]const u8{ "New", "Open", "Recent", "Quit", "Scene 1", "Scene 2" }`
- `replay = @embedFile("testdata/menu.replay")`: Input for `Example(.default)` at 800 x 600, one commented line per frame.
- `fn checkMenu(comptime State: type, comptime def: Def, comptime menu: []const u8) ?[]const u8`: `null` if `State` can hold menu `def` bound to `menu`, else the compile error: `menu` must be a `MenuState`, and every item needs a string field of its name.
- `fn Menu(comptime th: Theme, comptime name: @EnumLiteral(), comptime menu: @EnumLiteral(), comptime def: Def) type`: Menu `name`, with items `def`, bound to the `MenuState` field `menu`, in theme `th`: its spec builders and the hooks `popover.ComptimePage` calls.
- `fn highlighted(comptime def: Def, st: MenuState, k: u8) bool`: Whether item `k` (in the one sequence) is highlighted: the active item of its list.
- `fn listStyle(th: *const Theme, at: Rect) Style`: The list, floating `th.gap` below the trigger's rect `at` (last frame's), left edges aligned: the popover's panel (panel fill, control outline), with half a gap of padding and the items touching.
- `fn subStyle(th: *const Theme, at: Rect) Style`: The submenu: the list's look, to the right of the rect `at` of the item that opens it, its first item level with that item.
- `fn itemStyle(th: *const Theme, active: bool) Style`: An item row, as wide as the list: padded like a button, filled when highlighted.
- `fn itemFills(th: *const Theme) base.OnOff`: An item's fill by highlight, indexed by it: none, then the hot control colour.
- `fn advance(comptime def: Def, st: MenuState, hits: Hits, evs: []const Input.Event) MenuState`: One frame of a menu (APG menu button and menu).
- `fn semantics(t: *const Tree, s: *a11y.Semantics, comptime name: []const u8, comptime def: Def, st: MenuState) void`: The semantics of menu `name` the tree's columns cannot hold, written into `s` after `Semantics.clear` and before `a11y.names` and `a11y.states`.
- `fn trigger(f: *Frame, th: *const Theme, comptime name: []const u8, text: []const u8) Response`: The trigger: a button named `name` showing `text`.
- `fn show(f: *Frame, th: *const Theme, comptime name: []const u8, comptime def: Def, st: *MenuState, texts: []const []const u8) u8`: Builds menu `name` (items `def`, labels `texts` in the one sequence) if it is open.
- `fn showFace(f: *Frame, th: *const Theme, comptime name: []const u8, comptime def: Def, st: *MenuState, texts: []const []const u8, face: Style, text: types.Te…`: `show` with the list and the submenu laid out by `face` instead of `listStyle` and the items' text drawn in `text`; they still float where `listStyle` and `subStyle` place them (`popover.anchored`), and the items' fill still follows the highlight.
- `fn Comptime(comptime th: Theme, comptime name: [:0]const u8, comptime menu: []const u8, comptime def: Def) type`: The custom widgets (`Spec.widget`) of `ct.button` and `ct.list`: the button's `step` reads its own, the scrim's and the items' responses and the keys through `advance`, as `show` does at run time, and keeps its rect for the list's `push`.
- `fn Example(comptime th: Theme) type`: The example page: a toolbar with the File button and a label, and its menu, in both front ends, in theme `th`.

### `Def` (struct)
A menu's items, fixed when compiling.

Fields: `items`, `sub_at`, `sub`

- `fn count(comptime d: Def) u8`: Items in all: the top list and the submenu.
- `fn name(comptime d: Def, comptime k: u8) [:0]const u8`: The name of item `k` (in the one sequence).

### `MenuState` (struct)
A menu's state, owned by the application.

Fields: `open`, `active`, `sub`, `sub_active`, `hover`, `chosen`

- `fn visible(s: MenuState) bool`: Whether the menu is shown: what `spec.when` over this field selects by (`ct.list`).

### `Menu(..)` (generic struct)
Menu `name`, with items `def`, bound to the `MenuState` field `menu`, in theme `th`: its spec builders and the hooks `popover.ComptimePage` calls.

- `fn button(comptime text: anytype) Spec`: The button that opens and closes the menu, showing the string field `text`.
- `fn layer() Spec`: The scrim, the list and the submenu, shown while the menu is open.
- `fn check(comptime State: type) ?[]const u8`
- `fn step(sig: anytype, v: anytype) void`: The trigger, the scrim and the items' responses, then the keys, through `advance`.
- `fn part(comptime n: Spec, st: anytype, v: anytype) ?Part`

### `Hits` (struct)
What the pointer did to a menu this frame, from its widgets' responses.

Fields: `toggle`, `outside`, `clicked`, `hovered`

- `fn add(h: *Hits, k: u8, r: Response) void`: Notes item `k`'s response.

### `ct` (struct)

- `fn button(comptime th: Theme, comptime name: @EnumLiteral(), comptime menu: @EnumLiteral(), comptime def: Def, comptime text: anytype) Spec`: The button that opens menu `name`, bound to the `MenuState` field `menu`, showing `text` (a literal or a string field).
- `fn list(comptime th: Theme, comptime name: @EnumLiteral(), comptime menu: @EnumLiteral(), comptime def: Def, comptime texts: []const []const u8) Spec`: The scrim and the list of menu `name` (items `def`, without a submenu, captioned by `texts`), live while it is open (`spec.when`, `MenuState.visible`); put it last in the page's root.
- `fn listFace(comptime th: Theme, comptime name: @EnumLiteral(), comptime menu: @EnumLiteral(), comptime def: Def, comptime texts: []const []const u8, comptime…`: `list` with the list laid out by `face` instead of `listStyle` and the items' text in `text`; it still floats below the button (`popover.anchored`).

### `Comptime(..)` (generic struct)
The custom widgets (`Spec.widget`) of `ct.button` and `ct.list`: the button's `step` reads its own, the scrim's and the items' responses and the keys through `advance`, as `show` does at run time, and keeps its rect for the list's `push`.


### `Comptime(..).Trigger` (struct)

- `fn bindError(comptime State: type, comptime bound: []const u8) ?[]const u8`
- `fn step(ui: anytype, comptime row: u32, event: anytype, in: *const Input) void`
- `fn push(ui: anytype, comptime n: Spec, parent: u32, next: *u32, in: *const Input) void`

### `Comptime(..).List` (struct)

- `fn push(ui: anytype, comptime n: Spec, parent: u32, next: *u32, in: *const Input) void`

### `Example(..)` (generic struct)
The example page: a toolbar with the File button and a label, and its menu, in both front ends, in theme `th`.

- `toolbar_style: Style = .{ .dir = .x, .size = .{ .fill, .fit }, .pad = @splat(8), .gap = 8, .cross = .center, .fill = th.panel }`
- `M = Menu(th, .file, .menu, file_menu)`
- `Ui = popover.ComptimePage(State, spec, th, M)`
- `limits: context.Limits = .{ .nodes = 64, .texts = 16, .customs = 1, .strings = 1024, .depth = 8 }`
- `Run = base.Harness(App, Ui, limits)`

### `Example(..).App` (struct)

Fields: `menu`, `chosen`

- `fn build(app: *App, f: *Frame) void`

### `Example(..).State` (struct)

- `Run = Example(.default).Run`

## `lcgf.widgets_menubar` (src/widgets/menubar.zig)

Menu bar (tier 2): a row of buttons, each opening a menu, the WAI-ARIA APG menubar pattern.

### Top level

- `edit_menu: menu.Def = .{ .items = &.{ "undo", "redo" } }`: The example's bar: File (menu.zig's example menu) and Edit (Undo, Redo).
- `bar_def: BarDef = .{ .names = &.{ "file", "edit" }, .menus = &.{ menu.file_menu, edit_menu } }`
- `bar_texts = [_][]const u8{ "File", "Edit" }`
- `item_texts = [_][]const []const u8{ &menu.file_texts, &.{ "Undo", "Redo" } }`
- `replay = @embedFile("testdata/menubar.replay")`: Input for `Example(.default)` at 800 x 600, one commented line per frame.
- `fn checkBar(comptime State: type, comptime def: BarDef, comptime field: []const u8) ?[]const u8`: `null` if `State` can hold menu bar `def` bound to `field`, else the compile error.
- `fn MenuBar(comptime th: Theme, comptime name: @EnumLiteral(), comptime field: @EnumLiteral(), comptime def: BarDef) type`: Menu bar `name` with menus `def`, bound to the `BarState` field `field`, in theme `th`: its spec builders and the hooks `popover.ComptimePage` calls.
- `fn barStyle(th: *const Theme) Style`: The bar: a panel-coloured strip across the page, half a gap of padding around its buttons.
- `fn buttonStyle(th: *const Theme, open: bool, hovered: bool) Style`: A button of the bar: padded like a menu item, filled while its menu is open or the pointer is on it (`menu.itemFills`, indexed by either).
- `fn layerStyle() Style`: The layer the lists float in: out of flow, at the page's origin.
- `fn advance(comptime def: BarDef, st: BarState, hits: BarHits, in: *const Input) BarState`: One frame of a menu bar (APG menubar).
- `fn semantics(t: *const Tree, s: *a11y.Semantics, comptime name: []const u8, comptime def: BarDef, st: BarState) void`: The semantics of menu bar `name` the tree's columns cannot hold, written into `s` after `Semantics.clear` and before `a11y.names` and `a11y.states`.
- `fn bar(f: *Frame, th: *const Theme, comptime name: []const u8, comptime def: BarDef, st: *BarState, texts: []const []const u8) void`: The bar named `name`, in the page's flow, showing `texts` (one per button).
- `fn barFace(f: *Frame, th: *const Theme, comptime name: []const u8, comptime def: BarDef, st: *BarState, texts: []const []const u8, face: Style, text: types.T…`: `bar` laid out by `face` instead of `barStyle(th)`, its buttons' text drawn in `text`; the buttons' fill still follows the open menu and the pointer.
- `fn menus(f: *Frame, th: *const Theme, comptime name: []const u8, comptime def: BarDef, st: *const BarState, texts: []const []const []const u8) void`: The open menu's list (and submenu) of bar `name`, `texts` holding each menu's labels in menu.zig's one sequence.
- `fn menusFace(f: *Frame, th: *const Theme, comptime name: []const u8, comptime def: BarDef, st: *const BarState, texts: []const []const []const u8, face: Styl…`: `menus` with the lists laid out by `face` instead of `menu.listStyle` and the items' text drawn in `text`, as `menu.showFace`; the lists still float below their buttons.
- `fn Comptime(comptime th: Theme, comptime name: [:0]const u8, comptime field: []const u8, comptime def: BarDef) type`: The custom widgets (`Spec.widget`) of `ct.bar` and `ct.menus`: the bar's `step` reads its own, its buttons', the lists' and the items' responses and the keys through `advance`, as `bar` does at run time, and keeps the open menu's button rect for the lists' `push`.
- `fn Example(comptime th: Theme) type`: The example page: the bar above a status label, and its menus, in both front ends, in theme `th`.

### `BarDef` (struct)
A menu bar's menus, fixed when compiling: one name per button (its id is `bar_name`; in the comptime front end its label shows the string field of that name) and one `menu.Def` per button, whose ids are `bar_name_item` and so on, as menu.zig names them with `bar_name` as the menu's name.

Fields: `names`, `menus`


### `BarState` (struct)
A menu bar's state, owned by the application.

Fields: `open`, `menu`, `hover`, `down`, `from`

- `fn alternative(s: BarState) u32`: Which menu's list is shown, past the last when none: what `spec.choice` over this field selects by (`ct.menus`).

### `MenuBar(..)` (generic struct)
Menu bar `name` with menus `def`, bound to the `BarState` field `field`, in theme `th`: its spec builders and the hooks `popover.ComptimePage` calls.

- `fn row() Spec`: The bar: a row of buttons, in the page's flow.
- `fn layer() Spec`: The menus' lists, of which the open one is shown.
- `fn check(comptime State: type) ?[]const u8`
- `fn step(sig: anytype, v: anytype) void`: Every widget's response, then the keys, through `advance`.
- `fn part(comptime n: Spec, st: anytype, v: anytype) ?Part`

### `BarHits` (struct)
What the pointer did to a menu bar this frame, from its widgets' responses.

Fields: `clicked`, `hovered`, `inside`, `menu`

- `fn addButton(h: *BarHits, i: u8, r: Response) void`: Notes button `i`'s response.
- `fn addItem(h: *BarHits, open: bool, k: u8, r: Response) void`: Notes the response of item `k` of a menu, which is the open one when `open`.

### `ct` (struct)

- `fn bar(comptime th: Theme, comptime name: @EnumLiteral(), comptime field: @EnumLiteral(), comptime def: BarDef, comptime texts: []const []const u8) Spec`: The bar of menu bar `name` (menus `def`, buttons captioned by `texts`), bound to the `BarState` field `field`, in the page's flow; `spec.styled` and `spec.textStyled` restyle it as `barFace` does.
- `fn menus(comptime th: Theme, comptime name: @EnumLiteral(), comptime field: @EnumLiteral(), comptime def: BarDef, comptime items: []const []const []const u8)…`: The open menu's list of menu bar `name`, its items captioned by `items` (one slice per menu), floating below its button; put it last in the page's root.
- `fn menusFace(comptime th: Theme, comptime name: @EnumLiteral(), comptime field: @EnumLiteral(), comptime def: BarDef, comptime items: []const []const []const…`: `menus` with the lists laid out by `face` instead of `menu.listStyle` and the items' text in `text`, as `menusFace` does.

### `Comptime(..)` (generic struct)
The custom widgets (`Spec.widget`) of `ct.bar` and `ct.menus`: the bar's `step` reads its own, its buttons', the lists' and the items' responses and the keys through `advance`, as `bar` does at run time, and keeps the open menu's button rect for the lists' `push`.


### `Comptime(..).Bar` (struct)

- `fn bindError(comptime State: type, comptime bound: []const u8) ?[]const u8`
- `fn step(ui: anytype, comptime row: u32, event: anytype, in: *const Input) void`
- `fn push(ui: anytype, comptime n: Spec, parent: u32, next: *u32, in: *const Input) void`

### `Comptime(..).Menus` (struct)

- `fn push(ui: anytype, comptime n: Spec, parent: u32, next: *u32, in: *const Input) void`

### `Example(..)` (generic struct)
The example page: the bar above a status label, and its menus, in both front ends, in theme `th`.

- `B = MenuBar(th, .main, .bar, bar_def)`
- `spec = spec_zig.col(page_style, .{ B.row(), base.ct.label(th, .status), B.layer() })`

- `limits: context.Limits = .{ .nodes = 64, .texts = 16, .customs = 1, .strings = 1024, .depth = 8 }`
- `Run = base.Harness(App, Ui, limits)`

Fields: `bar`

## `lcgf.widgets_contextmenu` (src/widgets/contextmenu.zig)

Context menu (tier 2): a menu that opens where the secondary button is pressed on a target, the WAI-ARIA APG menu pattern opened from a context.

### Top level

- `edit_menu: menu.Def = .{ .items = &.{ "cut", "copy", "paste", "arrange" }, .sub_at = 3, .sub = &.{ "front", "back" } }`: The example's menu: Cut, Copy, Paste and Arrange (a submenu: To front, To back).
- `edit_texts = [_][]const u8{ "Cut", "Copy", "Paste", "Arrange", "To front", "To back" }`
- `replay = @embedFile("testdata/contextmenu.replay")`: Input for `Example(.default)` at 800 x 600, one commented line per frame.
- `fn checkContext(comptime State: type, comptime def: menu.Def, comptime field: []const u8) ?[]const u8`: `null` if `State` can hold context menu `def` bound to `field`, else the compile error: `field` must be a `ContextState`, and the items as `menu.checkMenu` wants them.
- `fn ContextMenu(comptime th: Theme, comptime name: @EnumLiteral(), comptime target: @EnumLiteral(), comptime field: @EnumLiteral(), comptime def: menu.Def) type`: Context menu `name` of the widget `target`, with items `def`, bound to the `ContextState` field `field`, in theme `th`: its spec builder and the hooks `popover.ComptimePage` calls.
- `fn listStyle(th: *const Theme, at: [2]f32) Style`: The list, its top-left corner at `at`: menu.zig's list (panel fill, control outline, half a gap of padding), placed at a point instead of below a button.
- `fn advance(comptime def: menu.Def, st: ContextState, target: Rect, hits: menu.Hits, in: *const Input) ContextState`: One frame of a context menu.
- `fn semantics(t: *const Tree, s: *a11y.Semantics, comptime name: []const u8, comptime target: []const u8, comptime def: menu.Def, st: ContextState) void`: The semantics of context menu `name` of widget `target` the tree's columns cannot hold, written into `s` after `Semantics.clear` and before `a11y.names` and `a11y.states`.
- `fn show(f: *Frame, th: *const Theme, comptime name: []const u8, comptime target: []const u8, comptime def: menu.Def, st: *ContextState, texts: []const []cons…`: Builds context menu `name` of widget `target` (items `def`, labels `texts` in menu.zig's one sequence) if it is open.
- `fn Example(comptime th: Theme) type`: The example page: a caption, the canvas the menu belongs to, and the menu, in both front ends, in theme `th`.

### `ContextState` (struct)
A context menu's state, owned by the application.

Fields: `menu`, `at`, `right`


### `ContextMenu(..)` (generic struct)

- `fn layer() Spec`: The scrim, the list and the submenu, shown while the menu is open, as menu.zig builds them.
- `fn check(comptime State: type) ?[]const u8`
- `fn step(sig: anytype, v: anytype) void`: The target's rect, the scrim's and the items' responses and the keys, through `advance`.
- `fn part(comptime n: Spec, st: anytype, v: anytype) ?Part`

The example page: a caption, the canvas the menu belongs to, and the menu, in both front ends, in theme `th`.

## `lcgf.widgets_dialog` (src/widgets/dialog.zig)

Dialog (tier 2): a modal window over the page, with a title, a message and buttons, the WAI-ARIA APG modal dialog pattern.

### Top level

- `dim: Color = .rgba(0, 0, 0, 140)`: What the backdrop lays over the page: black at 55% opacity.
- `ring_width = [2]f32{ 0, 2 }`: The focus ring's width and colour, indexed by focus: none, then 2 px of the accent.
- `no_button: u8 = 0xff`: No button.
- `role: a11y.Role = .dialog`: The panel's role (APG modal dialog); its value is 0.
- `confirm: Def = .{ .buttons = &.{ "cancel", "ok" } }`: The example's dialog: Cancel and Delete.
- `confirm_texts = [_][]const u8{ "Cancel", "Delete" }`
- `replay = @embedFile("testdata/dialog.replay")`: Input for `Example(.default)` at 800 x 600, one commented line per frame.
- `fn checkDialog(comptime State: type, comptime def: Def, comptime field: []const u8) ?[]const u8`: `null` if `State` can hold dialog `def` bound to `field`, else the compile error.
- `fn Dialog(comptime th: Theme, comptime name: @EnumLiteral(), comptime field: @EnumLiteral(), comptime def: Def) type`: Dialog `name` with buttons `def`, bound to the `DialogState` field `field`, in theme `th`: its spec builders and the hooks `popover.ComptimePage` calls.
- `fn backdropStyle(th: *const Theme, viewport: [2]f32) Style`: The backdrop: over the whole viewport, dimming the page, centring the dialog.
- `fn panelStyle(th: *const Theme) Style`: The dialog's panel: panel-coloured with the control outline, twice a button's padding and corner radius, its parts a double gap apart, at least 260 wide.
- `fn rowStyle(th: *const Theme) Style`: The row of buttons: across the dialog, the buttons at its end.
- `fn buttonStyle(th: *const Theme, r: Response, focused: bool) Style`: A button of the dialog: a theme button, filled by its response; the focused one has the focus ring, a 2 px accent border (by table index, `ring`).
- `fn ring(th: *const Theme) base.OnOff`
- `fn advance(comptime def: Def, st: DialogState, hits: Hits, evs: []const Input.Event) DialogState`: One frame of a modal dialog (APG modal dialog).
- `fn semantics(t: *const Tree, s: *a11y.Semantics, comptime name: []const u8) void`: The semantics of dialog `name` the tree's columns cannot hold, written into `s` after `Semantics.clear` and before `a11y.names` and `a11y.states`: the dialog is named by its title, the panel's first child (`aria-labelledby`).
- `fn trapFocus(t: *const Tree, sc: *focus.Scopes, comptime name: []const u8) void`: Marks dialog `name`'s panel as a modal layer in `sc` (`focus.Scopes.trap`), for an application that runs `focus.Focus` over the tree: Tab then stays inside it, and focus goes back to what had it when the dialog closes.
- `fn trigger(f: *Frame, th: *const Theme, comptime name: []const u8, text: []const u8) Response`: The button that opens dialog `name`, showing `text`.
- `fn show(f: *Frame, th: *const Theme, comptime name: []const u8, comptime def: Def, st: *DialogState, title: []const u8, message: []const u8, texts: []const […`: Builds dialog `name` (buttons `def`, labelled `texts`) showing `title` and `message`, if it is open.
- `fn begin(f: *Frame, th: *const Theme, comptime name: []const u8, open: *bool) bool`: Opens the backdrop and the panel of dialog `name` if `open.*`, and returns whether it did; the caller then adds the content and calls `end`.
- `fn beginFace(f: *Frame, th: *const Theme, comptime name: []const u8, open: *bool, face: Style, backdrop: Color) bool`: `begin` with the panel laid out by `face` instead of `panelStyle(th)` and the backdrop filled with `backdrop` instead of `dim`, as `ct.dialogFace` lays out the comptime one (CSS on a `<dialog>` and its `::backdrop`).
- `fn end(f: *Frame) void`: Closes the panel and the backdrop `begin` opened.
- `fn Modal(comptime th: Theme, comptime name: [:0]const u8, comptime open: []const u8, comptime rows: u32) type`: The custom widget (`Spec.widget`) of `ct.dialog`'s backdrop: it pushes the backdrop and the panel as `begin` does, then the content; its `step` closes on Escape and keeps focus inside (focus outside the dialog, by a Tab past an end or a dialog just opened, goes to its first widget in Tab order, or its last after Shift+Tab).
- `fn ModalFill(comptime th: Theme, comptime name: [:0]const u8, comptime open: []const u8, comptime rows: u32, comptime backdrop: Color) type`: `Modal` with its backdrop filled with `backdrop` (`ct.dialogFace`).
- `fn Example(comptime th: Theme) type`: The example page: a toolbar with the button that opens the dialog and a label, and the dialog, in both front ends, in theme `th`.

### `Def` (struct)
A dialog's buttons, fixed when compiling, in focus order.

Fields: `buttons`


### `DialogState` (struct)
A dialog's state, owned by the application.

Fields: `open`, `focus`, `result`


### `Dialog(..)` (generic struct)
Dialog `name` with buttons `def`, bound to the `DialogState` field `field`, in theme `th`: its spec builders and the hooks `popover.ComptimePage` calls.

- `fn button(comptime text: anytype) Spec`: The button that opens the dialog, showing the string field `text`.
- `fn layer(comptime title: anytype, comptime message: anytype) Spec`: The backdrop and the dialog showing the string fields `title` and `message`, then its buttons.
- `fn check(comptime State: type) ?[]const u8`
- `fn step(sig: anytype, v: anytype) void`: The opener's and the buttons' responses, then the keys, through `advance`.
- `fn part(comptime n: Spec, st: anytype, v: anytype) ?Part`

### `Hits` (struct)
What the pointer did to a dialog this frame, from its widgets' responses.

Fields: `open`, `pressed`, `clicked`

- `fn add(h: *Hits, k: u8, r: Response) void`: Notes button `k`'s response.

### `ct` (struct)

- `fn dialog(comptime th: Theme, comptime id: @EnumLiteral(), comptime open: @EnumLiteral(), comptime children: anytype) Spec`: Dialog `id`, shown while the bool field `open` is true, holding `children` (any spec nodes, as `spec.col` takes them).
- `fn dialogFace(comptime th: Theme, comptime id: @EnumLiteral(), comptime open: @EnumLiteral(), comptime face: Style, comptime backdrop: Color, comptime childr…`: `dialog` with its panel laid out by `face` instead of `panelStyle(th)` and its backdrop filled with `backdrop` instead of `dim` (CSS on a `<dialog>` and its `::backdrop`); the runtime twin is `beginFace`.

### `Modal(..)` (generic struct)
The custom widget (`Spec.widget`) of `ct.dialog`'s backdrop: it pushes the backdrop and the panel as `begin` does, then the content; its `step` closes on Escape and keeps focus inside (focus outside the dialog, by a Tab past an end or a dialog just opened, goes to its first widget in Tab order, or its last after Shift+Tab).


### `ModalFill(..)` (generic struct)
`Modal` with its backdrop filled with `backdrop` (`ct.dialogFace`).

- `holds_content = true`
- `fn bindError(comptime State: type, comptime bound: []const u8) ?[]const u8`
- `fn step(ui: anytype, comptime row: u32, event: anytype, in: *const Input) void`
- `fn push(ui: anytype, comptime n: Spec, parent: u32, next: *u32, in: *const Input) void`

### `Example(..)` (generic struct)
The example page: a toolbar with the button that opens the dialog and a label, and the dialog, in both front ends, in theme `th`.

- `toolbar_style: Style = .{ .dir = .x, .size = .{ .fill, .fit }, .pad = @splat(8), .gap = 8, .cross = .center, .fill = th.panel }`
- `page_style: Style = .{ .size = .{ .fill, .fill } }`

## `lcgf.widgets_toast` (src/widgets/toast.zig)

Toast (tier 2): a short message that appears at the bottom of the window for a few seconds, the WAI-ARIA APG alert pattern (a live region that does not take focus).

### Top level

- `duration: f32 = 3`: Seconds a toast stays, not counting the time the pointer rests on it.
- `fade: f32 = 0.3`: Seconds over which a toast fades out at the end of its time.
- `role: a11y.Role = .alert`: The toast's role: an alert, which assistive technology announces when it appears without moving focus (APG alert).
- `replay = @embedFile("testdata/toast.replay")`: Input for `Example(.default)` at 800 x 600, one commented line per frame.
- `fn checkToast(comptime State: type, comptime field: []const u8) ?[]const u8`: `null` if `State` can hold a toast bound to `field`, else the compile error.
- `fn Toast(comptime th: Theme, comptime name: @EnumLiteral(), comptime field: @EnumLiteral(), comptime trigger: @EnumLiteral()) type`: Toast `name`, bound to the `ToastState` field `field` and shown when the widget `trigger` is clicked, in theme `th`: its spec builder and the hooks `popover.ComptimePage` calls.
- `fn layerStyle(th: *const Theme, viewport: [2]f32) Style`: The layer: over the whole viewport, the toast at its bottom centre, twice a button's padding from the edge.
- `fn toastStyle(th: *const Theme, t: ToastState) Style`: The toast: a control-coloured plate with the control outline, padded like a wide button, fading out over its last `fade` seconds.
- `fn advance(t: ToastState, trigger: bool, hovered: bool, dt: f32) ToastState`: One frame of a toast: `trigger` shows it for `duration` seconds (again from the start if it is shown); time runs by `dt` except while the pointer rests on it (`hovered`), so a reader keeps it; it goes when its time is up.
- `fn semantics(t: *const Tree, s: *a11y.Semantics, comptime name: []const u8) void`: The semantics of toast `name` the tree's columns cannot hold, written into `s` after `Semantics.clear` and before `a11y.names`: the alert is named by its message, its only child (alert does not take its name from content).
- `fn show(f: *Frame, th: *const Theme, comptime name: []const u8, t: *ToastState, trigger: bool, text: []const u8) void`: Builds the layer of toast `name` and, while it is shown, the toast showing `text`.
- `fn Example(comptime th: Theme) type`: The example page: a toolbar with a Save button and a label, and the toast Save shows, in both front ends, in theme `th`.

### `ToastState` (struct)
A toast's state, owned by the application.

Fields: `shown`, `left`


### `Toast(..)` (generic struct)
Toast `name`, bound to the `ToastState` field `field` and shown when the widget `trigger` is clicked, in theme `th`: its spec builder and the hooks `popover.ComptimePage` calls.

- `fn layer(comptime text: anytype) Spec`: The layer and the toast showing the string field `text`.
- `fn check(comptime State: type) ?[]const u8`
- `fn step(sig: anytype, v: anytype) void`: The trigger's click and the toast's hover, through `advance`.
- `fn part(comptime n: Spec, st: anytype, v: anytype) ?Part`

### `Example(..)` (generic struct)
The example page: a toolbar with a Save button and a label, and the toast Save shows, in both front ends, in theme `th`.

- `T = Toast(th, .saved, .toast, .save)`
- `Ui = popover.ComptimePage(State, spec, th, T)`
- `limits: context.Limits = .{ .nodes = 64, .texts = 16, .customs = 1, .strings = 1024, .depth = 8 }`
- `Run = base.Harness(App, Ui, limits)`

Fields: `toast`

## `lcgf.widgets_select` (src/widgets/select.zig)

Select (tier 2): a button showing the chosen option of a fixed list, which opens the list below it, the WAI-ARIA APG select-only combobox pattern.

### Top level

- `Group = struct { at: u8, label: []const u8 }`
- `shapes: menu.Def = .{ .items = &.{ "circle", "square", "triangle" } }`: The example's options.
- `shape_texts = [_][]const u8{ "Circle", "Square", "Triangle" }`
- `replay = @embedFile("testdata/select.replay")`: Input for `Example(.default)` at 800 x 600, one commented line per frame.
- `fn positional(comptime n: usize) menu.Def`: The `menu.Def` of `n` options named by position: "0", "1", ..., so option k's id is `name_k`.
- `fn checkSelect(comptime State: type, comptime def: menu.Def, comptime field: []const u8, comptime text: []const u8) ?[]const u8`: `null` if `State` can hold select `def` bound to `field`, its button showing `text`, else the compile error: `field` must be a `SelectState`, `text` and every option's field strings, and the options a flat list (no submenu).
- `fn Select(comptime th: Theme, comptime name: @EnumLiteral(), comptime field: @EnumLiteral(), comptime text: @EnumLiteral(), comptime def: menu.Def) type`: Select `name` with options `def`, bound to the `SelectState` field `field`, its button showing the string field `text`, in theme `th`: its spec builders and the hooks `popover.ComptimePage` calls.
- `fn buttonStyle(th: *const Theme, r: Response) Style`: The button: a theme button filled by its response, at least 120 wide, its text at the start.
- `fn faced(th: *const Theme, face: Style, r: Response) Style`: The button's `face` (its style, or as CSS restyles it) filled by its response, as a button's is.
- `fn listStyle(th: *const Theme, at: Rect) Style`: The list: menu.zig's list below the button's rect `at` (last frame's), at least as wide as the button.
- `fn optionStyle(th: *const Theme, active: bool, selected: bool) Style`: An option row: menu.zig's item, filled from `optionFills` by highlight and choice.
- `fn optionFaced(th: *const Theme, face: Style, active: bool, selected: bool) Style`: An option row laid out by `face` (CSS on `<option>`): its own fill while neither highlighted nor chosen, else `optionFills`'.
- `fn headerStyle(th: *const Theme) Style`: A group's header row: an option's padding, no fill.
- `fn headerText(th: *const Theme) types.TextStyle`: A group's header text: the label's, in the hot control colour (dimmer than the options).
- `fn optionFills(th: *const Theme) base.StateColors`: An option's fill, indexed by `active << 1 | selected`: none, the control colour for the chosen one, the hot control colour for the highlighted one, chosen or not.
- `fn advance(comptime def: menu.Def, st: SelectState, hits: menu.Hits, evs: []const Input.Event) SelectState`: One frame of a select (APG select-only combobox): the list works as `menu.advance` works a menu (the button's click toggles it, a press outside closes it, the pointer and Up, Down, Home and End move the highlight, Enter, Space or a click choose and close, Escape closes), but it opens with the chosen option highlighted, and a choice becomes the value.
- `fn valued(s: Style, st: SelectState) Style`: The button's style with the value column set: the chosen option's position.
- `fn semantics(t: *const Tree, s: *a11y.Semantics, comptime name: []const u8, comptime def: menu.Def, st: SelectState, label: u32) void`: The semantics of select `name` the tree's columns cannot hold, written into `s` after `Semantics.clear` and before `a11y.names` and `a11y.states`.
- `fn button(f: *Frame, th: *const Theme, comptime name: []const u8, comptime def: menu.Def, st: *SelectState, texts: []const []const u8) Response`: The button of select `name` (options `def`, labelled `texts`), in the page's flow, showing the chosen option.
- `fn buttonFace(f: *Frame, th: *const Theme, comptime name: []const u8, comptime def: menu.Def, st: *SelectState, texts: []const []const u8, face: Style) Respo…`: `button` laid out by `face` instead of `buttonStyle`, as `spec.styled` restyles `ct.select` (CSS on a `<select>`); the fill still follows hover and held.
- `fn list(f: *Frame, th: *const Theme, comptime name: []const u8, comptime def: menu.Def, st: *const SelectState, texts: []const []const u8) void`: The list of select `name`, if it is open.
- `fn listGrouped(f: *Frame, th: *const Theme, comptime name: []const u8, comptime def: menu.Def, st: *const SelectState, texts: []const []const u8, comptime gr…`: `list`, with group headers (`Options.groups`) among the options.
- `fn listFace(f: *Frame, th: *const Theme, comptime name: []const u8, comptime def: menu.Def, st: *const SelectState, texts: []const []const u8, comptime group…`: `listGrouped` with every option row laid out by `face` (`optionFaced`) and its text in `text`, as `Options.option` and `Options.option_text` restyle `ct.list` (CSS on `<option>`).
- `fn Comptime(comptime th: Theme, comptime id: [:0]const u8, comptime field: []const u8, comptime opts: Options) type`: The custom widgets (`Spec.widget`) of `ct.select` and `ct.list`: the button's `step` reads every part's response and the keys through `advance`, as `button` does at run time, and keeps the button's rect for the list's `push`.
- `fn ComptimeWith(comptime th: Theme, comptime id: [:0]const u8, comptime field: []const u8, comptime opts: Options, comptime Face: ?type) type`: `Comptime` whose button `Face.push` builds instead (a recipe's look, `recipes.ct.select`); the list, the keys and the pointer stay this select's.
- `fn Example(comptime th: Theme) type`: The example page: a toolbar with a caption and the select, and the select's list, in both front ends, in theme `th`.

### `SelectState` (struct)
A select's state, owned by the application.

Fields: `selected`, `menu`

- `fn visible(s: SelectState) bool`: Whether the list is shown: what `spec.when` over this field selects by (`ct.list`).

### `Options` (struct)
The options of a select known by their captions only (`ct.select`, HTML `<option>`), and the headers of their groups (`<optgroup label>`).

Fields: `texts`, `groups`, `option`, `option_text`


### `Select(..)` (generic struct)
Select `name` with options `def`, bound to the `SelectState` field `field`, its button showing the string field `text`, in theme `th`: its spec builders and the hooks `popover.ComptimePage` calls.

- `fn button() Spec`: The button, showing the chosen option (the string field `text`).
- `fn layer() Spec`: The scrim and the list, shown while it is open, as menu.zig builds a menu's.
- `fn check(comptime State: type) ?[]const u8`
- `fn step(sig: anytype, v: anytype) void`: The button's, the scrim's and the options' responses and the keys, through `advance`; then the button's text follows the value.
- `fn part(comptime n: Spec, st: anytype, v: anytype) ?Part`

### `ct` (struct)
The select as spec nodes of `base.ComptimeUi`: the button in the page's flow, and its list, last in the page's root (so it is drawn above the rest), live while the list is open (`spec.when` over the field, `SelectState.visible`).

- `fn select(comptime th: Theme, comptime id: @EnumLiteral(), comptime field: @EnumLiteral(), comptime opts: Options) Spec`: The button of select `id`, bound to the `SelectState` field `field`, showing the chosen one of `opts.texts`.
- `fn list(comptime th: Theme, comptime id: @EnumLiteral(), comptime field: @EnumLiteral(), comptime opts: Options) Spec`: The scrim and the list of select `id`, with the same arguments as its `select`.
- `fn listOf(comptime W: type, comptime name: [:0]const u8, comptime field: @EnumLiteral(), comptime opts: Options) Spec`: `list` for the comptime select `W` (`ComptimeWith`), whose button draws another way.

### `Comptime(..)` (generic struct)
The custom widgets (`Spec.widget`) of `ct.select` and `ct.list`: the button's `step` reads every part's response and the keys through `advance`, as `button` does at run time, and keeps the button's rect for the list's `push`.


### `ComptimeWith(..)` (generic struct)
`Comptime` whose button `Face.push` builds instead (a recipe's look, `recipes.ct.select`); the list, the keys and the pointer stay this select's.

- `def = positional(opts.texts.len)`

### `ComptimeWith(..).Button` (struct)

- `wakeAfter = if (Face) |F| (if (@hasDecl(F, "wakeAfter")) F.wakeAfter else {}) else {}`: A Face that eases its look asks for frames while it moves.
- `fn bindError(comptime State: type, comptime bound: []const u8) ?[]const u8`
- `fn step(ui: anytype, comptime row: u32, event: anytype, in: *const Input) void`
- `fn push(ui: anytype, comptime n: Spec, parent: u32, next: *u32, in: *const Input) void`

### `ComptimeWith(..).List` (struct)

- `fn push(ui: anytype, comptime n: Spec, parent: u32, next: *u32, in: *const Input) void`

### `Example(..)` (generic struct)
The example page: a toolbar with a caption and the select, and the select's list, in both front ends, in theme `th`.

- `toolbar_style: Style = .{ .dir = .x, .size = .{ .fill, .fit }, .pad = @splat(8), .gap = 8, .cross = .center, .fill = th.panel }`
- `page_style: Style = .{ .size = .{ .fill, .fill } }`

## `lcgf.widgets_combobox` (src/widgets/combobox.zig)

Combo box (tier 2): a one-line text box with a list of options below it, the WAI-ARIA APG editable combobox with list autocomplete.

### Top level

- `capacity = 24`: Bytes the box holds.
- `fruits: menu.Def = .{ .items = &.{ "apple", "apricot", "banana", "cherry" } }`: The example's options.
- `fruit_texts = [_][]const u8{ "Apple", "Apricot", "Banana", "Cherry" }`
- `replay = @embedFile("testdata/combobox.replay")`: Input for `Example(.default)` at 800 x 600, one commented line per frame.
- `fn checkCombobox(comptime State: type, comptime def: menu.Def, comptime field: []const u8, comptime text: []const u8) ?[]const u8`: `null` if `State` can hold combo box `def` bound to `field`, its box showing `text`, else the compile error: `field` must be a `ComboState`, `text` and every option's field strings, and the options a flat list of at most 64 (the filter is one bit per option).
- `fn Combobox(comptime th: Theme, comptime name: @EnumLiteral(), comptime field: @EnumLiteral(), comptime text: @EnumLiteral(), comptime def: menu.Def) type`: Combo box `name` with options `def`, bound to the `ComboState` field `field`, its box showing the string field `text`, in theme `th`: its spec builders and the hooks `popover.ComptimePage` calls.
- `fn inputStyle(th: *const Theme, focused: bool, r: Response) Style`: The box: a theme button filled by its response, at least 160 wide, its text at the start, outlined in the accent colour while it has the keyboard (`focusBorders`).
- `fn focusBorders(th: *const Theme) base.OnOff`: The box's outline, indexed by focus: the hot control colour, then the accent.
- `fn listStyle(th: *const Theme, at: Rect) Style`: The list: menu.zig's list below the box's rect `at` (last frame's), at least as wide as the box.
- `fn optionStyle(th: *const Theme, active: bool, selected: bool) Style`: An option row: menu.zig's item, filled from `optionFills` by highlight and choice.
- `fn optionFills(th: *const Theme) base.StateColors`: An option's fill, indexed by `active << 1 | selected`: none, the control colour for the chosen one, the hot control colour for the highlighted one, chosen or not.
- `fn within(r: Rect, p: [2]f32) bool`: Whether `p` is inside `r` (an empty rect holds nothing).
- `fn filter(texts: []const []const u8, typed: []const u8) u64`: The options shown for `typed`: those whose text starts with it, ASCII case folded, one bit per option (all of them for no text).
- `fn nextShown(shown: u64, n: u8, from: u8, back: bool) u8`: The next shown option after `from` (before it when `back`), wrapping, among `n`; from `menu.no_item` the first (last) shown one; `menu.no_item` when none is shown.
- `fn advance(texts: []const []const u8, st: ComboState, hits: Hits, in: *const Input) ComboState`: One frame of a combo box (APG editable combobox with list autocomplete, manual selection).
- `fn valued(s: Style, st: ComboState) Style`: The box's style with the value column set: the chosen option's position, -1 for none (the text itself is the box's content, its label).
- `fn semantics(t: *const Tree, s: *a11y.Semantics, comptime name: []const u8, comptime def: menu.Def, st: ComboState, label: u32) void`: The semantics of combo box `name` the tree's columns cannot hold, written into `s` after `Semantics.clear` and before `a11y.names` and `a11y.states`.
- `fn input(f: *Frame, th: *const Theme, comptime name: []const u8, comptime def: menu.Def, st: *ComboState, texts: []const []const u8) Response`: The box of combo box `name` (options `def`, labelled `texts`), in the page's flow, showing its text.
- `fn list(f: *Frame, th: *const Theme, comptime name: []const u8, comptime def: menu.Def, st: *const ComboState, texts: []const []const u8) void`: The list of combo box `name`, if it is open.
- `fn Example(comptime th: Theme) type`: The example page: a toolbar with a caption and the combo box, and the combo box's list, in both front ends, in theme `th`.

### `ComboState` (struct)
A combo box's state, owned by the application: plain data, copied by value, so both front ends keep their own and the tests compare them.

Fields: `buf`, `len`, `caret`, `anchor`, `selected`, `open`, `active`, `hover`, `focused`, `down`

- `fn text(st: *const ComboState) []const u8`: The box's text.
- `fn setText(st: *ComboState, s: []const u8) void`: Replaces the text with `s` (cut to `capacity` bytes), the caret at its end.

### `Combobox(..)` (generic struct)
Combo box `name` with options `def`, bound to the `ComboState` field `field`, its box showing the string field `text`, in theme `th`: its spec builders and the hooks `popover.ComptimePage` calls.

- `fn input() Spec`: The box, showing the string field `text`.
- `fn layer() Spec`: The scrim and the list, shown while it is open, as menu.zig builds a menu's.
- `fn check(comptime State: type) ?[]const u8`
- `fn step(sig: anytype, v: anytype) void`: The box's, the scrim's and the options' responses, the rects of last frame and the keys, through `advance`; then the box's text field points at the state's text.
- `fn part(comptime n: Spec, st: anytype, v: anytype) ?Part`

### `Hits` (struct)
What the pointer did to a combo box this frame, from its parts' responses and last frame's rects.

Fields: `toggle`, `outside`, `clicked`, `hovered`, `down`, `inside`

- `fn add(h: *Hits, k: u8, r: Response) void`: Notes option `k`'s response.

### `Example(..)` (generic struct)

- `C = Combobox(th, .fruit, .fruit, .fruit_text, fruits)`
- `Ui = popover.ComptimePage(State, spec, th, C)`

Fields: `fruit`

## `lcgf.widgets_datepicker` (src/widgets/datepicker.zig)

Date picker (tier 2): a button showing the chosen date, which opens a dialog below it with the month's grid of days, the WAI-ARIA APG date picker dialog pattern.

### Top level

- `no_target: u8 = 0xff`: Nothing of the dialog.
- `cells = 42`: Cells in the grid: six weeks of seven days.
- `dialog_rows = 1 + (1 + 6) + (1 + 1 + 14) + 6 * (1 + 14)`: Rows `buildDialog` makes, and how many of them are text: the comptime front end's capacity.
- `dialog_texts = 3 + 7 + cells`
- `CellState = struct { hovered: bool = false, focused: bool = false, chosen: bool = false }`: What a cell shows besides its day.
- `today: i32 = 20773`: The example's first value: 2026-11-16, a Monday.
- `replay = @embedFile("testdata/datepicker.replay")`: Input for `Example(.default)` at 800 x 600, one commented line per frame.
- `fn checkDatepicker(comptime State: type, comptime field: []const u8) ?[]const u8`: `null` if `State` can hold a date picker bound to `field`, else the compile error.
- `fn partName(comptime name: []const u8, comptime part: []const u8) [:0]const u8`: Ids of the dialog's parts, from the picker's name: `name_dialog`, `name_prev`, `name_next`, `name_grid` and the cells `name_d0` to `name_d41`.
- `fn daysFromCivil(y: i32, m: u8, d: u8) i32`: The day number of `y`-`m`-`d` (Howard Hinnant's `days_from_civil`, the inverse of `date.Date.fromDays`).
- `fn civil(day: i32) date.Date`
- `fn weekday(day: i32) u8`: 0 for Sunday to 6 for Saturday (1970-01-01 was a Thursday).
- `fn monthLength(y: i32, m: u8) u8`: Days in month `m` of year `y`.
- `fn addMonths(day: i32, n: i32) i32`: `day` moved by `n` months, its day of month clamped to the new month's length.
- `fn gridStart(day: i32) i32`: The day in the grid's first cell for the month of `day`: the Sunday on or before the 1st.
- `fn title(out: []u8, day: i32) []const u8`: "November 2026": the month the grid shows, in `out`.
- `fn valueText(out: []u8, day: i32) []const u8`: "November 16, 2026": the chosen date in the CLDR long pattern, in `out`.
- `fn buildButton(b: anytype, th: *const Theme, comptime name: []const u8, st: DateState, r: Response) u32`: The button's rows: the button (filled by its response `r`) and its label, the chosen date.
- `fn buildDialog(b: anytype, th: *const Theme, comptime name: []const u8, st: DateState, at: Rect) void`: The dialog's rows, below the button's rect `at` (last frame's): a header (previous month, the month's title, next month), the weekdays, and six weeks of days.
- `fn targetAt(t: *const Tree, comptime name: []const u8, p: [2]f32) u8`: What the pointer at `p` is over in `t` (last frame's tree): a `Target`, or `no_target`.
- `fn within(r: Rect, p: [2]f32) bool`: Whether `p` is inside `r` (an empty rect holds nothing).
- `fn advance(st: DateState, hits: Hits, evs: []const Input.Event) DateState`: One frame of a date picker (APG date picker dialog).
- `fn valued(s: Style, st: DateState) Style`: The button's style with the value column set: the chosen date, days since 1970-01-01 (exact in an f32 for any date a calendar shows).
- `fn semantics(t: *const Tree, s: *a11y.Semantics, comptime name: []const u8, st: DateState) void`: The semantics of date picker `name` the tree's columns cannot hold, written into `s` after `Semantics.clear` and before `a11y.names` and `a11y.states`.
- `fn Datepicker(comptime name: @EnumLiteral(), comptime field: @EnumLiteral()) type`: Date picker `name` bound to the `DateState` field `field`: its spec builders, custom widgets of `base.ComptimeUi` (`Spec.widget`).
- `fn buttonStyle(th: *const Theme, r: Response) Style`: The button: a theme button filled by its response, at least 160 wide, its text at the start.
- `fn dialogStyle(th: *const Theme, at: Rect) Style`: The dialog: popover.zig's panel, floating `th.gap` below the button's rect `at`, left edges aligned.
- `fn headerStyle(th: *const Theme) Style`: The header: previous month, the title, next month, across the dialog.
- `fn navStyle(th: *const Theme, hovered: bool) Style`: The previous and next month buttons, filled from `navFills` by hover.
- `fn navFills(th: *const Theme) base.OnOff`: A month button's fill, indexed by hover: none, then the hot control colour.
- `fn titleStyle() Style`: The month's title, centred between the buttons.
- `fn gridStyle(th: *const Theme) Style`: The grid: the weekdays' row and six weeks, touching.
- `fn weekStyle() Style`: A week: seven cells, touching.
- `fn cellStyle(th: *const Theme, cs: CellState) Style`: A day cell (and a weekday heading, with no state): filled from `cellFills` by choice and hover, outlined in the text colour where the keyboard focus is (`focusBorders`).
- `fn cellFills(th: *const Theme) base.StateColors`: A cell's fill, indexed by `chosen << 1 | hovered`: none, the hot control colour under the pointer, the accent for the chosen day, hovered or not.
- `fn focusBorders(th: *const Theme) base.OnOff`: A cell's outline, indexed by focus: none, then the text colour.
- `fn dayText(th: *const Theme, in_month: bool) TextStyle`: A day's number, by whether it is in the month shown (`dayColors`).
- `fn dayColors(th: *const Theme) base.OnOff`: A day's colour, indexed by whether it is in the month shown: the hot control colour (dim), then the text colour.
- `fn button(f: *Frame, th: *const Theme, comptime name: []const u8, st: *DateState) Response`: The button of date picker `name`, in the page's flow, showing the chosen date.
- `fn dialog(f: *Frame, th: *const Theme, comptime name: []const u8, st: *const DateState) void`: The dialog of date picker `name`, if it is open.
- `fn Example(comptime th: Theme) type`: The example page: a toolbar with a caption and the date picker, and the date picker's dialog, in both front ends, in theme `th`.

### `DateState` (struct)
A date picker's state, owned by the application: plain data.

Fields: `value`, `focus`, `open`, `down`, `press`, `hover`

- `fn on(day: i32) DateState`: A closed picker on `day`, its grid on it too.

### `Target` (struct)
What the pointer can be over in the dialog: day cell 0 to 41 (row-major, six weeks), then these.

- `prev: u8 = 42`
- `next: u8 = 43`
- `dialog: u8 = 44`: The dialog, but none of the above.

### `TableBuilder` (struct)
The comptime front end's side of `buildDialog`: pushes onto the tables directly, as `Frame.open`, `close` and `text` do.

Fields: `t`, `tx`, `strings`, `stack`, `depth`

- `fn init(t: *Tree, tx: *Texts, strings: *Strings, parent: u32) TableBuilder`
- `fn open(b: *TableBuilder, id: Id, s: Style) u32`
- `fn close(b: *TableBuilder) void`
- `fn text(b: *TableBuilder, str: []const u8, ts: TextStyle) void`
- `fn role(b: *TableBuilder, i: u32, r: a11y.Role) void`

### `FrameBuilder` (struct)
The runtime front end's side of `buildDialog`.

Fields: `f`

- `fn open(b: *FrameBuilder, id: Id, s: Style) u32`
- `fn close(b: *FrameBuilder) void`
- `fn text(b: *FrameBuilder, str: []const u8, ts: TextStyle) void`
- `fn role(b: *FrameBuilder, i: u32, r: a11y.Role) void`

### `Hits` (struct)
What the pointer did to a date picker this frame.

Fields: `toggle`, `down`, `target`


### `Datepicker(..)` (generic struct)
Date picker `name` bound to the `DateState` field `field`: its spec builders, custom widgets of `base.ComptimeUi` (`Spec.widget`).

- `fn button() Spec`: The button, showing the chosen date.
- `fn layer() Spec`: The dialog, shown while it is open: one node whose subtree holds as many rows and texts as `buildDialog` makes.

### `Datepicker(..).Button` (struct)

- `fn bindError(comptime State: type, comptime bound: []const u8) ?[]const u8`
- `fn push(ui: anytype, comptime n: Spec, parent: u32, next: *u32, in: *const Input) void`

### `Datepicker(..).Layer` (struct)

- `fn step(ui: anytype, comptime row: u32, event: anytype, in: *const Input) void`: The button's click, the pointer over last frame's tree and the keys, through `advance`; and the button's rect, kept for `push`.
- `fn push(ui: anytype, comptime n: Spec, parent: u32, next: *u32, in: *const Input) void`

### `Example(..)` (generic struct)
The example page: a toolbar with a caption and the date picker, and the date picker's dialog, in both front ends, in theme `th`.

- `D = Datepicker(.due, .due)`
- `Ui = base.ComptimeUi(State, spec, th)`

Fields: `due`
