## `lcgf.widgets_image` (src/widgets/image.zig)

Image (tier 2): a picture from a texture, as one quad sampling it.

### Top level

- `Placed = struct { size: [2]f32, uv: [4]f32 }`: What a picture becomes in a box: the node's size and the texture rect it shows.
- `replay = @embedFile("testdata/image.replay")`: Input for `Page(.default)` at 320 x 240, one commented line per frame.
- `fn checkImage(pic: Image, opt: Options) ?[]const u8`: Why `pic` cannot be shown, or null: it needs a size above 0 and a texture rect that is not empty, and a box, if any, above 0.
- `fn place(pic: Image, opt: Options) Placed`: `pic` placed as `opt` says: its own size without a box, else fitted (`Fit`).
- `fn style(th: *const Theme, pic: Image, opt: Options) Style`: The image's node: one quad of `pic`'s texture over its placed rect (`place`), rounded by the theme's radius, tinted from `tints`: white (the picture's own colours), or the control colour while there is no picture yet (the white texture, a placeholder).
- `fn tints(th: *const Theme) base.OnOff`: An image's tint, indexed by "no picture yet": white, then the control colour.
- `fn name(s: *a11y.Semantics, row: u32, by: u32) void`: Names the image at tree row `row` by the text of row `by` (`aria-labelledby`: its caption, the alt text a page shows) in the caller's semantic columns.
- `fn image(f: *Frame, th: *const Theme, pic: Image, opt: Options) Node`: Shows `pic` as one leaf.
- `fn Page(comptime th: Theme) type`: The example page: a caption over a picture at its size, the same picture covering a box, and a button holding it as a thumbnail, in both front ends, in theme `th`.

### `Image` (struct)
Where a picture is: its texture, the rect it covers there (u0, v0, u1, v1), and its size, logical pixels.

Fields: `texture`, `uv`, `size`

- `fn inAtlas(atlas: *const image_atlas.Atlas, r: image_atlas.Rect, texture: Texture) Image`: A picture packed in `atlas` at `r` (`Atlas.add`), the atlas uploaded as `texture`, shown at one logical pixel per texel.
- `fn whole(e: embed.Image, texture: Texture) Image`: A decoded picture `e` uploaded whole as `texture`.

### `Asset` (struct)
A picture decoded at build time, and the texture it is drawn from.

Fields: `texture`, `image`

- `fn picture(a: Asset) Image`: The whole picture, at one logical pixel per texel.

### `Options` (struct)
How it is shown.

Fields: `box`, `fit`, `radius`


### `Fit` (enum)
How a picture fits a box (CSS `object-fit`).


### `ct` (struct)
The image as a spec node.

- `fn image(comptime th: Theme, comptime pic: Image, comptime opt: Options) Spec`: Shows `pic`.

### `Page(..)` (generic struct)
The example page: a caption over a picture at its size, the same picture covering a box, and a button holding it as a thumbnail, in both front ends, in theme `th`.

- `photo: Image = .{ .texture = @fromBackingInt(1), .uv = .{ 0, 0, 0.5, 0.5 }, .size = .{ 64, 48 } }`
- `boxed: Options = .{ .box = .{ 96, 96 }, .fit = .cover }`
- `thumb: Options = .{ .box = .{ 24, 24 } }`
- `Ui = base.ComptimeUi(State, spec, th)`
- `Run = base.Harness(App, Ui, .{ .nodes = 32, .texts = 8, .customs = 1, .strings = 256, .depth = 8 })`

### `Page(..).App` (struct)

Fields: `zooms`

- `fn build(app: *App, f: *Frame) void`

### `Page(..).State` (struct)

Fields: `caption`


## `lcgf.widgets_textfield` (src/widgets/textfield.zig)

Text field: one line of editable text.

### Top level

- `bands = 4`: Selection bands a text field keeps per line, so that its rows are fixed: one per run of one direction the selection covers.
- `mask = "*"`: What a password field shows once per grapheme of its text: how long it is, nothing else.
- `fn Text(comptime cap: u32) type`: A text field's state, `cap` bytes of text.
- `fn isText(comptime T: type) bool`: Whether `T` is a `Text(cap)`.
- `fn textError(comptime State: type, comptime widget: []const u8, comptime name: []const u8) ?[]const u8`: Why `State`'s field `name` cannot be the text of a `widget` (a `Text(cap)`), or null.
- `fn bindError(comptime State: type, comptime name: []const u8, comptime hint: []const u8) ?[]const u8`: Why `State` cannot hold a text field over `name` with its placeholder in string field `hint`, or null when it can.
- `fn hintSpec(comptime hint: anytype) Spec`: A comptime text control's placeholder, taken as `ct.label` takes its text: a literal (`"Your name"`) or a string field (`.name_hint`).
- `fn hintError(comptime State: type, comptime widget: []const u8, comptime hint: Spec) ?[]const u8`: Why `State` cannot show the placeholder `hint` (`hintSpec`) of `widget`, or null.
- `fn advance(th: *const Theme) f32`: Width of one byte of text, px: the front ends measure with `Mono`.
- `fn lineHeight(th: *const Theme) f32`: Height of one line of text, px.
- `fn padding(th: *const Theme) [4]f32`: The field's padding: the theme's `pad` across, half of it up and down.
- `fn fieldStyle(th: *const Theme, focused: bool, scroll: f32) Style`: The box: as wide as it is given (at least 80), one line tall, inset (`control_held`), its border the accent while focused; it clips its text, scrolled by `scroll` px.
- `fn focusFace(th: *const Theme, face: Style, focused: bool) Style`: A text control's root `face` (its theme style, or as CSS restyles it) as the control shows itself: the accent border while focused, `face`'s own otherwise.
- `fn fieldFace(th: *const Theme, face: Style, focused: bool, scroll: f32) Style`: The field's root `face` shown focused or not and scrolled `scroll` across (`fieldStyle` over a restyled root).
- `fn hintColor(th: *const Theme) Color`: The placeholder's colour: the text colour at half strength.
- `fn textStyle(th: *const Theme, hint: bool) TextStyle`: The text, or the placeholder (`hint`) in its colour when the field is empty.
- `fn selectionColor(th: *const Theme) Color`: The selection's colour: the accent at less than half strength.
- `fn selectionStyle(th: *const Theme, sel: [2]f32, top: f32) Style`: The selection behind the text, from x `sel[0]` to `sel[1]` px, one line tall.
- `fn caretStyle(th: *const Theme, x: f32, top: f32, focused: bool) Style`: The caret: 1 px wide at x `x`, one line tall, in the text colour while focused and invisible otherwise (it keeps its row, so both front ends have the same rows either way).
- `fn shown(s: anytype, th: *const Theme, hint: []const u8) Shown`
- `fn shownAs(comptime secure: bool, s: anytype, th: *const Theme, hint: []const u8, buf: []u8) Shown`: `shown`, and for a password field (`secure`) its text as one `mask` per grapheme (`Editor.display`, written to `buf`, at least the field's capacity), with the caret and the selection over the mask.
- `fn fresh(s: anytype) ?moves.View`: `s.view` while it is the layout of the field's current text, else null.
- `fn caretX(s: anytype, th: *const Theme, at: u32) f32`: The caret's x at byte `at`, px from the text's left edge: from the view when fresh, else `at` advances.
- `fn stopNear(t: []const u8, x: f32) u32`: The grapheme boundary of `t` nearest to `x` advances from its start (a click), clamped.
- `fn reveal(scroll: f32, x: f32, view: f32, content: f32) f32`: The scroll that keeps x `x` (the caret) inside a view `view` px wide, moving as little as it can from `scroll`, never past the content's end (`content` px) or its start.
- `fn behave(s: anytype, th: *const Theme, id: Id, r: Response, last: Rect, in: *const Input) void`: Applies a frame's input to field `s` (a `*Text(cap)`) named `id`, laid out last frame at `last` (zero before the first frame): while the pointer holds the field, the caret follows it (a press also moves the anchor, unless Shift is held); while focused, its keys, typed text and the IME's composition go to the editor (`Editor.update`), with Enter submitting.
- `fn behaveAs(comptime secure: bool, s: anytype, th: *const Theme, id: Id, r: Response, last: Rect, in: *const Input) void`: `behave`, for a password field when `secure`: the editor's password mode, so its text is never copied or cut, word moves go to the ends and the host is asked for secure input.
- `fn point(s: anytype, th: *const Theme, r: Response, last: Rect, in: *const Input) *edit.Editor`: The first part of `behave`: resets what the field asks of the host, and places the caret where the pointer holds the field.
- `fn follow(s: anytype, th: *const Theme, r: Response, last: Rect) void`: The last part of `behave`, after the keys: scrolls the view to the caret, and asks for text input at it while focused.
- `fn keyboardOf(in: *const Input, id: Id, focused: bool) keyboard.Keyboard`: This frame's keys for field `id`: all of them while it is focused, none otherwise.
- `fn feed(ed: *edit.Editor, in: *const Input, id: Id, only: ?usize, cx: edit.Context) edit.Result`: `Editor.update` for focused field `id` with only event `only` of `in` delivered (none for null), for a widget that handles some keys itself and passes the rest on in order.
- `fn orResult(into: *edit.Result, r: edit.Result) void`: `into` with what `r` did added.
- `fn semantics(t: *const Tree, s: *a11y.Semantics, name: Id, label: u32) void`: The semantics the tree columns cannot carry, for field `name` in either front end's tree: its name is the row `label` (its caption, `aria-labelledby`; `none` for none).
- `fn textValue(t: *const Tree, s: *a11y.Semantics, str: *Strings, name: Id, field: anytype, hint: []const u8) void`: What field `name` (over `field`, a `*Text(cap)`, showing `hint` while empty) holds, for assistive technology: its text and its placeholder, copied to the frame's strings `str` (`a11y.setText`).
- `fn textfield(f: *Frame, th: *const Theme, id: Id, s: anytype, hint: []const u8) Response`: A text field named `id` over `s` (a `*Text(cap)`), showing `hint` while empty.
- `fn textfieldFace(f: *Frame, th: *const Theme, id: Id, s: anytype, hint: []const u8, face: Style) Response`: `textfield` with its root laid out by `face` instead of `fieldStyle(th, false, 0)`, as `spec.styled` restyles `ct.textfield` (CSS on an `<input type=text>`); focus still sets the border and the text still scrolls (`fieldFace`).
- `fn password(f: *Frame, th: *const Theme, id: Id, s: anytype, hint: []const u8) Response`: A password field (`<input type=password>`): a text field that shows one `mask` per grapheme, never puts its text on the clipboard and asks for secure input.
- `fn passwordFace(f: *Frame, th: *const Theme, id: Id, s: anytype, hint: []const u8, face: Style) Response`: `password` with its root laid out by `face`, as `textfieldFace`.
- `fn platform(f: *Frame, s: anytype) void`: Copies what field `s` asks of the host this frame to the runtime front end's `Platform`: text input with its purpose and, but for a password, the text and selection around the caret for the IME and autocorrect; the clipboard text it copied, and a paste.
- `fn Comptime(comptime name: [:0]const u8, comptime bind: []const u8, comptime hint: Spec, comptime secure: bool) type`: The comptime text field named `name` over field `bind`, placeholder `hint` (`hintSpec`) (`Spec.widget`): the same rows as `textfield`, and the same input applied (`behave`).
- `fn pushText(ui: anytype, parent: u32, str: []const u8, ts: TextStyle) void`: Pushes a text leaf showing `str` under row `parent` of comptime front end `ui`, as `Frame.textStyled(str, ts, .{})` does in the runtime front end.

### `Text(..)` (generic struct)
A text field's state, `cap` bytes of text.

Fields: `bytes`, `ed`, `copied`, `clipboard`, `ime`, `input`, `result`, `scroll`, `held`, `view`

- `capacity = cap`
- `fn init(initial: []const u8) Self`: A field holding `initial` (cut at `cap` bytes, at a code point), caret at its end.
- `fn editor(s: *Self) *edit.Editor`: The editor over this value's own bytes.
- `fn text(s: *Self) []const u8`: The text, valid until the next edit.
- `fn set(s: *Self, str: []const u8) void`: Replaces the whole text (not recorded for undo), caret at its end.

### `Shown` (struct)
What a single-line field shows this frame: its text or placeholder, and where the caret and the selection are, px from the text's left edge.

Fields: `str`, `hint`, `caret`, `sel`, `band`


### `ct` (struct)

- `fn textfield(comptime th: Theme, comptime id: @EnumLiteral(), comptime bind: @EnumLiteral(), comptime hint: anytype) Spec`: A text field named `id` over the `Text(cap)` field `bind`, showing `hint` while empty: a literal or a string field (`hintSpec`).
- `fn password(comptime th: Theme, comptime id: @EnumLiteral(), comptime bind: @EnumLiteral(), comptime hint: anytype) Spec`: `textfield` as a password field (`password`).

### `Comptime(..)` (generic struct)
The comptime text field named `name` over field `bind`, placeholder `hint` (`hintSpec`) (`Spec.widget`): the same rows as `textfield`, and the same input applied (`behave`).

- `focusable = true`
- `Payload = edit.Result`
- `fn bindError(comptime State: type, comptime field: []const u8) ?[]const u8`
- `fn push(ui: anytype, comptime n: Spec, parent: u32, next: *u32, in: *const Input) void`
- `fn step(ui: anytype, comptime row: u32, event: anytype, in: *const Input) @TypeOf(event)`

### `Page` (struct)
The text field's example page: Name (empty, placeholder "Your name") and City ("Oslo"), each under its caption, on a panel, in both front ends.

- `Field = Text(64)`
- `Ui = base.ComptimeUi(State, spec, th)`
- `Run = base.Harness(App, Ui, .{ .nodes = 32, .texts = 4, .customs = 1, .strings = 512, .depth = 8 })`
- `replay = @embedFile("testdata/textfield.replay")`
- `golden_frames = .{ .{ "rest", 0 }, .{ "typed", 2 }, .{ "selected", 3 }, .{ "drag", 8 } }`: Frames kept as golden images, by name: tools/golden/ref/widgets/textfield-<name>.png.

### `Page.App` (struct)

Fields: `name`, `city`, `submits`

- `fn build(app: *App, f: *Frame) void`

A host's layout of a text as text/layout draws it, reordered, one `adv` per code point, one line per line between newlines: what a host hands over as `Text.view`.

- `text = "abc \u{5D0}\u{5D1}\u{5D2} def"`

## `lcgf.widgets_search` (src/widgets/search.zig)

Search field: a text field with a clear button.

### Top level

- `Text = tf.Text`: The state type: a text field's (`textfield.Text(cap)`).
- `escape: u8 = 0x29`: Escape, as a USB HID scancode.
- `clear_text = "Clear"`: The clear button's caption.
- `semantics = tf.semantics`: The semantics the tree columns cannot carry: the field's name is the row `label` (its caption, `aria-labelledby`), as for the text field.
- `textValue = tf.textValue`: Its text and placeholder, as the text field's (`textfield.textValue`).
- `fn bindError(comptime State: type, comptime name: []const u8, comptime hint: []const u8) ?[]const u8`: Why `State` cannot hold a search field over `name` with placeholder field `hint`, or null.
- `fn clearId(id: Id) Id`: The id of search field `id`'s clear button.
- `fn boxStyle(th: *const Theme, focused: bool) Style`: The box around the field and the button: as wide as it is given (at least 80), inset, its border the accent while the field is focused.
- `fn inputStyle(th: *const Theme, scroll: f32) Style`: The field inside the box: a text field's (`textfield.fieldStyle`) layout, clipping and role, painted by the box instead.
- `fn clearStyle(th: *const Theme, r: Response, shown: bool) Style`: The clear button: a small button face by interaction state while there is text (`shown`), clear otherwise (it keeps its place, so the field does not move).
- `fn clearText(th: *const Theme, shown: bool) TextStyle`: The clear button's caption, in the text colour while shown.
- `fn behave(s: anytype, th: *const Theme, id: Id, r: Response, clear_clicked: bool, last: Rect, in: *const Input) void`: Applies a frame's input to search field `s` named `id`, laid out last frame at `last`: a click on the shown clear button (`clear_clicked`) or Escape on the focused field empties it; then everything a text field does (`textfield.behave`).
- `fn expose(t: *Tree, box: u32, button: u32, shown: bool) void`: Roles of the box (a search landmark around the field), of the field (a search box, its first child) and of the clear button (a button while shown, presentational otherwise), written to rows `box`, `box + 1` and `button` of `t`.
- `fn search(f: *Frame, th: *const Theme, id: Id, s: anytype, hint: []const u8) Response`: A search field named `id` over `s` (a `*Text(cap)`), showing `hint` while empty.
- `fn searchFace(f: *Frame, th: *const Theme, id: Id, s: anytype, hint: []const u8, face: Style) Response`: `search` with its box laid out by `face` instead of `boxStyle(th, false)`, as `spec.styled` restyles `ct.search`; focus still sets the border (`textfield.focusFace`).
- `fn Comptime(comptime name: [:0]const u8, comptime bind: []const u8, comptime hint: Spec) type`: The comptime search field named `name` over field `bind`, placeholder `hint` (`textfield.hintSpec`; `Spec.widget` of the box): the same rows as `search`, the same input applied (`behave`).

### `ct` (struct)

- `fn search(comptime th: Theme, comptime id: @EnumLiteral(), comptime bind: @EnumLiteral(), comptime hint: anytype) Spec`: A search field named `id` over the `Text(cap)` field `bind`, showing `hint` while empty, a literal or a string field (`textfield.hintSpec`); its clear button is the widget `<id>.clear`.

### `Stop` (struct)
The field inside a comptime search box: a focus stop whose events carry an `edit.Result`, pushed and stepped by the box (`Comptime`).

- `focusable = true`
- `Payload = edit.Result`
- `fn push(ui: anytype, comptime n: Spec, parent: u32, next: *u32, in: *const Input) void`

### `Comptime(..)` (generic struct)
The comptime search field named `name` over field `bind`, placeholder `hint` (`textfield.hintSpec`; `Spec.widget` of the box): the same rows as `search`, the same input applied (`behave`).

- `fn bindError(comptime State: type, comptime field: []const u8) ?[]const u8`
- `fn push(ui: anytype, comptime n: Spec, parent: u32, next: *u32, in: *const Input) void`
- `fn step(ui: anytype, comptime row: u32, event: anytype, in: *const Input) @TypeOf(event)`

### `Page` (struct)
The search field's example page: Find, placeholder "Search", under its caption on a panel, in both front ends.

- `Field = Text(64)`
- `Ui = base.ComptimeUi(State, spec, th)`
- `Run = base.Harness(App, Ui, .{ .nodes = 16, .texts = 4, .customs = 1, .strings = 512, .depth = 8 })`
- `replay = @embedFile("testdata/search.replay")`

## `lcgf.widgets_number` (src/widgets/number.zig)

Number field: a number typed as text or stepped by keys and the wheel (WAI-ARIA APG spinbutton).

### Top level

- `Range = struct { lo: f32 = 0, hi: f32 = 100, step: f32 = 1 }`: The ends of a number field's value and its step (Up and Down; Page Up and Page Down step ten times as far).
- `fn bindError(comptime State: type, comptime name: []const u8) ?[]const u8`: Why `State`'s field `name` cannot be a number field's state, or null when it can.
- `fn rangeError(comptime name: []const u8, comptime range: Range) ?[]const u8`: A compile error message for a range whose ends are not ordered or whose step is not positive, or null.
- `fn numberStyle(th: *const Theme, focused: bool, scroll: f32) Style`: The box: a text field's (`textfield.fieldStyle`), as wide as it is given, one line tall.
- `fn format(value: f32, buf: *[24]u8) []const u8`: `value` written as the field shows it: the shortest text that reads back as it.
- `fn show(s: *Number) void`: Writes the value as the text, caret at its end.
- `fn parse(text: []const u8, range: Range) ?f32`: The value typed in `text` (spaces around it ignored), clamped to `range`, or null when it is not a number.
- `fn stepped(value: f32, code: u8, range: Range) ?f32`: The value after key `code` on a focused field, or null for a key that does not step: Up and Down by the step, Page Up and Page Down by ten, Home and End to the ends.
- `fn commit(s: *Number, range: Range) void`: Takes the typed text as the value (or, if it is not a number, the value back as the text).
- `fn behave(s: *Number, th: *const Theme, id: Id, r: Response, last: Rect, range: Range, in: *const Input) bool`: Applies a frame's input to field `s` named `id`, laid out last frame at `last`: the pointer places the caret (as `textfield.behave`); while focused the stepping keys (`stepped`) change the value, Enter takes the typed text, and other keys and typed text edit it; the wheel over the field steps the value (up adds); losing focus takes the typed text.
- `fn expose(t: *Tree, i: u32, value: f32) void`: Marks row `i` of tree `t` a spin button (ARIA spinbutton) holding `value` in the value column, which is where the accessibility bridge reads them.
- `fn semantics(t: *const Tree, s: *a11y.Semantics, name: Id, label: u32, range: Range) void`: The semantics the tree columns cannot carry, for the field `name` in `t`: its name is the row `label` (its caption, `aria-labelledby`), as for the text field, and its range is `range` (`aria-valuemin`, `aria-valuemax`, and the step a screen reader's increment moves by, as Up and Down do).
- `fn number(f: *Frame, th: *const Theme, id: Id, s: *Number, range: Range) Response`: A number field named `id` over `s`, running `range`.
- `fn numberFace(f: *Frame, th: *const Theme, id: Id, s: *Number, range: Range, face: Style) Response`: `number` with its root laid out by `face` instead of `numberStyle(th, false, 0)`, as `spec.styled` restyles `ct.number`; focus still sets the border (`textfield.fieldFace`).
- `fn Comptime(comptime name: [:0]const u8, comptime bind: []const u8, comptime range: Range) type`: The comptime number field named `name` over field `bind`, running `range` (`Spec.widget`): the same rows as `number`, the same input applied (`behave`).

### `Number` (struct)
A number field's state.

Fields: `value`, `text`


### `ct` (struct)

- `fn number(comptime th: Theme, comptime id: @EnumLiteral(), comptime bind: @EnumLiteral(), comptime range: Range) Spec`: A number field named `id` over the `Number` field `bind`, running `range`.

### `Comptime(..)` (generic struct)
The comptime number field named `name` over field `bind`, running `range` (`Spec.widget`): the same rows as `number`, the same input applied (`behave`).

- `focusable = true`
- `Payload = f32`
- `value_range = range`: The ends and step of its value.
- `fn bindError(comptime State: type, comptime field: []const u8) ?[]const u8`
- `fn push(ui: anytype, comptime n: Spec, parent: u32, next: *u32, in: *const Input) void`
- `fn step(ui: anytype, comptime row: u32, event: anytype, in: *const Input) @TypeOf(event)`

### `Page` (struct)
The number field's example page: Quantity (0 to 10 by 1, at 3) and Scale (-1 to 1 by 0.25, at 0.5), each under its caption, on a panel, in both front ends.

- `quantity: Range = .{ .lo = 0, .hi = 10, .step = 1 }`
- `scale: Range = .{ .lo = -1, .hi = 1, .step = 0.25 }`
- `Ui = base.ComptimeUi(State, spec, th)`
- `Run = base.Harness(App, Ui, .{ .nodes = 16, .texts = 4, .customs = 1, .strings = 512, .depth = 8 })`
- `replay = @embedFile("testdata/number.replay")`
- `golden_frames = .{ .{ "rest", 0 }, .{ "stepped", 3 }, .{ "typing", 5 }, .{ "wheel", 8 } }`: Frames kept as golden images, by name: tools/golden/ref/widgets/number-<name>.png.

### `Page.App` (struct)

Fields: `qty`, `scale`

- `fn build(app: *App, f: *Frame) void`

### `Page.State` (struct)

Fields: `qty_text`, `qty`, `scale_text`, `scale`


## `lcgf.widgets_textarea` (src/widgets/textarea.zig)

Text area: several lines of editable text, scrolled by whole lines.

### Top level

- `Text = tf.Text`: The state type: a text field's (`textfield.Text(cap)`).
- `Line = struct { str: []const u8, sel: [2]f32 }`: One line shown: its bytes, and the selection on it as x px from the line's left edge.
- `textValue = tf.textValue`: Its text and placeholder, as the text field's (`textfield.textValue`).
- `fn bindError(comptime State: type, comptime name: []const u8, comptime rows: u32) ?[]const u8`: Why `State` cannot hold a text area over `name`, showing `rows` lines, or null when it can.
- `fn areaStyle(th: *const Theme, rows: u32, focused: bool) Style`: The box: as wide as it is given (at least 80), `rows` lines tall, inset, its border the accent while focused; it clips its lines.
- `fn lineAt(t: []const u8, k: u32) ?[2]u32`: Line `k` of `t` (counting from 0, lines end at newlines), or null past the last.
- `fn lineOf(t: []const u8, at: u32) u32`: The line holding byte offset `at`.
- `fn shownLine(t: []const u8, scroll: u32, k: u32, sel: [2]u32, adv: f32) Line`: What line `k` from the top of the view shows: the text's line `scroll + k`, and the part of the selection `sel` on it (a selection running past its end takes one more advance, for the newline).
- `fn lineBands(v: ?moves.View, line: u32, sel: [2]u32, mono: [2]f32) [tf.bands][2]f32`: The selection `sel` on the text's line `line` as bands, x px from the line's left edge: one per direction run from the layout `v` (`query.selection`), or the one `Mono` band `mono` without it.
- `fn caretAt(t: []const u8, at: u32, scroll: u32, rows: u32, th: *const Theme, v: ?moves.View) struct { x: f32, y: f32, shown: bool }`: Where the caret is drawn, px from the first shown line's left edge, and whether it is on a line that is shown: its x from the layout `v` when given, else its column in advances.
- `fn vertical(t: []const u8, at: u32, dir: i2, goal: f32) u32`: The caret `dir` lines (-1 up, +1 down) from `at`, in the column `goal` (in bytes, the column a run of vertical moves started from), at the nearest grapheme boundary; the text's start or end past the first or last line.
- `fn revealLine(scroll: u32, line: u32, rows: u32, lines: u32) u32`: The first line shown after the caret moved to line `line`: as little scrolling as keeps it among the `rows` lines shown, never past the last line.
- `fn behave(s: anytype, th: *const Theme, id: Id, r: Response, last: Rect, rows: u32, in: *const Input) void`: Applies a frame's input to text area `s` (a `*Text(cap)`) named `id` showing `rows` lines, laid out last frame at `last`: as `textfield.behave` (pointer, keys, typed text, IME), with Enter inserting a newline, Up and Down moving between lines in their column, and the wheel scrolling by lines while the pointer is over it.
- `fn semantics(t: *const Tree, s: *a11y.Semantics, name: Id, label: u32) void`: The semantics the tree columns cannot carry, for text area `name`: its name is the row `label` (its caption, `aria-labelledby`; `none` for none), as for the text field (`textfield.semantics`), and it is multiline (`aria-multiline`).
- `fn textarea(f: *Frame, th: *const Theme, id: Id, s: anytype, rows: u32, hint: []const u8) Response`: A text area named `id` over `s` (a `*Text(cap)`) showing `rows` lines (as many as its comptime twin, if any), the placeholder `hint` on its first line while empty.
- `fn textareaFace(f: *Frame, th: *const Theme, id: Id, s: anytype, rows: u32, hint: []const u8, face: Style) Response`: `textarea` with its root laid out by `face` instead of `areaStyle(th, rows, false)`, as `spec.styled` restyles `ct.textarea`; focus still sets the border (`textfield.focusFace`).
- `fn Comptime(comptime name: [:0]const u8, comptime bind: []const u8, comptime rows: u32, comptime hint: Spec) type`: The comptime text area named `name` over field `bind`, showing `rows` lines, placeholder `hint` (`textfield.hintSpec`; `Spec.widget`): the same rows as `textarea`, the same input applied (`behave`).

### `ct` (struct)

- `fn textarea(comptime th: Theme, comptime id: @EnumLiteral(), comptime bind: @EnumLiteral(), comptime rows: u32, comptime hint: anytype) Spec`: A text area named `id` over the `Text(cap)` field `bind`, showing `rows` lines and `hint` while empty, a literal or a string field (`textfield.hintSpec`).

### `Comptime(..)` (generic struct)
The comptime text area named `name` over field `bind`, showing `rows` lines, placeholder `hint` (`textfield.hintSpec`; `Spec.widget`): the same rows as `textarea`, the same input applied (`behave`).

- `focusable = true`
- `Payload = edit.Result`
- `fn bindError(comptime State: type, comptime field: []const u8) ?[]const u8`
- `fn push(ui: anytype, comptime n: Spec, parent: u32, next: *u32, in: *const Input) void`
- `fn step(ui: anytype, comptime row: u32, event: anytype, in: *const Input) @TypeOf(event)`

### `Page` (struct)
The text area's example page: Notes, three lines shown, holding "one\ntwo", under its caption on a panel, in both front ends.

- `Field = Text(128)`
- `rows = 3`
- `Ui = base.ComptimeUi(State, spec, th)`
- `Run = base.Harness(App, Ui, .{ .nodes = 32, .texts = 8, .customs = 1, .strings = 512, .depth = 8 })`
- `replay = @embedFile("testdata/textarea.replay")`
- `golden_frames = .{ .{ "rest", 0 }, .{ "typed", 3 }, .{ "scrolled", 5 }, .{ "selected", 7 } }`: Frames kept as golden images, by name: tools/golden/ref/widgets/textarea-<name>.png.

### `Page.App` (struct)

Fields: `notes`

- `fn build(app: *App, f: *Frame) void`

### `Page.State` (struct)

Fields: `notes_text`, `notes`, `notes_hint`


## `lcgf.a11y_tree` (src/a11y/tree.zig)

The semantic tree: what assistive technology sees of the node tree.

### Top level

- `Live = enum(u8) { off, polite, assertive }`: How a live region's changes are announced (`aria-live`).
- `fn roleName(byte: u8) []const u8`: The name of the role in a raw role byte, as in `Role` (`"tab"`, `"toggle_button"`), or `"unknown"` for a byte no role has.
- `fn setText(s: *Semantics, row: u32, str: *Strings, text: []const u8, hint: []const u8) void`: Sets row `row`'s text and placeholder (`Semantics.Columns.text`, `hint`), copying the bytes into the frame's strings `str`, and their hash.
- `fn names(t: *const Tree, s: *Semantics) void`: Accessible names, as text runs.
- `fn states(t: *const Tree, s: *Semantics) void`: Completes `state` from the tree: a checkable role is mixed when its `value` is strictly between 0 and 1, else checked (a toggle button pressed) when it is not 0, and a node inside a disabled node is disabled (as `aria-disabled` applies to descendants).
- `fn focused(t: *const Tree, s: *Semantics, focus: u32) void`: Marks the node with id `focus` (`Interaction.focus`, 0 for none) `focused`.
- `fn flatten(t: *const Tree, tx: *const Texts, strings: []const u8, s: *Semantics, out: *Export) void`: Writes the semantic tree of `t` to `out`.
- `fn diff(cur: *const Export, prev: *const Export, ch: *Changes) void`: Compares this frame's export with last frame's by id.
- `fn apply(it: *Interaction, actions: []const Action) void`: Applies this frame's actions to the interaction state as the user's own input would: `press` makes the target this frame's click and `focus` gives it focus, so widgets that read `Interaction.response` act on them with no code of their own.
- `fn setValue(actions: []const Action, id: types.Id, current: f32) f32`: The value assistive technology set for `id` this frame, or `current` when none did.
- `fn steps(actions: []Action, e: *const Export) void`: Turns each `increment` and `decrement` row into the `set_value` it means: the value before it (last frame's export `e`, or an earlier row's `set_value` for the same node) moved by the node's step and kept in its range.

### `Role` (enum)
What a node is to assistive technology: the roles the WAI-ARIA Authoring Practices patterns use.


### `State` (struct)
States a node reports, one bit each, in `Semantics.Columns.state`.

Fields: `checked`, `expandable`, `expanded`, `selected`, `disabled`, `mixed`, `pressed`, `current`, `multiline`, `focused`, `read_only`, `invalid`, `required`, `busy`

- `fn bitsOf(st: State) u16`

### `Semantics` (struct)
The accessibility column set: one row per tree row, beside `Tree.Columns`.

Fields: `cap`, `c`

- `fn init(carver: *mem.Carver, cap: u32) Semantics`: Carves every column from `carver`, `simd.rows(cap)` rows each.
- `fn clear(s: *Semantics) void`: Resets the inputs for a new frame: no node is labelled by another.

### `Semantics.Columns` (struct)

Fields: `labelled_by`, `label`, `text`, `hint`, `text_hash`, `state`, `range`, `first_text`, `name`, `ix`


### `Export` (struct)
The semantic tree as flat arrays, the form a bridge hands to the platform: one row per node with a role, in tree order (parents before children), parents as export rows.

Fields: `len`, `cap`, `c`

- `fn headingLevel(e: *const Export, row: usize) ?usize`: Semantic rank of an HTML-style heading.
- `fn init(carver: *mem.Carver, cap: u32) Export`: Carves every column from `carver`, `simd.rows(cap)` rows each, for a tree of `cap` nodes.

### `Export.Columns` (struct)

Fields: `id`, `node`, `parent`, `role`, `state`, `value`, `range`, `name_start`, `name_bytes`, `text`, `hint`, `name_hash`, `rect`, `kids`, `kids_hash`


### `Changes` (struct)
What changed in the semantic tree since last frame, in the form a bridge sends to the platform (an AccessKit `TreeUpdate`): the rows to send again and the ids to drop.

Fields: `updated`, `updated_len`, `removed`, `removed_len`

- `fn init(carver: *mem.Carver, cap: u32) Changes`: Room for a tree of `cap` nodes, plus the spare row that filtering writes to.

### `Action` (struct)
A request from assistive technology, as data: the bridge turns each platform action (AccessKit `ActionRequest`) into one row and the host passes the slice in with the frame's input.

Fields: `kind`, `_pad`, `target`, `value`


## `lcgf.a11y_settings` (src/a11y/settings.zig)

Accessibility settings (tier 2): the platform's accessibility preferences as plain inputs, and what they change.

### Top level

- `Settings = settings_data.Settings`
- `progress = settings_data.progress`
- `text_scale_min: f32 = 0.5`: The range `Settings.text_scale` is clamped to: past both ends of what platforms offer (Android 0.85, iOS accessibility sizes about 3.1).
- `text_scale_max: f32 = 4`
- `aa: f32 = 4.5`: WCAG 2 minimum contrast for body text: level AA (1.4.3) and AAA (1.4.6).
- `aaa: f32 = 7`
- `non_text: f32 = 3`: WCAG 1.4.11 non-text contrast, for marks and outlines at every level.
- `Pair = struct { fg: theme.Role, bg: theme.Role, min: f32 }`: A foreground role drawn over a background role, and the contrast it needs.
- `fn tokens(s: *const Settings, tok: *const Tokens) Tokens`: `tok` adjusted by the settings: the tokens the widget theme is built from.
- `fn widgets(s: *const Settings, tok: *const Tokens) base.Theme`: The widget theme of `tok` under the settings.
- `fn showFocus(s: *const Settings, fo: *Focus) void`: Applies `always_show_focus` to this frame's ring: call after `Focus.step`, before `Focus.ring`.
- `fn pairs(text_min: f32) [6]Pair`: The pairs the widgets draw, with body text held to `text_min` (`aa` or `aaa`): text on the panel and on every control face; a checked box and a slider's fill against the track; the focus outline against the panel.
- `fn check(tok: *const Tokens, text_min: f32) ?Pair`: The first pair of `pairs(text_min)` whose colours in `tok` contrast less than it needs, or null when every pair passes.
- `fn contrast(a: Color, b: Color) f32`: WCAG 2 contrast ratio of two opaque colours, 1 (same luminance) to 21 (black on white).
- `fn luminance(c: Color) f32`: WCAG 2 relative luminance of an sRGB colour, 0 to 1.
- `fn findings(comptime spec: Spec) []const Finding`: Every finding in `spec`, in row order.
- `fn message(comptime found: []const Finding) []const u8`: The compile error `lint` gives for `found`, one line per finding.
- `fn lint(comptime spec: Spec) void`: Fails the build when `spec` breaks a `Rule`: `comptime settings.lint(spec);` beside the `ComptimeUi(State, spec, th)` it checks.

### `Rule` (enum)
What a comptime spec can get wrong for assistive technology, checked while compiling.


### `Finding` (struct)

Fields: `rule`, `row`, `id`


## `lcgf.settings_data` (src/a11y/settings_data.zig)

Accessibility settings as plain data (tier 0): what the platform's accessibility preferences say, with no theme or widget code, so the runtime front end (`Context.settings`) and the animation code can read them.

### Top level

- `fn progress(s: *const Settings, elapsed: f32, seconds: f32) f32`: How far through a transition of `seconds` the UI is, `elapsed` seconds after it started: 0 to 1.

### `Settings` (struct)
What the user asked the platform for.

Fields: `high_contrast`, `text_scale`, `reduced_motion`, `always_show_focus`


## `lcgf.render_sw` (src/render/sw/sw.zig)

The software back end: draws a `DrawList` into a caller-owned framebuffer on the CPU, with the semantics of the stock quad shader (src/render/shaders/quad.wgsl) so its pixels match the GPU back ends' golden images (`ci/run.sh sw-golden`).

### Top level

- `bands = @import("bands.zig")`
- `canvas = @import("canvas.zig")`
- `Format = enum(u8) { rgba8, bgra8 }`: Byte order of a pixel in memory.
- `Software = SoftwareOf(all_kinds)`: The back end with every feature of the stock material compiled in, and the canvas.
- `all_kinds: []const features.Kind = std.enums.values(features.Kind)`: Every `features.Kind`, in declaration order.
- `missing_kind: Color = .rgb(255, 0, 255)`: Colour of a quad whose feature this build left out: drawn over its covered pixels, opaque, so a missing feature shows instead of turning into another one.
- `fn SoftwareOf(comptime kinds: []const features.Kind) type`: The back end compiled for the feature kinds in `kinds` only (`solid` must be one).
- `fn SoftwareCoords(comptime kinds: []const features.Kind, comptime fixed_point: bool) type`: Quantize bare-metal rectangle and clip coordinates to Q12.4 when requested.
- `fn SoftwareFor(comptime use: features.Usage) type`: Software renderer for the features collected from a comptime UI.
- `fn tile(fb: *const Framebuffer, k: u32, n: u32) [4]u32`: Tile `k` of `n`: a band of whole rows.
- `fn spanMask(x: u32, x0: u32, x1: u32) B`: Lanes of the vector at `x` whose pixel lies in [x0, x1).
- `fn overSa(c: [3]U, sa: U, old: U, red: u5) U`: Straight 8-bit r, g, b with source alpha `sa` (0..255), premultiplied over `old`.
- `fn overColor(color: [4]F, alpha: F, old: U, red: u5) U`: Source `color` (straight, 0..1) at coverage `alpha` premultiplied over the pixels `old`: the colour and its alpha times the coverage rounded to 8 bits, then `overSa`.
- `fn cornerRadius(px: F, py: F, hx: f32, hy: f32, radii: [4]f32) F`: The shader's `corner_radius`: the radius of p's quadrant, capped by the half size.
- `fn roundedBox(px: F, py: F, hx: f32, hy: f32, r: F) F`: The shader's `rounded_box`: signed distance to a box of half size (hx, hy) with radius r.
- `fn shade(comptime kind: features.Kind, t: Target, q: *const Quad, img: Image, scale: f32) void`: Shades `q` into `t` with the stock material's `kind` loop: quad.wgsl `vs` and `fs` per pixel.

### `Framebuffer` (struct)
The render target: `height` rows of `stride` pixels, one u32 each, owned by the caller.

Fields: `pixels`, `width`, `height`, `stride`, `format`

- `fn len(width: u32, height: u32) usize`: Pixels a `width` x `height` framebuffer needs.
- `fn init(pixels: []u32, width: u32, height: u32, format: Format) Framebuffer`
- `fn all(fb: *const Framebuffer) [4]u32`: Whole framebuffer as a scissor rect.
- `fn pack(fb: *const Framebuffer, c: Color) u32`: `c` in this framebuffer's byte order.
- `fn at(fb: *const Framebuffer, x: u32, y: u32) Color`: Pixel (x, y) as a colour.
- `fn fill(fb: *Framebuffer, box: [4]u32, c: Color) void`: Sets every pixel of `box` (x0, y0, x1, y1, clamped to the framebuffer) to `c`.
- `fn clear(fb: *Framebuffer, c: Color) void`

### `Image` (struct)
A caller-owned texture: `width` x `height` texels, RGBA8 (`Color` bits) in `pixels` or coverage in `alpha8`, sampled nearest with clamped coordinates, as the GPU back ends' samplers do.

Fields: `pixels`, `width`, `height`, `alpha8`

- `white: Image = .{ .pixels = &.{0xffff_ffff}, .width = 1, .height = 1 }`: Texture 0 (`types.Texture.white`): put it at row 0 of `Software.images`.

### `Target` (struct)
Where draws land: the framebuffer and a scissor rect in pixels (x0, y0, x1, y1).

Fields: `fb`, `scissor`


### `SoftwareOf(..)` (generic struct)
The back end compiled for the feature kinds in `kinds` only (`solid` must be one).


### `SoftwareCoords(..)` (generic struct)
Quantize bare-metal rectangle and clip coordinates to Q12.4 when requested.


### `SoftwareFor(..)` (generic struct)
Software renderer for the features collected from a comptime UI.


### `Setup` (struct)
What a shell that draws in software keeps, and its frame: clear the framebuffer and draw the whole list into it.

Fields: `gfx`, `pool`, `bins`

- `fn draw(s: *Setup, fb: *Framebuffer, list: *const DrawList, glyphs: iface.Glyphs, background: Color, scale: f32) void`: Draws `list` over `fb`, which holds the last frame: only the rects of `list.damage` (all of `fb` unless the context tracks damage, `Context.trackDamage`), each cleared and drawn.

## `lcgf.render_sw_font` (src/render/sw/font.zig)

The bundled bitmap font for the software renderer: DejaVu Sans Mono at 14 px (the examples' atlas, src/example/font/, under its LICENSE.txt), repacked into `Mono`'s 16 x 16 byte-indexed grid, so the glyph quads `Mono.quads` makes sample it as they are.

### Top level

- `width = 16 * cell_w`
- `height = 16 * cell_h`
- `image: sw.Image = .{ .alpha8 = &alpha, .width = width, .height = height }`

## `lcgf.png` (src/assets/png.zig)

PNG decoding into premultiplied RGBA8, over the caller's memory (https://www.w3.org/TR/png/).

### Top level

- `max_side = 16384`: Largest width or height decoded.
- `Color = enum(u8) { gray = 0, rgb = 2, palette = 3, gray_alpha = 4, rgba = 6, _ }`
- `fn info(png: []const u8) Error!Info`: Reads the header and walks every chunk.
- `fn decode(png: []const u8, pixels: []u8, scratch: []u8) Error!Info`: Decodes `png` into `pixels` (`info(png).pixelBytes()`, premultiplied RGBA8, row by row), using `scratch` (`scratchBytes()`).
- `fn encodeForTest(gpa: std.mem.Allocator, w: u32, h: u32, depth: u8, color: Color, interlaced: bool, extra: []const Chunk, raw: []const u8) ![]u8`: Test encoder: a PNG of `raw` (filtered scanlines, every pass, filter bytes included) with the given header and extra chunks before IDAT, compressed by std's deflate.
- `fn decodeForTest(gpa: std.mem.Allocator, file: []const u8) ![]u8`: Decodes with exactly the memory `info` asks for; the caller frees the pixels.
- `fn fixCrcs(file: []u8) void`: Rewrites every whole chunk's CRC, so a mutated file gets past the CRC check.

### `Info` (struct)
What a file holds and how much memory decoding it takes.

Fields: `w`, `h`, `depth`, `color`, `interlaced`, `plte`, `trns`, `zlib_len`, `raw_len`

- `fn pixelBytes(i: Info) usize`: Size of `decode`'s `pixels`: `w * h * 4`.
- `fn scratchBytes(i: Info) usize`: Size of `decode`'s `scratch`.

## `lcgf.embed` (src/assets/embed.zig)

Images decoded at compile time: `embed(@embedFile("icon.png"))` runs the PNG decoder (png.zig) in the compiler, so the binary holds the premultiplied RGBA8 pixels in read-only data and the program never decodes, allocates or reads a file for them.

### Top level

- `fn embed(comptime file: []const u8) Image`: Decodes `file` (a PNG, usually `@embedFile(...)`) at compile time.

### `Image` (struct)
A decoded image: `w * h` premultiplied RGBA8 pixels, row by row, in read-only data.

Fields: `w`, `h`, `pixels`


## `lcgf.image_jobs` (src/assets/image_jobs.zig)

PNG decoding off the UI thread.

### Top level

- `fn run(io: std.Io, job: *Job) void`: Decodes the job on the calling thread and signals it: the body of the worker.
- `fn start(io: std.Io, job: *Job) std.Io.ConcurrentError!std.Io.Future(void)`: Runs `run(io, job)` on another thread of `io`.

### `Job` (struct)

Fields: `file`, `pixels`, `scratch`, `wake`, `done`, `result`


## `lcgf.image_atlas` (src/assets/image_atlas.zig)

Image atlas: small images packed into one premultiplied RGBA8 texture, so quads showing different icons share a texture and merge into one batch (batches split on texture, prototype/ui.zig `Batch`).

### Top level

- `Rect = skyline.Atlas.Rect`
- `Skyline = skyline.Atlas.Skyline`

### `Atlas` (struct)

Fields: `w`, `h`, `pixels`, `max_side`, `packer`, `dirty`

- `fn init(w: u32, h: u32, pixels: []u8, sky: Skyline, max_side: u32) Atlas`: An empty atlas over the caller's `pixels` (`w * h * 4`, cleared here) and skyline columns (capacity `w` is always enough).
- `fn clear(a: *Atlas) void`: Forgets every image and clears the texture.
- `fn add(a: *Atlas, w: u32, h: u32, src: []const u8) ?Rect`: Copies a `w` x `h` premultiplied RGBA8 image (`src`, row by row) into the atlas and returns where it is, border excluded; `uv` turns that into a quad's `uv`.
- `fn uv(a: *const Atlas, r: Rect) [4]f32`: Texture coordinates of `r`: left, top, right, bottom in 0..1 (a quad's `uv`).
- `fn takeDirty(a: *Atlas) ?skyline.Atlas.Upload`: The region to upload this frame (wgpu `queue.writeTexture`: data `pixels[offset..]`, `bytes_per_row`, origin `x`, `y`, extent `w`, `h`), or null when nothing changed.

## `lcgf.image_mipmaps` (src/assets/image_mipmaps.zig)

Mipmap chains for images a back end samples with a mipmapped sampler (trilinear filtering of an image drawn smaller than its size).

### Top level

- `fn levels(w: u32, h: u32) u32`: Number of levels down to 1 x 1, level 0 included.
- `fn size(w: u32, h: u32, l: u32) [2]u32`: Width and height of level `l`.
- `fn offset(w: u32, h: u32, l: u32) usize`: Byte offset of level `l` in a chain.
- `fn chainBytes(w: u32, h: u32) usize`: Bytes of the whole chain: about 4/3 of level 0.
- `fn generate(w: u32, h: u32, chain: []u8) void`: Fills levels 1 and down of `chain` (`chainBytes(w, h)`), whose first `w * h * 4` bytes hold level 0.

## `lcgf.image_tests` (src/assets/image_tests.zig)

Image tests over the files in testdata/: every colour type and bit depth PNG defines, plain and Adam7, with tRNS, in files compressed by Python's zlib (testdata/make.py).

## `lcgf.render_wgpu` (src/render/wgpu/wgpu.zig)

The wgpu back end: draws a `DrawList` with wgpu.

### Top level

- `BufferUse = enum { vertex, uniform }`
- `AttrFormat = enum { float32, float32x2, float32x4, uint32x4, unorm8x4 }`
- `Attr = struct { location: u32, offset: u32, format: AttrFormat }`
- `quad_stride: u32 = quad_shader.stride`: Per-instance vertex layout of `Quad`, from src/render/shaders/quad.zig `attrs` so the shader, the dumpers and this back end cannot drift.
- `Globals = @import("../null.zig").Globals`: Group 0, binding 0.
- `Glyphs = iface.Glyphs`
- `fn Renderer(comptime gpu: type) type`: The renderer without the canvas pipeline: none of its code in the binary, and canvas commands draw nothing (`Desc.canvas_rows` must be 0).
- `fn CanvasRenderer(comptime gpu: type) type`: The renderer with the canvas pipeline, for a binding that names its lcgf_render.gpu (`Binding`, `canvas`).
- `fn RendererWith(comptime gpu: type, comptime with_canvas: bool) type`

### `Binding` (struct)
The functions and types `Renderer(gpu)` uses.

- `types = [_][]const u8{ "Device", "Queue", "RenderPass", "Buffer", "Pipeline", "BindGroup", "TextureView", "Sampler", "TextureFormat" }`
- `functions = [_][]const u8{ "createBuffer", "destroyBuffer", "writeBuffer", "createPipeline", "globalsBindGroup", "textureBindGroup", "releaseBindGroup", "set…`

### `Renderer(..)` (generic struct)
The renderer without the canvas pipeline: none of its code in the binary, and canvas commands draw nothing (`Desc.canvas_rows` must be 0).


### `CanvasRenderer(..)` (generic struct)
The renderer with the canvas pipeline, for a binding that names its lcgf_render.gpu (`Binding`, `canvas`).


### `RendererWith(..)` (generic struct)

Fields: `desc`, `quads`, `glyph_quads`, `globals`, `globals_group`, `pipelines`, `material_count`, `textures`, `views`, `texture_count`, `glyphs`, `canvas`, `device`, `list`, `extent`

- `Queue = gpu.Queue`
- `Pass = gpu.RenderPass`
- `max_textures = 64`
- `max_materials = quad_shader.max_materials`
- `fn init(device: gpu.Device, desc: Desc, white: gpu.TextureView, sampler: gpu.Sampler) Self`: `white` is a 1x1 opaque white texture; it becomes `Texture.white`.
- `fn initFor(comptime use: @import("../../draw/features.zig").Usage, device: gpu.Device, desc: Desc, white: gpu.TextureView, sampler: gpu.Sampler) Self`
- `fn deinit(self: *Self) void`
- `fn addTexture(self: *Self, device: gpu.Device, view: gpu.TextureView, sampler: gpu.Sampler) types.Texture`: Texture in: makes a view usable as `Style.texture`.
- `fn setTexture(self: *Self, device: gpu.Device, texture: types.Texture, view: gpu.TextureView, sampler: gpu.Sampler) void`: Points an existing handle at another view: a render target recreated at a new size, an atlas that grew.
- `fn addMaterial(self: *Self, device: gpu.Device, wgsl: [:0]const u8) types.Material`: A quad pipeline with your fragment logic.
- `fn prepare(self: *Self, queue: gpu.Queue, draw: *const DrawList, glyphs: Glyphs, viewport: [2]f32, scale: f32) void`: Uploads the frame: one write per buffer.
- `fn preparePatched(self: *Self, queue: gpu.Queue, draw: *const DrawList, glyphs: Glyphs, viewport: [2]f32, scale: f32, dirty: anytype) void`: Upload the rows touched since the last frame on this buffer.
- `fn drawCanvas(self: *const Self, pass: gpu.RenderPass, k: u32) void`: Canvas command `k` (`iface.drawsCanvas`): one instanced draw per range, with the canvas pipeline, scissored to the node.
- `fn drawBatch(self: *const Self, pass: gpu.RenderPass, batch: draw_list.Batch) void`: One instanced draw call for a run of quads.
- `fn drawText(self: *const Self, pass: gpu.RenderPass, run: u32) void`: One instanced draw call for a text run's glyphs.
- `fn drawGlyphSpan(self: *const Self, pass: gpu.RenderPass, span: [2]u32) void`: The `span[1]` glyph quads from `span[0]`: consecutive text runs in one draw (`iface.drawUntilCustom`).
- `fn render(self: *const Self, pass: gpu.RenderPass, draw: *const DrawList) void`: Diagonal: the whole list, skipping custom draws.

### `RendererWith(..).Desc` (struct)

Fields: `format`, `sample_count`, `max_quads`, `max_glyphs`, `canvas_rows`, `canvas_verts`, `canvas_batches`, `canvas_draws`, `canvas_overrides`


## `lcgf.text_buffer` (src/text/edit/buffer.zig)

A text buffer with a gap, over bytes the caller owns.

### `Buffer` (struct)

Fields: `buf`, `gap`, `after`

- `Error = error{NoSpace}`
- `fn init(buf: []u8, initial: []const u8) Error!Buffer`: A buffer over `buf` holding a copy of `initial`.
- `fn len(b: *const Buffer) u32`
- `fn free(b: *const Buffer) u32`: Free bytes.
- `fn moveGap(b: *Buffer, at: u32) void`: Moves the gap so that it starts at text offset `at`.
- `fn insert(b: *Buffer, at: u32, bytes: []const u8) Error!void`: Inserts `bytes` at offset `at`.
- `fn delete(b: *Buffer, from: u32, to: u32) void`: Removes the text in `[from, to)`.
- `fn replace(b: *Buffer, from: u32, to: u32, bytes: []const u8) Error!void`: Replaces `[from, to)` with `bytes`.
- `fn text(b: *Buffer) []const u8`: The whole text as one slice, valid until the next edit.
- `fn copy(b: *const Buffer, from: u32, to: u32, out: []u8) []u8`: The text `[from, to)` copied to `out`, which must be large enough.

## `lcgf.text_moves` (src/text/edit/moves.zig)

Where the caret goes: byte offsets of the next and previous stop in a text.

### Top level

- `fn nextGrapheme(text: []const u8, at: u32) u32`: The grapheme boundary after `at`, or `text.len` at the end.
- `fn prevGrapheme(text: []const u8, at: u32) u32`: The grapheme boundary before `at`, or 0 at the start.
- `fn nextWord(text: []const u8, at: u32) u32`: The end of the word at or after `at` (Ctrl+Right): spaces are skipped, and a caret inside a word goes to its end.
- `fn prevWord(text: []const u8, at: u32) u32`: The start of the word before `at` (Ctrl+Left): spaces are skipped, and a caret inside a word goes to its start.
- `fn wordAt(text: []const u8, at: u32) [2]u32`: The word segment holding `at` (a double click): from its start to its end, spaces being a segment of their own.
- `fn lineStart(text: []const u8, at: u32) u32`: The start of the line (between newlines) holding `at`.
- `fn lineEnd(text: []const u8, at: u32) u32`: The end of the line holding `at`, before its newline.
- `fn visual(text: []const u8, at: u32, dir: i2, base: bidi.Direction, view: ?View, s: BidiScratch) ?u32`: Left and Right in visual order (UAX #9 L1, L2), for a line with right-to-left text in it: the caret at `at` moves to the stop next to it on screen in direction `dir` (+1 right, -1 left), not to the next offset.

### `View` (struct)
A paragraph laid out by src/text/layout/ (`lines.place` has run) from `text`.

Fields: `l`, `g`, `text`

- `fn home(v: View, at: u32) u32`: The first stop of the laid-out line holding the caret at `at` (Home).
- `fn end(v: View, at: u32) u32`: The last stop of the laid-out line holding the caret at `at` (End): before a trailing space or newline, which belong to the line but are not stops on it.
- `fn vertical(v: View, at: u32, dir: i2, goal: f32) u32`: The stop on the line `dir` lines (-1 up, +1 down) from the caret at `at`, nearest to x `goal` px; the text's start or end past the first or last line.
- `fn hit(v: View, px: f32, py: f32) u32`: The stop nearest a click at (`px`, `py`), px from the paragraph's top left.
- `fn caretRect(v: View, at: u32) types.Rect`: A caret at `at` as a 1 px wide rectangle the height of its line, px from the paragraph's top left.
- `fn x(v: View, at: u32) f32`: The caret's x at `at`, px from the paragraph's left edge.

### `BidiScratch` (struct)
Columns for `visual`, each as long as the longest paragraph it moves over in code points, plus one row (`mem.Carver.columns` carves them all at once).

Fields: `cps`, `orig`, `levels`, `cls`, `next`, `pair`, `start`, `first`, `glevel`, `order`, `inv`


## `lcgf.text_edit` (src/text/edit/edit.zig)

Text editing: a buffer and a caret driven by the keyboard input of one widget.

### Top level

- `field = Id.from("field")`
- `fn replay(ed: *Editor, src: []const u8) !Result`: Replays `src` into `ed` as the focused widget `field`, one `update` per frame.
- `fn replayWith(ed: *Editor, src: []const u8, opt: Replayed) !Result`: As `replay`, run as `opt` says.

### `Keys` (struct)
The keys the editor reads, as USB HID scancodes (`Input.Keys`).

Fields: `backspace`, `delete`, `enter`, `keypad_enter`, `left`, `right`, `up`, `down`, `home`, `end`, `word`, `clipboard`, `undo`, `redo`


### `Pointer` (struct)
The pointer over the editor this frame, in px from the laid-out paragraph's top left.

Fields: `at`, `press`, `held`, `double`, `shift`

- `fn of(it: *const Interaction, id: Id, origin: [2]f32, mods: Input.Mods) Pointer`: The pointer for widget `id` from this frame's `Interaction`, with the paragraph's top left at `origin` (logical px, as `Input.pointer`).

### `Mode` (struct)
How the editor treats its text (Iced 0.14 `text_input` and its `secure` flag, DVUI's `TextEntryWidget` `multiline` and `password_char`).

Fields: `single_line`, `password`, `bullet`, `read_only`


### `Context` (struct)
What `update` reads besides the keyboard.

Fields: `view`, `pointer`, `clipboard`, `copy_buf`, `composition`, `bidi`, `direction`


### `Result` (struct)
What one `update` did.

Fields: `changed`, `full`, `copy_full`, `submitted`


### `Editor` (struct)

Fields: `b`, `caret`, `anchor`, `goal`, `keys`, `mode`, `history`, `run`, `preedit`, `preedit_sel`

- `Run = enum(u8) { none, typing, deleting }`
- `fn init(buf: []u8, initial: []const u8) Buffer.Error!Editor`: An editor over `buf` holding `initial`, with the caret at its end.
- `fn selection(ed: *const Editor) [2]u32`: The selection as an ordered byte range.
- `fn selected(ed: *Editor) []const u8`: The selected text, valid until the next edit.
- `fn composing(ed: *const Editor) bool`
- `fn shownCaret(ed: *const Editor) u32`: Where the caret is drawn: the end of the IME's highlight while composing, else `caret`.
- `fn requestInput(ed: *Editor, out: *keyboard.TextInput, v: moves.View, origin: [2]f32) void`: Asks the host for text input (prototype/ui.zig `Platform.wants_text`, `ime_rect`), with the IME's window placed at the caret, or at the start of the preedit while composing so the window does not follow every keystroke.
- `fn display(ed: *Editor, out: []u8) []const u8`: What to lay out and draw: the text, or for a password one `mode.bullet` per grapheme, written to `out` (at least `bullet.len` bytes per byte of text).
- `fn toDisplay(ed: *Editor, at: u32) u32`: The offset in `display` of text offset `at` (a grapheme boundary).
- `fn fromDisplay(ed: *Editor, at: u32) u32`: The text offset of offset `at` in `display` (rounded down to a whole bullet).
- `fn place(ed: *Editor, at: u32) void`: Puts the caret at `at` with nothing selected.
- `fn text(ed: *Editor) []const u8`: The text, contiguous, valid until the next edit.
- `fn update(ed: *Editor, k: *const Keyboard, id: Id, cx: Context) Result`: Applies this frame's input for widget `id` from `k`: typed text replaces the selection or is inserted at the caret, Backspace and Delete remove the selection or the grapheme (with `keys.word`, the word) before and after the caret, Enter inserts a newline, the arrows, Home and End move the caret (with Shift, select), and select-all selects all.

### `Replayed` (struct)
How `replayWith` runs the editor.

Fields: `width`, `clipboard`, `copy_buf`, `compositions`, `bidi`


## `lcgf.text_history` (src/text/edit/history.zig)

Undo and redo: a history of edits in columns and bytes the caller owns.

### `History` (struct)

Fields: `c`, `bytes`, `len`, `top`, `next_group`

- `Kind = enum(u8) { insert, delete }`
- `Selection = struct { caret: u32, anchor: u32 }`: The caret and anchor a step leaves.
- `fn init(carver: *mem.Carver, rows: u32, cap: u32) History`: A history of up to `rows` edits keeping up to `cap` bytes of their text.
- `fn push(h: *History, kind: Kind, at: u32, n: u32, caret: u32, anchor: u32, join: bool) ?[]u8`: Records an edit of `n` bytes at `at`, made with the caret and anchor at `caret` and `anchor`, in the step of the previous row when `join`.
- `fn clear(h: *History) void`
- `fn undo(h: *History, b: *Buffer) ?Selection`: Reverts the last applied step in `b`.
- `fn redo(h: *History, b: *Buffer) ?Selection`: Applies the next undone step again.

### `History.Columns` (struct)

Fields: `kind`, `at`, `len`, `from`, `group`, `caret`, `anchor`

Editing tests across the lane's files: a recorded session replayed from a file, and invariants of the editor over many frames of random replayed input (caret and anchor on grapheme boundaries, the text valid UTF-8 and equal to a copy taken around the gap, undo back to the start and redo forward again).

Font fallback: which of the caller's faces draws each part of a text.

- `fn resolveClusters(chain: *const Chain, text: []const u8, runs: *Runs) error{NoSpace}!void`: Cluster-preserving fallback.

## `lcgf.shell_materials` (src/shell/materials.zig)

Native window material descriptors, separate from portable surface filters.

### Top level

- `WindowsBackdrop = enum(u32) { auto = 0, none = 1, window = 2, transient = 3, tabbed = 4 }`: DWM_SYSTEMBACKDROP_TYPE.
- `WindowsCorners = enum(u32) { system = 0, square = 1, round = 2, small_round = 3 }`
- `AppKitBlend = enum(isize) { behind_window = 0, within_window = 1 }`
- `AppKitState = enum(isize) { follows_window = 0, active = 1, inactive = 2 }`
- `fn windowsEffective(requested: WindowsBackdrop, reduce_transparency: ?bool) WindowsBackdrop`: Preference policy is separate from native submission.
- `fn appKitEffective(requested: ?AppKit, reduce_transparency: ?bool) ?AppKit`

### `WindowsColor` (union)

Fields: `color`

- `fn colorref(value: WindowsColor) u32`

NSVisualEffectMaterial semantic roles (AppKit), independent of the effective appearance.

Fields: `material`, `blending`, `state`, `emphasized`

The shell contract: what every platform shell (Win32, macOS, Wayland, X11, web, Android, iOS, bare metal, headless) provides so one host loop runs on any of them.

- `fn check(comptime S: type) void`: Compile error unless `S` declares everything in `required`.
- `fn run(shell: anytype, ctx: *context.Context, app: anytype) void`: Diagonal: the host loop for the common case.

### `Events` (struct)
Everything a shell reports for one frame.

Fields: `in`, `composition`, `files`

- `fn semantics(_: *const App, t: *const Tree, s: *a11y.Semantics, str: *Strings) void`: What the tree `build` made does not say to assistive technology: the exposure slider's range, and its name, which no text on screen gives (`Semantics.label`, added to the frame's strings `str`).

## `lcgf.runtime_signals` (src/runtime/signals.zig)

Signals: change counters for the runtime front end (tier 1).

### Top level

- `Signal = enum(u32) { unset = std.math.maxInt(u32), _ }`: A row of `Signals.version`.
- `fn Cell(comptime T: type) type`: A value paired with its signal.
- `fn Derived(comptime T: type) type`: A value computed from other signals (a memo), with its own signal and the stamp of the inputs it was last computed from.

### `Signals` (struct)

Fields: `version`, `len`, `cap`, `staged`, `depth`, `dirty`

- `fn init(carver: *mem.Carver, cap: u32) Signals`
- `fn create(s: *Signals) Signal`: A new signal at version 0.
- `fn bump(s: *Signals, sig: Signal, changed: bool) void`: Records that the data behind `sig` changed (or did not: `changed` is folded in arithmetically, so callers need no `if`).
- `fn begin(s: *Signals) void`: Opens a transaction: changes until the matching `commit` become visible together, as one update.
- `fn commit(s: *Signals) void`: Closes a transaction.
- `fn touch(s: *Signals, sig: Signal) void`: `bump` for data that is known to have changed.
- `fn stamp(s: *const Signals, deps: []const Signal) u64`: Fingerprint (FNV-1a) of a dependency list at its current versions.

### `Cell(..)` (generic struct)
A value paired with its signal.

Fields: `value`, `signal`

- `fn init(signals: *Signals, value: T) Self`
- `fn of(value: T) Self`: A cell whose signal is created on its first change.
- `fn get(self: *const Self) T`
- `fn set(self: *Self, signals: *Signals, value: T) void`: Stores `value`; the signal is bumped only if it differs from the old one.

### `Derived(..)` (generic struct)
A value computed from other signals (a memo), with its own signal and the stamp of the inputs it was last computed from.

Fields: `cell`, `stamp`, `computed`

- `fn init(signals: *Signals, deps: []const Signal, value: T) Self`
- `fn of(value: T) Self`: A memo holding `value` until its first `update`, which always computes.
- `fn get(self: *const Self) T`
- `fn signal(self: *const Self) Signal`
- `fn stale(self: *const Self, signals: *const Signals, deps: []const Signal) bool`: An input changed since the last `set`.
- `fn set(self: *Self, signals: *Signals, deps: []const Signal, value: T) void`: Stores a value computed from `deps` at their current versions.
- `fn update(self: *Self, signals: *Signals, deps: []const Signal, comptime func: anytype, args: anytype) T`: The value, recomputed as `@call(.auto, func, args)` first when an input in `deps` changed since the last computation (or there was none).

### `Effect` (struct)
A reaction to signals that the caller runs (saving a file, sending a message), at most once per frame.

Fields: `stamp`, `frame`

- `fn due(e: *Effect, signals: *const Signals, deps: []const Signal, frame: u32) bool`: True when the effect should run now; then it counts as run in `frame`.

## `lcgf.anims` (src/anim/anims.zig)

Anims: every spring in the UI as one table of columns, stepped together (tier 0).

### Top level

- `fn stepVector(comptime width: usize, c: anytype, i: usize, dt: f32) @Vector(width, f32)`: One full SIMD group or the exact final group of a compile-time table.
- `fn BoundSprings(comptime State: type) type`: A generated UI declares its animated bool and f32 target fields with `pub const springs = .{ .volume = Spring{}, .glow = Spring{} };`.

### `Spring` (struct)
A damped spring's constants.

Fields: `stiffness`, `damping`


### `Anims` (struct)

Fields: `len`, `cap`, `c`, `next`, `overflow`

- `fn init(carver: *mem.Carver, cap: u32) Anims`
- `fn reserve(a: *Anims, id: Id, initial: f32) error{NoSpace}!u32`: Checked reservation.
- `fn reserveScan(a: *Anims, id: Id, initial: f32) error{NoSpace}!u32`
- `fn slot(a: *Anims, id: Id, initial: f32) u32`: Legacy row-returning operation.
- `fn slotScan(a: *Anims, id: Id, initial: f32) u32`: `slot` by a SIMD scan only, with no branch on the ids: for callers that look springs up inside a per-row loop (`flip.apply`), where the branch census applies.
- `fn place(a: *Anims, k: u32, initial: f32, found: u32) u32`: The row for id `k`, given where it was `found` (or `none`): creates it if new.
- `fn land(a: *Anims, i: u32) void`: Puts row `i` at its target, at rest (reduced motion: the end state, no movement).
- `fn step(a: *Anims, dt: f32) f32`: SIMD.
- `fn sweep(a: *Anims, frame: u32) void`: Drops springs nobody asked for in frame `frame` (`seen` differs).

### `Anims.Columns` (struct)
`simd.rows(cap)` rows each.

Fields: `id`, `x`, `v`, `target`, `stiffness`, `damping`, `seen`, `node`


### `BoundSprings(..)` (generic struct)
A generated UI declares its animated bool and f32 target fields with `pub const springs = .{ .volume = Spring{}, .glow = Spring{} };`.

Fields: `x`, `v`, `target`

- `count = n`
- `fn value(s: *const Self, state: *const State, comptime name: []const u8) f32`: A declared field's position, or the raw target for a field drawn directly.
- `fn pending(s: *const Self, state: *const State) bool`
- `fn frame(s: *Self, state: *const State, dt: f32, first: bool, reduced_motion: bool) void`: The runtime frame steps the previous targets, then receives this frame's target.

## `lcgf.tweens` (src/anim/tweens.zig)

Tweens: values that move from where they are to a new target over a fixed duration along an easing curve, all stepped together (tier 0).

### Top level

- `Ease = enum(u8) { linear, in_quad, out_quad, in_out, in_cubic, out_cubic }`: Easing curves, as rows of `ease_table`.

### `Transition` (struct)
How a style property moves to a new value (`Frame.transition`), after Iced 0.14's `Animation` (`quick`, `easing`): a duration and a curve.

Fields: `seconds`, `ease`


### `Tweens` (struct)

Fields: `len`, `cap`, `c`, `next`, `overflow`

- `fn init(carver: *mem.Carver, cap: u32) Tweens`
- `fn reserve(t: *Tweens, id: Id, initial: f32) error{NoSpace}!u32`: Checked reservation.
- `fn slot(t: *Tweens, id: Id, initial: f32) u32`: Legacy row-returning operation.
- `fn retarget(t: *Tweens, i: u32, to: f32, duration: f32, ease: Ease) void`: Points row `i` at `to`.
- `fn land(t: *Tweens, i: u32) void`: Ends row `i`'s leg now, at its target (reduced motion: the end state, no movement).
- `fn step(t: *Tweens, dt: f32) f32`: SIMD.
- `fn sweep(t: *Tweens, frame: u32) void`: Drops tweens nobody asked for in frame `frame`, by stream compaction (`simd.compact`).

### `Tweens.Columns` (struct)
`simd.rows(cap)` rows each.

Fields: `id`, `x`, `from`, `to`, `elapsed`, `duration`, `ease_a`, `ease_b`, `ease_c`, `seen`, `node`


## `lcgf.keyframes` (src/anim/keyframes.zig)

Keyframes: a value that passes through a list of stops at given times, each segment along its own easing curve (tier 0).

### Top level

- `fn sample(keys: []const Key, t: f32) f32`: Value of `keys` at `t` seconds: the first value before the first key, the last after the last key.
- `fn duration(keys: []const Key) f32`: Time of the last key: how long the sequence plays.

### `Key` (struct)
An extern struct: C, C++ and Rust pass a list of them as it is (`lcgf_key`).

Fields: `at`, `value`, `ease`


## `lcgf.flip` (src/anim/flip.zig)

Layout animation (tier 0): a node whose laid-out position changes between frames slides there on a spring instead of jumping.

### Top level

- `fn apply(t: *Tree, prev: *const Tree, fl: *Flips, an: *Anims, frame: u32) f32`: Moves every registered subtree of `t` (positions laid out by `place`) to where its springs show it, and points the springs at the new layout.

### `Flips` (struct)
The nodes asked to animate their layout this frame.

Fields: `len`, `cap`, `c`, `overflow`

- `fn init(carver: *mem.Carver, cap: u32) Flips`
- `fn reserve(fl: *Flips, node: u32, id: Id, spring: Spring) error{NoSpace}!void`: Checked registration.
- `fn add(fl: *Flips, node: u32, id: Id, spring: Spring) void`: Compatibility operation; failed registration is ignored and observable in overflow.

### `Flips.Columns` (struct)

Fields: `node`, `id`, `stiffness`, `damping`, `delta`


## `lcgf.anim_values` (src/anim/values.zig)

Values: what `Frame.animate` and `Frame.tween` can move.

### Top level

- `fn Of(comptime T: type) type`: The type a target of type `T` animates as: an untyped number literal moves as an f32.
- `fn count(comptime T: type) comptime_int`: How many numbers a value of `T` is made of; a compile error naming the first part of `T` that is not a number (a bool, a pointer, an optional, a union).
- `fn flatten(comptime T: type, v: T) [count(T)]f32`: The components of `v`, in order.
- `fn build(comptime T: type, xs: *const [count(T)]f32) T`: The value made of `xs`, in `flatten`'s order.
- `fn intOf(x: f32, lo: i64, hi: i64) i64`: An animated number as an integer of the range [lo, hi]: rounded half away from zero, clamped in f64 (where the ends of every type up to 32 bits are exact: an f32 of maxInt(u32) is 2^32), NaN to 0.

### `Of(..)` (generic struct)
The type a target of type `T` animates as: an untyped number literal moves as an f32.


## `lcgf.shell_headless` (src/shell/headless.zig)

The headless shell: a shell with no window, for tests.

### `Headless` (struct)

Fields: `io`, `replay`, `replay_buffers`, `fb`, `gfx`, `font`, `glyphs`, `background`, `scale`, `applied`, `polls`, `presents`, `waits`, `waited`, `woken`, `wakes`, `linger`, `err`

- `fn init(io: std.Io, recording: []const u8, width: u32, height: u32, buf: Buffers) Headless`: A shell replaying `recording` into a `width` x `height` framebuffer (physical pixels).
- `fn poll(h: *Headless, ev: *Events) bool`: The next recorded frame.
- `fn apply(h: *Headless, p: *const Platform) void`
- `fn present(h: *Headless, out: *const Output) void`: Draws the frame into `fb`: clear, then every command but the custom ones.
- `fn wait(h: *Headless, seconds: f32, pace: Pace) void`: While recorded frames remain, returns at once: the next one is the next event, and it is already here.
- `fn wake(h: *Headless) void`: Thread-safe: ends a `wait` in progress, and has the next `poll` give a frame.

### `Headless.Buffers` (struct)
Caller-owned storage.

Fields: `replay`, `pixels`, `glyphs`, `first`, `count`


## `lcgf.shell_baremetal_flush` (src/shell/baremetal/flush.zig)

The bare-metal flush interface: how a frame reaches a display with no GPU and no OS, after LVGL's flush driver model (docs.lvgl.io 9.5, "Requirements": a draw buffer of the whole screen or of a few lines, and a flush that copies one area of it to the panel).

### Top level

- `fn flush(d: *const Display, gfx: anytype, draw: *const DrawList, background: Color, board: anytype) u32`: Draws `draw` with `gfx`, a `*const sw.SoftwareOf(kinds)` already `prepare`d for this frame, and flushes it to `board`, band by band and damage rect by damage rect: each area is cleared to `background`, drawn with every command but the custom ones (the host draws those), and flushed.

### `Area` (struct)
A rectangle of the display in physical pixels, x0 and y0 inclusive, x1 and y1 exclusive.

Fields: `x0`, `y0`, `x1`, `y1`

- `fn w(a: Area) u32`
- `fn h(a: Area) u32`

### `Display` (struct)
The panel and the caller's draw buffer.

Fields: `width`, `height`, `rows`, `stride`, `pixels`, `format`

- `fn len(width: u32, rows: u32) usize`: Pixels a buffer of `rows` rows of a `width` wide display needs.
- `fn init(pixels: []u32, width: u32, height: u32, rows: u32, format: sw.Format) Display`

## `lcgf.shell_baremetal_input` (src/shell/baremetal/input.zig)

Touch and encoder input for bare-metal boards, after LVGL's input device model (docs.lvgl.io 9.5, "Requirements"): the board reads its devices once per frame into a `Sample`, plain data, and `Reader` turns samples into the `Input` the core reads.

### Top level

- `max_steps = 8`: Detents one frame turns into key events; a faster turn is clamped.
- `enter: u8 = 0x28`: The scancode of the encoder's button: Enter (USB HID), what `widgets/keys.zig` activates on.
- `fn sample(in: *const Input) Sample`: The devices a recorded frame stands for: the first finger, else the pointer with the left button, as the touch (scaled to physical pixels); the vertical wheel, rounded to whole notches, as encoder detents; the middle button as the encoder's button.

### `Touch` (struct)
One finger on the panel, in physical pixels.

Fields: `pos`, `down`


### `Encoder` (struct)
A rotary encoder: detents turned since the last sample (positive moves focus forward), and whether its button is held.

Fields: `steps`, `down`


### `Sample` (struct)
What a board's devices read for one frame.

Fields: `touch`, `encoder`


### `Reader` (struct)
Turns samples into `Input`, keeping the little it needs from the frame before.

Fields: `keymap`, `touched`, `at`, `pushed`, `events`

- `fn read(r: *Reader, s: Sample, in: *Input) void`: Writes `s` into `in`: the pointer, the left button, Enter in `keys`, and this frame's key events.

## `lcgf.shell_baremetal_tick` (src/shell/baremetal/tick.zig)

The tick source: the clock of a board with no OS, after LVGL's `lv_tick_inc` (docs.lvgl.io 9.5, "Requirements").

### Top level

- `fn millis(seconds: f32) u32`: Milliseconds a wait of `seconds` lasts, rounded up; `inf` (and anything from 4e9 ms on) is `maxInt(u32)`, 49.7 days.

### `Tick` (struct)
Milliseconds since start, wrapping after 2^32 (49.7 days); `Clock` and the shell's waits use differences only, which survive the wrap.

Fields: `ms`

- `fn inc(t: *Tick, period: u32) void`: From the timer interrupt: `period` milliseconds passed.
- `fn now(t: *const Tick) u32`

### `Clock` (struct)
Turns tick readings into `Input.time` and `Input.dt`.

Fields: `last`, `time`

- `fn init(now: u32) Clock`
- `fn step(c: *Clock, now: u32, in: *Input) void`: Sets `in.time` and `in.dt` for a frame read at tick `now`.

## `lcgf.shell_baremetal_shell` (src/shell/baremetal/shell.zig)

The bare-metal shell: the shell contract (src/shell/iface.zig) on a board with no OS, no window system and no GPU.

### Top level

- `fn checkBoard(comptime Board: type) void`: Compile error unless `Board` declares what the shell calls.
- `fn Baremetal(comptime Board: type) type`
- `fn BaremetalOf(comptime Board: type, comptime kinds: []const @import("").Kind) type`: The shell drawing with only the feature kinds in `kinds` compiled in (`sw.SoftwareOf`): a microcontroller build lists the kinds its UI uses and leaves the other shading loops out.

### `BaremetalOf(..)` (generic struct)
The shell drawing with only the feature kinds in `kinds` compiled in (`sw.SoftwareOf`): a microcontroller build lists the kinds its UI uses and leaves the other shading loops out.

Fields: `board`, `tick`, `display`, `scale`, `gfx`, `font`, `font_images`, `glyphs`, `reader`, `clock`, `background`, `frame_ms`, `polled_at`, `applied`, `woken`, `polls`, `presents`, `flushed`

- `Buffers = struct { pixels: []u32 = &.{}, glyphs: []Quad, first: []u32, count: []u32 }`: Caller-owned storage.
- `fn init(board: *Board, clock: *const tick.Tick, width: u32, height: u32, rows: u32, format: sw.Format, scale: f32, buf: Buffers) Self`: A shell on a `width` x `height` panel (physical pixels) drawn through a buffer of `rows` rows (`height` for a whole framebuffer).
- `fn poll(s: *Self, ev: *Events) bool`: One frame of the board's devices and the clock.
- `fn apply(s: *Self, p: *const Platform) void`
- `fn present(s: *Self, out: *const Output) void`: Draws the frame's damage through the draw buffer and flushes it to the board.
- `fn wait(s: *Self, seconds: f32, pace: Pace) void`: Idles the board until an input event, a `wake`, or `seconds` have passed (`inf`: no time limit).
- `fn wake(s: *Self) void`: Interrupt- and thread-safe: ends a `wait` in progress, or the next one at once.

## `lcgf.shell_baremetal_sim` (src/shell/baremetal/sim.zig)

The simulator board: runs a bare-metal application on the desktop, as LVGL's desktop simulators do, with the board's devices replaced by a recording and its panel by memory.

### `Sim` (struct)

Fields: `replay`, `replay_buffers`, `clock`, `panel`, `sent`, `err`

- `fn init(recording: []const u8, buffers: Replay.Buffers, panel: sw.Framebuffer) Sim`: A simulator replaying `recording` onto `panel`, which has the panel's size.
- `fn read(b: *Sim) ?input.Sample`
- `fn flush(b: *Sim, a: flushing.Area, pixels: []const u32, stride: u32) void`
- `fn idle(_: *Sim) bool`: The next recorded frame is already here: an input event, at once.

## `lcgf.shell_web` (src/shell/web/web.zig)

The web shell: a `<canvas>` in a browser page.

### Top level

- `fn Web(comptime host: type) type`
- `fn replay(shell: anytype, ctx: *context.Context, app: *example.App, recording: []const u8) !u32`: Plays a recording (src/core/input.zig `Replay`) into the shell the way shell.js forwards DOM events: what changed since the previous frame becomes one call each, then one animation frame.

### `Web(..)` (generic struct)

Fields: `in`, `last`, `open`, `woken`, `polls`, `wakes`, `cursor`, `ime`, `composing`, `composition`, `pixels`, `text`, `text_len`, `events`, `event_len`, `contacts`, `contact_len`, `lifting`, `gone`, `dropped`

- `max_text = 256`
- `max_events = 64`
- `max_contacts = 16`
- `WheelMode = enum(u32) { pixel, line, page }`: `wheel`'s `mode`, the DOM's WheelEvent.deltaMode.
- `fn resize(s: *Self, css_w: f32, css_h: f32, dpr: f32, px_w: u32, px_h: u32) void`: The canvas's size in CSS pixels (logical, `Input.viewport`), the device pixel ratio (`Input.scale`) and its backing store in device pixels (`pixels`).
- `fn pointer(s: *Self, x: f32, y: f32) void`: The pointer moved to (x, y), CSS pixels from the canvas's top left.
- `fn leave(s: *Self) void`: The pointer left the canvas.
- `fn button(s: *Self, b: u32, down: bool) void`: Mouse button `button` (DOM numbering: 0 left, 1 middle, 2 right) went down or up.
- `fn mods(s: *Self, m: Input.Mods) void`: Modifier keys held, from any event's shiftKey, ctrlKey, altKey and metaKey.
- `fn key(s: *Self, kind: Input.Event.Kind, code: u8, m: Input.Mods) void`: A key went down (`.press`, `.repeat`) or up (`.release`).
- `fn compose(s: *Self, bytes: []const u8, range: [2]u32) void`: The IME's composition changed: `bytes` (empty when it ended), with the highlighted clause or caret at byte range `range`.
- `fn blur(s: *Self) void`: The canvas lost focus: keys held now will never see their key-up, so none is held.
- `fn typed(s: *Self, bytes: []const u8, m: Input.Mods) void`: Text typed (UTF-8), one `.text` event.
- `fn wheel(s: *Self, dx: f32, dy: f32, mode: WheelMode) void`: A wheel event's deltas.
- `fn contact(s: *Self, c: Input.Contact) void`: A finger or pen (PointerEvent with pointerType touch or pen) went down, moved or hovers: its row is added or updated.
- `fn lift(s: *Self, id: u32) void`: Contact `id` lifted or was cancelled: reported with `down = false` in the next frame, then removed.
- `fn time(s: *Self, seconds: f64) void`: The animation frame's timestamp, seconds.
- `fn close(s: *Self) void`
- `fn poll(s: *Self, ev: *Events) bool`: What arrived since the last poll.
- `fn apply(s: *Self, p: *const Platform) void`: Applies a frame's effects.
- `fn present(_: *Self, out: *const Output) void`
- `fn wait(_: *Self, seconds: f32, pace: Pace) void`: Asks the page for the next animation frame: in `seconds` (shell.js: a timer, or at once for 0), never for inf (idle: the next event asks for one).
- `fn wake(s: *Self) void`: The next `poll` gives a frame even with no event, and the page runs one soon.
