- `xkb_per_client_flags_size = 28`
- `fake_input_size = 36`
- `fn get(comptime T: type, msg: []const u8, at: usize) T`: Little-endian reads at a byte offset of a message.
- `fn pad4(n: usize) usize`: Bytes to the next multiple of 4.
- `fn cookie(file: []const u8, number: u32) Auth`: The MIT-MAGIC-COOKIE-1 for display `number` in the bytes of an Xauthority file, or none.
- `fn setup(b: *Buf, auth: Auth) void`: The setup request: byte order, protocol 11.0, and the authorization.
- `fn setupSize(auth: Auth) usize`
- `fn resourceDpi(resources: []const u8) ?f32`: `Xft.dpi` from the resource database string (the root's RESOURCE_MANAGER property), the DPI desktops set for scaling; null when it has none.
- `fn scale(xft_dpi: ?f32, width_px: u16, width_mm: u16) f32`: Physical pixels per logical pixel.
- `fn messageSize(head: *const [32]u8) usize`: Every message is 32 bytes, except replies and generic events, which say how many 4-byte words follow.
- `fn eventWindow(msg: []const u8) u32`: The window an event is about, or 0 for events that name none (errors, replies, mapping).
- `fn createWindow(b: *Buf, id: u32, parent: u32, w: u16, h: u16, events: u32) void`: An InputOutput window of the parent's depth and visual, with no background (the shell draws every pixel, so the server clearing it first would only flicker) and `events`.
- `fn changeCursor(b: *Buf, window: u32, cursor: u32) void`: Sets the window's cursor (`Attr.cursor`); 0 is the parent's.
- `fn moveWindow(b: *Buf, window: u32, x: i16, y: i16) void`: ConfigureWindow moving `window` to (x, y) in its parent.
- `fn onWindow(b: *Buf, opcode: u8, window: u32) void`: A request whose only field is a window: MapWindow, DestroyWindow.
- `fn internAtomSize(name: []const u8) usize`
- `fn internAtom(b: *Buf, name: []const u8) void`: Asks for the atom of `name`, creating it.
- `fn changePropertySize(data_len: usize) usize`
- `fn changeProperty(b: *Buf, window: u32, property: u32, kind: u32, format: u8, data: []const u8) void`: Replaces `property` of `window` with `data`, items of `format` bits (8, 16 or 32).
- `fn appendProperty(b: *Buf, window: u32, property: u32, kind: u32, format: u8, data: []const u8) void`: `changeProperty` in Append mode: `data` goes after what the property holds.
- `fn changePropertyHead(b: *Buf, window: u32, property: u32, kind: u32, format: u8, len: usize) void`: ChangeProperty without its data, for data too large for the output buffer: the caller sends the `len` bytes and the padding to 4 right after.
- `fn setSelectionOwner(b: *Buf, owner: u32, selection: u32) void`
- `fn convertSelection(b: *Buf, requestor: u32, selection: u32, target: u32, property: u32) void`: Asks the owner of `selection` to put it, as `target`, in `property` of `requestor`; a SelectionNotify says when it has.
- `fn selectionNotify(requestor: u32, selection: u32, target: u32, property: u32) [32]u8`: The SelectionNotify an owner sends the requestor; `property` 0 refuses.
- `fn getProperty(b: *Buf, window: u32, property: u32, delete: bool, words: u32) void`: Reads up to `words` 4-byte words of `property` (any type).
- `fn changeEventMask(b: *Buf, window: u32, mask: u32) void`: Replaces this client's event mask on `window` (used on the root, for PropertyNotify).
- `fn deleteProperty(b: *Buf, window: u32, property: u32) void`
- `fn randrSelectInput(b: *Buf, randr: u8, window: u32) void`: RandR SelectInput (minor 4): ScreenChangeNotify events about `window`'s screen, whose code is the extension's first event.
- `fn randrSetScreenSize(b: *Buf, randr: u8, window: u32, w: u16, h: u16, mm_w: u32, mm_h: u32) void`: RandR SetScreenSize (minor 7): the screen's size in pixels and millimetres.
- `fn openFontSize(name: []const u8) usize`
- `fn openFont(b: *Buf, id: u32, name: []const u8) void`
- `fn createCursor(b: *Buf, id: u32, source: u32, mask: u32) void`: Core CreateCursor: a depth-one bitmap and mask, black on white, hotspot at (0,0).
- `fn clearBitmap(b: *Buf, bitmap: u32, gc: u32) void`: One zero bit, padded to the server's 32-bit scanline unit.
- `fn createGlyphCursor(b: *Buf, id: u32, font: u32, glyph: u16) void`: A cursor from glyph `glyph` of the core "cursor" font, its mask the next glyph, black on white.
- `fn xfixesQueryVersion(b: *Buf, xfixes: u8) void`: XFIXES QueryVersion 4.0 (minor 0), required before other XFIXES requests; has a reply.
- `fn xfixesGetCursorImage(b: *Buf, xfixes: u8) void`: XFIXES GetCursorImage (minor 4): the cursor shown now.
- `fn getSelectionOwner(b: *Buf, selection: u32) void`: The owner window of `selection` is at byte 8 of the reply (0: none).
- `fn clientMessage(window: u32, kind: u32, format: u8, data: []const u8) [32]u8`: A ClientMessage event of `format` 8 (20 bytes of `data`, zero-padded) or 32 (five u32).
- `fn translateCoordinates(b: *Buf, src: u32, dst: u32, x: i16, y: i16) void`: Where (x, y) of `src` is in `dst`: the reply has it at bytes 12 and 14.
- `fn uriListPaths(list: []const u8, names: []u8, ends: []u32) usize`: The local paths of a text/uri-list (RFC 2483): one URI per line, `#` lines are comments, only `file:` URIs on this host (`file:///p`, `file://localhost/p`, `file:/p`) are kept, and %XX escapes are decoded.
- `fn sendEvent(b: *Buf, window: u32, mask: u32, event: *const [32]u8) void`: Sends the 32-byte `event` to `window` (to its owner when `mask` is 0).
- `fn setInputFocus(b: *Buf, window: u32) void`: Gives `window` the keyboard focus (revert to the root; CurrentTime).
- `fn getInputFocus(b: *Buf) void`: Has a reply and no effect: a round trip, after which the server has handled every earlier request.
- `fn createPixmap(b: *Buf, id: u32, drawable: u32, width: u16, height: u16, depth: u8) void`
- `fn copyArea(b: *Buf, src: u32, dst: u32, gc: u32, width: u16, height: u16) void`: Copies the complete frame from the pixmap to the window, origin to origin.
- `fn copyAreaRect(b: *Buf, src: u32, dst: u32, gc: u32, x: i16, y: i16, width: u16, height: u16) void`: Copies the same physical rectangle from the backing pixmap to the window.
- `fn createGc(b: *Buf, id: u32, drawable: u32) void`: A graphics context for PutImage, without GraphicsExpose events.
- `fn putImage(b: *Buf, drawable: u32, gc: u32, w: u16, h: u16, x: i16, y: i16, depth: u8) void`: The header of a ZPixmap PutImage of `w` x `h` pixels of 32 bits at (x, y); the caller sends the `w * h * 4` pixel bytes right after it.
- `fn getImage(b: *Buf, drawable: u32, x: i16, y: i16, w: u16, h: u16) void`: Reads `w` x `h` ZPixmap pixels back; the reply's data starts at byte 32.
- `fn queryExtensionSize(name: []const u8) usize`
- `fn queryExtension(b: *Buf, name: []const u8) void`: Whether the server has extension `name`.
- `fn getKeyboardMapping(b: *Buf, first: u8, count: u8) void`: The keysyms of `count` keycodes from `first`.
- `fn xkbUseExtension(b: *Buf, xkb: u8) void`: XKB UseExtension 1.0 (minor 0): required before any other XKB request.
- `fn xkbDetectableAutoRepeat(b: *Buf, xkb: u8) void`: XKB PerClientFlags (minor 21) turning on DetectableAutoRepeat for the core keyboard: a held key then repeats as presses alone, not as release and press pairs.
- `fn fakeInput(b: *Buf, xtest: u8, kind: u8, detail: u8, root: u32, x: i16, y: i16) void`: XTEST FakeInput (minor opcode 2): the server acts as if the device did it.

### `Buf` (struct)
A request being written into the connection's output buffer.

Fields: `bytes`, `len`

- `fn int(b: *Buf, comptime T: type, v: T) void`
- `fn zeros(b: *Buf, n: usize) void`
- `fn str(b: *Buf, s: []const u8) void`: `s`, then zeros to a multiple of 4.
- `fn head(b: *Buf, opcode: u8, data: u8, words: usize) void`: The 4-byte request header: opcode, one byte of data, length in 4-byte units.

### `Display` (struct)
`DISPLAY` parsed: only local displays (`:N` or `:N.S`, optionally `unix:N`), reached through the socket `/tmp/.X11-unix/XN`.

Fields: `number`, `screen`

- `fn parse(name: []const u8) error{UnsupportedDisplay}!Display`
- `fn path(d: Display, buf: []u8) []const u8`: The socket path into `buf`.

### `Auth` (struct)
An authorization to send in the setup: a protocol name and its data.

Fields: `name`, `data`


### `Setup` (struct)
What the shell keeps from a successful setup reply.

Fields: `id_base`, `id_mask`, `max_request`, `min_keycode`, `max_keycode`, `root`, `root_visual`, `root_depth`, `width_px`, `height_px`, `width_mm`, `height_mm`

- `Error = error{ SetupRefused, SetupTruncated, UnsupportedVisual }`
- `fn parse(r: []const u8, screen: u32) Error!Setup`: Parses the full reply `r` (8-byte header plus `get(u16, r, 6) * 4` bytes), for `screen`.

### `Pointer` (struct)
Pointer and key events: position in the event window, and the modifier and button state.

Fields: `x`, `y`, `state`

- `fn of(msg: []const u8) Pointer`

### `State` (struct)
Modifier and button bits of an event's `state`.

- `shift = 1`
- `lock = 2`
- `control = 4`
- `mod1 = 8`: Mod1: Alt on every common keymap.
- `mod2 = 16`: Mod2: Num Lock on every common keymap.
- `mod4 = 64`: Mod4: Super on every common keymap.
- `button1 = 0x100`
- `button2 = 0x200`
- `button3 = 0x400`

### `op` (struct)

- `create_window = 1`
- `change_window_attributes = 2`
- `destroy_window = 4`
- `map_window = 8`
- `configure_window = 12`
- `intern_atom = 16`
- `change_property = 18`
- `delete_property = 19`
- `get_property = 20`
- `set_selection_owner = 22`
- `get_selection_owner = 23`
- `convert_selection = 24`
- `send_event = 25`
- `set_input_focus = 42`
- `get_input_focus = 43`
- `translate_coordinates = 40`
- `open_font = 45`
- `create_pixmap = 53`
- `free_pixmap = 54`
- `create_gc = 55`
- `free_gc = 60`
- `copy_area = 62`
- `put_image = 72`
- `get_image = 73`
- `create_cursor = 93`
- `create_glyph_cursor = 94`
- `query_extension = 98`
- `get_keyboard_mapping = 101`

### `Attr` (struct)
Window attribute bits, in the order their values follow (`createWindow`).

- `back_pixmap = 0x1`
- `event_mask = 0x800`
- `cursor = 0x4000`

### `Mask` (struct)
Event mask bits.

- `key_press = 0x1`
- `key_release = 0x2`
- `button_press = 0x4`
- `button_release = 0x8`
- `enter_window = 0x10`
- `leave_window = 0x20`
- `pointer_motion = 0x40`
- `exposure = 0x8000`
- `structure_notify = 0x20000`
- `focus_change = 0x200000`
- `property_change = 0x400000`

## `lcgf.shell_x11_keys` (src/shell/x11/keys.zig)

Keys of the X11 shell: X keycodes to the USB HID scancodes `Input.Keys` and the widgets use, and keycodes plus modifier state to keysyms and typed text, from the server's keyboard mapping.

### Top level

- `fn hid(keycode: u8) u8`: HID scancode of X keycode `keycode`.
- `fn keycodeOf(code: u8) u8`: X keycode of HID scancode `code`, or 0 (for tests that fake key presses).
- `fn codepoint(sym: u32) u21`: The code point a keysym types, or 0 for keys that type nothing (arrows, modifiers, function keys, Return and Tab, which widgets read as keys) and for legacy non-Latin-1 keysyms.

### `Keymap` (struct)
The first four keysyms of every keycode: group 1 levels 1 and 2, group 2 levels 1 and 2.

Fields: `syms`

- `fn load(k: *Keymap, reply: []const u8, first: u8) void`: Fills the map from a GetKeyboardMapping reply for keycodes `first` onwards.
- `fn keysym(k: *const Keymap, keycode: u8, state: u16) u32`: The keysym of `keycode` under the modifier `state` of a key event (core protocol rules).

## `lcgf.shell_x11_xim` (src/shell/x11/xim.zig)

The X Input Method protocol (XIM 1.0, "The Input Method Protocol", X Consortium), as far as the X11 shell's IME client needs it: the messages it sends and the fields of those it reads, and the decoding of COMPOUND_TEXT commits.

### Top level

- `style_preedit_position = 0x0004`: Over-the-spot: the input method draws the composition itself, next to `spotLocation`, and sends only the committed text.
- `style_status_nothing = 0x0400`
- `create_ic_size = 4 + 4 + 8 * 3 + 4 + 8`
- `set_spot_size = 8 + 4 + 8 + 4`
- `ic_size = 8`
- `forward_event_size = 4 + 8 + 32`
- `fn size(head: []const u8) usize`: Bytes of a whole message (header and body) whose first 4 bytes are `head`.
- `fn connect(b: *Buf) void`: XIM_CONNECT: little-endian, protocol 1.0, no authentication.
- `fn open(b: *Buf, locale: []const u8) void`: XIM_OPEN for `locale` (an STR: length byte and the name).
- `fn encodingNegotiation(b: *Buf, im: u16) void`: XIM_ENCODING_NEGOTIATION offering COMPOUND_TEXT, the encoding every server has.
- `fn openReply(msg: []const u8) struct { im: u16, attrs: Attrs }`: The input method id and the IC attribute ids from an XIM_OPEN_REPLY message.
- `fn createIc(b: *Buf, im: u16, a: Attrs, window: u32, x: i16, y: i16) void`: XIM_CREATE_IC for over-the-spot input in `window`, with the spot at (x, y) in its pixels.
- `fn setSpot(b: *Buf, im: u16, ic: u16, a: Attrs, x: i16, y: i16) void`: XIM_SET_IC_VALUES moving the spot to (x, y).
- `fn onIc(b: *Buf, major: u8, im: u16, ic: u16) void`: A message whose body is the input method and context ids: XIM_SET_IC_FOCUS, XIM_UNSET_IC_FOCUS, XIM_SYNC_REPLY.
- `fn forwardEvent(b: *Buf, im: u16, ic: u16, flag: u16, ev: []const u8) void`: XIM_FORWARD_EVENT with the X event `ev` (32 bytes) and the top 16 bits of its serial.
- `fn compoundToUtf8(ct: []const u8, out: []u8) usize`: COMPOUND_TEXT to UTF-8 in `out`, returning the length.

### `op` (struct)

- `connect = 1`
- `connect_reply = 2`
- `err = 20`
- `open = 30`
- `open_reply = 31`
- `register_triggerkeys = 34`
- `set_event_mask = 37`
- `encoding_negotiation = 38`
- `encoding_negotiation_reply = 39`
- `create_ic = 50`
- `create_ic_reply = 51`
- `set_ic_values = 54`
- `set_ic_values_reply = 55`
- `set_ic_focus = 58`
- `unset_ic_focus = 59`
- `forward_event = 60`
- `sync = 61`
- `sync_reply = 62`
- `commit = 63`

### `Attrs` (struct)
IC attribute ids the server gave in XIM_OPEN_REPLY; `missing` where it has none.

Fields: `input_style`, `client_window`, `focus_window`, `preedit_attributes`, `spot_location`

- `missing = 0xffff`
- `fn complete(a: Attrs) bool`: Whether over-the-spot input can be asked for.

### `Commit` (struct)
What an XIM_COMMIT carries: the flags, the committed COMPOUND_TEXT and a keysym, either of which may be absent.

Fields: `flag`, `text`, `keysym`

- `synchronous = 1`
- `fn parse(msg: []const u8) Commit`

## `lcgf.shell_win32_translate` (src/shell/win32/translate.zig)

Win32 window messages as `Events`: the part of the Win32 shell (win32.zig) that touches no OS, so it builds and is tested on any host.

### Top level

- `Msg = struct { msg: u32, wparam: usize = 0, lparam: isize = 0 }`: One window message, as the window procedure receives it.
- `dpi_default = 96`: The DPI at which one logical pixel is one physical pixel (`USER_DEFAULT_SCREEN_DPI`).
- `infinite: u32 = 0xffff_ffff`: `INFINITE`: a wait with no timeout.
- `fn lparam(lo: i32, hi: i32) isize`: `MAKELPARAM(lo, hi)`: two 16-bit words, as most mouse and size messages pack them.
- `fn cursorExact(cursor: Cursor) bool`
- `fn appearance(apps_use_light_theme: ?u32, accent_color: ?u32) Input.Appearance`: The system's light or dark mode and accent colour, as Settings > Personalization sets them, from the registry values the shell reads (null when missing): `AppsUseLightTheme` (0 is dark; missing is light, as Windows before 10 1809 shows apps) under HKCU\Software\Microsoft\Windows\CurrentVersion\Themes\Personalize, and `AccentColor` (0xAABBGGRR) under HKCU\Software\Microsoft\Windows\DWM.
- `fn waitMs(seconds: f32, pace: Pace, hz: u32) u32`: Milliseconds `wait` blocks for (`MsgWaitForMultipleObjectsEx`), for a wait of `seconds` (`scheduler.wake`) at `pace` on a display refreshing `hz` times a second.
- `fn physicalRect(r: Rect, scale: f32) [4]i32`: `r` (logical pixels) in physical pixels at `scale`, rounded out to whole pixels: left, top, right, bottom, for the IME's candidate and composition windows.
- `fn clipboardText(utf8: []const u8, out: []u16) ?[]u16`: `utf8` as clipboard text (`CF_UNICODETEXT`): UTF-16 with `\r\n` line ends and a NUL, in `out`, which needs `clipboardUnits(utf8.len)` units.
- `fn clipboardUnits(len: usize) usize`: UTF-16 units `clipboardText` may need for `len` bytes of UTF-8: one per byte at most, two for a `\n`, and the NUL.

### `Wm` (enum)
The window messages the translator reads, by their winuser.h values.


### `Translator` (struct)

Fields: `in`, `client`, `closed`, `text`, `events`, `n_text`, `n_events`, `dropped`, `high`, `preedit`, `composition`, `names`, `ends`, `n_names`, `n_files`, `dropped_files`, `drop_at`, `out`

- `Buffers = struct { text: []u8, events: []Input.Event, preedit: []u8 = &.{}, names: []u8 = &.{}, ends: []u32 = &.{} }`: Each buffer is split in two halves: the messages arriving, and the frame handed out.
- `fn init(buf: Buffers) Translator`
- `fn feed(t: *Translator, m: Msg) void`: Reads one message.
- `fn setDpi(t: *Translator, dpi: u32) void`: Physical pixels per logical pixel from the window's DPI (`GetDpiForWindow`, `WM_DPICHANGED`): 1 at 96, 1.5 at 144, 2 at 192.
- `fn dropFile(t: *Translator, path: []const u16) void`: One path of a drop (`DragQueryFileW`), UTF-16.
- `fn drop(t: *Translator, pos: [2]i32) void`: The drop itself (`WM_DROPFILES`), at `pos` in client pixels (`DragQueryPoint`), after its paths.
- `fn clock(t: *Translator, seconds: f64) void`: The clock, in seconds since the shell started: sets `Input.time`, and `dt` to the time since the last frame.
- `fn compose(t: *Translator, units: []const u16, attrs: []const u8, cursor: u32) void`: The IME's composition string (`ImmGetCompositionStringW` with `GCS_COMPSTR`), its attributes (`GCS_COMPATTR`, one byte per UTF-16 unit) and the caret (`GCS_CURSORPOS`, in UTF-16 units), as `Events.composition`: UTF-8, with the range the IME converts (the target clause) or, without one, the caret.
- `fn commit(t: *Translator, units: []const u16) void`: Text the IME committed (`GCS_RESULTSTR`): typed, as one `.text` event.
- `fn paste(t: *Translator, units: []const u16) void`: Clipboard text (`CF_UNICODETEXT`) delivered as typed text, the way `keyboard.Clipboard` asks a host to answer a paste: one `.text` event, line ends as `\n`, other control characters but tabs left out.
- `fn frame(t: *Translator, ev: *Events) bool`: This frame's events: the messages since the last `frame`, copied to `out`, where `ev` points until the next call.

### `Replay` (struct)
Recorded window messages, replayed one frame per `next`, so tests drive the translator (and, on Windows, a real window) with the messages a session produced.

Fields: `src`, `at`, `line`

- `Error = error{ Syntax, BufferFull }`
- `fn next(r: *Replay, buf: []Msg) Error!?[]const Msg`: The messages of the next frame, in `buf`, or null after the last frame.

## `lcgf.shell_ios_translate` (src/shell/ios/translate.zig)

UIKit events as `Events`: the part of the iOS shell (ios.zig) that touches no OS, so it builds and is tested on any host.

### Top level

- `Phase = enum(u8) { began, moved, stationary, ended, cancelled }`: `UITouchPhase`, by value.
- `Kind = enum(u8) { direct, indirect, pencil, pointer, eraser }`: `UITouchType`, by value: a finger on the screen, a finger on a remote surface, an Apple Pencil, and a trackpad or mouse click on iPad.
- `State = enum { active, inactive, background }`: The application's state: in front and taking events, in front but interrupted (a call, Control Center, the app switcher), or in the background.
- `max_contacts = 16`: Most fingers and pencils followed at once; iOS reports at most 10 fingers on a screen.

### `Touch` (struct)
One UITouch as `touchesBegan:withEvent:` and the others report it.

Fields: `id`, `phase`, `pos`, `kind`, `pressure`, `tilt`


### `Translator` (struct)

Fields: `in`, `closed`, `state`, `rows`, `fresh`, `lift`, `n_rows`, `out_rows`, `primary`, `primary_mouse`, `primary_fresh`, `release_next`, `leave_next`, `dropped`, `text`, `events`, `n_text`, `n_events`, `out`, `preedit`, `composition`, `out_preedit`

- `max_preedit = 256`: Longest marked text shown; a longer one is cut at a character.
- `Buffers = struct { text: []u8, events: []Input.Event }`: Each buffer is split in two halves: what arrives, and the frame handed out.
- `fn init(buf: Buffers) Translator`
- `fn resize(t: *Translator, w: f32, h: f32) void`: The view's bounds changed (`layoutSubviews`): `w` x `h` points.
- `fn setScale(t: *Translator, scale: f32) void`: Physical pixels per point: the view's `contentScaleFactor`, which the shell sets to the screen's `nativeScale` (2 or 3 on iPhones; 2.608 on a display-zoomed Plus or Max).
- `fn setInsets(t: *Translator, top: f32, left: f32, bottom: f32, right: f32) void`: The view's `safeAreaInsets` (top, left, bottom, right in points, UIEdgeInsets' order), as `Input.safe_area`: what the notch, the Dynamic Island, the home indicator and the status bar cover.
- `fn lifecycle(t: *Translator, state: State) void`: The application's state changed (the delegate's `applicationWillResignActive:`, `applicationDidEnterBackground:`, `applicationWillEnterForeground:`, `applicationDidBecomeActive:`).
- `fn visible(t: *const Translator) bool`: Whether frames can be shown: not in the background, where iOS forbids GPU work (a Metal command buffer committed there gets the app killed) and nothing is on screen.
- `fn terminate(t: *Translator) void`
- `fn touch(t: *Translator, c: Touch) void`: A touch began, moved, ended or was cancelled.
- `fn key(t: *Translator, code: u32, down: bool, flags: u64) void`: A hardware key went down or up (`pressesBegan:` / `pressesEnded:`).
- `fn keyEvent(t: *Translator, code: u8, kind: Input.Event.Kind, mods: Input.Mods) void`: A key event in the core's terms: HID usage `code` pressed, repeated or released with `mods` held.
- `fn insert(t: *Translator, utf8: []const u8) void`: Text the keyboard typed or the IME committed (`insertText:`): one `.text` event.
- `fn deleteBackward(t: *Translator) void`: The keyboard's delete key (`deleteBackward`): Backspace, as a press and a release, unless a hardware Backspace is held.
- `fn mark(t: *Translator, utf8: []const u8, range: [2]u32) void`: The IME's marked text changed (`setMarkedText:selectedRange:`): `utf8`, with the byte range it selects (the clause being converted, or the caret).
- `fn unmark(t: *Translator) void`: The IME let go of its marked text without replacing it (`unmarkText`): it stays as typed text.
- `fn paste(t: *Translator, utf8: []const u8) void`: Pasteboard text (`UIPasteboard.string`, UTF-8) delivered as typed text, the way `keyboard.Clipboard` asks a host to answer a paste: one `.text` event, line ends as `\n`, other control characters but tabs left out.
- `fn pending(t: *const Translator) bool`: A frame is owed without a new event: a release, a pointer leaving or a lift that the last frame deferred.
- `fn frame(t: *Translator, ev: *Events) bool`: This frame's events, in `ev` until the next call.

### `Replay` (struct)
Recorded UIKit events, replayed one frame per `next` into a translator, so tests drive it (and, on iOS, the real view) with what a session produced.

Fields: `src`, `at`, `line`

- `Error = error{Syntax}`
- `fn next(r: *Replay, t: *Translator) Error!bool`: Feeds the next frame's events to `t`.

## `lcgf.shell_android_translate` (src/shell/android/translate.zig)

NativeActivity events as `Events`: the part of the Android shell (android.zig) that touches no OS, so it builds and is tested on any host.

### Top level

- `Tool = enum(u8) { unknown = 0, finger = 1, stylus = 2, mouse = 3, eraser = 4, _ }`: `AMOTION_EVENT_TOOL_TYPE_*`.
- `Lifecycle = enum { start, resume_, pause, stop }`: The activity lifecycle callbacks that change what the app may do.
- `density_default = 160`: The density at which one logical pixel is one physical pixel (ACONFIGURATION_DENSITY_MEDIUM).
- `combining_accent = 0x8000_0000`: `KeyCharacterMap.COMBINING_ACCENT`: the character is a dead key's accent.
- `KeyAction = enum(u8) { down = 0, up = 1, multiple = 2, _ }`: `AKEY_EVENT_ACTION_*`.
- `fn utf16ToUtf8(units: []const u16, out: []u8) ?[]u8`: `units` (UTF-16) as UTF-8 in `out`, or null if it is not valid UTF-16 or does not fit.
- `fn utf8ToUtf16(utf8: []const u8, out: []u16) ?[]u16`: `utf8` as UTF-16 for a Java String (`ClipData.newPlainText`) in `out`, which needs as many units as `utf8` has bytes; null if it is not valid UTF-8 or does not fit.

### `Action` (enum)
`AMOTION_EVENT_ACTION_*`, the low byte of `AMotionEvent_getAction`; the next byte is the index of the pointer a `pointer_down` or `pointer_up` is about.


### `Pointer` (struct)
One pointer of a motion event, as `AMotionEvent_getPointerId`, `_getToolType`, `_getX`, `_getY`, `_getPressure` and the tilt and orientation axes give it.

Fields: `id`, `tool`, `pos`, `pressure`, `tilt`, `orientation`


### `Motion` (struct)
One motion event: its action word, its pointers, the mouse buttons held (`AMotionEvent_getButtonState`) and, for `scroll`, AXIS_HSCROLL and AXIS_VSCROLL.

Fields: `action`, `pointers`, `buttons`, `scroll`


### `Translator` (struct)

Fields: `m`, `window`, `content`, `has_window`

- `Buffers = mobile.Translator.Buffers`
- `fn init(buf: Buffers) Translator`
- `fn resize(t: *Translator, w: f32, h: f32) void`: The native window was created or resized (`onNativeWindowCreated`, `onNativeWindowResized`): `w` x `h` physical pixels.
- `fn windowLost(t: *Translator) void`: The native window is gone (`onNativeWindowDestroyed`): the surface with it.
- `fn lifecycle(t: *Translator, event: Lifecycle) void`: The activity's lifecycle (`onStart`, `onResume`, `onPause`, `onStop`) as the mobile model's state: resumed is active, started or paused is visible but not in front (inactive), stopped is the background.
- `fn focus(t: *Translator, focused: bool) void`: The window gained or lost input focus (`onWindowFocusChanged`): without it, held keys and modifiers are let go, since their key-ups go elsewhere.
- `fn visible(t: *const Translator) bool`: Whether a frame can be shown: a window exists and the activity is not stopped.
- `fn setDensity(t: *Translator, dpi: u32) void`: The screen's density (`AConfiguration_getDensity`, dots per inch): 160 is one physical pixel per logical one (Android's dp), 420 is 2.625.
- `fn setContent(t: *Translator, left: f32, top: f32, right: f32, bottom: f32) void`: The content rect (`onContentRectChanged`), window pixels.
- `fn motion(t: *Translator, m: Motion) void`: A motion event.
- `fn key(t: *Translator, action: KeyAction, code: u32, meta: u32, repeat: u32, char: u32) bool`: A key event: Android keycode `code`, `AKeyEvent_getMetaState`'s `meta`, the repeat count, and the character the key types with that meta state (`KeyCharacterMap.get`, 0 for none; a dead key's has `combining_accent` set and types nothing).
- `fn characters(t: *Translator, units: []const u16, scratch: []u8) void`: Text a key event carries whole (ACTION_MULTIPLE with AKEYCODE_UNKNOWN, `getCharacters`): what a soft keyboard commits that no key types (emoji, most scripts).
- `fn paste(t: *Translator, units: []const u16, scratch: []u8) void`: Clipboard text (`ClipData.Item.coerceToText`, a Java String's UTF-16) typed into the next frame, as `keyboard.Clipboard.paste` asks a host to answer a paste (the mobile translator's `paste`).
- `fn destroy(t: *Translator) void`: The activity is being destroyed for good (`onDestroy`): no more frames.
- `fn frame(t: *Translator, ev: *Events) bool`: This frame's events, in `ev` until the next call.

### `Replay` (struct)
Recorded NativeActivity events, replayed one frame per `next` into a translator, so tests drive it with what a session produced.

Fields: `src`, `at`, `line`

- `Error = error{Syntax}`
- `fn next(r: *Replay, t: *Translator) Error!bool`: Feeds the next frame's events to `t`.

## `lcgf.shell_macos_translate` (src/shell/macos/translate.zig)

AppKit events as `Events`: the part of the macOS shell (macos.zig) that touches no OS, so it builds and is tested on any host.

### Top level

- `Mark = struct { text: []const u8, selected: [2]u32 = .{ 0, 0 } }`
- `flag_caps_lock: u64 = 1 << 16`: `NSEventModifierFlags`: the device-independent modifiers.
- `flag_shift: u64 = 1 << 17`
- `flag_control: u64 = 1 << 18`
- `flag_option: u64 = 1 << 19`
- `flag_command: u64 = 1 << 20`
- `MenuItem = struct { title: [:0]const u8 = "", action: [:0]const u8 = "", key: [:0]const u8 = "", option: bool = false, named: bool = false }`: One item of the menu bar the shell builds: its title, the action AppKit sends up the responder chain, its key equivalent with Command (and Option when `option`).
- `fn cursorExact(cursor: Cursor) bool`
- `fn waitSeconds(seconds: f32, pace: Pace, hz: f64) ?f64`: Seconds `wait` blocks for (the `untilDate:` of `nextEventMatchingMask:`), for a wait of `seconds` (`scheduler.wake`) at `pace` on a display refreshing `hz` times a second; null waits for the next event however long it takes (`distantFuture`).
- `fn windowRect(r: Rect, height: f32) [4]f64`: `r` (logical pixels, origin top left) as an NSRect in window coordinates (origin bottom left) of content `height` points tall: x, y, width, height.

### `Kind` (enum)
The NSEvent types the translator reads, by their `NSEventType` values.


### `Event` (struct)
The fields of one NSEvent the translator reads.

Fields: `kind`, `at`, `flags`, `code`, `repeat`, `delta`, `precise`


### `Record` (union)
One thing the window reported: what the view and window-delegate methods hand the translator.

Fields: `event`, `insert`, `mark`, `drag`, `drop`, `file`, `resize`, `scale`


### `Translator` (struct)

Fields: `in`, `closed`, `text`, `events`, `n_text`, `n_events`, `dropped`, `preedit`, `composition`, `out`, `names`, `ends`, `n_names`, `n_files`, `dragging`, `dropped_files`, `drag_at`

- `Buffers = struct { text: []u8, events: []Input.Event, preedit: []u8 = &.{}, names: []u8 = &.{}, ends: []u32 = &.{} }`: Each buffer is split in two halves: the events arriving, and the frame handed out.
- `fn init(buf: Buffers) Translator`
- `fn feed(t: *Translator, r: Record) void`: Reads one record.
- `fn resize(t: *Translator, size: [2]f64) void`: The content view's size in points, which is the viewport in logical pixels.
- `fn dropFile(t: *Translator, path: []const u8) void`: One path of a drop.
- `fn setScale(t: *Translator, s: f64) void`: Physical pixels per logical pixel.
- `fn event(t: *Translator, e: Event) void`: Reads one NSEvent.
- `fn mark(t: *Translator, text: []const u8, selected: [2]u32) void`: The composition (`setMarkedText:...`) as `Events.composition`: UTF-8, with the selected range (UTF-16 units, as NSRange counts) as a byte range.
- `fn clock(t: *Translator, seconds: f64) void`: The clock, in seconds since the shell started: sets `Input.time`, and `dt` to the time since the last frame.
- `fn marked(t: *const Translator) bool`: The composition is being shown (`hasMarkedText`).
- `fn commit(t: *Translator, utf8: []const u8) void`: Text an input method committed (`insertText:replacementRange:`), or a key typed: typed, and any composition ends.
- `fn insert(t: *Translator, utf8: []const u8) void`: Text typed (`insertText:`) as one `.text` event.
- `fn paste(t: *Translator, utf8: []const u8) void`: The pasteboard's text delivered as typed text, the way `keyboard.Clipboard` asks a host to answer a paste: as `insert`, one `.text` event with `\n` line ends.
- `fn frame(t: *Translator, ev: *Events) bool`: This frame's events: those since the last `frame`, copied to `out`, where `ev` points until the next call.

### `Replay` (struct)
Recorded AppKit events, replayed one frame per `next`, so tests drive the translator (and, on macOS, a real window) with what a session produced.

Fields: `src`, `at`, `line`

- `Error = error{ Syntax, BufferFull }`
- `fn next(r: *Replay, buf: []Record) Error!?[]const Record`: The records of the next frame, in `buf`, or null after the last frame.

### `Appearance` (struct)
The system's light or dark appearance and accent colour, as System Settings > Appearance sets them.

Fields: `dark`, `accent`

- `fn fromSystem(dark: bool, accent: ?[3]f64) Appearance`: From what the shell reads: whether NSApp's effective appearance is closer to Dark Aqua than to Aqua, and `controlAccentColor` in sRGB, components 0 to 1.
- `fn tokens(a: Appearance) theme.Tokens`: The theme tokens that follow it: `theme.dark` or `theme.light`, with the system's accent for checked boxes, slider fills and focus.

## `lcgf.i18n_catalogue` (src/i18n/catalogue.zig)

Message catalogues: one table of translated strings per locale, indexed by an application enum of message keys.

### Top level

- `fn Catalogue(comptime Key: type) type`: A catalogue of one locale for the message keys of `Key`, an enum whose values are 0 to n-1.
- `fn check(comptime Key: type, comptime locale: rules.Locale, comptime messages: anytype) ?[]const u8`: The first problem with `messages` as a catalogue for `Key`, or null.
- `fn placeholders(template: []const u8) error{Malformed}!u10`: A bit per placeholder `{0}` to `{9}` that `template` uses.
- `fn fill(out: []u8, template: []const u8, args: []const []const u8) error{NoSpaceLeft}![]u8`: Writes `template` into `out` with `{k}` replaced by `args[k]` and returns the written part.

### `Catalogue(..)` (generic struct)
A catalogue of one locale for the message keys of `Key`, an enum whose values are 0 to n-1.

Fields: `locale`, `texts`

- `count = names.len`
- `fn init(comptime locale: rules.Locale, comptime messages: anytype) @This()`: Builds a catalogue from a struct literal with one field per key: `.init(.en, .{ .greeting = "Hello, {0}", .files = .{ .one = "...", .other = "..." } })`.
- `fn text(c: *const @This(), key: Key) []const u8`: The template of `key`; for a plural message, its "other" form.
- `fn plural(c: *const @This(), key: Key, n: rules.Operands) []const u8`: The form of `key` that the number `n` takes in this catalogue's locale.
- `fn formatCount(c: *const @This(), out: []u8, key: Key, value: i64, frac_digits: u8) error{NoSpaceLeft}![]u8`: The one-call form for counts: formats `value / 10^frac_digits` for the locale, picks the plural form of `key` it takes and fills `{0}` with it.

## `lcgf.i18n_plural` (src/i18n/plural.zig)

Plural selection: which CLDR cardinal category ("one", "few", ...) a number takes in a locale.

### Top level

- `Locale = enum(u8) { en, de, fr, ru, pl, ja, ar, pseudo }`: The locales this module has data for.
- `Category = enum(u3) { zero, one, two, few, many, other }`
- `fn category(locale: Locale, n: Operands) Category`
- `fn categories(locale: Locale) u6`: A bit per category (`@intFromEnum`) that `locale` can select.

### `Operands` (struct)
CLDR plural operands of an absolute value: `i` the integer digits, `v` the number of visible fraction digits, `f` those digits as an integer (1.50 is i=1, v=2, f=50).

Fields: `i`, `v`, `f`

- `fn int(n: u64) Operands`

## `lcgf.i18n_number` (src/i18n/number.zig)

Number formatting: decimal numbers with the locale's digits, decimal separator, digit grouping and minus sign.

### Top level

- `fn symbols(locale: plural.Locale) Symbols`
- `fn format(out: []u8, locale: plural.Locale, value: i64, frac_digits: u8) error{NoSpaceLeft}![]u8`: Writes `value / 10^frac_digits` into `out` with `frac_digits` digits after the separator and returns the written part: `format(buf, .de, -123456, 2)` is "-1.234,56".
- `fn operands(value: i64, frac_digits: u8) plural.Operands`: The plural operands of `value / 10^frac_digits`, for `Catalogue.plural`.

### `Symbols` (struct)

Fields: `decimal`, `group`, `minus`, `min_grouping`, `digits`


## `lcgf.i18n_date` (src/i18n/date.zig)

Date formatting: Gregorian dates in the locale's CLDR short and long date patterns, with its month names (format context, so Russian and Polish months are genitive) and digits.

### Top level

- `Style = enum { short, long }`
- `fn pattern(locale: plural.Locale, style: Style) []const u8`
- `fn months(locale: plural.Locale) [12][]const u8`
- `fn format(out: []u8, locale: plural.Locale, style: Style, date: Date) error{NoSpaceLeft}![]u8`: Writes `date` in the locale's pattern for `style` and returns the written part.

### `Date` (struct)

Fields: `year`, `month`, `day`

- `fn fromDays(days: i64) Date`: The date `days` after 1970-01-01 (negative before it).

## `lcgf.i18n_bundle` (src/i18n/bundle.zig)

Switching language at run time: a bundle is the comptime list of an application's catalogues plus one runtime index, the active one.

### Top level

- `fn Bundle(comptime Key: type, comptime catalogues: []const catalogue.Catalogue(Key)) type`
- `fn check(comptime Key: type, comptime catalogues: []const catalogue.Catalogue(Key)) ?[]const u8`: The first problem with `catalogues` as a bundle, or null.
- `fn localeFromTag(tag: []const u8) ?plural.Locale`: The locale for a BCP 47 language tag such as "de-AT" or "pt_BR" (its language subtag only), or null when this module has no data for it.

### `Bundle(..)` (generic struct)

Fields: `active`

- `all = catalogues`
- `fn current(b: *const @This()) *const catalogue.Catalogue(Key)`
- `fn select(b: *@This(), locale: plural.Locale) bool`: Makes `locale` the active language.

## `lcgf.i18n_pseudo` (src/i18n/pseudo.zig)

Pseudo-localisation, a test mode for finding UI that is not ready for translation: every ASCII letter gets an accent (text that bypassed the catalogue stays plain and stands out), the text grows by about 40% (German and Finnish run that much longer than English, so clipping shows) and is bracketed (a missing "]" means it was cut off).

### Top level

- `fn maxLen(len: usize) usize`: The most bytes `transform` writes for a template of `len` bytes: three per letter, padding and brackets.
- `fn transform(out: []u8, template: []const u8) error{NoSpaceLeft}![]u8`: Writes the pseudo-localised form of `template` into `out` and returns the written part: "Hello, {0}!" becomes "[Ĥéļļö, {0}! ~~~~]".
- `fn catalogue(comptime Key: type, comptime reference: *const catalogues.Catalogue(Key)) catalogues.Catalogue(Key)`: The pseudo-localised copy of `reference`, built at comptime, with locale `.pseudo`.

## `lcgf.ct_interop` (src/comptime/interop.zig)

Interop between the two front ends (tier 1): a runtime `Frame` inside a region of a comptime UI, and a comptime UI inside a runtime `Frame`.

### Top level

- `fn region(comptime id: @EnumLiteral(), comptime style: Style) Spec`: A region: an empty column named `id` whose content another front end builds.
- `fn fitRegion(comptime id: @EnumLiteral(), comptime style: Style) Spec`: A region sized by its content (markup E1): on each axis whose `style` size is `fit`, it takes the size its owner measured last (`setFit`).
- `fn FitRegion(comptime name: [:0]const u8) type`: The `Spec.widget` of a `fitRegion` named `name`.
- `fn setFit(ui: anytype, comptime id: @EnumLiteral(), size: [2]f32) bool`: Records the content size `size` of the `fitRegion` named `id` in comptime UI `ui`, for its next frame.
- `fn canvas(comptime id: @EnumLiteral(), comptime style: Style, comptime data: u64) Spec`: A leaf whose custom command gives the host its final bounds, clip, id and opaque payload.
- `fn rectOf(ram: anytype, row: usize) [4]f32`: Row `row`'s rect (x0, y0, x1, y1) in a comptime layout's RAM (`Columns.Dynamic`), as f32 whatever the coordinate type.
- `fn widgetRects(comptime E: type, ram: anytype) E.Rects`: The widget rect table `Events(spec).Rects` from a comptime layout's RAM: each widget's visible rect (`vis`), in the layout's own coordinates, as `Rects.fromTree` takes it from a runtime tree.
- `fn regionInput(outer: *const Input, rect: [4]f32) Input`: The input of a front end hosted in `rect` (x0, y0, x1, y1, in `outer`'s coordinates): the rect's size as its viewport, and the pointer moved by the rect's origin.
- `fn compose(out: *DrawList, host: *const DrawList, guest: *const DrawList, region_id: u32, strings: []u8) error{ NoRegion, NoRoom }!void`: `host` with `guest` drawn in the region `region_id`: the host's custom command whose `CustomDraw.id` is `region_id` is replaced, in paint order, by all of `guest`'s commands.

### `FitRegion(..)` (generic struct)
The `Spec.widget` of a `fitRegion` named `name`.

- `custom_slots = 0`: The region's own custom command; no others.
- `fn push(ui: anytype, comptime n: Spec, parent: u32, next: *u32, in: *const Input) void`

## `lcgf.abi` (src/abi/abi.zig)

The mapping from a Zig function to its C export (docs/plans/bindings-rust.md §6.1), by comptime reflection over its signature.

### Top level

- `fn Handle(comptime T: type) type`: The C form of a pointer to a handle type `T` (a `*Frame`): an opaque type, cast back.
- `fn Words(comptime T: type) type`: The C form of a struct of no C layout: its words, in `words.put` order.
- `fn wordable(comptime T: type) bool`: Whether `T` crosses as words: floats, ints of 32 bits or fewer, bools, enums, bitpacks, optional enums, and arrays and structs of them.
- `fn CForm(comptime T: type) type`: The C form of a struct of no C layout that cannot be words (`Input`): an extern struct of its fields in order, each in its C form: a slice is a pointer (null when empty) and a `_len`, a nested struct its own C form, a bitpack its backing integer, an enum its tag.
- `fn Fields(comptime T: type) type`
- `fn Form(comptime T: type) type`: `T`'s C form (words or `CForm`).
- `fn decode(comptime T: type, c: *const Form(T)) T`: `T` from its C form.
- `fn encode(comptime T: type, v: T, out: *Form(T)) void`: `T`'s C form of `v`.
- `fn CParams(comptime T: type, comptime handles: anytype) []const type`: The C parameters a Zig parameter of type `T` becomes; `handles` are the types passed as opaque pointers.
- `fn CResult(comptime T: type) type`: The C result of a Zig result `T`; a slice returns its pointer and writes its length out, and a struct of no C layout writes its words out.
- `fn cParamsOf(comptime F: type, comptime handles: anytype) []const type`: Every C parameter of `F`'s export, in order: each Zig parameter's, then `*usize` for a slice result's length or `*Words(R)` for a words result.
- `fn fromC(comptime T: type, comptime handles: anytype, c: anytype, comptime at: usize) T`: Zig parameter `T` from its C arguments, `c[at..]` (`CParams(T)` of them).
- `fn toC(comptime T: type, v: T) CResult(T)`: Zig result `v` as its C result.
- `fn Wrap(comptime f: anytype, comptime handles: anytype) type`: The `callconv(.c)` export of `f`: one C function per arity, its arguments handed to `invoke`.

### `Handle(..)` (generic struct)
The C form of a pointer to a handle type `T` (a `*Frame`): an opaque type, cast back.


### `Words(..)` (generic struct)
The C form of a struct of no C layout: its words, in `words.put` order.

Fields: `w`

- `Zig = T`
- `words_form = true`

### `CForm(..)` (generic struct)
The C form of a struct of no C layout that cannot be words (`Input`): an extern struct of its fields in order, each in its C form: a slice is a pointer (null when empty) and a `_len`, a nested struct its own C form, a bitpack its backing integer, an enum its tag.

Fields: `f`

- `Zig = T`
- `c_form = true`

### `Form(..)` (generic struct)
`T`'s C form (words or `CForm`).


### `CResult(..)` (generic struct)
The C result of a Zig result `T`; a slice returns its pointer and writes its length out, and a struct of no C layout writes its words out.


### `Wrap(..)` (generic struct)
The `callconv(.c)` export of `f`: one C function per arity, its arguments handed to `invoke`.


## `lcgf.abi_words` (src/abi/words.zig)

The word codec: a value as u32 words, written field by field in declaration order, and the layout hash over the field lists.

### Top level

- `fn wordsOf(comptime T: type) comptime_int`: Words of a value of `T` written field by field (`put`).
- `fn put(comptime T: type, v: T, out: []u32) void`: A value as words and back: f32 as its bits, ints, bools and enums as u32, packed structs as their backing integer, arrays and structs element by element.
- `fn get(comptime T: type, in: []const u32) T`
- `fn layoutHashOf(comptime list: anytype) u32`: The layout hash of `list`: FNV-1a of the field names and kinds of each type.

## `lcgf.abi_plan` (src/abi/plan.zig)

plan(): what the widgets' comptime front end (`ComptimeUi`, src/widgets/base.zig) decides while compiling, as a pure function over arrays.

### Top level

- `version: u32 = 1`
- `none: u32 = std.math.maxInt(u32)`
- `FieldType = enum(u8) { str, bool, f32, u32, u8, other, color }`: What a state field holds, as the generated state structs lay it out (C rules): `str` is a pointer and a length (16 bytes on 64-bit targets), `bool` and `u8` one byte (`u8` is an enum's tag, a `choice` selector), `f32`, `u32` and `color` (rgba bits) four.
- `Kind = enum(u8) { container, button, label, checkbox, slider, custom, widget }`: The reference widgets' spec kinds, after `ct.*` built them (a button is a row with an id).
- `RegionKind = enum(u8) { none, when, list, choice }`: What makes a node's rows live only sometimes (spec.zig's `when`, `list`, `choice`).
- `Spring = struct { field: u32, stiffness: f32, damping: f32 }`: A spring on an f32 or bool field: the slider bound to it shows the sprung value.
- `Format = struct { text: []const u8, segs: []const fmt.Seg, args: []const u32 }`: A formatted label: its text, its segments (src/core/fmt.zig; a segment's `arg` indexes `args`) and the fields its arguments are, by index.
- `Op = enum(u8) { container, button, label, checkbox, box, slider, thumb, custom, widget }`: What a row pushes.
- `Widget = struct { row: u32, kind: Kind, focusable: bool }`
- `Error = error{ BadTree, BadBind, BadKind, BadRegion, BadSpring }`
- `wordsOf = codec.wordsOf`
- `put = codec.put`
- `get = codec.get`
- `layoutHashOf = codec.layoutHashOf`
- `layout_hash: u32 = codec.layoutHashOf(.{ Style, TextStyle, Color, RowWords, Header, Dir })`: The layout hash of the tables: of every type they carry, and of the row encoding.
- `Dir = extern struct { id: u32, class: u32, words_per_row: u32, offset: u32, len: u32 }`: One entry of the column directory: where a column's words are.
- `Column = enum(u32) { rows, styles, widgets, fields, springs, theme, formats, segs, text }`
- `column_count = @typeInfo(Column).@"enum".field_names.len`
- `Class = enum(u32) { dynamic, constant, dropped }`: Every column is constant (rodata): the caller's buffer holds the frame's working columns.
- `header_words = wordsOf(Header)`
- `FormatSize = struct { formats: usize = 0, segs: usize = 0, text: usize = 0 }`: What the formatted labels of some nodes take in the tables.
- `fn argKind(t: FieldType) ?fmt.Kind`: The format kind a field of type `t` is written as, or null when it cannot be an argument.
- `fn Buffers(comptime n: comptime_int) type`: Room for a plan of `n` nodes: one row, one style and at most one widget per node, plus the three theme styles.
- `fn layoutState(fields: []Field) struct { size: u32, alignment: u32 }`: Lays out the state struct as C lays out the generated one: writes each field's `offset`.
- `fn plan(nodes: []const Node, fields: []Field, springs: []const Spring, th: *const Theme, rows: []Row, styles: []Style, widgets: []Widget, formats: []Format) …`: The plan of `nodes` with theme `th`.
- `fn count(comptime spec: Spec) comptime_int`: Nodes in `spec`, itself included.
- `fn fieldsOf(comptime State: type) [@typeInfo(State).@"struct".field_names.len]Field`: The fields of `State`, in declaration order: strings, bools and f32s.
- `fn fieldsOfZig(comptime State: type) [@typeInfo(State).@"struct".field_names.len]Field`: `fieldsOf` for `ComptimeUi`: a field of another type is `other`, for Zig code only.
- `fn springsOf(comptime State: type) [springCount(State)]Spring`: The springs `State` declares (`pub const springs = .{ .volume = Spring{} }`), in that order.
- `fn fromSpec(comptime State: type, comptime spec: Spec) [count(spec)]Node`: `spec`'s nodes, depth-first, binding the fields of `State`.
- `fn fromSpecZig(comptime State: type, comptime spec: Spec) [count(spec)]Node`: `fromSpec` for `ComptimeUi`: a Zig custom widget (`Spec.widget`) is a `widget` node and its subtree is lenient (its `push` draws it).
- `fn tablesOf(comptime State: type, comptime spec: Spec, comptime th: Theme) [encodedWords(count(spec), fieldsOf(State).len, springCount(State), formatSize(&fr…`: The encoded tables of `ComptimeUi(State, spec, th)`'s UI, made while compiling.
- `fn PlanOf(comptime spec: Spec) type`: `ComptimeUi(State, spec, th)`'s plan, made while compiling: the rows and styles it pushes, the same `plan` the seam's tables are encoded from.
- `fn planOf(comptime State: type, comptime spec: Spec, comptime th: Theme) PlanOf(spec)`
- `fn emit(host: anytype, c: anytype, looks: *const Looks, r: u32, row: Row, style: Style, parent: u32) u32`: Pushes row `r` under tree row `parent` and returns its tree row: the one place a row's op becomes a tree row, for both front ends of the tables (`ComptimeUi.push` and the seam's frame).
- `fn encodedWords(n: usize, f: usize, springs: usize, fm: FormatSize) usize`: An upper bound on the words of a plan of `n` nodes and `f` fields, and of formats `fm` (`formatSize`).
- `fn formatSize(nodes: []const Node) FormatSize`
- `fn encode(p: *const Plan, out: []u32) usize`: Writes `p` into `out` (at least `encodedWords` long); returns the words used.

### `Field` (struct)

Fields: `name`, `type`, `offset`


### `Node` (struct)
One spec node, depth-first.

Fields: `kind`, `style`, `id`, `bind`, `parent`, `region`, `region_field`, `data`, `lenient`, `format`


### `Row` (struct)
One tree row the frame pushes.

Fields: `op`, `parent`, `style`, `id`, `field`, `widget`, `end`, `sealed`, `when`, `gate`, `gate_field`, `gate_index`, `data`, `format`


### `Plan` (struct)
What `plan` produces.

Fields: `rows`, `styles`, `widgets`, `formats`, `fields`, `springs`, `label`, `fills`, `box`, `thumb`, `runs`, `state_size`, `state_align`


### `Buffers(..)` (generic struct)
Room for a plan of `n` nodes: one row, one style and at most one widget per node, plus the three theme styles.


### `Looks` (struct)
The theme's parts `emit` adds to a row's own style.

Fields: `label`, `fills`, `box`


### `PlanOf(..)` (generic struct)
`ComptimeUi(State, spec, th)`'s plan, made while compiling: the rows and styles it pushes, the same `plan` the seam's tables are encoded from.


### `Header` (struct)
The header: the first words of the tables.

Fields: `magic`, `version`, `layout_hash`, `words`, `index_bits`, `coord`, `lanes`, `features`, `rows`, `runs`, `widgets`, `fields`, `styles`, `springs`, `state_size`, `state_align`, `columns`


### `Tables` (struct)
Tables read back, as the frame uses them: slices into the caller's words.

Fields: `header`, `words`, `rows`, `styles`, `widgets`, `fields`, `springs`, `formats`, `segs`, `text`, `label`, `fills`, `box`, `thumb`

- `DecodeError = error{ NotTables, Version, LayoutHash, Truncated }`
- `fn decode(words: []const u32) DecodeError!Tables`: Checks the header (magic, version, layout hash, length) and finds the columns.
- `fn row(t: *const Tables, i: u32) Row`
- `fn format(t: *const Tables, k: u32) struct { first: u32, count: u32, at: u32, len: u32 }`: Format `k`: its segments are `seg(first ..< first + count)`, its text is `textByte(at ..< at + len)`.
- `fn seg(t: *const Tables, i: u32) struct { kind: fmt.Kind, prec: u8, field: u32, start: u32, len: u32 }`: Segment `i` of the segs column: what it writes, with the field of its argument.
- `fn textByte(t: *const Tables, b: u32) u8`: Byte `b` of the text column.
- `fn style(t: *const Tables, i: u32) Style`
- `fn widget(t: *const Tables, k: u32) Widget`
- `fn spring(t: *const Tables, k: u32) Spring`
- `fn field(t: *const Tables, k: u32) struct { type: FieldType, offset: u32 }`

### `Tables.Section` (struct)

Fields: `at`, `per`, `len`


## `lcgf.abi_seam` (src/abi/seam.zig)

The prebuilt frame behind the C seam: `ComptimeUi.frame` (src/widgets/base.zig) driven by tables (src/abi/plan.zig) instead of by a comptime spec.

### Top level

- `none_widget = plan.none`: The `widget` of a frame without an event.
- `Output = struct { draw: *const DrawList, tree: *const Tree, widget: u32 = none, value: f32 = 0, focus: u32 = none, wake: f32 = types.inf }`: What a frame returns: the draw list, the tree it was painted from, and the event the pointer made (`widget` is a slot in widget order, `none` for no event).
- `CInput = abi.Form(Input)`: The input a host passes: all of `Input`, in its C form (`lcgf_input`, src/abi/abi.zig).
- `Status = enum(c_int) { ok = 0, not_tables = 1, version = 2, layout_hash = 3, truncated = 4, buffer_too_small = 5, unaligned = 6 }`: Result codes of the C functions: 0 is success.
- `frame_symbol = std.fmt.comptimePrint("lcgf_frame_u32_f32_l{d}", .{simd.lanes})`: The exported names, specialised as this build is: 32-bit rows, f32 coordinates, and the lane count of `simd`.
- `fn cDraw(d: *const DrawList) CDraw`
- `fn exportAll() void`: Exports the seam's functions.
- `fn ExternState(comptime State: type) type`: The state struct a C++ or Rust program would pass for `State`: strings as pointer and length, in declaration order.
- `fn externDefaults(comptime State: type) ExternState(State)`: `State`'s defaults, as the extern struct.
- `fn SeamUi(comptime State: type, comptime spec: spec_zig.Spec, comptime th: base.Theme) type`: A UI over the seam in Zig: the tables made while compiling, the frame behind the seam.

### `Ui` (struct)

Fields: `tables`, `rows`, `runs`, `customs`, `pressed`, `down`, `focus`, `rects`, `row_of`, `spring_x`, `spring_v`, `spring_t`, `work`, `looks`, `cols`, `font`, `built`

- `Error = plan.Tables.DecodeError || error{ BufferTooSmall, Unaligned }`
- `fn bytes(tables: []const u32) Error!usize`: Bytes one UI of `tables` needs in the caller's buffer.
- `fn init(tables: []const u32, buf: []u8) Error!*Ui`: Checks `tables` and places a UI at the start of `buf`.
- `fn frame(ui: *Ui, state: [*]u8, in: *const Input) Output`: One frame: the pointer's event applied to the state, then every row pushed, laid out and painted, as `ComptimeUi.frame` does for the reference widgets.

### `CDraw` (struct)
A frame's draw list as columns, for C, C++ and Rust to read in place.

Fields: `quads`, `quad_len`, `run_len`, `runs`, `strings`, `strings_len`, `cmd_kind`, `cmd_first`, `cmd_count`, `cmd_key`, `cmd_len`, `custom_len`, `customs`


### `CFrame` (struct)

Fields: `draw`, `widget`, `value`, `focus`, `wake`


### `ExternState(..)` (generic struct)
The state struct a C++ or Rust program would pass for `State`: strings as pointer and length, in declaration order.


### `SeamUi(..)` (generic struct)
A UI over the seam in Zig: the tables made while compiling, the frame behind the seam.

Fields: `buf`, `state`, `ui`

- `tables = words`
- `fn init(s: *Self) !void`
- `fn frame(s: *Self, in: *const Input) Output`

## `lcgf.abi_zon` (src/abi/zon.zig)

A UI spec as a `.zon` file: what C++ and Rust programs write where Zig writes a comptime `Spec` with the `ct.*` builders.

### Top level

- `Length = union(enum) { fit, fill, px: f32, pct: f32, grow: f32 }`
- `ThemeColor = enum { text, panel, control, control_hot, control_held, accent }`
- `Box = struct { style: ZStyle = .{}, children: []const ZNode = &.{}, when: ?[]const u8 = null }`
- `List = struct { count: []const u8, max: u32, dir: types.Axis = .y, style: ZStyle = .{}, template: []const ZNode }`
- `Choice = struct { select: []const u8, dir: types.Axis = .y, style: ZStyle = .{}, children: []const ZNode }`
- `ZText = struct { format: []const u8, args: []const []const u8 = &.{} }`
- `ThemeName = enum { default }`
- `Error = error{ OutOfMemory, ParseZon, UnknownField, FieldWithoutType, BadList, MissingText, NotStatic, BadField, BadFormat }`
- `StaticWidget = struct { name: []const u8, row: u32, slider: bool }`
- `MixerState = struct { title: []const u8 = "Mixer", mute_text: []const u8 = "Mute", muted: bool = false, volume: f32 = 0.5, pan: f32 = 0 }`: src/comptime/mixed.zig's panel, with a title and a pan slider in a column around it.
- `fn fieldColor(z: ZField) u32`: A colour field's default, as rgba bits (`Color.bitsOf`).
- `fn read(gpa: std.mem.Allocator, arena: std.mem.Allocator, source: [:0]const u8, path: []const u8) Error!Expanded`: Reads `source`.
- `fn parse(gpa: std.mem.Allocator, arena: std.mem.Allocator, source: [:0]const u8, path: []const u8) Error!ZSpec`: Parses `source` without expanding it.
- `fn fieldsOf(arena: std.mem.Allocator, spec: ZSpec) Error![]plan.Field`: The state's fields, in order, not yet laid out.
- `fn expand(arena: std.mem.Allocator, spec: ZSpec) Error!Expanded`
- `fn readStatic(gpa: std.mem.Allocator, arena: std.mem.Allocator, source: [:0]const u8, path: []const u8) Error!Static`: Reads `source` as a static UI: spec.zig's `col`, `row`, `label`, `checkbox` and `slider`, one row each, and its regions `when`, `list`, `choice` and `virtual_list` (comptime UIs, ct_layout and ct_paint).
- `fn expandStatic(arena: std.mem.Allocator, spec: ZSpec) Error!Static`
- `fn staticTables(arena: std.mem.Allocator, x: *const Static) (Error || abi_ct.Error)![]u32`: The words of a static UI, in `arena` (`abi_ct.make`).
- `fn tables(arena: std.mem.Allocator, x: *const Expanded) (Error || plan.Error)![]u32`: The tables of an expanded spec, in `arena`.

### `ZColor` (union)

Fields: `rgb`, `rgba`, `theme`


### `ZStyle` (struct)
The parts of `Style` a spec sets; defaults are `Style`'s.

Fields: `size`, `min`, `max`, `pad`, `gap`, `main`, `cross`, `shrink`, `clip`, `fill`, `border_color`, `border`, `radius`, `opacity`, `fill_bind`, `border_bind`

- `fn style(z: ZStyle, th: *const Theme, dir: types.Axis) Style`

### `ZNode` (union)

Fields: `row`, `col`, `label`, `text`, `button`, `checkbox`, `slider`, `list`, `choice`, `virtual_list`, `custom`


### `ZField` (struct)

Fields: `name`, `str`, `bool`, `f32`, `u32`, `u8`, `color`, `spring`


### `ZSpec` (struct)

Fields: `name`, `theme`, `viewport`, `state`, `ui`


### `Expanded` (struct)
A spec read and expanded: the nodes and fields `plan` takes.

Fields: `spec`, `theme`, `fields`, `nodes`, `springs`


### `Static` (struct)
A spec read as a static UI: the rows `abi_ct.make` takes and the laid-out state.

Fields: `spec`, `fields`, `nodes`, `state_size`, `state_align`, `widgets`


### `PanelState` (struct)
Every feature that crosses the seam besides the reference widgets: a `when`, a `list`, a `choice`, a custom draw node, a sprung slider, a formatted label and keyboard focus.

Fields: `title`, `show`, `show_text`, `len`, `mode`, `level`

- `Mode = enum(u8) { compact, detailed }`
- `springs = .{ .level = @import("../anim/anims.zig").Spring{} }`

## `lcgf.abi_api` (src/abi/api.zig)

The export list: which Zig functions liblcgf exports to C, under which names, and which C name each type that crosses has (docs/plans/bindings-rust.md §6).

### Top level

- `handles = .{ Frame, Context, canvas.Instances }`: Types passed as opaque pointers (`lcgf_frame *`).
- `value_handles = .{Frame}`
- `column_align = @import("../core/mem.zig").column_align`
- `idiom_enums = .{ .{ types.Axis, "Dir" }, .{ types.Align, "Align" }, .{ types.Role, "Role" }, .{ Ease, "Ease" } }`: The bindings' own forms of the Zig types a program writes (bindings/cpp/include/lcgf/ types.hpp, bindings/rust/lcgf/src/types.rs, by tools/abi-gen): the enums by name, and the structs field for field with Zig's defaults, laid out as their words (`lcgf_style`).
- `defaults = .{ .{ Input, "input" }, .{ context.Limits, "limits" } }`: The C structs whose defaults the bindings get as constants (`lcgf::input_default`, `sys::LCGF_INPUT_DEFAULT`): Zig's defaults in C form.
- `idiom_structs = .{ .{ types.Size, "Size" }, .{ types.Style, "Style" }, .{ types.TextStyle, "TextStyle" }, .{ base.Theme, "Theme" }, .{ Spring, "Spring" } }`
- `renames = .{.{ "float", "floating" }}`: Field names C, C++ or Rust cannot take, and what they are called there.
- `Loc = struct { file: []const u8, decl: ?[]const u8, name: []const u8 }`: Where a function is: its file, its container (`null` for the file's top level) and name.
- `containers = .{ frame, context_, widgets, anim_values }`
- `fn Export(comptime c_name: []const u8, comptime f: anytype, comptime api_loc: ?Loc, comptime impl_loc: Loc) type`: One export: its C name, the function that implements it, the Zig function it is (`api`, whose doc comment the header carries; null for a loose one) and where the implementation is (`impl`, whose parameter names the header carries).
- `fn snake(comptime name: []const u8) []const u8`: `fooBar` as `foo_bar`.
- `fn cName(comptime prefix: []const u8, comptime name: []const u8) []const u8`
- `fn exportAll() void`
- `fn isC(comptime f: anytype) bool`: A function written for C already (`callconv(.c)`), exported as it is.

### `Container` (struct)
A container whose `pub fn`s the tripwire checks: each is exported or in `skip`, with why.

Fields: `prefix`, `file`, `decl`, `skip`


### `Export(..)` (generic struct)
One export: its C name, the function that implements it, the Zig function it is (`api`, whose doc comment the header carries; null for a loose one) and where the implementation is (`impl`, whose parameter names the header carries).

- `c = c_name`
- `func = f`
- `api = api_loc`
- `impl = impl_loc`

### `Session` (struct)
What an `lcgf_context` is, at the start of the caller's buffer: the context, the input its frame reads until `lcgf_frame_end` (decoded from the caller's `lcgf_input`, so that may be a temporary), the font the frame is laid out with, and the palettes `lcgf_frame_canvas` makes from its options, in two generations as the canvas rows are (a reused canvas keeps last frame's).

Fields: `ctx`, `input`, `font`, `palettes`, `palette_len`


## `lcgf.abi_view` (src/abi/view.zig)

The inline-push view (docs/plans/bindings-rust.md §6.3): where `Tree.pushRow` and `Frame.enter` store, so C++ and Rust push a row with the stores Zig's inlined `leaf` makes (bindings/cpp/include/lcgf/rows.hpp, bindings/rust/lcgf/src/rows.rs, from tools/abi-gen) and no call.

### Top level

- `depth_bit: u5 = @bitOffsetOf(context.Status, "depth")`: Bit of `CView.status` that `enter` sets when the stack overflows (`Status.depth`).
- `fn of(f: *const Frame) CView`: The view of frame `f`'s tree.

### `CView` (struct)
The frame's tree as `open` and `leaf` write it (`lcgf_view`).

Fields: `len`, `xlen`, `overflow`, `depth`, `status`, `cap`, `depth_cap`, `stack`, `id`, `parent`, `end`, `min`, `max`, `grow`, `pct`, `pad_lo`, `pad_hi`, `offset`, `scroll`, `main`, `gap`, `nkids`, `flow`, `shrink`, `align_main`, `align_cross`, `clips`, `sense`, `look`, `role`, `value`, `text`, `custom`, `extra`, `scope`, `x_node`, `x_row`


## `lcgf.abi_canvas` (src/abi/canvas.zig)

The canvas batch behind the C seam (lcgf.h `lcgf_canvas_*`): `canvas.Instances` in the caller's memory, its builders, `kill`, `clearDirty` and `hit`, for C, C++ (lcgf/runtime.hpp `lcgf::Canvas`) and Rust (`lcgf::runtime::Canvas`).

### Top level

- `CLine = extern struct { layer: u8 = 0, weight: u8 = 1, cap: u8 = 0, dash: u8 = 0, width: u32 = 0 }`: lcgf_canvas_line: segments and polylines.
- `CBox = extern struct { layer: u8 = 0, weight: u8 = 1, fill: u8 = 1, stroke: u8 = 1, radius: u32 = 0, width: u32 = 0 }`: lcgf_canvas_box: rects and circles.
- `CArc = extern struct { layer: u8 = 0, weight: u8 = 1, cap: u8 = 0, dash: u8 = 0, start: f32 = 0, sweep: f32 = std.math.tau, width: u32 = 0 }`: lcgf_canvas_arc.
- `CArea = extern struct { layer: u8 = 0, weight: u8 = 1, fill: u8 = 1, stroke: u8 = 0, width: u32 = 0 }`: lcgf_canvas_area: polygons.
- `CText = extern struct { px: u32, layer: u8 = 0, turns: u8 = 0, pad: [2]u8 = .{ 0, 0 } }`: lcgf_canvas_text: a line of `protocol.Mono` glyphs.
- `CCamera = extern struct { origin: [2]i64 = .{ 0, 0 }, scale: f32 = 1, offset: [2]f32 = .{ 0, 0 }, zoom: f32 = 1 }`: lcgf_camera.

### `COptions` (struct)
lcgf_canvas_options: what `Frame.canvas` and `hit` read, all of it the caller's.

Fields: `camera`, `layers`, `layers_len`, `zoom_scale`, `ranges`, `ranges_len`, `overrides`, `overrides_len`

- `fn cam(o: *const COptions) canvas.Camera`
- `fn palette(o: *const COptions) canvas.Palette`
- `fn spans(o: *const COptions) []const canvas.Range`
- `fn marks(o: *const COptions) []const canvas.Override`

## `lcgf.abi_ct` (src/abi/ct.zig)

Static UIs across the seam: the comptime pipeline (ct_layout, ct_paint) for programs written in C++ or Rust.

### Top level

- `none = types.none`
- `version: u32 = 2`
- `pad_lanes = 16`: Lanes every column is padded for: the widest build reads them too.
- `default_string_cap = 1024`: Bytes of label text one frame holds, by default.
- `ColumnArray = struct { field: []const u8, axis: u32, size: u32 }`: One array of `Tree.Columns`: a `[]T` field, or one axis of a `[k][]T` field.
- `Seg = extern struct { kind: fmt.Kind, prec: u8, _pad: u16 = 0, arg: u32, start: u32, len: u32 }`: One segment of a label's format (src/core/fmt.zig's `Seg`), its argument the field's byte offset in the state.
- `RegionKind = enum(u32) { none, list, when, choice }`
- `Error = error{ OutOfMemory, BadTree, BadLabel, BadRegion, BadBind, Transform }`
- `Widget = struct { row: u32, slider: bool, focusable: bool = true }`: One widget of a static UI: its row, whether it is a slider (else a click target) and whether keyboard focus stops at it.
- `Select = dynamic.Select`: One region's selection this frame (ct_dynamic.Select).
- `frame_symbol = std.fmt.comptimePrint("lcgf_ct_frame_u32_f32_l{d}", .{simd.lanes})`
- `frame_words_symbol = std.fmt.comptimePrint("lcgf_ct_frame_words_u32_f32_l{d}", .{simd.lanes})`
- `cols_len = 2 * column_arrays.len`: The number of column pointers `frameWith`'s `cols` holds.
- `CShortcut = extern struct { code: u8, mods: u8, _pad: u16 = 0, widget: u32 }`: One keyboard shortcut as C lays it out (lcgf.h's `lcgf_shortcut`).
- `Controls = struct { title: []const u8 = "Volume", muted: bool = false, volume: f32 = 0.5, pan: f32 = 0 }`: Sized controls, for the events gate: the examples' checkboxes and sliders have no size of their own, so nothing can hit them.
- `Palette = struct { title: []const u8 = "Palette", accent: types.Color = .rgb(200, 40, 40), edge: types.Color = .rgb(40, 40, 200) }`: Colours patched into quads, for the patch gate: a swatch filled with `accent` and a bar filled with `accent` and bordered with `edge` (ct_paint.Patches).
- `Meter = struct { name: []const u8 = "Gain", level: f32 = 0.5, count: u32 = 3, on: bool = true, peak: f32 = 0 }`: Formatted labels, for the format gate: every argument kind (string, f32 at two precisions, u32, bool), braces, and a slider in the options form.
- `Bind = struct { field: []const u8, node: u32, border: bool = false }`: A patch binding (ct_paint.Patches' `Binding`), by field name.
- `fn rowsMax(n: usize) usize`: Rows of a column of `n` rows for any lane count up to `pad_lanes`: whole vectors plus one spare, as `simd.rows` gives for the widest.
- `fn written(name: []const u8, shows: bool, live: bool, windows: bool) bool`: Whether a static UI's frame writes column `name` (ct_layout.Plain's `writes` for a UI with no regions): the layout scratch but the precomputed gap totals, the outputs, `sense` (finalize clears its padding rows, so it is copied in each frame) and `value` when a checkbox or slider shows a state field.
- `fn headerOf(comptime words: []const u32) Header`: The header of `words`, read while compiling.
- `fn nodesOf(comptime State: type, comptime spec: spec_zig.Spec) [@import("../comptime/flatten.zig").count(spec)]Node`: The rows of a comptime spec, with labels bound to `State`'s fields.
- `fn coordBits(viewport: ?[2]f32) u32`: The coordinate bits for a fixed screen (columns.Coord): 0 (f32) without one.
- `fn make(arena: std.mem.Allocator, nodes: []const Node, o: Options) Error![]u32`: The words of the static UI of `nodes`, in `arena`.
- `fn exportAll() void`
- `fn cFrameWords(words: [*]const u32, len: usize, cols: ?[*]const [*]const u8, buf: [*]align(mem.column_align) u8, buf_len: usize, state: [*]const u8, width: f…`: `cFrame` with the words passed in and every table read from them: what a frame specialized to constant words calls (exported by src/abi/ct_c.zig only).
- `fn frameInto(comptime words: []const u32, cols: ?[*]const [*]const u8, buf: []align(mem.column_align) u8, state: [*]const u8, viewport: [2]f32, out: *anyopaq…`: One frame of the UI `init` placed in `buf`, specialized to `words` and `cols` when they are constants (`Ui.frameWith`), its draw list kept for the other C functions and written to `out` (an `lcgf_draw`).
- `fn bindingsOf(comptime name: []const u8) []const Bind`: The bindings of a static example.
- `fn Patched(comptime e: anytype) type`: ct_paint.Patches over `e`'s bindings (`bindingsOf`): what the static UI's frame paints through.
- `fn gatherText(comptime State: type, comptime nodes: []const Node, state: *const State, buf: []u8, spans: []Span) u32`: The labels' text of `state`, as the static UI's frame gathers it: each label's string field, or its format written (src/core/fmt.zig), in label order into `buf`, cut where `buf` ends.
- `fn stateFor(comptime State: type, f: u32, text: []const []const u8) State`: The state of frame `f` of the gates (here and bindings/check/): string field k shows `texts[(f + 3k) mod len]`, a bool is `(f + k) mod 3 != 0`, a count alternates between small (`(f + k) mod 11`) and large (`(7919f + k) mod 200000`), an enum(u8) is `(f + k) mod 2`, an f32 (a slider's value, a list's scroll) `((131f + 17k) mod 5000) * 0.75` and a colour `colorFor(f, k)`.
- `fn colorFor(f: u32, k: u32) u32`: Colour field k of frame f's state (rgba bits): r (13f + 40k) mod 256, g 7f mod 256, b 90k mod 256, opaque.
- `fn cStateOf(comptime State: type, s: *const State, fields: []const plan.Field, buf: []align(8) u8) void`: `s` as C lays out the generated state struct (`plan.layoutState`).
- `fn wordsOf(arena: std.mem.Allocator, comptime e: examples.Example) ![]u32`: The words of example `e`, as lcgf-gen makes them from its `.zon`.

### `Block` (struct)
One tree of the words: the whole UI, or the skeleton its passes run over.

Fields: `n`, `xlen`, `cols`, `extras`, `sense`, `label_rows`, `regions`, `max_src`, `flow_src`, `scroll_src`


### `Region` (struct)
One dynamic region (ct_dynamic.Info) and where its selector is in the state.

Fields: `kind`, `row`, `end`, `stride`, `max`, `alts`, `field_offset`, `field_type`, `scroll_offset`, `height`


### `Header` (struct)
The first words.

### `Ui` (struct)
A static UI, placed at the start of the caller's buffer by `init`.

Fields: `words`, `h`, `full`, `skel`, `tx`, `spans`, `strings`, `sel`, `slot_source`, `d`, `quad_count`, `label_count`, `batch_count`, `node`, `clip_node`, `templates`, `keys`, `label_plans`, `batch_first`, `batch_counts`, `batch_key`, `alpha`, `clip_radii`, `input`, `dirty`, `rects`, `widget_rows`, `widget_sliders`, `focus_slots`, `focus_pos`

- `InitError = error{ NotTables, Version, LayoutHash, Truncated, BufferTooSmall, Unaligned }`
- `fn check(words: []const u32) InitError!*const Header`: Checks the header of `words`.
- `fn bytes(words: []const u32) InitError!usize`: Bytes one UI of `words` needs, for any lane count.
- `fn init(words: []const u32, buf: []u8) InitError!*Ui`: Checks `words` and places a UI at the start of `buf` (aligned to `mem.column_align`).
- `fn frame(ui: *Ui, state: [*]const u8, viewport: [2]f32) *const DrawList`: One frame: the labels' text and the regions' selectors from `state`, laid out in a `viewport`-sized area and painted.
- `fn frameWith(words: []const u32, cols: ?[*]const [*]const u8, buf: []align(mem.column_align) u8, state: [*]const u8, viewport: [2]f32) DrawList`: One frame of the UI `init` placed in `buf`, every table read from `words` alone (none from the `Ui` in `buf`): inlined, so a caller that passes constant words gets the frame specialized to them, as Zig's comptime pipeline is to its tables.
- `fn frameKnown(comptime words: []const u32, cols: ?[*]const [*]const u8, buf: []align(mem.column_align) u8, state: [*]const u8, viewport: [2]f32) DrawList`: `frameWith` over words known while compiling (lcgf-gen --static --lang zig): the labels' text is written as straight-line code too.
- `fn relayout(ui: *Ui, state: [*]const u8, field: u32) bool`: Lays out again only what state field `field` moves (ct_layout's `relayout`), after a frame: nothing when no label shows it; the field's dirty span (`dirtySpan`) when the UI does not fold and the span is not the whole tree without regions; else the whole frame again in last frame's viewport and selection.
- `fn patch(ui: *Ui, state: [*]const u8, field: u32) void`: The colour field `field`'s rgb into its quads (ct_paint.Patches.apply): each changed quad becomes dirty.
- `fn dirtyQuads(ui: *Ui) []const u8`: The quads a patch changed since `clearDirty`, one byte each; all of them after a frame.
- `fn clearDirty(ui: *Ui) void`
- `fn widgetAt(ui: *Ui, x: f32, y: f32) u32`: The topmost widget under (x, y) in last frame's layout (ct_events.at), as its row, or `none`.
- `fn pointer(ui: *Ui, in: *const Input) ?ct_events.Hit`: This frame's pointer event (ct_events.pointer), its widget as a row.
- `fn focusKeys(ui: *Ui, in: *const Input, tab: u8) void`: Moves focus for every Tab press and repeat in `in` (ct_events.focusKeys).
- `fn nextFocus(ui: *const Ui, from: u32, back: bool) u32`: The widget Tab (or Shift+Tab, `back`) moves focus to from widget row `from` (`none`: no focus), as a row (ct_events.nextFocus).
- `fn focus(ui: *const Ui) u32`: The focused widget's row, or `none`.
- `fn setFocus(ui: *Ui, row: u32) void`
- `fn rectOf(ui: *const Ui, _: void, i: u32) V4`: Rect of row i as the comptime UI keeps it: in its coordinate type.
- `fn clipOf(ui: *const Ui, _: void, i: u32) [4]f32`
- `fn sourceOf(_: *const Ui, i: u32) u32`
- `fn outsetOf(_: *const Ui, _: u32) [4]f32`
- `fn worldOf(_: *const Ui, _: void, _: u32) [6]f32`
- `fn paramsOf(ui: *const Ui, i: u32) [4]f32`
- `fn parentOf(ui: *const Ui, i: u32) u32`
- `fn presentOf(ui: *const Ui, _: void, i: u32) u32`
- `fn visibleOf(ui: *const Ui, _: void, i: u32) u32`
- `fn paintsOf(_: *const Ui, _: u32) u32`
- `fn alphaOf(ui: *const Ui, i: u32) f32`
- `fn clipRadiiOf(ui: *const Ui, i: u32) [4]f32`
- `fn liveOf(ui: *const Ui, mask: []const u32, i: u32) u32`

### `Patched(..)` (generic struct)
ct_paint.Patches over `e`'s bindings (`bindingsOf`): what the static UI's frame paints through.


## `lcgf.ct_tables` (src/comptime/tables.zig)

The tables of a comptime UI as pure functions over arrays.

### Top level

- `none = types.none`
- `Fold = struct { folded_rows: usize }`: What a fold plan decides: see `foldPlan`.
- `Label = extern struct { node: u32, parent: u32, quad_end: u32, padding: [4]f32, color: u32 }`: How a label's run is placed among the sorted quads.
- `fn gapTotal(p: anytype, n: usize, out: []f32) void`: `prepare`'s gap totals: the gap times the in-flow child count less one.
- `fn foldPlan(p: anytype, n: usize, labels: []const u32, volatile_rows: ?[]const bool, has_label: []bool, indep: []bool, root: []bool, inside: []bool) Fold`: Which rows fold (ct_layout.FoldPlan).
- `fn skeletonRows(n: usize, inside: []const bool, skel_row: []u32) u32`: The skeleton rows of a fold: row j of the UI is skeleton row `skel_row[j]`, or `none` for a row inside a fold root.
- `fn foldedLeaf(s: types.Style, size: [2]f32) types.Style`: The style a fold root becomes in the skeleton: a leaf of its laid-out size.
- `fn expand(n: usize, root: []const bool, inside: []const bool, pos: [2][]const f32, size: [2][]const f32, base: []u32, rel: [2][]f32, out_size: [2][]f32, keep…`: For each row: the skeleton row its position starts from (itself, or its fold root), the constant offset from there, and, for folded rows, its constant size and 0 in `keep_size` (1 for skeleton rows, whose size comes from the passes).
- `fn inherit(p: anytype, alpha: []f32, clip_radii: [][4]f32) void`: `draw.inherit`'s opacity and clip sweeps over the extras rows: `alpha[r]` and `clip_radii[r]` for extras row r (`p.xlen` rows).
- `fn keyOf(look: tree.Look) u32`
- `fn paintRows(p: anytype, n: usize, alpha: []const f32, out: []u32) usize`: The rows that paint a quad, in row order: `paint.emit`'s condition without `visible`.
- `fn foldGroups(p: anytype, n: usize, root: []const bool, group: []u32) void`: The fold root each row lies under (itself for a root), or `none`.
- `fn sortByKey(p: anytype, rows: []const u32, group: []const u32, pos: [2][]const f32, size: [2][]const f32, out: []u32, done: []bool) void`: `rows` (the nodes that paint, in row order) reordered so that equal batch keys follow each other where that cannot change the picture (see ct_paint's `sortByKey`).
- `fn paintOrder(n: usize, in_row_order: []const u32, painted: []const u32, out: []u32) void`: Every descriptor in paint order: sorted quads occupy the original quad positions; other rows keep theirs.
- `fn labelPlans(p: anytype, order: []const u32, painted: []const u32, labels: []const u32, alpha: []const f32, out: []Label) void`: The labels' runs: each a boundary in the sorted quad stream, after the quads painted before it (its own background included).
- `fn templates(p: anytype, painted: []const u32, alpha: []const f32, clip_radii: []const [4]f32, out: []Quad) void`: Template k: the quad `painted[k]` paints, `rect` and `clip` zero.
- `fn batches(p: anytype, painted: []const u32, keys: []u32, first: []u32, count: []u32, key: []u32) usize`: Batch keys of the templates, and the commands when every template is visible: consecutive templates with equal keys form one batch.
- `fn batchCount(p: anytype, painted: []const u32) usize`: Batch count of `painted` without writing the batches.
- `fn boundaries(p: anytype, labels: []const u32, out: []u32) void`: The relayout boundary of each label (ct_layout.Plain): its nearest ancestor, or itself, fixed on both axes (`min == max`), else the root.
- `fn dirtySpan(p: anytype, bounds: []const u32, changed: []const bool) ?[2]u32`: The rows to lay out again when the labels marked in `changed` change: the subtree of the lowest row whose subtree holds every one of their boundaries.

## `lcgf.embedded_framebuffer` (src/embedded/framebuffer.zig)

RGB565 and 1-bit framebuffers for the embedded profile.

### Top level

- `Format = enum { rgb565, mono }`
- `fn Unit(comptime format: Format) type`: The storage unit of a row: a pixel, or for `mono` a byte of eight.
- `fn units(comptime format: Format, width: u32) u32`: Units a row of `width` pixels takes.
- `fn color(p: u32, src: sw.Format) Color`: Pixel `p` of a framebuffer in `src` byte order as a colour.
- `fn pack565(c: Color) u16`
- `fn unpack565(p: u16) Color`: Each channel widened back to 8 bits by repeating its top bits, so white stays 255.
- `fn lit(c: Color) u1`: 1 when `c` is white on a 1-bit panel.
- `fn convert(comptime format: Format, dst: []Unit(format), src: []const u32, n: u32, order: sw.Format) void`: Converts `n` pixels of `src` (sw pixels in `order`) into `dst`.
- `fn Framebuffer(comptime format: Format) type`: A whole panel in `format`, rows `stride` units apart.

The storage unit of a row: a pixel, or for `mono` a byte of eight.
